### [1] [Budget-Aware Adaptive Adversarial Patches for Black-Box Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18318)
*Pedram MohajerAnsari,Amir Salarpour,David Fernandez,Mert D. Pesé*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种查询高效、预算自适应的黑盒对抗补丁攻击方法，通过耦合上下文汤普森采样定位器与NES式像素更新，在严格查询预算下联合优化补丁的位置、纹理和尺寸，并在CNN和Transformer检测器上验证了其有效性及物理世界可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗补丁研究存在三个关键差距：(1) 缺乏在严格查询预算下联合优化补丁位置、纹理和尺寸的基于分数的黑盒攻击；(2) 攻击成功率很少与补丁的视觉足迹关联分析；(3) 评估常混淆期望变换鲁棒性与纯图像抑制效果。本文旨在弥合这些差距，提供可操作的洞察。

Method: 提出一种预算自适应黑盒攻击方法，核心组件包括：(1) 轻量级上下文汤普森采样定位器，用于智能选择补丁放置位置；(2) NES风格的像素值更新策略；(3) 自适应尺寸增长机制，仅在优化停滞时扩大补丁面积。评估采用严格的纯图像抑制测试，EOT仅作为辅助审计指标而非成功替代标准，并通过可选的外观/可打印性权重揭示强度与可见性之间的权衡。

Result: 在YOLOv5、Faster R-CNN和YOLOS上，该方法对CNN检测器实现了强抑制效果，对Transformer检测器取得了显著抑制效果，使用紧凑补丁即可达成。与固定尺寸和启发式基线相比，清晰展示了查询预算与视觉足迹之间的权衡关系。打印-拍摄试点实验进一步证明了对未见物理物体和视角的跨域迁移能力。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了对抗补丁攻击在联合优化、视觉足迹关联分析和评估标准方面的空白，所提方法在有限查询预算下高效生成紧凑且有效的对抗补丁，为理解目标检测器鲁棒性提供了更全面视角，并验证了物理世界攻击的可行性。

Abstract: Adversarial patches pose a practical threat to modern object detectors. Prior work shows vulnerability, but three gaps limit actionable insight: (i) few \emph{score-based black-box} attacks \emph{jointly} optimize patch \emph{location, texture, and size} under tight query budgets; (ii) success is rarely tied to the patch's \emph{visual footprint}; and (iii) evaluations often conflate EOT robustness with plain-view suppression. We present \method{}, a query-efficient, budget-adaptive black-box attack that couples a lightweight \emph{Contextual Thompson-Sampling} placer with NES-style pixel updates, growing the patch only when progress stalls. Reporting is anchored by a \emph{strict plain-image} suppression test; EOT is audited but never used as a substitute for success, and optional appearance/printability weights expose strength--visibility trade-offs. Across YOLOv5, Faster R-CNN, and YOLOS, \method{} achieves strong suppression on CNN-based detectors and substantial suppression on the transformer-based detector, using compact patches and exposing clear query--footprint trade-offs relative to fixed-size and heuristic baselines. A print--capture pilot further shows transfer across unseen physical objects and viewpoints.

</details>


### [2] [CAOA -- Completion-Assisted Object-CAD Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18429)
*Hiranya Garbha Kumar,Minhas Kamal,Balakrishnan Prabhakaran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CAOA方法通过结合语义感知的点云补全与对称感知的相对位姿估计，显著提升了室内RGB-D扫描中CAD模型与真实物体的9自由度对齐精度，在Scan2CAD基准上较最优方法提高17%准确率。同时发布了面向真实场景补全的S2C-Completion数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内RGB-D扫描存在噪声、不完整及分割错误导致的几何畸变，使得CAD模型与扫描物体的9自由度（位置、旋转、三轴尺度）精确对齐极具挑战。现有点云补全方法在合成数据上训练，难以泛化至真实扫描；同时对齐任务缺乏对对称性歧义的鲁棒处理。

Method: 提出CAOA框架：(1) 设计面向室内场景的合成数据生成策略，缩小合成到真实的域差异；(2) 构建语义与上下文感知的点云补全模块；(3) 引入对称感知损失函数，将物体对称性信息融入相对位姿估计算法；(4) 发布S2C-Completion数据集，含来自Scan2CAD的8500余对真实室内单物体补全标注。

Result: 合成数据生成策略在定量对比中显著缩小了域差距；在Scan2CAD基准上，CAOA实现了较现有最优方法17%的对齐准确率提升。

Conclusion: CAOA通过补全辅助与对称感知两大核心设计，有效解决了真实扫描中CAD对齐的核心难题。S2C-Completion数据集为单物体补全提供了新的真实场景基准，有望推动该领域进一步研究。

Abstract: Accurately aligning CAD models to their corresponding objects in indoor RGB-D scans is a central challenge in 3D semantic reconstruction. The task requires estimating a 9-Degree-of-Freedom (DoF) pose-position, rotation, and scale along three axes-but is hindered by noisy and incomplete scans, as well as segmentation errors that cause geometric distortions. We present Completion-Assisted Object-CAD Alignment (CAOA), a method that integrates a semantically and contextually aware point cloud completion module with a symmetry-aware relative pose estimation algorithm, enabling precise alignment of CAD models to scanned objects. Existing completion methods are typically trained and evaluated on synthetic datasets, which often fail to generalize to real-world scans. To bridge this gap, we introduce a synthetic data generation strategy tailored to indoor scenes, significantly reducing the synthetic-to-real domain gap-validated through quantitative comparisons with widely used completion datasets. In addition, we release S2C-Completion, an expert-annotated dataset of over 8,500 object-CAD pairs from Scan2CAD, created for real-world indoor single-object completion and intended as a new benchmark for this task. For object-CAD alignment, we incorporate symmetry information via a symmetry-aware loss, improving robustness to symmetric ambiguities. On the Scan2CAD benchmark, CAOA achieves a 17% accuracy improvement over state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [3] [RegimeVGGT: Layer-Wise Spatially Preserving Redundancy Removal for Visual Geometry Grounded Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18439)
*Jinhao You,Shuo Lyu,Zhuohang Lyu,Tanxuan Li,Zibo Zhao,Jiaxiang Hu,Kai Tang,Yichen Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RegimeVGGT通过分层分析发现VGGT的浅层、中层、深层存在三种不同机制，据此提出沿两个轴进行U型压缩：显著性引导的带状合并保护几何与边缘token，选择性保护的K/V下采样保留跨帧空间覆盖与位姿关键路径，实现6.7倍加速且重建质量不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VGGT可从多视图图像一次性恢复稠密3D场景结构，但其跨帧注意力具有二次计算复杂度，限制了可扩展性。现有免训练加速方法沿单一维度均匀削减计算，忽略了不同层的异质性。

Method: 首先通过谱分析、探针分析和因果分析揭示VGGT的三阶段机制：(1)浅层缺乏跨视图结构；(2)中层驱动跨视图对齐；(3)深层对稠密几何冗余但跨帧注意力对位姿仍关键。基于此提出RegimeVGGT，沿两个轴进行层状U型压缩：轴一为显著性引导的带状合并（Saliency-Guided Banded Merging），保护几何显著和边缘显著token；轴二为选择性保护的K/V下采样（Selectively Protected K/V Downsampling），通过相位偏移空间网格、参考帧锚点以及未压缩的相机/寄存器token来保留跨帧空间覆盖和位姿关键路径。

Result: RegimeVGGT免训练，在匹配的重建质量下，相较VGGT*实现6.7倍加速。

Conclusion: 不同层在视觉几何任务中扮演异质角色，针对各层的差异化压缩策略能在不损失精度的情况下大幅提升推理效率。

Abstract: Visual Geometry Grounded Transformer (VGGT) recovers dense 3D scene structure from multi-view images in one forward pass, but quadratic cross-frame attention limits its scalability. Existing training-free accelerators reduce computation uniformly along one axis, missing layer heterogeneity. Our spectral, probing, and causal analyses reveal three regimes: shallow layers lack cross-view structure, middle layers drive cross-view alignment, and deep layers are redundant for dense geometry yet their cross-frame attention remains essential for pose. RegimeVGGT applies layer-wise U-shaped compression along two axes: Saliency-Guided Banded Merging protects geometry- and edge-salient tokens, while Selectively Protected K/V Downsampling preserves cross-frame spatial coverage and the pose-critical path through a phase-shifted spatial grid, a reference-frame anchor, and uncompressed camera/register tokens. Training-free, RegimeVGGT achieves a 6.7x speedup over VGGT* at matched reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [4] [Reasoning as Intersection: Consensus-Frame Alignment for Visual Focus in Video-MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18441)
*Chengwen Liu,Zhe Huang,Jisheng Dang,Hong Peng,Qi Tian,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CF-GRPO（Consensus Frame GRPO），一种无需人工时序标注的、针对视频多模态大语言模型的过程级奖励框架。其核心思想是受多感官整合启发，通过从视频内在线索（时序覆盖、场景转场、查询相关度）构建"共识帧先验"，并与模型自身的帧使用评分计算一致性奖励（CFR），从而引导模型关注支持答案的关键视觉证据。实验表明VideoCFR在复杂视频推理基准上取得了有竞争力的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在视频多模态大语言模型的强化学习训练中，仅使用结果奖励（outcome-only reward）无法为模型指明"哪些视觉帧应当支撑答案"，缺乏对推理过程中视觉证据使用的细粒度引导。

Method: 提出CF-GRPO框架：(1) 从视频内在线索构建"共识帧先验"——综合时序覆盖度、场景转接线索和查询条件化的视觉相关性；(2) 从模型的视觉和响应表征中计算"帧使用评分"；(3) 通过一致性帧奖励（CFR）优化先验与模型评分之间的一致性；(4) 引入显著性感知的稀疏聚合与分布锐化，产生高对比度奖励信号。整个过程无需人工时序标注。

Result: VideoCFR在多个复杂视频推理基准上取得有竞争力的性能，在若干指标上超越了代表性的Video-MLLM和RL基线方法；同时，共识先验提供了对训练中强调的证据帧的可解释视角。

Conclusion: CF-GRPO通过无监督方式构建的共识帧先验与过程级一致性奖励，有效弥补了结果奖励在视频推理中缺乏细粒度视觉证据引导的不足，在性能提升与可解释性方面均展现出优势。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has improved the reasoning ability of large language models, but applying outcome-only rewards to video multimodal large language models (Video-MLLMs) provides limited guidance on which visual evidence should support the answer. Inspired by multisensory integration, where consistent cues can enhance the salience and reliability of perceptual estimates, we introduce Consensus Frame GRPO (CF-GRPO), a temporal-annotation-free process-level reward framework for evidence-aware video reasoning. CF-GRPO constructs a consensus frame prior from intrinsic video cues, including temporal coverage, scene-transition cues, and query-conditioned visual relevance. It then computes a model-side frame-use score from visual and response representations and optimizes their agreement through the Consensus Frame Reward (CFR). With salience-aware sparse aggregation and distribution sharpening, CFR provides a high-contrast reward signal without requiring human temporal annotations. Experiments show that VideoCFR achieves competitive performance across complex video reasoning benchmarks and improves several metrics over representative Video-MLLM and RL baselines, while the consensus prior provides an interpretable view of the evidence frames emphasized during training. The implementation is available at https://github.com/1Pansy/VideoCFR.

</details>


### [5] [Domain Generalizable Adaptation of 3D Vision-Language Models via Regularized Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18472)
*Sneha Paul,Zachary Patterson,Nizar Bouguila*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReFine3D 是一种面向3D大语言多模态模型的领域泛化微调框架，通过选择性层微调、多视角一致性正则化、同义词文本增强、点渲染视觉监督及测试时增强等策略，在多个3D领域泛化基准上全面超越先前最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前3D多模态基础模型在下游领域数据有限时微调，容易出现过拟合和灾难性遗忘问题，亟需一种能够保持领域泛化能力的微调方法。

Method: ReFine3D 包含五方面设计：(1) 选择性层微调，仅调优关键层以减少过拟合；(2) 增强点云的多视角一致性正则化；(3) 利用LLM生成同义词提示实现文本多样性正则化；(4) 引入点渲染视觉监督信号；(5) 测试时增强与基于置信度的聚合机制。

Result: 在3D领域泛化基准上，ReFine3D 实现基础到新类泛化提升1.36%，跨数据集迁移提升2.43%，鲁棒性（抗腐蚀）提升1.80%，少样本准确率提升最高达3.11%，且计算开销极小。

Conclusion: ReFine3D 通过多种正则化策略的协同，有效缓解了3D大模型在下游微调中的过拟合与遗忘问题，在多项泛化指标上均达到SOTA水平，为3D多模态模型的领域适应提供了高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Domain adaptation remains a central challenge in 3D vision, especially for multimodal foundation models that align 3D point clouds with visual and textual data. While these models demonstrate strong general capabilities, adapting them to downstream domains with limited data often leads to overfitting and catastrophic forgetting. To address this, we introduce ReFine3D, a regularized fine-tuning framework designed for domain-generalizable tuning of 3D large multimodal models (LMMs). ReFine3D combines selective layer tuning with two targeted regularization strategies: multi-view consistency across augmented point clouds and text diversity through synonym-based prompts generated by large language models. Additionally, we incorporate point-rendered vision supervision and a test-time augmentation mechanism with confidence-based aggregation to further enhance robustness. Extensive experiments across different 3D domain generalization benchmarks show that ReFine3D improves base-to-novel class generalization by 1.36%, cross-dataset transfer by 2.43%, robustness to corruption by 1.80%, and few-shot accuracy by up to 3.11%, outperforming prior state-of-the-art methods with minimal added computational overhead.

</details>


### [6] [Data-Forcing Distillation: Restoring Diversity and Fidelity in Few-Step Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18478)
*Siyi Chen,Shaowei Liu,Yixuan Jia,Zian Wang,Huan Ling,Qing Qu,Jun Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DFD 是一种简单的后训练框架，仅需一行代码改动即可修复 DMD 系列方法中的多样性下降和过饱和伪影问题，通过教师分数差异引导学生走向真实数据分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DMD 及其变体 DMD2 虽在视频扩散模型蒸馏中取得了良好效果，但由于其反向 KL 散度目标，存在两个持续失败模式：(1) 样本多样性显著下降（模式坍缩）；(2) 输出过饱和，偏离真实视频外观。需要一种简单有效的后训练方法来恢复多样性和保真度。

Method: 提出 Data-Forcing Distillation (DFD)，核心是引入教师分数差异（teacher score discrepancy），通过该差异引导学生在后训练中：(1) 拉向缺失模式，缓解模式坍缩；(2) 推离真实数据中不存在的问题模式，避免过饱和。该方法仅需一行代码改动，在 DMD 基础上进行 100–300 步微调即可。

Result: 在 Wan2.1-1.3B 和 Cosmos-Predict2.5-2B 模型上，DFD 仅需 100–300 步微调即可有效恢复多样性和保真度，消除过饱和伪影，显著改善视频动态和外观，甚至超越教师模型。方法在文生视频、图生视频和自回归视频生成三个任务上均得到验证。

Conclusion: DFD 提供了一种理论扎实且实现极简的解决方案，有效修复了基于反向 KL 散度的视频扩散蒸馏方法中的多样性和过饱和问题，展示了教师分数差异作为引导信号的强大潜力。

Abstract: Recent progress has shown promise in distilling multi-step video diffusion models into efficient few-step students. Among them, Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) and its successor DMD2 achieved strong generation quality and fast convergence. However, due to the nature of the reverse Kullback--Leibler (KL) objective, these methods exhibit two persistent failure modes: a substantial drop in sample diversity, and visibly over-saturated outputs that deviate from real-video appearance. In this work, we propose Data-Forcing Distillation (DFD), a simple post-training framework that restores diversity and fidelity in DMD with only a single-line of code change. At its core is the teacher score discrepancy to guide the student toward the real-data distribution, pulling it to missing modes (mitigating mode collapse) and away from problematic modes absent in real data (avoiding over-saturation). We provide an in-depth theoretical analysis of our framework and validate our approach on text-to-video, image-to-video, and autoregressive video generation. With only 100--300 steps of finetuning, DFD effectively restores diversity and fidelity on both Wan2.1-1.3B and Cosmos-Predict2.5-2B model, resolving the over-saturation artifacts with significantly better video dynamics and appearance, and even outperforms the teacher model.

</details>


### [7] [Vines-DB: An RGB image dataset for multi-species ornamental vine segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18484)
*Saroj Burlakoti,Utsav Bhandari,Aaron Etienne,Shital Poudyal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Vines-DB 是一个包含 1,218 张高分辨率 RGB 图像的七种观赏藤本植物实例分割数据集，在真实田间条件下采集，经标注和增强后扩展至 2,307 张，用于精准园艺和城市生态中的深度学习模型开发与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏在真实田间条件下、涵盖多物种和多时间节点的观赏藤本植物实例分割数据集，限制了精准园艺中自动化冠层覆盖估算、物种识别和田间表型分析的深度学习模型发展。

Method: 在犹他州 Greenville 研究农场种植 168 株七种藤本植物（2022年移栽），于 2023-2024 年生长季（7-10月）每月重复拍摄。使用 iPhone 16 Pro（48 MP）在上午 10 点至中午、1 米距离、黑白泡沫板背景下拍摄。在 Roboflow 平台进行多边形实例分割人工标注（八类：七种植物+背景），经预处理和数据增强后，按分层抽样划分为训练集（2,019张）、验证集（192张）和测试集（96张）。

Result: 构建了 Vines-DB 数据集：原始 1,218 张图像，增强后 2,307 张，含七种藤本植物的多边形实例分割掩码。数据集捕获了跨月份的时间变异，平衡的类别分布通过分层抽样得以保持。

Conclusion: Vines-DB 为多类实例分割提供了首个覆盖多物种、多时间节点的田间观赏藤本植物基准数据集，可支撑冠层覆盖自动估算、物种识别和可扩展田间表型分析等应用，并可用于真实田间条件下的分割模型鲁棒性评估。

Abstract: The Vines-DB dataset contains 1,218 original high-resolution RGB images of seven ornamental vine species collected under field conditions at the Utah Agricultural Experiment Station's Greenville Research Farm in Logan, Utah, USA. The dataset was generated from 168 individual vine plants that were transplanted in 2022 and photographed repeatedly across multiple months during the 2023 and 2024 growing seasons (July-October). Images were captured with an iPhone 16 Pro equipped with a 48 MP camera between 10:00 AM and 12:00 PM under daylight. Vines were grown on 1.2m x 2.4m trellises and photographed from a distance of 1m against black or white Styrofoam backdrops to improve contrast and reduce background noise. The dataset includes Akebia quinata, Campsis radicans, Hydrangea anomala petiolaris, Lonicera x heckrottii, Campsis x tagliabuana 'Madame Galen', Parthenocissus quinquefolia, and Wisteria floribunda. All original images were manually annotated in Roboflow by trained annotators to produce polygon-based instance segmentation masks for eight classes, including seven species and background. After preprocessing and data augmentation, the working dataset was expanded to 2,307 images for model development and evaluation. The augmented dataset was divided into 2,019 training images, 192 validation images, and 96 test images using stratified sampling to maintain balanced representation. Vines-DB supports the development and evaluation of deep learning models for multi-class instance segmentation in precision horticulture and urban ecology. The dataset enables applications such as automated canopy cover estimation, species identification, and scalable field phenotyping. In addition, repeated monthly imaging of the plants captures temporal variation in canopy development and plant appearance, increasing the dataset's utility for segmentation benchmarking under realistic field conditions.

</details>


### [8] [Neural Phase Correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18496)
*Cole Reynolds*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可学习的相位相关（phase correlation）泛化框架，通过学习变换分解的基函数，将传统仅限于全局平移的相位相关方法扩展到密集非刚性变形和酉动力学。在心脏MRI和超声心动图配准任务上达到或超越现有方法，并能在量子谐振子波函数对中恢复本征态和能级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相位相关方法直接在傅里叶域中度量图像间关系，但其固定的基函数限制了它只能处理全局平移。而主流基于学习的方法将每幅图像独立编码，变换本身并非架构中的"一等公民"。作者旨在融合两者优势：既保持相位相关直接建模图像间关系的代数特性，又通过学习基函数来突破刚性变换的限制。

Method: 提出可学习的广义相位相关（learned generalization of phase correlation），核心思想是学习变换分解所在的基函数，而非使用固定的傅里叶基。该代数原语可扩展至密集非刚性变形和酉动力学（unitary dynamics）。框架保持相位相关"直接建模图像间关系"的本质，同时具备灵活性。

Result: 在ACDC心脏MRI基准上，该框架在双向配准任务中匹配或超越已有基线方法；在CAMUS超声心动图上达到当前最优水平，且无需辅助评分或自适应平滑机制；在1-D量子谐振子波函数对演化任务中，仅从观测对即可恢复Hermite函数本征态和量子化能级。

Conclusion: 通过学习基函数来泛化相位相关，能够将这种直接建模图像间关系的代数方法有效扩展到复杂变形和动力学场景，在医学图像配准和物理系统识别中均展现出强大潜力。

Abstract: Correspondence is fundamentally relational: it seeks the unknown transformation between two observations of a common scene, not the content of either. Yet the dominant learning-based methods do not represent the transformation as a first-class object in the architecture. They encode each image independently and let a learned similarity function or a deep decoder discover the mapping implicitly. Phase correlation is the canonical exception, measuring the inter-image relationship directly in the Fourier domain, but the rigidity of its fixed basis confines it to global translation.
  We introduce a learned generalization of phase correlation that lifts this restriction by learning the basis on which the transformation decomposes. The same algebraic primitive extends to dense non-rigid deformations and to unitary dynamics. On the ACDC cardiac-MRI benchmark the framework matches or exceeds prior published baselines on both registration directions. On CAMUS echocardiography it matches state-of-the-art without auxiliary scoring or adaptive-smoothness mechanisms. Applied to time-evolved wavefunction pairs of the 1-D quantum harmonic oscillator, the same framework recovers the Hermite-function eigenstates and the quantized energy levels of the unknown Hamiltonian from observation pairs alone.

</details>


### [9] [Architectural Bias in Face Presentation Attack Detection: A Comparative Study of Vision Transformers and Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18510)
*Ngela Landon Ntung,Floride Tuyisenge,Jema David Ndibwile*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文比较了Vision Transformer与CNN在人脸活体检测中的跨人口群体公平性表现，发现预训练的DeiT-S在准确率、公平性和零样本泛化能力上均显著优于ResNet18基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人脸活体检测系统在不同人口群体间存在系统性性能差异，尤其对深肤色个体不利。本文旨在探究Vision Transformer架构是否能相较于卷积神经网络减少这种人口统计学偏差。

Method: 在CASIA-SURF CeFA数据集上，针对非洲、东亚及零样本中亚三个群体，对比评估三种架构：从头训练的多模态ViT-Tiny、ResNet18 CNN基线，以及在CeFA上微调的预训练DeiT-S。

Result: DeiT-S取得最高总体准确率97.27%和最低EER 0.86%，优于ResNet18的90.15%。公平性方面，DeiT-S将非裔与东亚被试间的ACER差距缩小至0.13%，较此前LBP方法的0.75%降低83%。关键的是，在零样本中亚群体上，ResNet18的BPCER为10.44%，而DeiT-S仅2.89%，泛化优势达3.6倍。

Conclusion: 预训练Vision Transformer在PAD中实现了更高准确率、更小的群体间性能差距以及更公平的跨群体泛化，表明架构设计可能是影响人脸PAD跨人口公平性的重要因素。

Abstract: Face Presentation Attack Detection (PAD) systems constitute a critical security layer in biometric authentication; however, existing approaches exhibit systematic performance disparities across demographic groups, disproportionately affecting individuals with darker skin tones. This paper presents a comparative empirical investigation of whether Vision Transformer architectures reduce demographic bias in face PAD systems relative to convolutional baselines. Experiments are conducted on the CASIA-SURF Cross-Ethnicity Face Anti-Spoofing (CeFA) dataset. Three architectures are evaluated: a Multimodal ViT-Tiny trained from scratch, a ResNet18 CNN baseline, and a pretrained DeiT-S fine-tuned on CeFA across African, East Asian, and zero-shot Central Asian demographic groups. DeiT-S achieves the highest overall accuracy of 97.27% and the lowest EER of 0.86%, outperforming ResNet18 at 90.15% accuracy. In terms of fairness, DeiT-S reduces the inter-ethnic ACER gap between African and East Asian subjects to 0.13%, compared to 0.75% reported in an LBP-based work [6], representing an 83% reduction. Most notably, while ResNet18 records a BPCER of 10.44% on zero-shot Central Asian subjects, DeiT-S maintains 2.89% on the same unseen group, demonstrating a 3.6x generalization advantage. These results suggest that pretrained Vision Transformers achieve superior PAD accuracy, produce smaller demographic performance gaps, and generalize more equitably across unseen demographic groups, indicating that cross-demographic fairness in PAD may partly be influenced by architectural design.

</details>


### [10] [A Prototypical Signature Approach for Writer-Independent Offline Signature Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18528)
*Kecia G. de Moura,Robert Sabourin,Rafael M. O. Cruz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于原型签名的数据驱动策略，用于生成多样化且信息丰富的负样本，以提升离线手写签名验证中伪造检测的性能和训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法因缺乏真实伪造样本，通常从其他用户真实签名中随机抽取负样本，但这导致多样性不足、冗余度高、计算成本大。需要一种更高效的方式生成有信息量的训练负样本。

Method: 提出原型签名（prototypical signatures）概念，即对真实签名特征进行紧凑、不可识别的摘要表示，并基于此设计数据驱动策略，生成多样化且信息丰富的负样本用于训练。

Result: 实验表明：(i) 原型签名生成的负样本更有信息量，提升了对熟练伪造的检测能力；(ii) 方法具有骨干网络无关性，跨架构表现稳健；(iii) 结合原始形式线性SVM时，可作为RBF核模型的替代方案，显著提升可扩展性和计算效率。

Conclusion: 原型签名策略能有效生成高质量负样本，兼具架构灵活性和计算效率优势，为离线手写签名验证提供了实用的改进方案。

Abstract: Offline handwritten signature verification aims to distinguish genuine from forged signatures using static images. Since real forgeries are rarely available, negative samples are usually randomly drawn from genuine signatures of other users to create training data. However, this random selection often lacks diversity, increases redundancy, and escalates computational cost, leading to inefficient training. We propose a data-driven strategy to generate diverse, informative negative samples using prototypical signatures, which are compact, non-identifiable summaries of genuine signature features. Based on the experiments results, we conclude that (i) prototypical signatures yield more informative negative samples, improving the detection of skilled forgeries; (ii) the proposed approach is backbone-agnostic, showing robustness across architectures; and (iii) when combined with a primal-form linear SVM, it serves as an alternative to RBF-based models while significantly improving scalability and computational efficiency. Implementation of the method is available at https://github.com/kdmoura/proto_hsv.

</details>


### [11] [Hierarchical Multi-Modal Retrieval for Knowledge-Grounded News Image Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18553)
*Minh-Loi Nguyen,Xuan-Vu Le,Long-Bao Nguyen,Hoang-Bach Ngo,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种检索增强的图像描述框架，通过层次化多模态文章检索机制获取外部知识，结合VLM和LLM生成具有深层洞察（对象属性、事件上下文、潜在意义）的丰富描述，在ACM MM EVENTA 2025挑战赛中获得第5名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像描述方法难以生成全面且富含上下文的描述，尤其对于无法直接从视觉线索中观察到的细节（如对象属性、事件背景及深层意义）。为此，需要引入外部知识来增强描述的深度与广度。

Method: 提出检索增强图像描述框架，包含三个核心阶段：（1）层次化多模态文章检索——突破单一文本实体检索，综合考虑文章结构感知特征（标题、正文等加权文本组件及视觉布局模式），并采用内容-视觉、视觉-视觉和话语位置等多维度相似度计算，随后通过上下文相关性精炼阶段进一步优化检索结果；（2）使用VLM基于图像生成简洁描述；（3）基于该描述从检索文章中分割相关信息，再由LLM融合描述与提取的知识生成全面的、富含上下文细节的最终描述。

Result: 在ACM Multimedia EVENTA 2025挑战赛的OpenEvent-V1数据集私有测试集上，总体得分为0.2824，获得第5名。源代码已开源发布。

Conclusion: 该检索增强框架能有效利用外部知识生成更具深度和上下文丰富度的图像描述，层次化多模态检索与上下文精炼策略是性能提升的关键。未来可进一步优化检索精度与知识融合机制。

Abstract: Traditional image captioning methods often struggle to generate comprehensive, context-rich descriptions, especially for details not directly observable from visual cues. To overcome this, we propose a novel retrieval-augmented image captioning framework that generates captions with deeper insights, such as object attributes, event context, and underlying significance, by leveraging external knowledge. Our approach features a hierarchical multi-modal article retrieval mechanism that moves beyond monolithic text entities. This retrieval considers article structure-aware features, including weighted textual components (e.g., headlines, body sections) and visual placement patterns, alongside multi-faceted similarity computations (content--visual, visual--visual, and discourse positioning). A subsequent contextual relevance refinement stage further enhances the retrieved information. The retrieved articles then serve as the knowledge base for caption generation: first, a VLM generates a concise image description; second, we segment relevant information from the retrieved articles based on this description; and finally, an LLM utilizes both the description and extracted knowledge to generate a comprehensive, contextually detailed caption. We participated in the ACM Multimedia EVENTA 2025 Challenge and achieved 5th place with an overall score of 0.2824 on the private test set of the OpenEvent-V1 dataset. Source code is publicly released at https://github.com/mf0212/EVENTA-Challange.

</details>


### [12] [Forged Calamity: Benchmark for Cross-Domain Synthetic Disaster Detection in the Age of Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18554)
*Duc-Manh Phan,Quoc-Duy Tran,Duy-Khang Do,Anh-Tuan Vo,Hai-Dang Nguyen,Trong Le Do,Mai-Khiem Tran,Vinh-Tiep Nguyen,Tam V. Nguyen,Isao Echizen,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了Forged Calamity基准数据集，包含30,000张真实与合成灾害图像，用于评估现有检测器对AI生成灾难图像的泛化能力。实验表明，现有方法在跨模型、跨灾种场景下准确率下降高达50%，揭示了严重的泛化瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像扩散模型生成的高度逼真合成图像可被用于制造虚假的洪水、火灾、地震等灾害场景，从而传播错误信息、干扰应急响应。现有取证方法在合成图像检测上的泛化能力不足，缺乏专门针对灾害领域的基准评估。

Method: 构建名为Forged Calamity的数据集，包含6,000张真实灾害图像和24,000张由4种扩散模型生成的合成图像。在此数据集上对微调检测器和零样本通用检测器进行全面评估，涵盖分布内、跨生成器及跨灾种的测试设置。

Result: 微调检测器在分布内表现良好，但在未见过的生成器或灾害类型上准确率下降高达50%，存在对模型特定伪影的过拟合。零样本通用检测器同样难以维持稳定精度，仅少数表征鲁棒模型展现出有限韧性。

Conclusion: 当前取证方法存在持续的泛化差距，迫切需要开发领域无关且模型无关的检测方法，以保障扩散模型时代下的视觉真实性。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of text-to-image diffusion models has enabled the creation of highly photorealistic synthetic images that closely resemble real photographs, making it increasingly difficult to distinguish authentic content from AI-generated fabrications. This poses challenges for cybersecurity, digital forensics, and disaster response, where fake imagery of floods, fires, or earthquakes can spread misinformation or disrupt emergency operations. To address this, we introduce Forged Calamity, a benchmark dataset for synthetic disaster detection containing 30,000 images, including 6,000 real and 24,000 synthetic samples generated by four diffusion models. Comprehensive experiments across fine-tuned and zero-shot settings reveal consistent weaknesses in current forensic approaches. Fine-tuned detectors perform well in-distribution but lose up to 50\% accuracy on unseen generators or disaster types, showing overfitting to model-specific artifacts. Zero-shot generalized detectors also struggle to maintain stable accuracy, with only limited resilience in a few representation-robust models. These findings highlight persistent generalization gaps and the urgent need for domain- and model-agnostic detection methods to ensure visual authenticity in the diffusion era.

</details>


### [13] [Rethinking Text-to-Image as Semantic-Aware Data Augmentation for Indoor Scene Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18555)
*Trong-Vu Hoang,Quang-Binh Nguyen,Dinh-Khoi Vo,Hoai-Danh Vo,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用Stable Diffusion生成合成室内图像作为数据增强手段，以缓解室内图像识别中训练数据不足的问题；同时为防止SD合成图像被滥用，引入基于扩散重建误差（DIRE）的检测方法，仅用轻量模型即可达到100%的识别准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内图像识别面临光照、遮挡和物体排列复杂等挑战，而真实室内训练图像往往不足。因此需要有效的合成数据增强方法，同时也需防范合成图像被恶意使用。

Method: 方法分两部分：(1) 利用Stable Diffusion生成多样且逼真的室内场景合成图像，扩充训练数据池；(2) 提出基于扩散重建误差（DIRE）的检测手段，利用DIRE的强表征能力训练轻量级分类器（如MobileNetV3），以识别SD生成的图像。

Result: 在MIT Indoor Scene数据集上，SD合成图像有效提升了数据受限条件下深度模型的训练效果；DIRE方法在MobileNetV3上对SD生成图像的识别准确率达到100%。

Conclusion: Stable Diffusion可作为室内图像识别的高效数据增强工具，而DIRE方法能精准检测SD生成图像，防止其被滥用，二者结合兼顾了性能提升与安全性。

Abstract: In the realm of computer vision, indoor image recognition presents challenges due to the intricate interplay of lighting conditions, occlusions, and diverse object arrangements within confined spaces. To address the lacks of training indoor images, we introduce a novel approach leveraging Stable Diffusion (SD) for the generation of synthetic images, which serve as a powerful data augmentation tool. The utilization of SD offers a principled framework for synthesizing diverse and realistic indoor scenes, thereby enriching the training data pool for robust indoor image recognition models. Experimental findings on the MIT Indoor Scene dataset reveal the potential of our proposed approach in enhancing the training of deep models when authentic data is limited. Furthermore, to prevent the misuse of SD synthetic images, we introduce a counter measure based on DIffusion Reconstruction Error (DIRE). The powerful DIRE presentation enables training robust classifiers only using lightweight deep models. Experiments show that our approach can perfectly recognize SD generated images with the accuracy of 100% using MobilenetV3.

</details>


### [14] [MolmoMotion: Forecasting Point Trajectories in 3D with Language Instruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18558)
*Jianing Zhang,Chenhao Zheng,Yajun Yang,Max Argus,Rustin Soraki,Winson Han,Taira Anderson,Chun-Liang Li,Shuo Liu,Jiafei Duan,Zhongzheng Ren,Jieyu Zhang,Ranjay Krishna*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MolmoMotion是一个通用的3D点运动预测模型，通过大规模视频数据集MolmoMotion-1M和基准PointMotionBench，实现了基于目标和语言指令的3D轨迹预测，在运动预测和下游任务中均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动预测方法缺乏一种通用、类别无关、视角稳定且紧凑的表示。作者认为3D世界坐标点正是这样一种理想表示，并旨在将其形式化为目标条件的3D点运动预测任务，以支持机器人操作、视频生成等下游应用。

Method: (1) 构建MolmoMotion-1M数据集：从116万无约束视频中标注动作描述和物体锚定的3D点轨迹；(2) 建立PointMotionBench基准：涵盖111个物体类别和61种运动类型的人工验证基准；(3) 提出MolmoMotion模型：支持自回归坐标预测和基于流匹配的轨迹生成两种范式，根据视觉历史、3D查询点和目标语言描述预测未来3D轨迹。

Result: MolmoMotion在PointMotionBench上显著优于现有基线，能准确预测多样化运动模式；在机器人操作任务中提升了训练效率和泛化能力；其预测轨迹可为视频生成模型提供运动引导，合成更逼真的物体运动。

Conclusion: 3D点运动预测作为一种通用表示是有效的。MolmoMotion不仅在基准测试中表现领先，其学习到的3D运动先验还能有效迁移至机器人操作和视频生成等实际应用，验证了该方法的实用价值。

Abstract: Motion forecasting is central to visual intelligence: agents must anticipate how objects will move in order to plan actions, reason about physical interactions, and synthesize realistic futures. We argue that 3D points in world coordinates provide a general representation that is class-agnostic, view-stable, compact, and directly useful for downstream tasks. We formalize the task of goal-conditioned 3D point motion forecasting: given a short visual history, a set of 3D query points on an object of interest, and a language description of the intended goal, the model predicts the future 3D trajectory of each point. We introduce a full stack to study this task at scale: (1) MolmoMotion-1M is a large corpus of action-described, object-grounded 3D point trajectories annotated from 1.16M unconstrained videos; (2) PointMotionBench is a human-verified benchmark spanning 111 object categories and 61 motion types; and (3) MolmoMotion is a general motion forecasting model that supports both autoregressive coordinate prediction and flow-matching-based trajectory generation. MolmoMotion accurately predicts diverse motion patterns with different language instructions, and significantly outperforms existing motion prediction baselines on PointMotionBench. Finally, we show that the learned 3D motion prior transfers well to downstream applications: it improves training efficiency and generalization for robot manipulation, and its predicted trajectories provide effective motion guidance for generative models to synthesize videos with more realistic object motion.

</details>


### [15] [Experimental Analysis of Neural Network-Based Image Classification on the CIFAR-10 Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18565)
*Necati Kagan Erkek,Emre Balci,Berkin Halay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在CIFAR-10基准上，使用六层卷积加三层最大池化的架构进行图像分类实验，验证准确率约74.77%，并观察到验证损失在训练中后期回升，揭示了表征学习与记忆化之间的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为图像分类任务提供一个完整的、可复现的实验基线，涵盖从数据预处理到泛化评估的整个学习流程，并为后续的正则化、数据增强和更深架构研究奠定基础。

Method: 采用全连接与卷积网络两种形式进行对比；核心架构为六层卷积层加三层最大池化，使用图像向量化、归一化、独热编码、监督损失最小化、小批量训练（batch size=128）、Adam优化器（学习率0.001），训练10个epoch，基于验证集评估泛化性能。

Result: 验证准确率达到约74.77%；训练损失持续下降，但验证损失在训练中后期开始回升，呈现典型的过拟合迹象。

Conclusion: 实验结果清晰展示了表征学习与记忆化之间的实际差异。该工作为后续正则化、数据增强、更深架构等研究及可复现的图像分类教学提供了一个紧凑的实验基线。

Abstract: An experimental investigation of neural image classification on the CIFAR-10 benchmark is presented through fully connected and convolutional network formulations. The analysis emphasizes the complete learning pipeline: image vectorization, normalization, one-hot class encoding, supervised loss minimization, learning-rate selection, mini-batch training, convolutional feature extraction, max-pooling, and validation-based generalization assessment. A convolutional architecture with six convolutional layers and three max-pooling stages is evaluated for ten training epochs using a batch size of 128 and an Adam optimizer with a learning rate of 0.001. The validation accuracy reaches approximately 74.77%, while the validation loss begins to increase after the middle of training despite continued reduction in training loss. The resulting behavior illustrates the practical difference between representation learning and memorization, and it provides a compact experimental baseline for future studies on regularization, data augmentation, deeper architectures, and reproducible image-classification education.

</details>


### [16] [Multi-Modal Hyper-Graph Fusion for Low-Light Crowd Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18566)
*Hao-Yuan Ma,Li Zhang,Yushi Qiu,Jie Gao,Yan Zhang,Bangjun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对低光照环境下的人群计数问题，构建了三个新基准数据集（SHA_Dark、SHB_Dark和真实场景的LC-Crowd），并提出了一种多模态低光照计数网络LCNet。该网络融合RGB外观、深度几何与Canny边缘结构线索，通过多模态超图融合模块捕捉高阶互补关系，并采用可变形矩形稀疏注意力（DRSA）模块实现自适应计算分配，在三个基准上均取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光照环境下的人群计数在实际应用中至关重要，但现有方法主要聚焦于良好光照场景或仅依赖RGB单模态表示，在极端黑暗和复杂非均匀光照下不可靠。该问题目前缺乏专门的基准数据集和有效的多模态融合方法。

Method: 1) 构建三个低光照人群计数基准：合成数据集SHA_Dark和SHB_Dark，以及真实场景数据集LC-Crowd。2) 受Retinex物理建模启发，引入深度和Canny边缘作为几何与结构先验，增强低光照下的本征反射率表示。3) 提出多模态超图融合模块，将RGB、深度、边缘三种线索建模为统一超图中的节点，通过动态超边构建与消息传递显式捕捉高阶互补关系。4) 提出可变形矩形稀疏注意力（DRSA）模块，通过锚点感知估计和自适应矩形窗口建模，将计算集中于信息丰富的区域。

Result: 在SHA_Dark、SHB_Dark和LC-Crowd三个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，所提出的LCNet在低光照人群计数任务上相比现有最先进方法取得了最佳整体性能。

Conclusion: 通过多模态融合（RGB+深度+边缘）与超图建模、自适应稀疏注意力机制的结合，LCNet有效解决了低光照条件下的人群计数难题，填补了该领域基准数据集和方法论的空白。

Abstract: Crowd counting is a fundamental task in computer vision. However, crowd counting in low-light environments remains largely underexplored, despite its practical importance in the real world. Existing methods mainly focus on well-lit scenes or rely on single-modality Red-Green-Blue (RGB) representations, which often become unreliable under extreme darkness and complex non-uniform illumination. To handle this problem, we construct three new low-light crowd counting benchmarks, which consist of two synthetic datasets, SHA\_Dark and SHB\_Dark, and a real-world benchmark LC-Crowd (Low-light Crowd Dataset). Inspired by Retinex-based physical modeling, we introduce depth and Canny edge cues as complementary geometric and structural priors to enhance the intrinsic reflectance representation under low-light conditions. We propose a Multi-Modal Hyper-Graph Fusion module, which formulates RGB appearance, depth geometry, and edge structure cues as nodes in a unified hyper-graph and explicitly captures their high-order complementary relationships via dynamic hyperedge construction and message passing. Furthermore, to adaptively allocate computation in dense prediction, we propose a Deformable Rectangular Sparse Attention (DRSA) module, which concentrates computation on informative regions through anchor-aware estimation and adaptive rectangular window modeling. Based on these designs, we develop a unified Low-Light Counting Network (LCNet) for robust low-light crowd counting. Extensive experiments on three benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed method achieves the best overall performance against existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods. The code is in the supplementary material. The datasets will be made public upon acceptance.

</details>


### [17] [Technical Report for ICRA 2026 GOOSE 2D Fine-Grained Semantic Segmentation Challenge: Leveraging DINOv3 for Robust Outdoor Scene Understanding in Field Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18582)
*Jaeil Park,Hyobin Choi,Sangjin Lee,Hyungtae Lim,Sung-Hoon Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了GOOSE 2D细粒度语义分割挑战赛的第一名解决方案，结合DINOv3 ViT-L/16骨干网络、ViT-Adapter和Mask2Former解码器，并采用多尺度测试时增强与模型集成策略，在64个细粒度类别上取得了76.57%的综合得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对ICRA 2026野外机器人研讨会GOOSE 2D挑战赛中的非道路场景密集语义分割问题，需要在64个细粒度类别和11个粗类别上实现高精度分割，应对野外环境复杂、类别细粒度高的挑战。

Method: 方案包含两大改进：(a) 网络层面：采用自监督DINOv3 ViT-L/16作为骨干，结合ViT-Adapter实现多尺度特征适配，使用Mask2Former掩码分类解码器，并在全局[CLS] token上引入粗类别辅助损失；(b) 推理层面：采用多尺度和水平翻转的测试时增强(TTA)，并基于Codabench评分选择前三名检查点进行模型集成。

Result: 在GOOSE 2D挑战赛最终阶段排行榜上排名第一，官方综合得分为76.57%，其中细粒度类别mIoU为69.32%，粗类别级mIoU为83.81%。

Conclusion: 通过将强大的自监督视觉Transformer骨干与适配器模块、掩码分类解码器及粗类别辅助监督相结合，再辅以推理时的多尺度集成策略，可显著提升野外非道路场景细粒度语义分割的性能。

Abstract: The GOOSE 2D Fine-Grained Semantic Segmentation Challenge at the ICRA 2026 Workshop on Field Robotics evaluates dense semantic segmentation of off-road imagery over a fine-grained taxonomy of 64 classes and 11 evaluated non-void coarse categories. We present the first-place solution to this challenge. Our solution comprises two complementary improvements: (a) a network-level design that combines a self-supervised DINOv3 ViT-L/16 backbone, a ViT-Adapter, and a Mask2Former mask-classification decoder, together with a coarse-category auxiliary loss on the global [CLS] token; and (b) an inference-time aggregation strategy based on multi-scale and horizontal-flip test-time augmentation and an ensemble of the top three checkpoints selected using Codabench scores. Our method achieves an official composite score of 76.57%, consisting of 69.32% fine-class mIoU and 83.81% category-level mIoU, and ranks first on the final phase leaderboard: www.codabench.org/competitions/14257/#/results-tab.

</details>


### [18] [Aerial-ground LiDAR place recognition with patch-level self-supervised learning and expanded reciprocal re-ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18583)
*Yandi Yang,Xianghong Zou,Jianping Li,Haofeng Xie,Saurav Uprety,Hongzhou Yang,Naser El-Sheimy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对空地LiDAR位置识别中的域差异和初始检索误匹配问题，提出了一种结合多尺度自监督学习的检索网络和扩展互惠重排序（ER）框架，在多个数据集上显著提升了Recall@1性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面级LiDAR位置识别存在需预先访问、覆盖不全、视角受限等问题。机载激光扫描（ALS）数据作为先验地图可克服这些缺陷，但空地LiDAR点云之间存在严重的域差异（domain gap），且初始检索易产生假阳性匹配，需要专门的方法来弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：（1）检索网络：基于相邻点云块与锚块具有相似语义的先验，在多个尺度上引入块级自监督学习模块，并与场景级学习融合，增强空地点云全局特征的区分性；（2）扩展互惠（ER）重排序算法：利用ALS点云的结构化空间分布，最大化利用邻域信息，基于邻居特征细化每个特征，再更新相似度矩阵进行最终排序。

Result: 在CS-Urban-Scenes数据集上，检索网络相比SOTA方法平均Recall@1提升9.8%，平均Recall@1%提升3.2%；在CS-Campus3D上也取得最佳性能。ER重排序算法无需额外训练，在CS-Campus3D和CS-Urban-Scenes上分别进一步将平均Recall@1提升4.9%和10.2%。

Conclusion: 所提出的检索加重排序框架有效解决了空地LiDAR位置识别中的域差异和假阳性问题，多尺度自监督学习与扩展互惠重排序的结合大幅提升了检索精度，展示了利用ALS先验地图进行跨视角位置识别的优越性。

Abstract: LiDAR place recognition determines one's position on a prior point cloud map. The most studied ground-level LiDAR place recognition suffers from pre-visit requirements, incomplete coverage, and limited perspectives. Using pre-acquired, full-coverage Airborne Laser Scanning (ALS) data as an aerial prior map overcomes these drawbacks, making cross-view place recognition necessary and advantageous. However, aerial-ground LiDAR place recognition faces significant challenges, including the domain gap between aerial and ground point clouds, and false positives during initial retrieval. To address these challenges, we present a novel retrieval and re-ranking framework for aerial-ground LiDAR place recognition. Based on the priors that neighboring point cloud patches share similar semantics with anchor patch, our retrieval network introduces patch-level self-supervised learning modules at multiple scales and integrates with scene-level learning to improve global feature discriminativeness between aerial and ground point clouds. Furthermore, leveraging the structured spatial distribution of ALS point clouds, we introduce an Expanded Reciprocal (ER) re-ranking algorithm to exploit neighborhood information maximally and refine each feature based on neighbor features, which are then used to update the similarity matrix for final ranking. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our retrieval network outperforms existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods, achieving a 9.8\% improvement in average Recall@1 and a 3.2\% improvement in average Recall@1\% on the CS-Urban-Scenes, while also showing the best performance on the CS-Campus3D dataset. Additionally, our ER re-ranking algorithm further boosts the average Recall@1 by 4.9\% on CS-Campus3D and 10.2\% on CS-Urban-Scenes without additional training.

</details>


### [19] [APT: Atomic Physical Transitions for Causal Video-Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18586)
*Shang Wu,Haoran Lu,Songling Liu,Chenwei Xu,Lie Lu,Pranav Maneriker,Fan Du,Manling Li,Zhaoran Wang,Han Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出原子物理转换（APTs）——一种将视频中的物理事件分解为因果状态变化序列的细粒度表示方法，并设计了 APT-Tune 参数高效微调方案，使视觉语言模型能够学习这种因果物理表示，从而在保持事件级理解能力的同时显著提升物理转换检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解任务通常使用宏观事件标签（如"弹跳"），但这些标签掩盖了事件内部的因果物理过程（支撑丧失、接触开始、反弹、沉降等）。作者认为，要让模型真正理解物理事件，需要显式建模这些最小化的、时序定位的状态转换，从而从"发生了什么"推进到"为什么发生"。

Method: 首先定义APT为绑定可见线索与物理机制的最小状态转换单元，构建包含14种转换类型、27,303个标注实例的混合源数据集。然后提出APT-Tune微调方案，包含三个关键组件：(1) image-pad-aware supervision，解决视频帧填充带来的注意力偏差；(2) format-conditional co-training，交替训练APT生成与事件问答，防止灾难性遗忘；(3) mechanism-conditioned domain-to-type decoding，利用物理机制条件约束解码，增强物理可解释性。在Qwen3-VL-2B上仅用11M LoRA参数实现。

Result: 当前VLM在零样本APT检测上召回率最高仅14%，错误以漏检为主。直接微调虽提升转换检测但导致事件级遗忘。APT-Tune在显著提升APT召回率的同时，保持了甚至提升了事件级视频迁移能力，验证了APT作为因果监督信号的有效性。

Conclusion: APT并非一种新的答案格式，而是一种与人类认知对齐的因果监督信号，能够有效提升物理视频理解。APT-Tune实现了格式鲁棒且物理可解释的APT学习，为细粒度物理推理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Physical events are not understood by their names alone, but by the causal state changes that compose them. A clip-level label such as "bounce" can be correct while hiding the process that makes the event physically valid, from support loss and contact onset to rebound and settling. To make this hidden process explicit, we introduce Atomic Physical Transitions (APTs): minimal, temporally localized state changes that bind a visible cue to an active physical mechanism and before/after dynamical regimes. An APT chain represents a video as an ordered causal transition sequence rather than a single aggregate event label: event labels tell what happened; APT chains explain why it happened. To make APTs learnable by VLMs, we construct mixed-source APT data from human annotations and simulator ground truth, covering 14 transition types across contact, gravity, friction, and rotation/stability, with 27,303 timed instances over 1,246 trials. Using this data, we find that current VLMs miss transition-level physics, with zero-shot recall at most 14% and errors dominated by missed transitions. Direct fine-tuning on APT chains improves transition detection but causes event-level forgetting, indicating that the model learns a specialized answer format rather than a reusable physical representation. We therefore propose APT-Tune, a parameter-efficient recipe that teaches VLMs to use causal transitions without forgetting how to answer video questions. It combines image-pad-aware supervision, format-conditional co-training, and mechanism-conditioned domain-to-type decoding to make APT learning format-robust and physically grounded. With only 11 M LoRA parameters on Qwen3-VL-2B, APT-Tune substantially improves APT recall while also improving event-level video transfer. These results show that APTs are not a new answer format, but a human-aligned causal supervision signal for physical video understanding.

</details>


### [20] [Bridging Creative Intent and Visual Quality: Creator-Driven Recurrent Video Generation with Agentic Feedback Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18591)
*Denis Savytski,Aiden Lei,Heding Liu,Warren Yang,Sihan Liang,Alexander Liu,Zhe Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CHIEF 是一个以人为中心的人机协同视频生成框架，通过角色条件化多模态大语言模型提供主观观众反馈，支持创作者进行迭代式视频精炼，从短片到完整10分钟复杂剧情短片均有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI生成视频缺乏叙事连贯性和创意指导，尤其在长时长视频中问题更突出。与编码领域不同，视频生成需要关于情节、场景和叙事的主观反馈，这自然催生了融入人类创意指导的方法。现有自我评估无法捕捉观众视角的主观评价。

Method: 提出CHIEF框架：(1) 以创作者为中心的人机协同迭代精炼流程；(2) 专门的Refiner Agent负责将创作者的修改意见融入视频；(3) 利用角色条件化多模态LLM观看生成视频，从不同观众视角产生主观批评，构建反馈闭环。

Result: 与无电影制作经验的高中生和大学生合作测试，成功创作了从1分钟短视频到包含复杂剧情的完整10分钟短片。

Conclusion: CHIEF框架有效证明了将人类创意指导与AI自动主观反馈相结合的可行性，显著提升了AI生成视频的叙事质量和创意方向，使非专业创作者也能完成复杂的长时长视频创作。

Abstract: Generative AI has made content creation increasingly accessible, but many AI-generated videos lack narrative coherence and creative direction, issues that become more substantial at longer durations. Unlike coding, where AI generation benefits from reliable feedback and techniques such as recurrent self-improvement, video generation requires subjective feedback about plot, scenes, and narrative, which naturally motivates approaches that incorporate human creative direction. We introduce CHIEF, a human-AI co-creation video generation framework that places the creator at the center of human-in-the-loop iterative video refinement, and supports them by providing automatic subjective feedback. The creator incorporates their creative direction by driving each iteration, while their revisions are incorporated by a specialized refiner agent. The feedback loop is generated by persona-conditioned multimodal LLMs that watch generated videos and produce subjective critique from the audience perspectives, providing feedback that self-evaluation alone cannot capture. To test the effectiveness of our proposed framework, we work with high school and college students with no prior filmmaking experience to create videos, from short 1-minute videos to a complete short 10-minute film with a complicated plot.

</details>


### [21] [Hallucination Detection and Correction in Medical VLMs via Counter-Evidence Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18609)
*Nan Zhou,Ke Zou,Meng Liu,Linchao He,Jiaqi Zhu,Yi Zhang,Hu Chen,Huazhu Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的即插即用框架CoEV（反证据验证），通过双向验证文本断言与视觉证据之间的事实一致性，将每条陈述映射到四象限诊断图中，实现医学VLM幻觉的检测与校正，在多个医学数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM幻觉检测方法主要关注生成文本与参考数据间的事实不一致，或仅分析模型的图像注意力分布，却很少验证该注意力是否真正反映了支持生成文本的视觉证据。这种“证据-文本”对应关系的缺失，导致临床诊断中VLM的可靠性受到质疑。

Method: 提出Counter-Evidence Verification (CoEV)框架：(1) 在文本断言与视觉证据之间执行双向验证，测试每条陈述是否被对应证据区域所支持；(2) 将每条陈述分配到四象限诊断图中，该图同时捕捉文本事实性（text factuality）和视觉接地性（visual grounding）两个维度；(3) CoEV作为训练无关的即插即用事后精化工具，无需重新训练即可检测和校正幻觉。

Result: 在四个医学数据集上广泛实验：(1) 幻觉检测方面，平均PR-AUC和ROC-AUC分别提升3.0%和3.9%的绝对百分点，特定VQA场景下提升高达18.5%；(2) 幻觉校正方面，医学报告生成的Micro-F1最高提升12.5%，幻觉率降低超过11.9%，医学VQA准确率亦获提升。

Conclusion: CoEV实现了可靠的医学VLM幻觉检测与校正，通过提供基于证据的诊断线索，为临床医生提供了可信赖的辅助决策支持。

Abstract: Vision-Language models (VLMs) reliability in medical diagnosis is challenged by trust-undermining hallucinations. Existing hallucination detection approaches mainly focus on identifying factual inconsistencies between generated text and reference data. While some studies analyze where models attend in images, they seldom verify whether such attention truly reflects the visual evidence supporting the generated text. To address this gap, we propose Co}unter-Evidence Verification (CoEV), a training-free plug-and-play framework that detects and corrects hallucinations through evidence-based factual consistency verification. CoEV performs bidirectional verification between textual assertions and visual evidence, testing whether each statement is supported by its corresponding evidence region, and assigns each statement into a four-quadrant diagnostic map capturing combinations of text factuality and visual grounding. CoEV detects hallucinated content and serves as a post hoc refinement tool, correcting hallucinations without retraining. Extensive experiments on four medical datasets show that CoEV combats hallucinations in VLMs.For hallucination detection, CoEV consistently outperforms existing methods, improving average PR-AUC and ROC-AUC by 3.0% and 3.9% absolute points respectively, with notable gains of up to 18.5% in specific VQA scenarios. For hallucination correction, it improves Micro-F1 by up to 12.5%, reduces hallucination rates by over 11.9% on medical report generation, and also boosts medical VQA accuracy. These results show that CoEV enables reliable detection and correction of hallucinations, providing clinicians with dependable, evidence-based cues for diagnosis. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [22] [Intrinsic 4D Gaussian Segmentation from Scene Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18623)
*Hasan Yazar,Mohamed Rayan Barhdadi,Erchin Serpedin,Mehmet Tuncel,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Intrinsic-GS，一种无需训练、无需外部掩码的 4D 高斯分割方法，直接利用高斯原语的固有属性（外观、朝向、尺度、形变轨迹、渲染边界）构建亲和图，通过 Leiden 社区检测实现动态场景分割，在 Neu3D 和 HyperNeRF 上取得与掩码监督方法相当甚至更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态 4D 高斯场景分割依赖 SAM 等基础模型生成 2D 掩码并蒸馏到高斯表示，跨多帧多视图生成掩码成本高昂，且分割质量严重依赖外部掩码的质量和一致性。作者希望探究高斯原语自身蕴含了多少目标级结构信息，从而摆脱对外部掩码的依赖。

Method: 提出 Intrinsic-GS，一种免训练、免掩码的方法：(1) 从高斯原语的外观（RGB颜色、球谐系数）、几何（位置、朝向、尺度）、运动（形变轨迹）以及无需学习的渲染边界（如深度不连续性、法线梯度）等多模态内在线索中提取特征；(2) 构建稀疏亲和图（affinity graph）；(3) 使用 Leiden 社区检测算法对图进行划分，实现目标级分割，无需任何基础模型或学习到的特征场。

Result: 在 Neu3D 上达到 0.746 mIoU（几何变体达 0.902 mIoU，与 SAM 监督的 TRASE 相当）；在 HyperNeRF 上达到 0.575 mIoU，且运行速度比掩码监督流程的掩码生成和特征渲染阶段快 12.5 倍。

Conclusion: 大量分割信号已编码于高斯原语自身之中，Intrinsic-GS 提供了一条快速、免掩码的 3D/4D 高斯分割方向，在外部掩码不可靠或获取昂贵的场景中具有更强的泛化性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Dynamic 4D Gaussian Splatting reconstructs deforming scenes with high fidelity and is increasingly adopted as a representation for dynamic 3D scenes. Putting such a scene to use, for editing, manipulation or motion analysis, first requires segmenting it: grouping the Gaussian primitives into coherent objects. Current pipelines obtain this grouping by importing 2D masks from foundation models such as SAM and lifting or distilling them into the Gaussian representation. In dynamic scenes these masks must be generated across many frames and views, which is costly, and the resulting segmentation can depend strongly on the quality and consistency of those external masks. We ask how much object-level structure can instead be recovered from the Gaussians themselves, and propose Intrinsic-GS, a training-free, mask-free method that builds a sparse affinity graph over Gaussian primitives from appearance, orientation, scale, deformation-trajectory and non-learned rendered-boundary cues. The graph is partitioned with Leiden community detection, requiring no foundation model and no learned feature field. On the standard 4D Gaussian segmentation benchmarks, Neu3D and HyperNeRF, Intrinsic-GS recovers substantial object structure without mask supervision, reaching 0.746 mIoU on Neu3D and 0.575 on HyperNeRF; on Neu3D, a geometry-only variant reaches 0.902 mIoU, matching SAM-supervised TRASE. On HyperNeRF, Intrinsic-GS runs 12.5x faster than the mask-generation and feature-rendering stages used by mask-supervised pipelines. These results suggest that much of the segmentation signal is already encoded in the Gaussians themselves, offering a fast, mask-free direction for 3D and 4D Gaussian segmentation that may also point toward more generalizable, robust segmentation in settings where external masks are unreliable or expensive.

</details>


### [23] [Spiking Pyramid Wavelet Transformation for High-efficient and Low-energy Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18644)
*Chen Zhao,Xiantao Hu,Song Wu,Qian Wang,Chen Wu,Rui Xie,Jian Yang,Ying Tai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于脉冲神经网络和离散小波变换的图像恢复模型SPWM，通过脉冲双金字塔小波块建模长程依赖，在保持图像质量的同时显著降低计算成本和能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脉冲CNN方法在图像恢复任务中受限于CNN操作的固有感受野限制，性能有待提升；同时需要探索更低能耗、更高效率的SNN架构以适用于资源受限设备。

Method: 提出脉冲金字塔小波模型（SPWM），核心是设计脉冲双金字塔小波（SDPW）块，利用离散小波变换在小波域中建模长程依赖关系，并充分挖掘退化特性。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，SPWM显著降低了计算成本和能量消耗，同时保持了图像恢复质量。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了SNN在图像恢复领域的潜力，为资源受限设备上的未来应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) have garnered significant interest in computer vision due to their potential for efficiency and biological inspiration. While spiking CNN-based methods have shown promise for image restoration (IR) tasks, their performance is constrained by the inherent receptive field limitations of CNN operations. In the paper, we explore the benefits of discrete wavelet transformation and propose a spiking pyramid wavelet-based model (SPWM) for high-efficient and low-energy target. Specifically, we develop a spiking dual pyramid wavelet (SDPW) block to model long-range dependency and exploit the properties of the degradation in the wavelet domain. Experimental results on several benchmarks demonstrate that SPWM significantly lowers computational costs and energy consumption while maintaining image quality. Our method showcases the potential of SNNs in the field of IR, offering new insights for future applications of resource-limited devices.

</details>


### [24] [On-Manifold Variational Learning with Heat-Kernel Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18658)
*Jiarui Xing,Tal Zeevi,Nian Wu,Jian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种流形锚定的变分框架，通过几何感知的EM算法，使用图中心点（graph medoid）代替欧氏平均来估计高斯混合先验，确保原型保持在数据流形上，解决了深度隐变量模型中原型漂移和退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度隐变量模型在无监督学习医学影像表示时，使用欧氏平均来估计高斯混合先验，导致原型偏离弯曲的数据流形，且在子群数量增加时退化严重。需要一种能保持原型在流形上的方法。

Method: 提出流形锚定的变分框架，核心是几何感知的EM算法：M步在热核加权的隐空间图上选择扩散中心性最高的图中心点作为每个子群的原型；引入Dirichlet能量正则化项保证隐空间几何平滑性；每个子群配有不确定性评分实现无标签质量评估。

Result: 在心脏疤痕和脑MRI基准测试上，该框架在所有对比方法中达到最高准确率，生成迄今最清晰的原型，并且在子群数量较大时保持稳定，而所有基线方法均出现退化。

Conclusion: 流形锚定的EM是一种通用的几何工具，可扩展到标准EM之外的其他隐变量模型。该方法有效解决了医学影像无监督表示学习中原型偏离流形的问题，具有优越的稳定性和表征质量。

Abstract: Learning unsupervised representations of medical imaging cohorts can reveal clinically meaningful prototypes without expert labels, which are often noisy and fail to capture true pathological heterogeneity. However, existing deep latent-variable models estimate Gaussian mixture priors via Euclidean averaging, producing prototypes that drift off the curved data manifold and degenerate as the number of sub-populations grows. We propose a manifold-anchored variational framework built on a geometry-aware Expectation-Maximization (EM) algorithm, whose M-step selects each sub-population prototype as the graph medoid with the highest diffusion centrality on a heat-kernel-weighted latent graph, ensuring that every prototype remains on-manifold. A Dirichlet energy regularizer enforces geometric smoothness of the latent space, and a per-sub-population uncertainty score enables label-free quality assessment. \rev{The manifold-anchored EM is a general-purpose geometric tool that extends standard EM and applies readily to other latent-variable models beyond this setting.} On cardiac scar and brain MRI benchmarks, our framework attains the highest accuracy among all compared methods, produces the sharpest prototypes reported to date, and remains stable at large sub-population counts where all baselines degenerate.

</details>


### [25] [LandslideAgent with Multimodal LandslideBench: A Domain-Rule-Augmented Agent for Autonomous Landslide Identification and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18661)
*Chengfu Liu,Dongyang Hou,Junwu Xiang,Cheng Yang,Xuezhi Cui,Zeyuan Wang,Liangtian Liu,Zelang Miao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种指令驱动的智能体框架，通过构建多模态细粒度数据集LandslideBench、微调滑坡专用视觉语言模型LandslideVLM，以及设计领域规则增强的LandslideAgent，实现了滑坡灾害的全流程智能识别与分析，在多个任务上显著超越现有主流模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前滑坡灾害智能解译范式难以同时提取视觉特征与高层次地学语义，通用视觉语言模型在复杂地质场景中存在感知局限和领域幻觉问题，亟需面向滑坡领域的专用多模态智能框架。

Method: 框架包含三部分：(1) LandslideBench：通过多VLM交叉验证与交互式标注，构建包含七种子类型标签、高分辨率影像、像素级掩码和高质量文本描述的多模态细粒度数据集；(2) LandslideVLM：基于LandslideBench对VLM进行LoRA微调，增强地质语义理解能力；(3) LandslideAgent：以LandslideVLM为认知核心，采用双规则控制器（结构化报告元数据约束 + 交叉验证识别约束）来规范自动化工具调用，实现自主多源空间数据推理。

Result: LandslideBench在细粒度分类和语义分割任务上为五种主流模型提供了有效基线；LandslideVLM在滑坡判别、细粒度分类和语义描述质量上分别提升10.96%、32.87%和15.91%；LandslideAgent实现了自主多源空间数据推理的全流程智能化。

Conclusion: 所提出的指令驱动Agent框架有效解决了滑坡解译中视觉特征与地学语义融合的难题，显著提升了滑坡识别与分析的精度和自动化水平，为地质灾害智能监测提供了新的技术范式。

Abstract: Intelligent landslide hazard interpretation is critical for disaster prevention, yet current paradigms struggle to simultaneously extract visual features and high-level geoscientific semantics, while general-purpose vision-language models (VLMs) suffer from perceptual limitations and domain hallucinations in complex geological scenarios. To address these challenges, we propose an instruction-driven agentic framework comprising three components. First, LandslideBench, a multimodal fine-grained dataset with seven subtype labels, high-resolution imagery, pixel-level masks, and high-quality textual descriptions, is constructed via multi-VLM cross-validation and interactive annotation. Then, LandslideVLM, a landslide-oriented VLM, is fine-tuned via LoRA on LandslideBench to enhance geological semantic understanding. Finally, LandslideAgent, a domain rule-enhanced agent taking LandslideVLM as its cognitive backbone, employs a dual-rule controller incorporating structured report metadata constraints and cross-validation identification constraints to regulate automated tool invocation. Experiments demonstrate that LandslideBench provides effective baselines across five mainstream models on fine-grained classification and semantic segmentation. LandslideVLM achieves accuracy improvements of 10.96%, 32.87%, and 15.91% on landslide discrimination, fine-grained classification, and semantic description quality, respectively. LandslideAgent further enables autonomous multi-source spatial data inference, realizing full-process intelligence for landslide identification and analysis.

</details>


### [26] [BrainFusionNet: a deep learning and XAI model to understand local, global, and sequential features of MRI images for improved brain tumour detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18675)
*Md Taimur Ahad,Bo Song,Yan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BrainFusionNet is a hybrid deep learning model combining CNNs, Vision Transformers, and GRUs for brain tumor classification from MRI images, achieving 98% accuracy on two public datasets. It integrates explainable AI (SHAP, LIME, GradCAM) and analyzes how pixel intensity distribution affects model performance.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRI噪声导致肿瘤边界模糊、位置和外观复杂，给深度学习带来挑战。现有方法难以有效提取小尺度肿瘤区域的局部与全局特征，且模型决策缺乏可解释性。此外，MRI图像质量（像素强度分布）对分类性能的影响尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 提出BrainFusionNet，融合三个组件：(1) 平衡的序列CNN架构，捕获底层和深层特征；(2) 定制的ViT，捕获局部特征、稳定梯度流、降低梯度消失风险；(3) GRU接收CNN与ViT输出进行最终分类。同时集成SHAP、LIME、GradCAM三种可解释AI方法可视化关键决策区域。在两个公开MRI数据集上采用K折验证评估，与六种SOTA CNN（含DenseNet121、VGG16等）及迁移学习对比，并分析像素强度分布对分类性能的影响。

Result: BrainFusionNet在两个数据集上均达到98%的K折验证准确率；SOTA CNN中DenseNet121和VGG16最高仅达96%。模型能有效提取小肿瘤区域的特征。此外发现MRI图像像素强度分布会影响深度学习性能，这是一个新颖的图像解释发现。

Conclusion: BrainFusionNet通过CNN-ViT-GRU混合架构有效融合空间、上下文和序列特征，在小尺度肿瘤区域表现优异，且兼具可解释性。像素强度分布影响DL性能的发现为MRI图像质量评估提供了新视角。

Abstract: The noise of Magnetic Resonance Imaging MRI poses challenges for Deep Learning DL when tumor boundaries are obscured tumor location and appearance are complex Therefore we develop BrainFusionNet that combines Convolutional Neural Networks CNNs Vision Transformers ViT and Gated Recurrent Units GRUs to extract spatial contextual and sequential features from MRI images for improved brain tumor classification Furthermore explainable AI such as SHAP LIME and GradCAM are integrated to visualise and highlight image regions that contribute to BrainFusionNets decisionmaking process The proposed BrainFusionNet model is evaluated on two publicly available MRI datasets Kfold validation suggests 98 accuracy on both datasets The model was compared with the six stateoftheart SOTA CNNs and transfer learning Among the SOTA CNNs DenseNet121 and VGG16 achieved the highest accuracy of 96 The novelty of BrainFusionNet is that the hybrid model effectively extracts local and global features from MRI images even in smallscale tumor regions and small tumor sizes The model has a balanced sequential CNN architecture to capture lowlevel and deeperlayer features a customized ViT that captures local features stabilizes gradient flow and reduces the risk of vanishing gradients during MRI image training The CNN and ViT outputs are fed into a GRU for final classification Furthermore we analyze pixel intensities to determine whether MRI image quality affects image classification Our findings are very novel in image interpretation as we found that the distribution of pixel intensities in MRI images affects DL performance

</details>


### [27] [Moving Beyond Diversity: Visual Token Pruning as Subspace Reconstruction for Efficient VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18681)
*Jaeyeon Lee,Shunjie Wen,Dong-Wan Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SPARE方法，将视觉标记剪枝重新定义为列子集选择问题，通过最小化重构误差进行子空间重构，而非仅依赖余弦相似度的多样性最大化。此外，发现"反相关性"现象——图像-文本相关性得分较低的标记反而能更好保留上下文信息，并将其纳入选择标准。SPARE在无需训练的情况下可移除94%的视觉标记，同时保留95%的基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM视觉标记剪枝方法依赖余弦归一化相似度进行多样性最大化，丢弃了幅度信息，无法忠实近似原始特征表示，导致在组合式多技能推理任务上表现不佳。

Method: SPARE将标记剪枝形式化为列子集选择问题（Column Subset Selection），通过迭代选择投影残差最大的标记来显式最小化重构误差，实现超越角度多样性的"重构驱动剪枝"。同时引入"反相关性"（anti-relevance）作为额外选择准则：优先保留图像-文本相关性得分较低但能更好保留上下文信息的标记。

Result: 在多个VLM和基准测试上，SPARE consistently达到SOTA性能，尤其在组合任务上增益显著。在LLaVA上可移除高达94%的视觉标记，同时保留95%的基线性能，且完全无需训练。

Conclusion: SPARE通过重构误差最小化和反相关性引导的上下文感知选择，实现了高效且高保真的视觉标记压缩，为VLM的推理效率优化提供了新的理论视角和实践方案。

Abstract: Despite their remarkable performance, Vision Language Models (VLMs) incur substantial computational overhead due to the large number of visual tokens. While diversity maximization has become a dominant strategy for token reduction, existing methods rely on cosine-based normalized similarity that discards magnitude information, failing to faithfully approximate the original feature representation and leading to suboptimal performance, particularly on compositional multi-skill reasoning tasks. In this paper, we introduce SPARE, a subspace reconstruction method that reformulates token pruning as a column subset selection problem and explicitly minimizes reconstruction error. By iteratively selecting tokens with large projection residuals, SPARE performs reconstruction-driven pruning beyond angular diversity. Moreover, we reveal a counterintuitive anti-relevance phenomenon: tokens with lower image-text relevance score can better preserve contextual information. Based on this finding, we incorporate anti-relevance into SPARE as an additional selection criterion to promote context-aware token selection. Extensive experiments across multiple VLMs and benchmarks demonstrate that SPARE consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance, with strong gains on compositional tasks. When applied to LLaVA, SPARE removes up to 94% of visual tokens while retaining 95% of the baseline performance, all in a fully training-free manner.

</details>


### [28] [Multi-Class Brain Tumor Classification Using Advanced Deep Learning Models: A Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18682)
*Asad Channa,Asghar Ali Chandio,Akhtar Hussain Jalbani,Mehwish Leghari,Shahzad Memon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究在约10,000张MRI图像的临床数据集上，系统比较了五种CNN架构（定制基线模型、VGG16、VGG19、DenseNet121、EfficientNetB0）在多类脑肿瘤分类中的表现，结果显示EfficientNetB0以95%的总体准确率和89%的脑膜瘤召回率表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深度学习已取得显著进展，但从MRI图像中准确分类脑肿瘤仍然具有挑战性，尤其是脑膜瘤因其在MRI上的细微表现而难以检测。现有研究缺乏在统一实验框架下对多种预训练模型进行系统对比，亟需找到在分类精度、参数量和临床实用性之间的最佳平衡。

Method: 使用约10,000张MRI图像的临床来源数据集，在完全相同的实验框架下训练和测试五种CNN架构：一个自定义基线CNN，以及四个预训练模型（VGG16、VGG19、DenseNet121、EfficientNetB0）。采用总体准确率和各类肿瘤的召回率（尤其是脑膜瘤召回率）作为评估指标，兼顾整体性能和临床相关表现。

Result: EfficientNetB0取得最佳总体分类准确率（95%），其次为VGG16（94.37%）、VGG19（92.29%）、DenseNet121（90.91%），自定义CNN最低（78.00%）。在脑膜瘤检测召回率方面，自定义CNN仅约20%，而EfficientNetB0达89%。此外，更深的VGG19表现反而差于较浅的VGG16。

Conclusion: EfficientNetB0在分类准确率、模型参数量和临床有意义的性能之间提供了最优权衡。模型架构效率在医学图像任务中可能比深度更为重要。EfficientNetB0对脑膜瘤检测的显著提升具有重要临床价值。

Abstract: Despite recent advancements in deep learning, accurately classifying brain tumors from MRI images continues to pose challenges. In this research, we present a comprehensive evaluation of five different convolutional neural networks (CNN) architectures, including a customized baseline model and four pre-trained models - for use in classifying multi-class brain tumors using a clinically-sourced dataset of approximately 10,000 MRI images. We have utilized five different architectures; VGG16, VGG19, DenseNet121, and EfficientNetB0, which were all tested and trained within an identical experimental framework. Performance was measured by both overall accuracy and tumor-wise recall as a means to measure the clinically-relevant performance of each architecture. We found that EfficientNetB0 had the best overall classification accuracy at 95%, when compared to the other architectures tested; specifically VGG16 (94.37%), VGG19 (92.29%), DenseNet121 (90.91%) and the customized CNN (78.00%). An especially important finding of our research was the considerable improvement in detecting meningiomas; specifically, while simple CNNs could detect meningiomas with a recall rate of approximately 20%, EfficientNetB0 was able to detect meningiomas with a recall rate of 89%. Meningiomas are often difficult to detect because they can appear very subtly on MRI images. Additionally, an interesting finding was that the deeper VGG19 performed worse than the shallower VGG16. This indicates that in many cases the architectural efficiency of a CNN model may be more important than its depth when working with medical images. Overall, EfficientNetB0 appears to provide the optimal trade-off between classification accuracy, number of parameters used in the model and clinically meaningful performance.

</details>


### [29] [Spatially Stratified Distillation for Heterogeneous Radar Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18687)
*Sagun Singh Shrestha,Samuel Harding,Abdelwahed Khamis,Saimunur Rahman,Peyman Moghadam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出空间分层蒸馏（SSD）策略，通过基于物理雷达回波的非对称空间对齐，解决异构雷达（4D汽车雷达 vs 密集旋转雷达）地点识别中因极端稀疏性导致的性能退化问题，在HeRCULES数据集上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构雷达地点识别（4D汽车雷达匹配旋转雷达参考地图）受限于4D传感器的极端稀疏性和窄视场，仅能捕获旋转雷达结构密度的一小部分。现有方法通过统一表示空间来弥合差异，但在多会话环境中性能退化严重，缺乏对物理回波分布差异的显式建模。

Method: 提出空间分层蒸馏（SSD），以非对称空间对齐替代传统均匀蒸馏，权重直接来源于物理雷达回波：在两种雷达回波重叠区域强制执行强特征对齐；在4D学生雷达无回波但教师雷达在共享视场内存在有效结构的稀疏区域，大幅降低蒸馏权重。

Result: 在HeRCULES数据集上进行了广泛评估，SSD显著优于现有地点识别方法，在其具有挑战性的动态序列上取得了最先进的结果。

Conclusion: SSD通过物理回波驱动的空间分层蒸馏，有效解决了异构雷达地点识别中的稀疏性-密度失配问题，在多会话动态环境下鲁棒性显著提升。

Abstract: Scalable, all-weather place recognition increasingly relies on heterogeneous radar place recognition to bridge diverse hardware platforms. A notable application is matching queries from cost-effective 4D automotive radars against high-fidelity reference maps built by dense spinning radars. This process is fundamentally limited by the extreme sparsity (and narrow field-of-view) of the 4D sensor, which captures only a fraction of the structural density present in the spinning radar database. Prior efforts address this issue by unifying different radar signals. That is, projecting both signals into a common representational space. Yet, they suffer performance degradation in multi-session environments. In this paper, we propose spatially-stratified distillation (SSD); a strategy that replaces standard uniform distillation with an asymmetric spatial alignment derived directly from physical radar returns. In regions where both radars exhibit overlapping returns, SSD enforces strong feature alignment. Crucially, in sparse regions where the 4D student lacks returns but the teacher contains valid structure within the shared field of view, SSD applies heavily discounted distillation weights. Extensive evaluations of the recent HeRCULES dataset demonstrate that SSD significantly outperforms prior place recognition methods, achieving state-of-the-art results on its challenging dynamic sequences.

</details>


### [30] [UniTemp: Unlocking Video Generation in Any Temporal Order via Bidirectional Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18702)
*Lin Zhang,Sicheng Mo,Zefan Cai,Jinhong Lin,Zihao Lin,Jiuxiang Gu,Krishna Kumar Singh,Yuheng Li,Yin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniTemp提出了一种双向蒸馏框架，通过引入分块锚定潜在变量（blockwise anchor latents）解决了因果3D VAE在反向生成时导致的不连续性问题，训练单一自回归学生模型即可实现任意时间方向的视频生成，支持双向扩展、中间帧生成、循环视频等多种工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归视频扩散模型仅限于前向时间生成，而实际视频创作常需要灵活的顺序——比如基于未来上下文向后扩展，或基于前后帧进行中间生成。核心障碍是因果3D VAE的潜在编码严格依赖过去上下文，反向生成时会导致块间不连续。

Method: 提出UniTemp双向蒸馏框架：(1) 设计blockwise anchor latents，在反向生成时通过辅助潜在变量恢复块边界处缺失的过去上下文；(2) 基于此构建单一自回归学生模型，通过蒸馏训练使其支持任意时间方向的生成条件控制；(3) 推理时可灵活以任意过去和/或未来帧为条件进行生成。

Result: UniTemp在短视频和长视频生成任务上保持了与前向-only方法相当的竞争力，同时实现了双向视频扩展、中间帧生成、循环视频生成、场景过渡和视觉故事生成等多样化工作流。

Conclusion: UniTemp首次打破了自回归视频扩散模型仅支持前向生成的限制，通过blockwise anchor latents有效解决了因果VAE在反向生成中的不连续问题，使单一模型支持任意方向生成，显著提升了视频创作的可控性和灵活性。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models have emerged as a promising approach for long video generation, achieving strong performance in streaming settings. However, existing methods are restricted to forward temporal generation, whereas practical video creation often requires flexible generation order, e.g., conditioning on future context to extend backward, or on both past and future context for inbetween generation. We bridge this gap by training an autoregressive model that supports generation in arbitrary temporal directions. A key technical challenge arises from the Causal 3D VAE widely used in video diffusion models, which encodes latents strictly conditioned on past context. While suited for forward generation, this causal structure causes inter-block discontinuities when generation proceeds backward. To address this, we introduce blockwise anchor latents, a set of auxiliary latents that restore the missing past context at block boundaries during backward generation. Built on this design, we propose UniTemp, a bidirectional distillation framework that trains a single autoregressive student model for any-direction video generation. At inference time, UniTemp conditions on arbitrary past and/or future frames, improving controllability for both bidirectional and inbetween generation. Experiments show that UniTemp maintains competitive performance on short and long video generation compared to forward-only methods, while enabling diverse workflows such as bidirectional video extension, inbetween generation, looping video generation, scene transition, and visual story generation. Project website: https://lzhangbj.github.io/projects/unitemp/

</details>


### [31] [PEFT-MedSAM: Efficient Fine-Tuning of Medical Foundation Models for Explainable Skin Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18707)
*Asad Channa,Abdullah Khan,Asghar Ali Chandio,Aamir Akbar,Shahzad Memon,Aqib Hussain,Ameer Hamza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种参数高效微调方法PEFT-MedSAM，通过仅训练轻量级掩码解码器而冻结预训练的图像编码器和提示编码器，将MedSAM适配到皮肤镜图像皮损自动分割任务，在ISIC 2018上取得Dice 0.9411和IoU 0.8918的优异性能，并在PH2外部验证集上达到Dice 0.9467。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习皮肤镜图像分割方法性能不佳，而MedSAM等基础模型虽具备零样本分割能力，但直接应用于皮肤镜皮损分割效果不足（零样本Dice仅0.8997），需要一种高效、低计算成本的微调策略来适配该医学影像任务。

Method: 提出PEFT-MedSAM方法，基于MedSAM架构采用参数高效微调策略：冻结预训练图像编码器和提示编码器，仅训练轻量级掩码解码器。采用Grad-CAM可解释性分析与Pointing Game评估方法对CNN基线模型进行验证，并通过Wilcoxon符号秩检验和Bootstrap估计95%置信区间进行统计验证。

Result: 在ISIC 2018基准数据集上，PEFT-MedSAM取得Dice系数0.9411、IoU 0.8918，显著优于全训练U-Net基线（Dice 0.8715）和零样本MedSAM（Dice 0.8997）。在PH2外部验证集上Dice达0.9467（±0.0310标准差）。Wilcoxon检验p值<0.0001，Bootstrap 95%置信区间为[0.9364, 0.9447]。Grad-CAM解释性评估在519张验证图像上分类准确率达98.27%。

Conclusion: PEFT-MedSAM以极低的参数训练代价实现了优于全训练U-Net和零样本MedSAM的皮损分割性能，并通过可解释性分析和统计测试增强了临床可信度，证明了参数高效微调策略在皮肤镜图像自动分割任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Automated segmentation of skin lesions using deep learning models for dermoscopic images can be very helpful in finding melanomas earlier than they would normally be detected. However, most deep learning methods available do not perform well. The aim of this paper is to present a parameter-efficient fine-tuning method called PEFT-MedSAM for adapting the Medical Segment Anything Model (MedSAM) to automatically segment dermoscopic skin lesions. The PEFT-MedSAM method uses only the lightweight mask decoder for training the model while keeping the pre-trained image encoder and prompt encoder frozen. The experiments performed on the ISIC 2018 benchmark dataset shows that PEFT-MedSAM obtains a dice coefficient of .9411 and an intersection over union value of .8918 when compared to both a fully trained U-Net baseline (.8715 dice coefficient) and zero-shot MedSAM inference (.8997 dice coefficient). The external validation of the model using PH2 dataset shows .9467 dice coefficient with +/- .0310 standard deviation. Supportive evidence for these claims include a p-value less than .0001 for Wilcoxon signed rank tests comparing the two datasets and bootstrap-estimated 95% confidence intervals of [.9364,.9447] that represent the estimated range of possible values for the average dice coefficient obtained by repeating the test. To increase clinical trustworthiness, we used Grad-CAM explainability along with a pointing game based evaluation methodology to evaluate the CNN baseline model on the validation set. The results showed that we had an accuracy rate of 98.27% on the validation set of 519 images and confirmed that the model classified regions containing skin lesions.

</details>


### [32] [Rethinking the Pointer Loss in Table Structure Recognition: Geometry-Aware Pointer Loss for Spatial Locality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18721)
*Hong-Jun Choi,Jongho Lee,Jaeyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Geometry-Aware Pointer (GAP) Loss，通过基于空间邻近度的逆距离加权重调交叉熵损失，使梯度集中于相邻单元格错误，在PubTabNet和SynthTabNet上达到SOTA，且无额外推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析发现指针网络在表格结构识别中79.6%的失败发生在曼哈顿距离≤2的空间相邻单元格之间，而标准交叉熵损失对所有负样本一视同仁，未利用空间几何信息，导致模型对相邻易混淆单元格的区分能力不足。

Method: 提出Geometry-Aware Pointer (GAP) Loss：在标准交叉熵损失基础上，根据预测位置与真实标签之间的空间邻近度进行逆距离加权——相邻单元格获得更强的梯度信号，远处单元格梯度减弱。仅修改损失计算，模型架构和推理流程完全不变。

Result: 在PubTabNet和SynthTabNet上验证，GAP Loss持续减少相邻单元格错误，取得新的SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 在损失函数层面注入几何归纳偏置是提升表格结构识别鲁棒性的简单而有效的方法，无需额外推理开销即可显著改善指针网络对相邻单元格的区分能力。

Abstract: Table Structure Recognition (TSR) using a pointer network achieves impressive results by predicting HTML sequences while aligning tags to detected text (or cell) regions. However, our analysis reveals that when pointer networks fail, 79.6% of errors occur between spatially adjacent cells (Manhattan distance <= 2). Despite this, standard cross-entropy loss weights all negative candidates equally. In this work, we propose Geometry-Aware Pointer (GAP) Loss, which reweights the cross-entropy objective based on spatial proximity to ground truth. By applying inverse distance weighting, GAP focuses gradient flow where the model struggles most: immediate neighbors receive stronger gradients than distant cells. Our approach requires only a straightforward modification to the loss computation, maintaining the same model architecture with zero additional inference cost. Extensive experiments on PubTabNet and SynthTabNet demonstrate that GAP consistently reduces adjacent-cell errors, achieving new state-of-the-art performance. Our findings suggest that incorporating geometric inductive biases at the loss level provides a simple yet effective approach to robust TSR. Our code is available at https://github.com/teamreboott/GAP

</details>


### [33] [Clinically Aligned Geometry Constraints for Robust IVUS Vessel Boundary Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18723)
*Yunshu Chen,Litao Yang,Giuseppe Di Giovanni,Jordan Tan,Deval Mehta,Andrew Lin,Derek Chew,Masasi Fujino,Julie Butters,Stephen Nicholls,Zongyuan Ge,Kyung Hoon Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoCat是一个用于IVUS图像管腔和外弹力膜分割的几何一致性网络，采用双笛卡尔-极坐标编码器、跨域注意力和时序融合，通过可微几何一致性损失直接监督临床相关描述符，在分割精度和临床几何保真度上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IVUS分割方法虽关注重叠分数，但常出现边界漂移和拓扑错误，这些错误会直接传播到斑块面积、斑块负荷等临床测量中，导致不准确的定量评估。因此需要一种能够保持几何一致性的分割方法。

Method: 提出GeoCat网络，处理5帧IVUS序列：(1)采用双笛卡尔-极坐标编码器提取特征；(2)跨域注意力机制融合笛卡尔与极坐标表示；(3)时序融合模块整合帧间信息；(4)设计可微几何一致性损失函数，直接监督直径(dmax/dmin)、角度方向和横截面积等临床相关几何描述符。在146名患者、两种商用IVUS系统采集的12,242帧标注数据上训练。

Result: Dice达0.93；95% Hausdorff距离降至0.14 mm；拓扑违反率仅1.0%；直径误差0.13-0.16 mm；角度误差约8度。

Conclusion: GeoCat在保持高分割精度的同时，显著提升了临床几何测量的保真度，能够支撑可靠的斑块负荷定量评估，证明了直接监督几何描述符对于临床下游任务的有效性。

Abstract: Intravascular ultrasound (IVUS) lumen and external elastic membrane (EEM) segmentation is important for quantitative coronary plaque burden assessment. Errors in lumen or EEM delineation directly propagate to plaque area, plaque burden and geometric measurements. However, standard methods prioritising overlap scores often suffer from boundary drift and topology errors, leading to inaccurate clinical measurements. We present GeoCat, a geometry-consistent network that processes 5-frame IVUS clips using dual Cartesian-polar encoders with cross-domain attention and temporal fusion. A differentiable geometry consistency loss directly supervises clinically relevant descriptors including diameters, orientations, and cross-sectional areas. The model is trained on 12,242 annotated frames from 146 patients acquired with two commercial IVUS systems. We evaluate performance using both segmentation accuracy and plaque-relevant clinical metrics, including Dice/IoU, boundary measures(95HD (mm), ASSD), topology violation rate, and clinical geometry errors (dmax/dmin, angles, and areas). On our dataset, GeoCat achieves a Dice of 0.93, reduces 95HD to 0.14 mm, and lowers topology violations to 1.0%. Importantly, it significantly improves geometric fidelity, yielding diameter errors of 0.13-0.16 mm and angular errors of ~8 degrees, supporting reliable plaque burden quantification.

</details>


### [34] [Toward Training-Free Zero-Shot Anomaly Detection in 3D Medical Images: A Batch-Based Approach Using 2D Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18749)
*Tai Le-Gia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CS3F，一种无需训练的3D医学影像零样本异常检测框架。将3D体数据沿多轴分解为切片，用冻结的2D视觉Transformer编码，再通过邻域池化生成局部体素token，利用跨主体互相似度计算异常分数，并引入由粗到细的token化策略以保留小病灶信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本异常检测方法多针对2D图像设计，直接扩展至3D医学体数据面临两大瓶颈：(1) 大规模3D基础模型稀缺；(2) 难以有效利用体素上下文。临床场景中采集协议、患者群体和病理类型异质性强，标注数据常不可得，因此亟需适用于3D医学影像的训练无关ZSAD方法。

Method: 提出CS3F (Cross-Subject Slice Similarity Framework)：(1) 体数据沿多解剖轴分解为切片序列；(2) 用冻结的2D视觉Transformer逐切片编码；(3) 邻域切片特征池化生成局部体素token；(4) 基于跨主体互相似度计算异常分数——若某token在其他主体中缺乏相似对应，则赋予高异常分；(5) 引入由粗到细的token化策略，缓解深度池化对局灶性病灶信号的衰减，在避免穷举匹配的前提下实现精细分辨率体素评分。

Result: 在脑MRI上对转移瘤、胶质瘤和卒中三类病灶进行评估，并在肺部CT上验证其泛化性（超越图谱对齐的脑MRI场景）。结果表明：冻结的2D基础模型可有效支撑3D医学影像异常定位；细粒度token化的收益高度依赖于病灶对比度和成像模态。

Conclusion: CS3F证明了2D冻结基础模型在3D医学影像零样本异常检测中的可行性，为缺乏3D基础模型场景下的体积医学数据分析提供了新路径。病灶对比度和模态特性是决定细粒度token化策略收益的关键因素。

Abstract: Zero-shot anomaly detection (ZSAD) is attractive for medical imaging because clinical systems must handle heterogeneous acquisition protocols, changing patient populations, and pathologies for which annotated training data may be unavailable. Most existing zero-shot anomaly detection methods are designed for 2D images, and their direct extension to 3D medical volumes is limited by the scarcity of large-scale volumetric foundation models or by the difficulty of utilizing volumetric context. We propose CS3F, a training-free batch-based framework for ZSAD in 3D medical images using 2D foundation models. Each volume is decomposed along multiple anatomical axes and encoded slice-wise by a 2D vision transformer. These are then converted into localized volumetric tokens by pooling neighboring slice features.
