This limitation restricts their performance in degradation suppression and structural detail recovery. To address these issues, this paper proposes DVANet, a deep unfolding network inspired by the half-quadratic splitting optimization algorithm, which formulates unified image restoration under complex degradations as a collaborative unfolding process between degradation-aware observation consistency and visual-prior-guided reconstruction. Specifically, in the degradation-aware observation consistency branch, a degradation representation module is employed to extract global degradation attributes and local degradation cues, and degradation-conditioned mapping is used to enhance the model's adaptability to different degradation types. In the visual-prior-guided reconstruction branch, DINOv3 is introduced to provide structural and semantic information as hierarchical visual priors, thereby complementing the missing structural information in damaged regions and improving detail recovery. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DVANet achieves superior or competitive performance on multi-scenario degradation and cross-domain image restoration tasks, showing favorable degradation adaptability and generalization ability.

</details>


### [70] [AMALIA-VL: A Native European Portuguese Open-Source Vision and Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19100)
*Diogo Glória-Silva,João Cardeira,Manuel Letras da Luz,Afonso Simplício,Gonçalo Vinagre,Diogo Tavares,Rafael Ferreira,Inês Calvo,Inês Vieira,David Semedo,João Magalhães*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AMALIA-VL 是首个专为欧洲葡萄牙语（pt-PT）构建的开源指令微调大规模视觉语言模型（LVLM），通过三阶段训练流程和 pt-PT 中心化的多模态数据组合，填补了该语言在多模态模型中的系统性空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源多模态模型系统性忽视了欧洲葡萄牙语（pt-PT），要么将其与巴西葡萄牙语混淆，要么在训练数据中严重低估其比例，导致 pt-PT 用户缺乏可用的 LVLM 资源。

Method: 提出 AMALIA-VL：1）结合高分辨率视觉编码器与动态图像分块（dynamic image tiling），通过可学习的连接器桥接视觉与语言模态；2）采用三阶段训练：视觉-语言对齐 → 通用视觉指令微调 → 偏好优化（preference optimization）；3）构建 pt-PT 中心化的多模态数据组合，融合经筛选和翻译的公开数据集以及专门针对 pt-PT 缺口设计的新数据集。

Result: 评估表明 AMALIA-VL 为开源 pt-PT LVLM 建立了强有力的基线（strong baseline）。

Conclusion: AMALIA-VL 证明了为欧洲葡萄牙语原生构建 LVLM 的可行性。作者承诺将开源模型权重、训练数据、构建流水线及机器翻译的 pt-PT 评测基准，以推动 pt-PT LVLM 开发的民主化。

Abstract: Large Vision and Language Models (LVLMs) have advanced rapidly, yet European Portuguese (pt-PT) remains systematically underserved by existing open-source multimodal models, which either conflate it with Brazilian Portuguese or severely under-represent it in their training data mixes. We introduce AMALIA-VL, the first open-source instruction-tuned LVLM built natively for pt-PT, pairing a high-resolution vision encoder with dynamic image tiling and a fully open pt-PT-optimized language model via a learned connector. We contribute with a purposefully designed three-stage training process - vision-language alignment, general visual instruction tuning, and preference optimization - together with a pt-PT-centric multimodal data mix combining curated and translated public datasets with novel datasets that address the near-total absence of European Portuguese multimodal resources. Our evaluation shows that AMALIA-VL establishes a strong baseline for open-source pt-PT LVLMs.We will release model weights, training data, and construction pipelines along with machine-translated pt-PT evaluation benchmarks to help democratize pt-PT LVLM development.

</details>


### [71] [ProductConsistency: Improving Product Identity Preservation in Instruction-Based Image Editing via SFT and RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19103)
*Mukund Khanna,Raj Singh Yadav,Kunal Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了ProductConsistency数据集，用于提升指令式产品图像编辑中的产品身份一致性。作者构建了包含87k样本的SFT数据集、869张独特产品图像的RL数据集以及ProductConsistency Benchmark评测基准，并提出循环一致性奖励（Cyclic Consistency reward）来约束RL训练。在Qwen-Image-Edit-2511和Flux.1-Kontext-dev上的微调实验表明，模型在OCR指标、感知指标和MLLM评估上均有显著提升，字符错误率降低5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前指令式图像编辑模型在产品电商场景下存在关键缺陷：难以在编辑过程中精确保持产品特征、品牌标识和文字元素的身份一致性。这一问题因缺乏专门针对文本保真度约束的指令式产品图像编辑数据集而更加严重，现有模型仅将产品一致性视为隐式能力而非显式优化目标。

Method: （1）构建ProductConsistency数据集：包含87k样本的监督微调数据集、869张独特产品图像构成的强化学习数据集，以及用于标准化评估的ProductConsistency Benchmark；（2）提出循环一致性奖励（Cyclic Consistency reward）：通过比较原始产品描述与编辑后图像反向生成的描述之间的语义相似度，约束产品身份的语义保持；（3）使用自建数据集分别对Qwen-Image-Edit-2511和Flux.1-Kontext-dev进行监督微调加强化学习的两阶段训练。

Result: 微调后的模型在OCR指标、感知指标和基于MLLM的评估上均一致优于基线模型，在产品一致性、文字渲染和整体视觉质量方面表现更强。其中Qwen-Image-Edit-2511模型实现了字符错误率（CER）5倍的降低。

Conclusion: 通过专门构建的产品编辑数据集和循环一致性奖励机制，可以有效提升指令式图像编辑模型在产品场景下的身份保持能力，实现更精确的文本渲染和更高的视觉质量。

Abstract: Recent advances in instruction-based image editing have enabled models to perform complex visual edits from natural language instructions. However, in product-centric scenarios where preserving product features, branding, and textual elements are critical, current open and closed source models often struggle to maintain this fine-grained object identity. This issue is further compounded by the lack of datasets for instruction-based product image editing with text fidelity constraints, leaving it largely treated as an implicit capability of instruction-based image editing models.
  In this work, we introduce the ProductConsistency dataset which is designed to improve product-centric image editing. Our approach includes a supervised fine-tuning (SFT) dataset of 87k samples for product editing, a reinforcement learning (RL) dataset with 869 unique product images, and a new benchmark dataset, the ProductConsistency Benchmark, to allow rigorous and standardized evaluation of editing models. To guide RL training, we propose a Cyclic Consistency reward that enforces semantic preservation of product identity by using caption similarity between the original product description and captions generated from the edited image. We fine-tune both Qwen-Image-Edit-2511 and Flux.1-Kontext-dev using our dataset and demonstrate consistent improvements over baseline models in OCR and Perceptual metrics, and MLLM-based evaluations as well, indicating stronger product consistency, text rendering, and overall visual quality; with the Qwen-Image-Edit-2511 model achieving a 5x reduction in the character error rate. The code and pipeline is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ProductConsistency-6FCC/README.md

</details>


### [72] [Hand-4DGS: Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting for 4D Hand Reconstruction from Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19156)
*Jeongmin Bae,Seoha Kim,Marc Pollefeys,Mahdi Rad,Youngjung Uh,Taein Kwon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Hand-4DGS，首个面向自中心视频的4D手部重建前馈框架，结合网格引导表示与时序卷积，无需3D真值标注即可实现~60 FPS的快速推理与强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自中心视频中的动态3D手部重建对AR/VR等下一代计算平台至关重要，但现有工作多聚焦于多视角重建或人体4D重建。自中心4D手部重建面临头部快速运动、手部快速动态、严重遮挡及单视角固有歧义性等挑战。

Method: 提出Hand-4DGS：首个面向自中心视频的4D手部重建前馈框架。核心技术包括：(1) 网格引导表示（mesh-guided representation）提供结构先验；(2) 时序卷积建模动态运动；(3) 通过高斯泼溅（Gaussian splatting）实现2D图像监督，无需昂贵的3D手部姿态真值标注。

Result: 在H2O和ARCTIC两个具有挑战性的自中心数据集上验证，相较基线方法取得显著提升，推理速度达~60 FPS。

Conclusion: Hand-4DGS证明了前馈网络结合高斯泼溅2D监督可有效解决自中心4D手部重建问题，在速度、精度和泛化能力上均具优势，同时摆脱了对昂贵3D标注的依赖。

Abstract: Dynamic 3D hand reconstruction from egocentric videos is essential for next-generation computing platforms such as AR/VR and AI glasses. Despite its importance, most prior works focus either on multi-view 3D hand reconstruction or on 4D human body reconstruction. Egocentric 4D hand reconstruction remains challenging due to fast head motion, rapid hand dynamics, severe occlusions, and inherent ambiguity from single-view observations. To address these challenges, we introduce Hand-4DGS, the first feed-forward framework for reconstructing dynamic 4D hands directly from egocentric videos, enabling both fast (~60 FPS) inference and strong generalization. Our approach incorporates a mesh-guided representation for structural priors and temporal convolutions to model dynamic motion. We evaluate our framework on two challenging egocentric datasets, H2O and ARCTIC, and demonstrate significant improvements over baselines. Our method benefits from the generalization capability of feed-forward networks and effective 2D image supervision through Gaussian splatting, without requiring expensive 3D hand pose ground-truth annotations.

</details>


### [73] [Native Active Perception as Reasoning for Omni-Modal Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19341)
*Zhenghao Xing,Ruiyang Xu,Yuxuan Wang,Jinzheng He,Ziyang Ma,Qize Yang,Yunfei Chu,Jin Xu,Junyang Lin,Chi-Wing Fu,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniAgent 是首个原生全模态智能体，将长视频理解建模为基于POMDP的“观察-思考-行动”迭代循环，通过按需行动将视听线索提炼为持久文本记忆，从而将推理复杂度与视频时长解耦。论文提出Agentic SFT和Agentic RL（含TAURA）两种训练策略，在多个基准上取得开源模型最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频理解模型采用被动“全看完”范式，无论查询难度如何都均匀处理帧，计算成本随视频时长线性增长。交互式框架虽已出现，但依赖全局预扫描，上下文开销仍与视频长度挂钩。因此需要一种真正按需感知、推理代价与原始视频时长解耦的原生主动感知方案。

Method: (1) 将视频理解形式化为POMDP迭代循环（Observation-Thought-Action），智能体按需执行动作，选择性提取视听线索并压缩为持久文本记忆，以解耦推理复杂度与视频时长。(2) Agentic SFT：通过Best-of-N轨迹合成与双阶段质量控制，引导模型习得原生主动感知能力。(3) Agentic RL with TAURA：提出Turn-aware Adaptive Uncertainty Rescaled Advantage，利用轮次级熵值引导信用分配聚焦于关键发现轮次，优化探索效率与训练稳定性。

Result: OmniAgent 展现出正向的测试时扩展（test-time scaling）特性，推理轮次增加时性能持续提升，验证了主动感知的有效性。在VideoMME、LVBench等10个基准上取得开源模型SOTA。LVBench上，7B参数的OmniAgent超越10倍规模的Qwen2.5-VL-72B（50.5% vs 47.3%）。

Conclusion: OmniAgent验证了原生主动感知范式的有效性：通过POMDP迭代循环实现推理与视频时长的解耦，配合Agentic SFT和TAURA强化学习策略，不仅取得了领先性能，更展现了正向测试时扩展能力——增加推理轮次可稳定提升表现，为长视频理解开辟了效率与精度兼得的新路径。

Abstract: Passive models for long video understanding typically rely on a "watch-it-all" paradigm, processing frames uniformly regardless of query difficulty, causing computational cost to grow with video duration. Although interactive frameworks have emerged, they often rely on global pre-scanning, and their context cost still scales with video length. We propose OmniAgent, the first native omni-modal agent that formulates video understanding as a POMDP-based iterative Observation-Thought-Action cycle. OmniAgent executes on-demand actions to selectively distill audio-visual cues into a persistent textual memory, effectively decoupling reasoning complexity from raw video duration. To operationalize this, we introduce (1) Agentic Supervised Fine-Tuning to bootstrap native active perception via best-of-N trajectory synthesis with dual-stage quality control, and (2) Agentic Reinforcement Learning with TAURA (Turn-aware Adaptive Uncertainty Rescaled Advantage), which leverages turn-level entropy to steer credit assignment toward pivotal discovery turns. Crucially, OmniAgent exhibits positive test-time scaling, where performance improves as the number of reasoning turns increases, validating the efficacy of active perception. Empirical results across ten benchmarks (e.g., VideoMME, LVBench) demonstrate that OmniAgent achieves state-of-the-art performance among open-source models. Notably, on LVBench, our 7B agent outperforms the 10$\times$ larger Qwen2.5-VL-72B (50.5% vs. 47.3%).

</details>


### [74] [When AUC Misleads: Polarization-Aware Evaluation of Deepfake Detectors under Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19184)
*Dat Nguyen,Cosmin Radoi,Romain Hermary,Marcella Astrid,Nesryne Mejri,Enjie Ghorbel,Djamila Aouada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的深度伪造检测评估指标Cross-AUC，通过结合各域AUC均值与基于Wasserstein距离的预测极化度量，更真实地反映检测器在域迁移下的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测方法的泛化评估通常采用跨数据集分别测量AUC的方式，这无法反映真实场景中检测器同时面对多源混合数据和不同伪造类型的情况，因而无法准确衡量对域迁移的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出Cross-dataset AUC (Cross-AUC) 指标：计算各域（数据集）AUC的均值，并引入由类别得分分布之间的Wasserstein距离量化的预测极化程度作为惩罚项，综合评估泛化性能。

Result: 在七个基准数据集上的实验验证了Cross-AUC的实用相关性，该指标不仅能更真实地评估域迁移下的泛化能力，还能解释性能下降的原因。

Conclusion: Cross-AUC是一个更具现实意义和可解释性的深度伪造检测泛化评估指标，弥补了传统逐数据集AUC评估的不足。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative AI, such as diffusion models and face-swapping tools, have enabled the creation of highly realistic deepfakes, leading to real-world harms including financial fraud and non-consensual explicit content. In response, deepfake detection has become an active research area, with recent methods increasingly focusing on improving generalization to unseen manipulations. This is typically evaluated using the Area Under the ROC Curve (AUC) measured separately across multiple datasets. However, such an evaluation fails to reflect real-world scenarios where detectors face a mixture of data sources and varying artifact types. To address this limitation, we introduce a novel metric, Cross-dataset AUC (Cross-AUC) that averages per-domain AUCs with a measure of prediction polarization for taking into account the robustness to domain shift. The polarization extent is quantified by the Wasserstein Distance between class score distributions. Cross-AUC not only assesses the generalization capabilities of deepfake detectors under domain shifts more realistically, but it is also interpretable as it better explains the reason behind a drop in performance. Experiments performed on seven benchmark datasets demonstrate its practical relevance.

</details>


### [75] [Moebius: 0.2B Lightweight Image Inpainting Framework with 10B-Level Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19195)
*Kangsheng Duan,Ziyang Xu,Wenyu Liu,Xiaohu Ruan,Xiaoxin Chen,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Moebius 是一个高效轻量级图像修复框架，通过 Local-λ Mix Interaction (LλMI) 模块重构扩散骨干网络，以固定尺寸线性矩阵总结空间上下文和全局语义先验，大幅削减参数量的同时保留复杂潜在交互。配合自适应多粒度蒸馏策略，在潜在空间中动态平衡多重梯度损失，以不到 FLUX.1-Fill-Dev 2% 的参数量（0.22B vs. 11.9B）实现与之相当甚至更优的生成质量，推理速度加速超 15 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 10B 级工业基础模型在图像修复上性能卓越，但其计算开销高昂，严重阻碍实际部署。构建高度优化的任务专用模型是可行方案，但极端结构压缩会引发严重的表示瓶颈。本文旨在解决这一矛盾：如何在极小参数量下仍保持高保真修复能力。

Method: 提出 Moebius 框架，核心包括两部分：（1）Local-λ Mix Interaction (LλMI) 模块，由 Local-λ 和 Interactive-λ 两个子模块组成，将空间上下文和全局语义先验归纳为固定尺寸线性矩阵，在大量减少参数的同时保持潜在空间的复杂交互能力；（2）自适应多粒度蒸馏策略，严格在潜在空间中操作以避免昂贵的像素空间解码，动态平衡多个基于梯度的损失，实现高保真对齐。

Result: 在自然场景和人像修复基准上的广泛实验表明，Moebius 的生成质量可与 10B 级工业通用模型 FLUX.1-Fill-Dev 媲美甚至超越，仅使用不到 2% 的参数量（0.22B vs. 11.9B），且总推理时间加速超 15 倍。

Conclusion: Moebius 通过架构创新与蒸馏策略的协同设计，在极小参数量下实现了高保真图像修复，为高效图像修复设立了新的效率标准，证明了任务专用轻量化模型在生成质量与计算效率之间可以取得最优平衡。

Abstract: While 10B-level industrial foundation models have pushed the boundaries of image inpainting, their prohibitive computational costs severely hinder practical deployment. Constructing a highly optimized task-specific specialist offers a promising solution; however, extreme structural compression inevitably triggers a severe representation bottleneck. To conquer this, we propose Moebius, a highly efficient lightweight inpainting framework. We systematically reconstruct the diffusion backbone by introducing the Local-$λ$ Mix Interaction ($LλMI$) block. Comprising Local-$λ$ and Interactive-$λ$ modules, it elegantly summarizes spatial contexts and global semantic priors into fixed-size linear matrices, preserving complex latent interactions while drastically shedding parameters. Furthermore, to unlock the full representational capacity of this highly compact architecture, we synergistically pair it with an adaptive multi-granularity distillation strategy. Operating strictly within the latent space to avoid expensive pixel-space decoding, this strategy dynamically balances multiple gradient-based losses to achieve high-fidelity alignment. Extensive experiments across natural and portrait benchmarks demonstrate that this optimal synergy enables Moebius to rival or even surpass the generation quality of the 10B-level industrial generalist FLUX.1-Fill-Dev. Remarkably, Moebius achieves this using less than 2\% of the parameters (0.22B vs. 11.9B) while delivering a $>15\times$ acceleration in total inference time, setting a new efficiency standard for high-fidelity inpainting. Project page at https://hustvl.github.io/Moebius.

</details>


### [76] [ROSA-TFormer: A Radar-Optical Sensor-Aware Temporal Transformer for Pinus sylvestris Plantation Classification in Northern Shaanxi Using GEE-Derived Sentinel-1/2 Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19204)
*Nengbo Zhang,Chang sheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ROSA-TFormer，一种融合 Sentinel-1 SAR 与 Sentinel-2 光学时序数据的传感器感知时间 Transformer，用于陕北樟子松人工林点级分类，精度达 99.67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确识别樟子松人工林对监测陕北地区造林质量与生态恢复至关重要，但现有方法未能充分融合雷达与光学多源时序特征。

Method: 提出 ROSA-TFormer 模型，包含分离的 SAR 与光学嵌入分支、传感器感知门控机制（sensor-aware gate）以及时序注意力池化（temporal attention pooling），在 Google Earth Engine 上生成 Sentinel-1/2 时序数据，从月和半月两种粒度进行点级分类实验。

Result: 在 HalfMonth-dataBig 数据集上，ROSA-TFormer 取得 99.67% 总体精度、99.56% 宏平均 F1 和 98.91% 樟子松 F1。空间分块验证与消融实验证实了雷达-光学时序融合及传感器感知建模的有效性。

Conclusion: ROSA-TFormer 在点级樟子松人工林分类上展现出优异潜力，但更大范围的 wall-to-wall 验证仍有待开展。

Abstract: Accurate identification of Pinus sylvestris var. mongolica plantations is important for monitoring afforestation quality and ecological restoration in northern Shaanxi. This paper proposes ROSA-TFormer, a radar-optical sensor-aware temporal Transformer for P. sylvestris classification using Sentinel-1/2 time-series data generated on Google Earth Engine. The model integrates separate SAR and optical embedding branches, a sensor-aware gate, and temporal attention pooling to capture multi-source seasonal features. Experiments on monthly and half-month point-level datasets show that ROSA-TFormer achieves strong classification performance, with 99.67% overall accuracy, 99.56% macro F1, and 98.91% P. sylvestris F1 on the HalfMonth-dataBig dataset. Spatial block validation and ablation results further indicate the effectiveness of radar-optical temporal fusion and sensor-aware modeling. The results demonstrate the potential of ROSA-TFormer for point-level P. sylvestris plantation classification, while broader wall-to-wall validation remains necessary.

</details>


### [77] [GUMP-Net: An interpretable model-data-driven intelligent algorithm for multi-class pelvic segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19215)
*Liheng Wang,Yinghui Zhang,Licheng Zhang,Hailin Xu,Qiyong Cao,Chong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GUMP-Net，一种融合改进测地线活动轮廓模型与深度网络的可解释多类骨盆分割算法，包含目标检测、边缘检测和迭代演化三个模块，在小样本情况下表现优于现有方法，并可推广至踝关节等其他解剖结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 骨盆分割对骨折精准诊疗和手术规划至关重要，但现有深度学习方法可解释性差，且在小样本情况下性能受限。作者希望结合水平集方法的几何可解释性与深度学习的表征能力，构建一个模型-数据双驱动的智能分割框架。

Method: 提出GUMP-Net，由三个网络模块组成：(1) 目标检测模块，用于水平集自动初始化；(2) 边缘检测器模块，学习解剖感知的边缘检测函数；(3) 迭代模块，实现深度水平集演化。整体结合改进的测地线活动轮廓模型与深度神经网络。

Result: 在骨盆数据集上，GUMP-Net分割精度、鲁棒性和一致性均优于现有最先进方法，尤其在小训练数据场景下优势明显。扩展至踝关节数据集的实验验证了该方法对其他解剖结构的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该算法不仅为复杂骨折复位提供了高效的分割手段，还为理解深度学习分割提供了可解释的几何视角，具有广泛的临床应用潜力。

Abstract: Pelvic segmentation is one of the most important and fundamental research problems in precise and intelligent diagnosis and treatment, as well as surgical planning and navigation for pelvic fractures. By combining an improved geodesic active contour model with deep neural networks, we propose GUMP-Net, an interpretable model-data-driven intelligent algorithm for multi-class pelvic segmentation, in which three network modules are designed to constitute the overall segmentation framework together: the object detection module for automatic level set initialization, the edge detector module for learning an anatomy-aware edge detector function and the iteration module for deep level set evolution. Leveraging the advantages of level set representation and deep learning, GUMP-Net shows more accurate, robust and consistent segmentation performance, especially in small training data situation, compared to the state-of-the-art methods. Extensive experiments on pelvic datasets demonstrate the rationality and effectiveness of the proposed algorithm. Further experiments extended to ankle dataset indicate broader applications to other anatomies. The proposed algorithm not only provides an efficient segmentation method for complex fracture reduction, but also gives an interpretable geometric perspective for understanding deep learning segmentation.

</details>


### [78] [Transformer Geometry Observatory TGO-I: Spectral Geometry Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19249)
*Kaustubh Kapil,Kishor P. Upla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Transformer Geometry Observatory (TGO)框架，系统分析ViT表征的谱几何特性，发现训练过程中维度利用率持续提升、方差在各维度间重新分布，CLS token具有最高有效维度和最低各向异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管ViT广泛应用，对其表征的维度和几何特性的基础理解仍不足，需要系统性的观测框架来揭示表征几何的动态演化规律。

Method: 提出TGO分析框架，首期TGO-I聚焦谱几何。使用ViT-Small/16在ImageNet-100上训练，分析有效秩、稳定秩、参与率、谱熵、谱平坦度、谱各向异性、协方差结构、特征谱和奇异值谱等指标。

Result: 训练过程中，维度利用率持续增加，各向异性下降，谱熵和参与率上升，特征谱趋于平坦。与"训练应使信息集中于少数主导方向"的直觉相反，方差在表征维度间逐步重新分布。CLS token表征的有效维度最高、各向异性最低。

Conclusion: ViT训练并未将表征压缩到少数主导方向，而是促进了方差的广泛再分布，CLS token在其中发挥了特殊的聚合作用。

Abstract: Despite the widespread adoption of Vision Transformers (ViTs) and their success across numerous computer vision applications, the fundamental understanding of their dimensional and representational geometry remains relatively underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce Transformer Geometry Observatory (TGO), a systematic framework of experiments and analysis pipelines designed to investigate the representational geometry and dynamics of Vision Transformers. TGO-I, the first installment of the framework, focuses on the spectral geometry of ViT representations. Using a ViT-Small/16 model trained on ImageNet-100, we analyze Effective Rank, Stable Rank, Participation Ratio, Spectral Entropy, Spectral Flatness, Spectral Anisotropy, covariance structure, eigenspectra, and singular value spectra throughout training. Our results reveal a consistent increase in dimensional utilization, accompanied by decreasing anisotropy, increasing spectral entropy, increasing participation ratio, and progressively flatter eigenspectra. Contrary to the common intuition that training should concentrate information into a small number of dominant directions, we observe a progressive redistribution of variance across representational dimensions. This phenomenon is particularly pronounced in the final CLS token representation, which exhibits the highest effective dimensionality and lowest anisotropy within the network.

</details>


### [79] [OneCanvas: 3D Scene Understanding via Panoramic Reprojection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19253)
*Bartłomiej Baranowski,Dave Zhenyu Chen,Matthias Nießner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OneCanvas 将所有视图的 patch 特征聚合到单张等距柱状全景画布上，通过深度和相机姿态反投影到3D世界坐标，并添加3D位置嵌入来恢复深度信息，使预训练VLM能像处理普通图像一样进行空间推理。该方法还引入空间预训练课程，以极少的训练计算量在多个基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景理解的VLM方法要么依赖复杂的、模型特定的几何编码器，要么需要大量训练预算才能实现空间推理。本文旨在以更简单、更高效的方式赋予VLM从任意视点进行空间推理的能力，同时满足机器人和具身AI中常见的"情境化推理"需求。

Method: 提出OneCanvas方法：(1)将每个patch通过其深度和相机姿态反投影到3D世界坐标，映射到以画布原点为中心的连续经纬度位置上，不做栅格化或跨视图聚合；(2)将patch的米制3D坐标的位置嵌入加到其特征上，以恢复因压缩到角度坐标而丢失的深度信息；(3)所有帧的patch共享同一空间坐标系，无需融合或对backbone做重大架构修改；(4)画布可围绕任意感兴趣位姿居中，支持特定视点的情境推理；(5)引入空间预训练课程：从真实图像中提取物体patch特征，程序化地将其放置在空白画布上选定的3D世界位置，生成覆盖广泛空间推理任务的即时监督信号，并通过控制答案分布减少空间推理捷径。

Result: OneCanvas在SQA3D和VSI-Bench上达到最先进精度，在SPBench上泛化到分布外数据，且训练计算量比最强竞争方法少一个数量级。

Conclusion: OneCanvas通过简洁的单画布表示实现了高效、可泛化的3D场景理解，无需复杂的几何编码器或大规模训练，为VLM空间推理提供了一种轻量且强大的解决方案。

Abstract: Existing approaches to 3D scene understanding in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) either rely on complex, model-specific geometry encoders or large training budgets in pursuit of spatial reasoning. Instead, OneCanvas aggregates patch features from all views onto a single equirectangular panoramic canvas. Namely, each patch is unprojected to a 3D world coordinate using its depth and camera pose, then placed on the canvas at the continuous longitude and latitude of that point as seen from the canvas origin, with no rasterization or aggregation across overlapping views. A 3D position embedding of the patch's metric coordinates is added to its feature, restoring the depth lost when collapsing the world position to an angular canvas coordinate. Patches from all frames thus share one spatial coordinate system with no fusion or major architectural modifications of the backbone. The pretrained VLM consumes this representation as if it were an ordinary image. Because the canvas can be centered on any pose of interest, the same representation directly supports situated reasoning from a specific viewpoint, a common requirement in robotics and embodied AI. Thanks to this representation, we can also introduce a spatial pretraining curriculum: by procedurally placing patch features of objects, drawn from real images, at chosen 3D world positions on an otherwise empty canvas, we generate on-the-fly supervision spanning a broad range of spatial reasoning tasks, with answer distributions controlled to reduce spatial reasoning shortcuts. OneCanvas achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on SQA3D and VSI-Bench, and generalizes to out-of-distribution data on SPBench, using an order of magnitude less training compute than the strongest competing methods.

</details>


### [80] [CABLE: Cloud-Assisted Bandwidth-efficient LMM-based Encoding for V2X Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19258)
*Haohua Que,Zhipeng Bao,Qianyi Wu,Handong Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CABLE提出了一种面向边缘-云协同感知的带宽高效LMM编码框架，通过在边缘端利用历史分割掩码进行运动补偿和ROI提取，仅上传ROI区域图像，在保证感知质量的同时大幅减少通信开销，实现了73-87%的ROI像素覆盖缩减和5-8倍的LMM预填充加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将云端大模型用于V2X感知面临两难：直接上传全分辨率帧导致通信开销巨大和云端预填充延迟高昂，亟需一种带宽高效的边缘-云协同编码方案。

Method: CABLE构建了一个mask-to-ROI-to-LMM反馈环路：边缘端利用自运动补偿传播上一帧的云端分割掩码，通过残差运动线索进行细化，并使用走廊包络（corridor envelope）合并断裂区域，生成鲁棒的ROI；仅上传ROI掩码后的图像至云端，云端LMM分割结果再反馈作为下一帧的先验。

Result: 在nuScenes、WOD-ZB、Waymo、KITTI和CADC五个数据集上，CABLE实现了73-87%的ROI像素覆盖率缩减，通信量显著降低，估计LMM预填充速度提升5-8倍，且检测质量相对全帧推理仅付出适度折损。

Conclusion: CABLE以轻量边缘处理配合云端强大LMM能力，通过时序反馈环路实现高效边缘-云协同感知，在通信效率与感知精度之间取得了良好的工程平衡。

Abstract: Cloud-hosted large multimodal models (LMMs) can provide strong open-vocabulary perception for Vehicle-to-Everything systems, but naively transmitting full-resolution frames from edge to cloud causes severe communication overhead and high cloud-side prefill latency. We present CABLE, a cloud-assisted bandwidth-efficient LMM-based encoding framework for edge-cloud perception. CABLE propagates the previous cloud segmentation mask on the edge using ego-motion compensation, refines it with residual-motion cues, and consolidates disconnected regions via a corridor envelope to form a robust region of interest (ROI). Only ROI-masked images are uploaded, while the cloud segmentation output is fed back as the prior for the next frame, forming a mask-to-ROI-to-LMM feedback loop. Experiments on five datasets (nuScenes, WOD-ZB, Waymo, KITTI, and CADC) show consistent communication savings while largely preserving perception, achieving $73$--$87\%$ ROI pixel-coverage reduction with $5$--$8\times$ estimated LMM prefill speedup at a modest detection-quality trade-off relative to full-frame inference.

</details>


### [81] [A Multi-Domain Benchmark for Detecting AI-Generated Text-Rich Images from GPT-Image-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19259)
*Yijin Wang,Shuyi Wang,Wenhan Zhang,Yuqi Ouyang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一个用于检测GPT Image 2生成的多域文本富图像的基准，评估了现有检测器在零样本下的表现，发现性能高度域依赖且对JPEG压缩敏感，指出需开发文本与布局感知的检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测基准主要关注以对象为中心的图像，对文本语义和布局组织为核心的场景覆盖不足，而文本富图像常含隐私、交易或决策关键信息，检测需求迫切。

Method: 构建了涵盖商业海报、信息图、学术海报、收据、表格和UI截图六类共8,602张GPT Image 2生成图像的多域基准；在此基准上以零样本方式评估五种代表性检测器的整体、类别级和后处理鲁棒性；还探索性评估了多模态视觉语言模型的表现与局限。

Result: 检测器性能高度依赖领域：在某类别表现好的方法在其他类别常失效；最强的传统检测器对JPEG压缩极度敏感；多模态视觉语言模型在结构化格式上展现潜力但也暴露局限。

Conclusion: 文本富图像的AI生成检测需要专门针对文本与布局感知的方法，现有检测器尚无法在跨域场景中提供一致可靠的检测能力，该基准为未来研究提供了重要测试平台。

Abstract: Text-rich images often contain privacy-sensitive, transactional, or decision-relevant information. As recent multimodal image generation models become increasingly capable of synthesizing realistic textual content and structured visual designs, detecting AI-generated text-rich images has become an important challenge for digital trust and content authenticity. Existing benchmarks, however, largely focus on object-centric images and provide limited coverage of scenarios where textual semantics and layout organization are central. In this paper, we introduce a multi-domain benchmark for detecting text-rich images generated by OpenAI's GPT Image 2. The benchmark contains 8,602 images across six representative categories: commercial posters, infographics, academic posters, receipts, tables, and UI screenshots. Using this benchmark, we evaluate five representative AI-generated image detectors in a zero-shot setting and analyze their overall, category-wise, and post-processing robustness. Our results show that detector performance is highly domain-dependent: methods that perform well in some categories often fail on others, and even the strongest conventional detector exhibits severe sensitivity to JPEG compression. We further conduct an exploratory evaluation with a multimodal vision-language model, revealing both its promise and its limitations on structured formats. These findings highlight the need for text- and layout-aware detection methods for modern AI-generated images. Our dataset is released at XXX.

</details>


### [82] [A Unified Framework for Efficient Remote Sensing Visual Question Answering: Adapting Dual, Hybrid, and Encoder-Decoder Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19277)
*Timothy Agboada,Shikha Chandel,Yadav Raj Ghimire,Leila Hashemi-Beni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对遥感视觉问答(RSVQA)中三种VLM架构(CLIP、BLIP、FLAVA)应用RS Adapter参数高效微调策略进行了比较分析，发现混合架构FLAVA在多模态推理和检索方面表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感VQA面临高分辨率、多尺度目标分布和语义复杂性等挑战，通用基础模型因领域偏移巨大和全量微调计算成本过高而难以直接应用，亟需探索资源高效的适配方案。

Method: 提出统一的架构手术管线，在冻结的VLM骨干网络的注意力层和MLP层中注入轻量级瓶颈适配器(RS Adapter)，以不到5%的可训练参数实现快速领域适配；在CLIP(双编码器)、BLIP(编码器-解码器)和FLAVA(混合架构)三种架构上进行比较实验。

Result: 在RSVQA x高分辨率数据集上，所有适配模型均能收敛；混合架构FLAVA在多模态推理与检索能力之间取得了优于单模态对应架构的平衡。

Conclusion: 该研究为灾害评估和城市监测等场景建立了资源高效VQA的新基线，验证了PEFT策略在遥感领域视觉问答中的有效性和架构选择的重要性。

Abstract: Visual Question Answering (VQA) in the Remote Sensing (RS) domain presents unique challenges due to the high resolution, multi scale object distribution, and semantic complexity of aerial imagery. While general domain Foundation Models have achieved remarkable success, their direct application to RSVQA is hindered by massive domain shifts and the computationally prohibitive nature of full fine tuning. This study presents a comparative analysis of RS Adapter, a Parameter Efficient Fine Tuning (PEFT) strategy, applied across three distinct Vision Language Model (VLM) architectures: the Dual Encoder CLIP, the Encoder Decoder BLIP, and the Hybrid FLAVA. We introduce a unified architectural surgery pipeline that injects lightweight bottleneck adapters into the attention and MLP layers of frozen backbones, enabling rapid adaptation with less than 5 percent of trainable parameters. Experimental results on the high resolution RSVQA x dataset demonstrate that while all adapted models achieve convergence, the Hybrid FLAVA architecture offers a superior balance of multimodal reasoning and retrieval capabilities compared to its unimodal counterparts. Our findings establish a new baseline for resource efficient VQA in disaster assessment and urban monitoring.

</details>


### [83] [Confidence is Not Reliability: Rethinking MC Dropout in Brain Tumour Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19300)
*Xin Ci Wong,Duygu Sarikaya,Kieran Zucker,Marc De Kamps,Nishant Ravikumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了蒙特卡洛Dropout不确定性估计在胶质瘤MRI分割中的应用，发现尽管全局不确定性-错误对齐表现优异（AUROC≈0.97），但模型在临床关键的增强肿瘤子区域存在严重校准失败（ECE=0.915），这一缺陷无法被传统Dice和AUROC指标暴露，呼吁临床部署前必须进行子区域特异性校准评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前胶质瘤分割模型仅依赖Dice等重叠指标进行评估，无法检测模型在临床关键子区域（如增强肿瘤）上的静默失败，这对患者安全构成风险。需要探究MC Dropout不确定性估计是否能可靠识别这些关键区域的错误，以及标准报告指标是否能捕捉校准失败。

Method: 在126例BraTS21患者数据上进行双模型案例研究，对比高性能预训练SegResNet和本地训练的带残差单元UNet（UNet-Res）。使用MC Dropout进行体素级不确定性估计，以熵（H）作为不确定性度量，采用AUROC评估不确定性-错误对齐质量，并引入期望校准误差（ECE）进行子区域特异性校准评估。

Result: MC Dropout几乎不影响分割精度（|ΔDice|<0.01），熵的AUROC达约0.97，表明不确定性排序能力强。熵分层可识别出低性能亚组（全肿瘤Dice中位数0.835 vs 0.925）。但UNet-Res在增强肿瘤子区域出现接近零的熵（0.054）和极高ECE（0.915），Dice仅0.714，揭示了严重的校准失败。

Conclusion: 强不确定性-错误对齐是临床安全的必要条件而非充分条件。临床部署模型选择时，必须在AUROC评估之外辅以子区域特异性校准评估，否则关键的校准失败将被掩盖。

Abstract: Glioma segmentation in multiparametric MRI is a critical component of treatment planning. A segmentation model that fails silently on treatment-critical sub-regions represents a patient safety risk that overlap-based metrics such as Dice scores cannot expose. We ask whether voxel-level uncertainty estimation via Monte Carlo (MC) Dropout can reliably identify segmentation errors in clinically critical sub-regions, and whether calibration failure modes are detectable from standard reporting metrics alone. In an empirical two-model case study on 126 BraTS21 patients, we evaluate a high-performance pretrained SegResNet and a locally trained UNet with residual units (UNet-Res). MC dropout preserved segmentation accuracy ($|Δ\text{Dice}|$ $<0.01$) while achieving strong uncertainty-error alignment (AUROC for entropy (H) $\approx$0.97), indicating uncertainty correctly ranks erroneous voxels above correct ones. Entropy-based patient stratification identified a high-uncertainty subgroup with substantially lower segmentation performance (median whole-tumour Dice $0.835$ vs. $0.925$), supporting uncertainty as a practical triage signal. However, global alignment can mask important region-specific differences. Despite similar AUROC, UNet-Res exhibited near-zero enhancing tumour entropy ($0.054$) and Expected Calibration Error (ECE) of $0.915$, with a Dice of only $0.714$, indicating severely miscalibrated confidence on the most clinically critical sub-region, a failure mode invisible to standard Dice and AUROC reporting. These findings demonstrate that strong uncertainty-error alignment is necessary but insufficient for clinical safety: sub-region-specific calibration assessment must accompany AUROC evaluation when selecting models for clinical deployment.

</details>


### [84] [NeuMesh++: Towards Versatile and Efficient Volumetric Editing with Disentangled Neural Mesh-based Implicit Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19316)
*Chong Bao,Yuan Li,Bangbang Yang,Yujun Shen,Hujun Bao,Zhaopeng Cui,Yinda Zhang,Guofeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于网格的神经辐射场表征方法，通过在网格顶点上解耦几何、纹理和语义编码，实现了包括网格引导几何编辑、纹理交换/填充/绘制以及语义引导编辑在内的一系列高效编辑功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经渲染方法在编辑功能上存在严重局限，大多仅支持刚性变换和类别特定的编辑，缺乏一种能够同时支持几何、纹理和语义等多维度、精细化的通用编辑框架。

Method: 提出基于网格的神经辐射场表征（NeuMesh++），在网格顶点上编码解耦的几何、纹理和语义信息。核心技术包括：(1) 局部空间参数化以提升渲染质量与训练稳定性；(2) 顶点上的可学习修正颜色以提升纹理编辑保真度；(3) 空间感知优化策略实现精确纹理编辑；(4) 语义辅助区域选择以简化隐式场编辑的标注负担。

Result: 在真实和合成数据集上的大量实验与编辑示例表明，该方法在表征质量和编辑能力方面均表现出优越性。

Conclusion: NeuMesh++通过将神经辐射场与解耦的网格表征相结合，首次实现了集几何编辑、纹理编辑和语义编辑于一体的通用神经渲染编辑框架，显著拓展了神经隐式渲染技术在3D内容编辑中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Recently neural implicit rendering techniques have evolved rapidly and demonstrated significant advantages in novel view synthesis and 3D scene reconstruction. However, existing neural rendering methods for editing purposes offer limited functionalities, e.g., rigid transformation and category-specific editing. In this paper, we present a novel mesh-based representation by encoding the neural radiance field with disentangled geometry, texture, and semantic codes on mesh vertices, which empowers a set of efficient and comprehensive editing functionalities, including mesh-guided geometry editing, designated texture editing with texture swapping, filling and painting operations, and semantic-guided editing. To this end, we develop several techniques including a novel local space parameterization to enhance rendering quality and training stability, a learnable modification color on vertex to improve the fidelity of texture editing, a spatial-aware optimization strategy to realize precise texture editing, and a semantic-aided region selection to ease the laborious annotation of implicit field editing. Extensive experiments and editing examples on both real and synthetic datasets demonstrate the superiority of our method on representation quality and editing ability. Project page: https://zju3dv.github.io/neumeshplusplus/

</details>


### [85] [Beyond the Current Observation: Evaluating Multimodal Large Language Models in Controllable Non-Markov Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19338)
*Shengyuan Ding,Xilin Wei,Xinyu Fang,Haodong Duan,Dahua Lin,Jiaqi Wang,Yuhang Zang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RNG-Bench 是一个专门用于评估多模态基础模型"回忆并利用已消失的过往观测"能力的基准测试套件，包含配对卡片和3D迷宫两款游戏，通过记忆间隙指标揭示遗忘是主要失败原因，并证明微调可提升性能且可迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准要么暴露完整状态、要么将隐藏状态重建与其他智能体技能混淆、要么仅在回合结束后测试回忆能力，缺乏对闭环策略中"基于已不可见观测进行决策"这一核心能力的隔离评估。

Method: 提出 RNG-Bench，包含两款互补游戏：Matching Pairs（回忆特定位置曾揭示的卡片身份）和 3D Maze（整合自我中心视角构建空间地图）。两者在统一框架下评估，设有三个受控难度轴（网格大小、视觉图案、观测模态）。引入头对头对决协议控制实例级方差，以及记忆间隙（Memory Gap）指标解耦遗忘与糟糕动作选择。

Result: 最困难配置需约128K token上下文和每回合350张图像输入，前沿多模态大语言模型远未饱和。记忆间隙分析表明，大多数残差错误源于遗忘早期观测而非次优决策。在Qwen3.5-9B上使用最优策略轨迹和过滤后的模型演示进行微调，在RNG-Bench上性能提升，并能迁移至现有基准，且不损害通用多模态能力。

Conclusion: RNG-Bench 成功隔离并诊断了多模态模型的非马尔可夫记忆瓶颈，记忆间隙指标证明遗忘而非决策是当前模型的主要短板，且通过针对性微调可以有效提升该能力并实现跨基准迁移。

Abstract: Deploying multimodal foundation models as closed-loop policies increasingly requires conditioning actions on observations that are no longer visible. However, existing benchmarks either expose the full state, conflate hidden-state reconstruction with other agent skills, or test recall only after an episode has ended. We introduce RNG-Bench (Reconstructive Non-Markov Games), a benchmark suite designed to isolate a base model's ability to reconstruct past observations and act on them during multi-step interaction. RNG-Bench includes two complementary games: Matching Pairs, where card identities briefly revealed at specific locations must later be recalled, and 3D Maze, where egocentric views must be integrated into a spatial map. Both games are evaluated under a unified harness with three controlled difficulty axes: grid size, visual pattern, and observation modality. The benchmark further introduces a head-to-head duel protocol to control for instance-level variance and a Memory Gap metric that disentangles forgetting from poor action selection. The hardest configurations require contexts of roughly 128K tokens and 350 image inputs per episode, and remain far from saturated by frontier MLLMs. Memory Gap analysis shows that most residual errors stem from forgetting earlier observations rather than from suboptimal decision making. Finally, fine-tuning Qwen3.5-9B on optimal-policy rollouts and filtered model demonstrations improves performance on RNG-Bench and transfers to existing benchmarks without degrading general multimodal capability.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [86] [Continuous Audio Thinking for Large Audio Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18273)
*Gyojin Han,Dong-Jae Lee,Changho Choi,Jongsuk Kim,Junmo Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoAT 为音频语言模型引入一个连续的潜在思考空间，通过专家蒸馏在生成文本回复前组织声学信息，无需额外自回归解码成本，在多个音频理解任务上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型音频语言模型（LALMs）训练目标是生成文本对齐的回复，其隐藏状态逐渐偏向文本生成，导致语音细节、韵律、声音事件、情感、音高等丰富声学信息在推理过程中丢失，无法被有效利用。

Method: 提出 Continuous Audio Thinking (CoAT) 框架：在 LALM 中嵌入连续潜在思考空间，通过音频专家模型（如音频编码器）的蒸馏提供辅助监督信号，使模型在生成回复前能在此空间中组织和保留声学信息。该连续思考块可在单次 prefill 中处理，不引入额外自回归解码开销。

Result: 在 Qwen2-Audio、Qwen2.5-Omni-7B 和 Audio Flamingo 3 三种 LALM 上验证，涵盖音频推理、音频理解、音乐分类、语音情感和语音转录等任务，CoAT 均带来性能提升。进一步分析证实辅助监督信号可从思考位置传播至模型的文本回复。

Conclusion: CoAT 通过在文本生成前引入连续声学思考空间，有效缓解了 LALM 中声学信息流失问题，且几乎不增加推理成本，是一种通用、高效的音频语言模型增强方法。

Abstract: Large audio language models (LALMs) have shown impressive capabilities on diverse audio understanding tasks, ranging from speech transcription to music analysis. However, because LALMs are typically trained to produce text-aligned responses, their hidden states are progressively shaped for text generation rather than for preserving acoustic information. As a result, the diverse acoustic content that audio carries, such as phonetic detail, prosody, sound events, affect, and pitch, is lost along the way and difficult to leverage in the response. We introduce Continuous Audio Thinking (CoAT), a framework that equips audio language models with a continuous latent workspace for organizing acoustic information prior to response generation, grounded by distillation from audio experts. Within the thinking space, the model can utilize the rich acoustic information provided by expert distillation when generating its response. Furthermore, the proposed continuous thinking block can be processed in a single prefill, so CoAT does not require additional autoregressive decoding cost over the baseline. Across three LALMs, Qwen2-Audio, Qwen2.5-Omni-7B, and Audio Flamingo~3, performance gains on a broad benchmark suite spanning audio reasoning, audio understanding, music classification, speech emotion, and speech transcription demonstrate the effectiveness of CoAT. Further analysis confirms that the auxiliary supervision propagates from the thinking positions to the model's textual responses.

</details>


### [87] [Redact or Keep? A Fully Local AI Cascade for Educational Dialogue De-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18372)
*Haocheng Zhang,Zhuqian Zhou,Kirk Vanacore,Bakhtawar Ahtisham,René F. Kizilcec*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对教育对话文本的去标识化问题，本文提出一个完全本地化的级联框架，将任务从开放域实体识别重构为受限的隐私分类。该框架通过"召回优先的候选生成器+上下文感知的审核器"两阶段设计，在数学辅导对话数据集上取得0.958的宏F1分数，显著超越同家族LLM基线(0.767)和商业API(0.706)，表明在特定领域的去标识化任务中，问题建模方式比模型规模更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育对话转录文本是宝贵但敏感的研究资源。核心矛盾在于：商业大语言模型(LLM)虽能处理课程术语与人名的歧义（如"Riemann"既可指学生也可指数学概念），但需将数据发送至第三方，存在隐私治理风险；而本地命名实体识别(NER)系统虽能保护数据主权，却倾向于过度编辑课程术语。现有方法在治理合规与识别精度之间难以两全。

Method: 提出一个完全本地化的级联框架，将去标识化从开放域实体识别重构为受限的隐私分类任务。框架包含两个阶段：(1) 召回优先的联合提议器（Recall-First Union Proposer）——结合两个轻量级编码器与确定性规则，对候选隐私片段进行过度生成，确保高召回；(2) 上下文感知的审核器（Context-Aware Reviewer）——利用对话上下文和说话人角色信息，对每个候选片段做出二分类决策（Redact/Keep）。实验评估了三种审核器配置，并与同家族LLM基线及商业API进行对比。

Result: 在两个大型数学辅导平台的对话数据上，最强本地配置达到0.958宏F1，远高于同家族LLM-only基线（0.767）和商业API（0.706），且完全运行于单台笔记本电脑。在专门设计的"课程术语-人名声歧义"挑战集上，该配置仅下降0.03 F1，而较小审核器的下降幅度为0.19至0.25。

Conclusion: 在教育文本去标识化任务中，问题建模方式比模型规模更重要。通过将任务重新定义为受限的隐私分类而非开放域实体识别，并采用级联架构配合上下文感知决策，可以在完全本地化、无需向第三方发送数据的前提下，实现优于大模型和商业API的精度。

Abstract: Educational dialogue is a valuable but sensitive resource for research: the same transcripts that capture authentic learning often capture personally identifiable information (PII) entangled with curricular content, where "Riemann" may refer to a real student or to a mathematical concept. Existing approaches force a tradeoff between governance and accuracy. Commercial Large Language Models (LLMs) can handle this ambiguity but require sending student data to third parties, while local named entity recognition (NER) systems preserve governance but over-redact curricular terms. We propose a fully local cascade framework that reframes de-identification from open-ended entity recognition to constrained privacy triage. A recall-first union proposer combines two lightweight encoders with deterministic rules to over-generate candidate spans; a context-aware reviewer then makes a binary Redact/Keep decision for each candidate using surrounding dialogue and speaker role. We evaluate three reviewer configurations against same-family LLM-only baselines and a commercial API on math tutoring transcripts from two large platforms. The strongest local configuration reaches 0.958 macro F1, compared with 0.767 for a same-family LLM-only baseline and 0.706 for the commercial API, while running entirely on a single laptop. On a targeted challenge set of curricular-personal name ambiguity, the same configuration degrades by only 0.03 F1 versus 0.19 to 0.25 for smaller reviewers. These results suggest that for educational de-identification, problem formulation matters more than model scale.

</details>


### [88] [SproutRAG: Attention-Guided Tree Search with Progressive Embeddings for Long-Document RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18381)
*Amirhossein Abaskohi,Issam H. Laradji,Peter West,Giuseppe Carenini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SproutRAG 提出了一种基于注意力引导的分层检索增强生成框架，利用学习到的句间注意力构建二值分块树，将句子级块组织为多粒度语义单元，通过分层束搜索实现多粒度检索，无需额外LLM调用或压缩摘要，在四个基准上平均信息效率提升6.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统的分块策略存在三方面缺陷：LLM引导的分块依赖昂贵的LLM调用，单层上下文扩展仅限单一粒度，层次化摘要则引入信息损失。因此需要一种无需外部LLM、支持多粒度检索且不损失信息的方法来平衡检索粒度与上下文连贯性。

Method: SproutRAG采用注意力引导的层次化框架：(1)利用学习到的跨句注意力权重构建二值分块树，自动学习哪些注意力头和层最能捕捉语义文档结构；(2)句子级块自底向上合并为更大且语义连贯的单元；(3)检索时采用分层束搜索，在多粒度级别检索候选；(4)端到端训练，联合优化嵌入质量与树结构。

Result: 在科学、法律、开放域四个基准数据集上，SproutRAG相比最强基线平均信息效率(IE)提升6.1%。

Conclusion: SproutRAG通过注意力引导的层次化分块与多粒度检索，在不依赖外部LLM调用和损失性摘要的前提下，有效解决了RAG系统中检索粒度与上下文连贯性之间的权衡问题，显著提升了信息效率。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems must balance retrieval granularity with contextual coherence, a challenge that existing methods address through LLM-guided chunking, single-level context expansion, or hierarchical summarization. These approaches variously depend on costly LLM calls during indexing or retrieval, limit context aggregation to a single granularity level, or introduce information loss through summarization. We present SproutRAG, an attention-guided hierarchical RAG framework that addresses this trade-off by organizing sentence-level chunks into progressively larger but semantically coherent units, using learned inter-sentence attention to construct a binary chunking tree. Unlike prior approaches that rely on external LLMs, fixed context expansion, or lossy summarization, SproutRAG learns which attention heads and layers best capture semantic document structure, enabling multi-granularity retrieval without additional LLM calls or compressed summaries. At retrieval time, SproutRAG uses hierarchical beam search to retrieve candidates at multiple granularities, capturing multi-sentence relevance beyond flat retrieval. The framework is trained end-to-end with a joint objective that improves both embeddings and tree structure. Experiments across four benchmarks spanning scientific, legal, and open-domain settings demonstrate that SproutRAG improves information efficiency (IE) by 6.1% on average over the strongest baseline. Code is available on https://github.com/AmirAbaskohi/SproutRAG.

</details>


### [89] [Want Better Synthetic Data? Steer It: Activation Steering for Low-Resource Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18389)
*Jan Cegin,Daniil Gurgurov,Yusser Al Ghussin,Simon Ostermann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究激活引导（activation steering）作为低资源语言合成数据生成的替代方案，通过语言引导和质量引导两种策略，在多个开源LLM和11种语言上验证：早期层引导能持续提升生成数据的多样性及下游模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前低资源语言合成数据生成的最佳方法依赖few-shot prompting，但存在推理成本高和词汇锚定（lexical anchoring）导致多样性下降的问题。因此需要探索更高效的替代方案。

Method: 提出两种激活引导策略：(1) 语言引导（Language Steering），目标是语言的语种身份；(2) 质量引导（Quality Steering），通过对比人类书写与回译文本的表征来捕获文本规范性。在4个开源LLM的多层上，对11种类型多样语言进行实验，生成情感和主题分类数据并微调小型分类器。在zero-shot和few-shot设置下与非引导方法对比。

Result: 早期层上的激活引导能持续提升生成数据的多样性，并通常带来更强的下游模型性能，尤其在低资源语言上表现显著。

Conclusion: 激活引导是一种有效的低资源合成数据生成方法，既能降低推理成本，又能通过提升多样性改善下游任务表现，早期层引导尤为关键。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become an effective tool for synthetic data generation, including for low-resource languages, where generated data can improve downstream task performance. Current best-performing approaches typically rely on few-shot prompting with target-language examples, which increases inference costs and may reduce diversity through lexical anchoring. In this work, we investigate activation steering as an alternative for low-resource synthetic data generation. We study two steering strategies: Language Steering, which targets the linguistic identity of a language, and Quality Steering, which captures well-formedness by contrasting human-written and backtranslated text representations. We evaluate these methods across four open-source LLMs, multiple layers, and 11 typologically diverse languages by generating sentiment and topic classification data and finetuning smaller classifiers. Steering is applied in both zero-shot and few-shot prompting settings and compared against non-steered counterparts. Our results show that steering on early layers consistently improves the diversity of generated data while often yielding stronger downstream model performance, particularly for low-resource languages.

</details>


### [90] [JetFlow: Breaking the Scaling Ceiling of Speculative Decoding with Parallel Tree Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18394)
*Lanxiang Hu,Zhaoxiang Feng,Yulun Wu,Haoran Yuan,Yujie Zhao,Yu-Yang Qian,Bojun Wang,Daxin Jiang,Yibo Zhu,Tajana Rosing,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: JetFlow 是一种新的投机解码框架，通过因果并行草稿头在融合隐藏状态上训练，同时实现单次前向传播的草稿效率与分支级因果条件化，解决了现有方法中自回归草稿成本高、双向扩散草稿一致性问题，从而突破投机解码的扩展瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投机解码方法面临"因果-效率困境"：自回归草稿器能产生路径条件化的候选序列，接受长度高但草稿成本随树深度线性增长；双向块扩散草稿器虽一次生成所有位置，但其分支无关的边缘分布导致树内部不一致，浪费预算并降低接受率。这限制了草稿预算增加带来的加速收益。

Method: JetFlow 在冻结的目标模型之上训练一个因果并行草稿头（causal parallel draft head），该草稿头在融合的隐藏状态上工作，生成的候选树得分与目标模型的自回归分解对齐。具体而言，利用目标模型各层的隐藏状态进行融合，通过因果注意力机制确保每个草稿token只依赖已生成的上下文，同时在一次前向传播中并行生成整个候选树，实现分支级因果条件化。

Result: 在数学、编程和对话基准测试上，JetFlow 在密集模型和MoE Qwen3模型上均一致优于双向头方法和基于树的投机解码基线。H100 GPU上，JetFlow在MATH-500上实现最高9.64倍加速，在开放式对话任务上实现4.58倍加速，并通过vLLM集成在真实服务负载下进一步展示延迟增益。

Conclusion: JetFlow 成功突破了投机解码的扩展瓶颈，通过将一次前向草稿效率与分支因果条件化相结合，使更大的草稿预算能够转化为更长的接受前缀和更高的端到端加速比，在多种任务和模型上验证了其有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates autoregressive Large Language Models (LLMs) by drafting multiple tokens and verifying them in parallel, but it faces a scaling limitation: increasing the draft budget improves speed only when acceptance remains high and drafting overhead stays low. This ceiling has been difficult to break because prior head-based SD methods face a causality-efficiency dilemma. Autoregressive drafters produce path-conditioned candidates that are effective for tree speculative decoding with higher acceptance length, but their drafting cost grows with tree depth. Bidirectional block-diffusion drafters generate all positions in one pass, but their branch-agnostic marginals can form individually plausible yet mutually inconsistent trees, wasting budget and reducing acceptance. We propose JetFlow, a head-based SD framework that combines one-forward drafting efficiency with branch-wise causal conditioning. JetFlow trains a causal parallel draft head over fused hidden states from the frozen target model, producing candidate trees whose scores align with the target model's autoregressive factorization. This enables JetFlow to convert larger draft budgets into longer accepted prefixes and higher end-to-end speedup. Across math, coding, and chat benchmarks on dense and MoE Qwen3 models, JetFlow consistently outperforms bidirectional-head and tree-based SD baselines. On H100 GPUs, JetFlow achieves up to 9.64x speedup on MATH-500 and 4.58x on open-ended conversational workloads, with further latency gains demonstrated through vLLM integration under realistic serving loads. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/hao-ai-lab/JetFlow.

</details>


### [91] [CoreMem: Riemannian Retrieval and Fisher-Guided Distillation for Long-Term Memory in Dialogue Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18406)
*Jiaqi Chen,Yongqin Zeng,Shaoshen Chen,Yijian Zhang,Hai-Tao Zheng,Chunxia Ma,XiuTeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoreMem 提出了一种基于信息几何统一理论框架的边缘-云记忆架构，通过黎曼检索和Fisher引导的离散token蒸馏，在8GB VRAM的边缘设备上实现了高效的长时记忆个性化对话。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化对话系统的长时记忆方案依赖余弦相似度检索和启发式压缩，缺乏统一理论支撑，存在高维检索中的hubness问题和压缩过程中的句法碎片化问题，且难以在消费级硬件上高效部署。

Method: 提出CoreMem架构：(1) 黎曼检索——用局部自适应Fisher-Rao度量替代余弦匹配，通过马氏距离惩罚hub记忆，并采用O(Ndr) Woodbury加速实现实时搜索；(2) Fisher引导的离散token蒸馏(FDTD)——利用Fisher信息迹导出敏感度分数，建立有原则的压缩-KL权衡，并加入显式结构句法保护，实现层次化的句子到token压缩。

Result: 在LOCOMO和LongMemEval-S基准上，CoreMem在开放域推理（+4.51 pp）和时间推理（+4.17 pp）上取得显著提升，且在8GB VRAM严格预算内无缝运行。

Conclusion: CoreMem成功弥合了资源受限边缘设备与具备理论基础的终身记忆智能体之间的鸿沟，为边缘-云协同的个性化对话记忆系统提供了统一的信息几何解决方案。

Abstract: Personalized dialogue agents require continuous long-term memory to maintain coherent interactions across multiple sessions. However, deploying these capabilities on consumer-grade hardware (e.g., 8 GB VRAM edge devices) introduces severe memory and compute bottlenecks. Existing systems typically rely on isotropic cosine similarity for retrieval and heuristic rules for context compression. These approaches lack a unified theoretical foundation, frequently suffering from the hubness problem in high-dimensional retrieval and syntactic fragmentation during compression. To overcome these limitations, we propose CoreMem, a resource-efficient edge-cloud memory architecture fundamentally unified by information geometry. First, Riemannian retrieval replaces cosine matching with a locally adaptive Fisher-Rao metric, effectively penalizing hub memories via Mahalanobis distance with O(Ndr) Woodbury acceleration for real-time search. Second, Fisher-guided discrete token distillation (FDTD) introduces a hierarchical sentence-to-token compression mechanism. It derives sensitivity scores from Fisher information traces, providing a principled compression-KL tradeoff augmented with explicit structural syntax protection. Evaluated on the LOCOMO and LongMemEval-S benchmarks, CoreMem achieves strong accuracy improvements, yielding substantial gains in Open-domain (+4.51 pp) and Temporal (+4.17 pp) reasoning. Extensive profiling confirms that CoreMem operates seamlessly within a strict 8 GB VRAM budget, successfully bridging the gap between resource-constrained edge devices and the demand for theoretically grounded, lifelong memory agents.

</details>


### [92] [VISUALSKILL: Multimodal Skills for Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18448)
*Ziyan Jiang,Li An,Yujian Liu,Jiabao Ji,Qiucheng Wu,Jacob Andreas,Yang Zhang,Shiyu Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: VISUALSKILL 提出一种分层多模态技能库，将视觉图形保留在技能工件中供 GUI 代理使用，相比纯文本技能在长期任务和未见软件上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能库将技能仅表示为文本，忽视了 GUI 交互的视觉本质。在长时域任务和未见过的软件上，计算机使用代理（CUA）仍然表现挣扎，需要一种能保留视觉信息的技能表示方法。

Method: 提出 VISUALSKILL：一种分层多模态技能，按目标应用定制，组织为中央索引加按主题分文件的结构。代理通过 load_topic MCP 工具按需获取相关主题的文本和图形。技能构建采用两阶段流水线：结合人工撰写的文档与实时应用 UI 探索。

Result: 在 CUA-World 和 OSExpert-Eval 两个基准上，搭载 Claude Opus 4.6 的 Claude Code CLI 代理使用 VISUALSKILL 平均得分 0.456，较无技能基线（0.303）绝对提升 +15.3 个百分点。相比仅模态不同的纯文本技能（0.373），VISUALSKILL 进一步获得 +8.3 个百分点的绝对提升。

Conclusion: 在技能工件中保留视觉图形（而非将其转换为文字描述）有助于代理识别 UI 元素并在每次操作后验证工作流状态，为多模态技能库在 GUI 代理中的有效性提供了直接证据。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) approach human-level performance on standardised benchmarks but still struggle on long-horizon tasks and unseen software. Existing skill libraries address this with reusable skills, but represent the skill artifact as text only, despite the visual nature of GUI interaction. We propose VISUALSKILL: a hierarchical multimodal skill, tailored to each target application and organised as a central index over per-topic files, which the agent consumes through a load_topic MCP tool that fetches the relevant topic's text and figures on demand. We construct each skill with a two-stage pipeline that combines authored documentation with live-application UI exploration. On two CUA benchmarks, CUA-World and OSExpert-Eval, a Claude Code CLI agent backed by Claude Opus 4.6 reaches an average score of 0.456 with VISUALSKILL, a +15.3 point absolute lift over the no-skill baseline (0.303). Against a matched text-only skill that is generated from the same source content and differs from VISUALSKILL only in modality, VISUALSKILL yields a further +8.3 point absolute gain over the matched text-only skill (0.373 vs. 0.456), providing direct evidence that retaining visual figures in the skill artifact, rather than verbalizing them away, helps the agent both identify UI elements and verify workflow state after each action. Our code is available at https://github.com/XMHZZ2018/VisualSkills.

</details>


### [93] [LLM Parameters for Math Across Languages: Shared or Separate?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18453)
*Behzad Shomali,Luisa Victor,Tim Selbach,Ali Hamza Bashir,David Berghaus,Joachim Koehler,Mehdi Ali,Markus Frey*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过对多语言大语言模型的数学推理能力进行跨语言机制分析，发现数学相关参数在不同语言间存在部分重叠，且英语激活的参数集最大，低资源语言参数集较小，表明数学推理既非完全语言无关也非完全语言特定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前尚不清楚大语言模型在不同语言间数学推理能力的差异是源于语言特定的参数，还是共享机制在不同语言中的差异化表现。本研究旨在通过跨语言机制分析来澄清这一问题。

Method: 采用跨语言机制分析方法，定位并比较不同语言下支持数学推理的模型参数，分析这些参数在模型各层的分布及跨语言重叠程度。

Result: 数学相关参数在不同语言间呈现部分重叠，重叠最强区域集中在模型中间层；英语始终产生最大规模的数学相关参数集，而低资源语言的相关参数集较小。

Conclusion: 多语言LLM的数学推理行为既非完全语言不变，也非完全语言特定，而是表现为部分跨语言参数重叠与系统性语言依赖差异并存的混合模式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit substantial cross-lingual variation in mathematical reasoning performance, but it remains unclear whether these differences reflect language-specific parameters or a shared mechanism that manifests differently by language. We present a cross-lingual mechanistic analysis of mathematical reasoning in LLMs, enabling us to localize and compare model parameters that support mathematical reasoning across languages. We find that the extracted math-associated parameters exhibit partial cross-lingual overlap, with the strongest overlap concentrated in intermediate model layers. We further observe that English consistently produces the largest set of math-relevant parameters, whereas lower-resource languages reveal smaller sets of relevant parameters. These results suggest that math-related behavior in multilingual LLMs is neither fully language-invariant nor fully language-specific, but instead exhibits partial cross-lingual parameter overlap with systematic language-dependent differences.

</details>


### [94] [Montreal Forced Aligner and the state of speech-to-text alignment in 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18466)
*Michael McAuliffe,Kaylynn Gunter,Michael Wagner,Morgan Sonderegger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MFA 3.0 经过十年迭代，在多语言覆盖、IPA词典、模型自适应等方面大幅升级，在英日韩三语基准测试中达到或接近SOTA，平均边界误差低于15ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MFA 自2016年发布以来虽被广泛使用，但缺乏对其3.0版本系统性升级的文档记录，以及跨语言、跨数据集条件下的最新性能基准评估。

Method: 本文整合了更大规模的开源数据集、统一的IPA词典、模型自适应训练、跨语言音素映射、发音概率建模与音系规则等多项改进，并在英语、日语、韩语的四个基准数据集上，与传统及神经网络强制对齐器进行对比评估。

Result: MFA 3.0 在所有四个基准数据集上均达到或接近最优水平，平均边界误差低于15毫秒；自适应训练和跨语言音素重映射对训练分布外的语言有效，发音概率建模与音系规则在特定条件下带来额外增益。

Conclusion: MFA 3.0 通过十年持续优化已成为当前性能最强的强制对齐工具之一，其多语言扩展策略与配套工具有效提升了跨语言对齐的鲁棒性与准确性。

Abstract: The Montreal Forced Aligner (MFA) was released in 2016 and has since become the most widely used tool for forced alignment in research and industry. In the decade since, MFA has undergone substantial development, including expanded coverage across more languages and dialects using larger open-source datasets, harmonized IPA dictionaries, model adaptation, cross-language phone remapping, and support utilities. This paper documents MFA 3.0's developments since version 1.0 and evaluates MFA's performance across English, Japanese, and Korean, benchmarked against classic and neural forced aligners. MFA 3.0 achieves state-of-the-art or near state-of-the-art performance across all four benchmark datasets with mean boundary errors below 15 ms. Adaptation and cross-language remapping are effective for languages outside MFA's training distribution, and pronunciation probability modeling and phonological rules provide gains in specific conditions.

</details>


### [95] [Possible or Definite? A Benchmark for Evaluating Diagnostic Uncertainty Preservation in Clinical Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18471)
*Hongbo Du,Zixin Lu,Jiaming Qu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLMs在临床文本任务中未能正确保留诊断不确定性，对不确定性表述的修改可能改变临床含义。该研究构建了包含9184条不确定性标注的基准数据集，评估三个LLM后发现模型保留原始不确定性线索的比例常不足一半，且在相邻不确定性等级之间难以做出精细区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM临床文本研究多关注流畅性和连贯性，但忽略了一个关键问题：LLM在生成或修改临床文本时是否正确保留了诊断不确定性。不确定性表述（如"可能的肺炎"）直接影响后续检查和治疗决策，其被篡改可能导致临床含义完全改变，带来安全隐患。

Method: 研究分两步进行：(1)构建了一个包含1200份临床文档、涵盖五个不确定性等级、共9184条标注的基准数据集；(2)在该基准上对三个大型语言模型进行系统评估，考察其保留原始不确定性线索的能力及对不同等级间细微差异的区分能力。

Result: (1)LLM保留原始不确定性线索的表现较差，正确保留比例通常不足50%；(2)LLM在相邻不确定性等级之间的细微区分上存在明显困难，倾向于混淆相近的等级。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了一种标准评估指标无法捕获的失效模式，即LLM在临床文本中无法可靠保留诊断不确定性，这对LLM在临床工作流程中的安全部署具有重要启示，提示需要关注此类语义保真度问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for clinical text tasks such as summarization and revision. While most studies evaluate the fluency and coherence of LLM-generated text, whether LLMs correctly preserve diagnostic uncertainty remains underexplored. In clinical practice, phrases such as ``possible pneumonia'' communicate the strength of available evidence and directly guide decisions about follow-up testing and treatment. Altering these uncertainty expressions can change the clinical meaning entirely. In this paper, we systematically evaluated this problem in two steps. First, we constructed a benchmark of 1,200 clinical documents with 9,184 uncertainty annotations across five levels. Second, we evaluated three LLMs on this benchmark. Our results show that (1) LLMs preserve the original uncertainty cues poorly, often less than half the time; (2) LLMs struggle with nuanced distinctions between adjacent levels. This work reveals a failure mode not captured by standard evaluation metrics and provides implications for the safe deployment of LLMs in clinical workflows.

</details>


### [96] [PreUnlearn: Auditing Collateral Knowledge Damage Before Large Language Model Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18473)
*Bo Su,Ankit Shah,Thai Le*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从数据视角研究大语言模型遗忘学习，发现遗忘操作造成的附带损害随语义距离衰减但不会消失，并提出"遗忘集审计"作为事前预测工具，利用遗忘集与评估集之间的交互特征来预警风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM遗忘学习中，需要遗忘的知识与需要保留的知识之间边界模糊，相关甚至远距离信息可能在模型中纠缠。现有方法缺乏对遗忘效应如何传播的系统理解，更缺乏在遗忘执行前预测附带损害的手段。

Method: 采用数据驱动视角：(1) 量化遗忘效应从遗忘集向同域和远域知识的传播模式，测量附带损害随语义距离的衰减规律；(2) 将遗忘集审计形式化为遗忘前的预测任务，分析哪些数据特征最能预测下游损害，重点考察遗忘集与评估集之间的交互特征。

Result: 发现一致的衰减模式：附带损害在遗忘集附近最强，随语义距离增加而减弱，但在领域边界处并不消失。遗忘集与评估集之间的交互特征提供了最强的预测信号，表明附带损害在模型更新前已部分反映在数据几何中。

Conclusion: 遗忘集审计可作为早期预警工具，在遗忘执行前识别高风险操作，为设计更可靠的遗忘流程提供指导。附带损害的可预测性意味着数据几何中蕴含了遗忘行为的关键先验信息。

Abstract: Machine unlearning for large language models (LLMs) aims to remove specified knowledge while preserving the rest of the model's capabilities. However, the boundary between knowledge to forget and knowledge to retain is often unclear, since related and even distant information may be entangled in the model. In this paper, we study LLM unlearning from a data-centric perspective and measure how unlearning effects propagate from the forget set to same-domain and distant-domain knowledge. We find a consistent decay pattern: collateral damage is strongest near the forget set, weakens with semantic distance, but does not disappear at domain boundaries. We further ask whether such damage can be audited before unlearning is executed. We formulate forget-set auditing as a pre-unlearning prediction task and analyze which data features are most predictive of downstream damage. Our results show that interaction features between the forget set and evaluation set provide the strongest signals, suggesting that collateral damage is partly reflected in data geometry before model updates occur. These findings position forget-set auditing as an early warning tool for identifying risky unlearning runs and designing more reliable unlearning procedures.

</details>


### [97] [Towards Scalable Customization and Deployment of Multi-Agent Systems for Enterprise Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18502)
*Paresh Dashore,Shreyas Kulkarni,Uttam Gurram,Nadia Bathaee,Kartik Balasubramaniam,Genta Indra Winata,Sambit Sahu,Shi-Xiong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个统一框架，通过两阶段流程解决LLM多智能体系统在生产环境中的定制化和高效部署问题：第一阶段进行智能体模型定制（持续预训练+SFT+偏好优化），第二阶段进行推理优化（投机解码+FP8量化），最终实现4.48倍吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统在复杂推理和任务执行上表现优异，但在实际生产部署中面临两大挑战：一是需要针对特定领域进行定制化适配，二是智能体工作流中存在高延迟和高推理成本问题。

Method: 提出统一的两阶段框架。第一阶段为"智能体模型定制"：融合持续预训练（continual pretraining）、监督微调（SFT）和偏好优化（preference optimization），将紧凑模型适配到垂直领域，同时保留其智能体能力。第二阶段为"推理优化"：结合推测性解码（speculative decoding）和FP8量化，并辅以针对性校准（targeted calibration），实现低成本高效推理。

Result: 在企业工作负载上，该框架实现了快速的领域适配，推理吞吐量提升4.48倍，同时保持了模型性能，并在长尾场景上提升了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该统一框架能有效解决多智能体系统在真实场景中的定制化和高效部署问题，兼顾了领域适配能力、推理效率与模型性能的平衡。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems demonstrate strong performance on complex reasoning and task execution, enabling broad enterprise applications. However, production deployment remains challenging due to domain-specific customization requirements and high latency and inference costs in agentic workflows. We propose a unified framework for customization and efficient deployment of multi-agent systems in real-world settings. The first stage, Agentic Model Customization, combines continual pretraining, supervised fine-tuning, and preference optimization to adapt a compact model to specialized domains while retaining strong agentic capabilities. The second stage, Inference Optimization, integrates speculative decoding and FP8 quantization with targeted calibration to enable cost-efficient serving with minimal quality loss. Across enterprise workloads, our framework enables rapid domain adaptation and achieves a 4.48x speedup in throughput while maintaining performance and improving robustness on long-tail scenarios.

</details>


### [98] [MCompassRAG: Topic Metadata as a Semantic Compass for Paragraph-Level Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18508)
*Amirhossein Abaskohi,Raymond Li,Gaetano Cimino,Peter West,Giuseppe Carenini,Issam H. Laradji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MCompassRAG提出了一种元数据引导的检索框架，通过主题级信号增强块表示，在不增加额外LLM调用的情况下实现主题感知检索，在信息效率上平均提升8.24%，延迟降低5倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统中文档分块粒度存在根本性权衡：细粒度块提高检索精度但增加搜索空间和延迟，粗粒度块减少候选数量但引入语义噪声。这一矛盾在跨大规模异构语料库的深度研究任务中尤为突出。

Method: 提出MCompassRAG框架：(1)在相同嵌入空间中用主题元数据丰富块表示；(2)通过LLM-teacher蒸馏训练轻量级检索器；(3)推理时实现主题感知检索，无需额外LLM调用。

Result: 在六个复杂检索基准测试上，MCompassRAG的信息效率（IE）平均提升8.24%，同时比最强的高效RAG基线延迟降低5倍以上。

Conclusion: 通过元数据引导的主题感知检索，MCompassRAG有效解决了分块粒度权衡问题，在保证检索精度的同时显著降低了推理延迟和成本。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems depend critically on how documents are chunked and searched. Fine-grained chunks can improve retrieval precision but expand the search space, increasing latency and cost; larger chunks reduce the number of candidates but make dense similarity less reliable, as the representation for each chunk mixes multiple topics and introduces more semantic noise. This trade-off becomes especially limiting in deep research tasks, where retrieval must be both fast and precise across large, heterogeneous corpora. We introduce MCompassRAG, a metadata-guided retrieval framework that uses topic-level signals as a semantic compass for selecting relevant evidence. Instead of relying only on cosine similarity between queries and noisy chunk embeddings, MCompassRAG enriches chunk representations with topic metadata in the same embedding space and trains a lightweight retriever through LLM-teacher distillation. At inference time, MCompassRAG performs topic-aware retrieval without additional LLM calls, improving both efficiency and evidence quality. Across six complex retrieval benchmarks, MCompassRAG improves information efficiency (IE) by 8.24% on average with over 5 times lower latency than the strongest efficient RAG baselines. Code is available on https://github.com/AmirAbaskohi/MCompassRAG.

</details>


### [99] [Speech-Driven End-to-End Language Discrimination towards Chinese Dialects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18584)
*Fan Xu,Jian Luo,MingWen Wang,GuoDong Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合语音驱动特征（MFCC）与端到端语音识别的中文方言鉴别方法，通过CNN融合词级嵌入和MFCC特征，在两个基准中文方言语料库上取得了优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的文本驱动方法在相似语言、变体和方言的鉴别任务上表现不佳，需要探索更有效的特征来源。语音信号中蕴含丰富的方言区分信息，但此前缺乏系统性的探索。

Method: 1. 系统探索MFCC语音特征在CNN方言分类中的适用性；2. 设计基于HMM-DNN的端到端语音识别模型预测中文方言词；3. 引入注意力机制提取与不同方言相关的判别性词语；4. 通过CNN融合词级嵌入表示与MFCC声学特征，进行最终方言分类。

Result: 在两个基准中文方言语料库上的评估表明，所提出的语音驱动方法在细粒度中文方言鉴别任务上优于当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: 语音驱动特征对细粒度中文方言鉴别具有显著的有效性和适用性，将声学特征与语言学特征融合是提升方言分类性能的有效策略。

Abstract: Language discrimination among similar languages, varieties, and dialects is a challenging natural language processing task. The traditional text-driven focus leads to poor results. In this paper, we explore the effectiveness of speech-driven features towards language discrimination among Chinese dialects. First, we systematically explore the appropriateness of speech-driven MFCC features towards CNN-based language discrimination. Then, we design an end-to-end speech recognition model based on HMM-DNN to predict Chinese dialect words. We adopt attention to extract the discriminative words related to different Chinese dialects. Finally, through a CNN, we combine the word-level embedding and the MFCC-based features. Evaluation of two benchmark Chinese dialect corpora shows the appropriateness and effectiveness of the proposed speech-driven approach to fine-grained Chinese dialect discrimination compared to the state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [100] [Dual Dimensionality for Local and Global Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18587)
*Zhiyuan Wang,Xuan Luo,Sirui Zeng,Xifeng Yan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出距离自适应表征（DAR），对局部上下文窗口内的token保留完整维度表征，而对远处token使用降维表征（如1/4维度），在保持性能的同时减少KV缓存开销，挑战了键值维度应跨位置统一的传统假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言中，下一个词受邻近token的影响远大于远处token，但现有Transformer对所有位置的Key和Value使用相同维度。作者假设局部token需要更丰富的表征来预测即时输出，而远处token作为长期记忆可用低维表征，由此提出距离自适应的非对称表征分配策略。

Method: 提出DAR（Distance-Adaptive Representation）：在局部上下文窗口内保留完整维度表征，对超出窗口的token分配降维表征（如原始维度的1/4）。该方案在受控设定下实现，并通过线性投影实现维度压缩与对齐。实验覆盖70M到410M参数的多尺度预训练，以及在1B模型上进行持续监督微调。

Result: DAR在多个预训练规模及1B模型微调中，性能均能紧密匹配全维度基线；而所有位置统一降维则导致性能显著下降。这验证了按距离非对称分配表征容量的有效性。

Conclusion: KV维度不必跨token位置统一；距离自适应表征为注意力架构设计开辟了新方向，可在推理时进一步缩减KV缓存，同时保持模型性能。

Abstract: Decoder-only Transformers compute attention over the KV cache of preceding tokens. Keys (and Values) are typically represented with the same dimensionality, regardless of its distance from the prediction target. In natural language, however, the next word is most strongly influenced by the immediately preceding tokens. We hypothesize that local and distant tokens impose asymmetric demands on representational capacity: local tokens are more critical for predicting immediate outputs and thus require richer representations, whereas distant tokens primarily serve as long-range memory, for which lower-dimensional representations may suffice. We formalize this idea as Distance-Adaptive Representation (DAR), implemented in a controlled setting that preserves full-dimensional representations within a local context window while assigning reduced-dimensional representations (e.g. 1/4 of the original dimensionality) to tokens beyond that window. Across multiple pretraining scales (70M to 410M parameters), as well as continued supervised fine-tuning on a 1B-scale model, this approach closely matches the performance of full-dimensional baselines. In contrast, uniformly reducing dimensionality across all token positions leads to worse performance. These results challenge the common assumption that key and value dimensionality should be uniform across token positions. Our findings suggest a new direction for designing attention architectures that adaptively allocate representational capacity across sequences, enabling further reductions in KV cache during inference.

</details>


### [101] [Low-resource Language Discrimination Towards Chinese Dialects with Transfer learning and Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18597)
*Fan Xu,Yangjie Dan,Keyu Yan,Yong Ma,Mingwen Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合迁移学习与数据增强的中文方言辨识框架CDDTLDA，通过在大规模语料上预训练源端ASR模型，利用变速、变调、加噪等数据增强手段扩充低资源目标方言数据后进行微调，并借助自注意力机制捕获共享语义特征，最终在目标ASR模型中提取隐藏语义表示实现方言辨识，在两个基准数据集上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中文方言辨识面临标注资源稀缺的瓶颈，传统方法难以在低资源场景下取得良好表现，亟需一种能够充分利用有限标注数据、同时借助迁移学习和数据增强来提升低资源方言辨识性能的框架。

Method: 提出CDDTLDA框架：(1) 首先在大规模中文方言语料上训练源端ASR模型（迁移学习预训练阶段）；(2) 采用速度扰动、音高扰动和噪声注入三种数据增强方法扩充目标端低资源方言数据；(3) 基于源端ASR模型对目标端ASR模型进行微调；(4) 利用自注意力机制捕获源端与目标端ASR模型之间潜在的共性语义特征；(5) 从微调后的目标ASR模型中提取隐藏语义表示用于最终方言分类。

Result: 在两个基准中文方言语料库上的实验结果表明，CDDTLDA模型显著优于现有的各类先进方法。

Conclusion: CDDTLDA框架通过迁移学习与数据增强的有效结合，成功缓解了中文方言辨识中标注资源不足的问题，验证了在大规模语料预训练基础上辅以数据扰动微调策略在低资源方言分类任务中的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Chinese dialects discrimination is a challenging natural language processing task due to scarce annotation resource. In this article, we develop a novel Chinese dialects discrimination framework with transfer learning and data augmentation (CDDTLDA) in order to overcome the shortage of resources. To be more specific, we first use a relatively larger Chinese dialects corpus to train a source-side automatic speech recognition (ASR) model. Then, we adopt a simple but effective data augmentation method (i.e., speed, pitch, and noise disturbance) to augment the target-side low-resource Chinese dialects, and fine-tune another target ASR model based on the previous source-side ASR model. Meanwhile, the potential common semantic features between source-side and target-side ASR models can be captured by using self-attention mechanism. Finally, we extract the hidden semantic representation in the target ASR model to conduct Chinese dialects discrimination. Our extensive experimental results demonstrate that our model significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods on two benchmark Chinese dialects corpora.

</details>


### [102] [Steerable Cultural Preference Optimization of Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18606)
*Minsik Oh,Advit Deepak,Sophie Wu,Douwe Kiela,Ekaterina Shutova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SCPO算法，用于训练可均衡融合多元文化偏好的奖励模型，在PRISM和GlobalOpinionQA数据集上，少数群体奖励模型性能提升最高7个点，训练数据效率提升最高280%，并有效缓解了过度偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM对齐研究主要聚焦于预测特定区域标注者的统一响应偏好，忽视了全球不同文化亚群体的差异化偏好。亟需开发具有全球化视野的对齐模型，能够准确代表各亚群体偏好且不表现出过度偏差。

Method: 提出Steerable Cultural Preference Optimization（SCPO）奖励模型训练算法，通过加权方法在多文化偏好数据上实现均衡训练，使奖励模型能够以平衡的方式融入多样化文化偏好。

Result: 在PRISM和GlobalOpinionQA两个数据集、覆盖7个国家的实验中，SCPO使少数群体奖励模型性能相比基线提升最高7个点；训练数据效率相比全量数据微调提升最高280%；加权方法有效缓解了对特定亚群体的过度偏差。

Conclusion: SCPO算法成功实现了在奖励模型中均衡融合多元文化偏好，为构建更具全球化视野和公平性的LLM对齐技术提供了有效路径。

Abstract: It is essential for large language model (LLM) technology to serve many different cultural sub-communities in a manner that is acceptable to each community. However, research on LLM alignment has so far predominantly focused on predicting a unified response preference of annotators from certain regions. This paper aims to advance the development of alignment models with a more global outlook, that are able to accurately represent the preferences of subcommunities and do not exhibit excessive bias towards any of them. We focus on the development of reward models for this purpose and present a novel reward model training algorithm (SCPO) that can incorporate diverse cultural preferences in a balanced manner. Our method results in performance increases of the minority reward model of up to 7 points over the baseline model across two datasets, PRISM and GlobalOpinionQA, and across 7 countries. SCPO is up to 280% more training data-efficient than full-data finetuning of reward models. In addition, we perform analysis of bias by separately evaluating on the preference of subcommunities and show that excessive bias is mitigated via our weighting method. Our code is available at https://github.com/minsik-ai/Steerable-Cultural-Preference

</details>


### [103] [Are LLMs Ready to Assist Physicians? PhysAssistBench for Interactive Doctor-Patient-EHR Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18613)
*Tianming Du,Peijie Yu,Sihan Shang,Danli Shi,My Linh Nguyen,Shengbo Gao,Guangyuan Li,Yinghong Yu,Yan Jiang,Qianlong Zhao,Behzad Bozorgtabar,Shaoxiong Ji,Jiazhen Pan,Daniel Rueckert,Jiancheng Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PhysAssistBench 是一个基于真实 MIMIC-IV 病例构建的交互式医-患-EHR 助手评估基准，通过可交互的智能体患者在保持临床事实准确性的前提下，将静态 EHR 记录转化为多轮临床场景，包含 1,296 轮人工审核和医生验证的双语评估集。实验表明，当前顶级 LLM 在此场景下仍不可靠，揭示出可靠的临床辅助需要协调知识、沟通与系统交互能力，而非孤立地提升其中任一维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医学 LLM 的评估通常孤立地测试临床知识、EHR 系统交互或医患沟通等单一能力，但现实中医生辅助需要同时协调这些能力：医生发出欠明确指令、患者含糊描述症状、EHR 系统要求精确的工具调用。缺乏一个能综合考察这三方交互的评估基准。

Method: 作者提出 PhysAssistBench 基准，利用 MIMIC-IV 真实病例，通过可扩展流水线构建"智能体患者"——这些交互式、扎根于病历的智能体将静态 EHR 记录转化为多轮临床场景，同时保证临床事实性。最终生成了 1,296 轮经人工审核和医生验证的双语（中英）评估集。

Result: 在 PhysAssistBench 上对领先 LLM 的实验表明，当前模型在医生-患者-EHR 三方交互场景中表现仍不可靠。

Conclusion: 可靠的临床 LLM 辅助需要跨知识、沟通和系统交互的协调能力，而非孤立地提升其中单一维度。PhysAssistBench 暴露了这一关键瓶颈，为未来医学 LLM 的综合能力评估提供了重要基准。

Abstract: The most plausible near-term role of medical LLMs is to assist rather than replace physicians, yet current evaluations often test isolated capabilities: clinical knowledge, EHR system interaction, or patient communication. Physician assistance instead requires coordinating these capabilities within the same interaction, where physicians issue underspecified requests, patients describe symptoms ambiguously, and EHR systems demand precise tool use. We introduce PhysAssistBench, a benchmark for interactive doctor-patient-EHR assistance. Built from real MIMIC-IV cases, PhysAssistBench uses a scalable pipeline to construct agentic patients: interactive, record-grounded agents that turn static EHR records into multi-turn clinical scenarios while preserving clinical factuality. PhysAssistBench provides a curated bilingual evaluation set of 1,296 manually reviewed and physician-validated turns. Experiments with leading LLMs show that current models remain unreliable in this setting, which exposes a key bottleneck for clinical LLMs: reliable assistance requires coordination across knowledge, communication, and systems, not isolated gains in any of them.

</details>


### [104] [BCL: Bayesian In-Context Learning Framework for Information Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18620)
*Haoliang Liu,Chengkun Cai,Xu Zhao,Han Zhu,Shizhou Huang,Xinglin Zhang,Tao Chen,Jenq-Neng Hwang,Zhang Huaping,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BCL 是首个将粒子滤波与贝叶斯更新相结合来系统化优化信息抽取任务中标签表示的框架，通过初始化、观测、权重更新和重采样四步流程，统一适用于序列标注和关系分类两种范式，在实验中取得了一致且显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于上下文学习的信息抽取方法存在两大痛点：一是不同模型规模下性能表现不一致，二是缺乏系统化的优化手段和跨任务泛化能力。需要一种能够系统化优化且具备通用性的新框架来弥合这些不足。

Method: 提出 BCL（Bayesian In-Context Learning）框架，核心是将粒子滤波与贝叶斯更新引入上下文学习中的标签表示优化。流程分为四步：(1) 初始化（Initialization）——初始化标签表示的粒子集合；(2) 观测（Observation）——利用标注示例观测模型预测与真实标签的差异；(3) 权重更新（Weight Update）——基于贝叶斯规则，根据观测结果更新各粒子的权重；(4) 重采样（Resampling）——按权重重采样粒子，迭代精化标签表示。该框架可泛化至序列标注和关系分类两种 IE 子任务。

Result: 在多个 IE 基准数据集上的大量实验表明，BCL 相比现有方法取得了显著且一致的性能提升，在不同模型规模下均表现稳定。

Conclusion: BCL 作为首个引入粒子滤波与贝叶斯更新来优化 IE 上下文学习的框架，兼具系统化优化能力和跨任务泛化性，为信息抽取领域的上下文学习提供了一个有效且通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Existing information extraction (IE) tasks increasingly adopt in-context learning (ICL) with large language models. However, current approaches either show inconsistent performance across model scales or lack systematic optimization and generalizability. Building on this, we propose BCL (Bayesian In-Context Learning Framework for Information Extraction), the first optimization framework that uses particle filtering with Bayesian updates to systematically refine label representations across IE tasks. Through four steps initialization, observation, weight update, and resampling, BCL generalizes to both sequence labeling and relation classification paradigms. Extensive experiments demonstrate substantial and consistent improvements over existing approaches.

</details>


### [105] [PragReST: Self-Reinforcing Counterfactual Reasoning for Pragmatic Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18624)
*Jihyung Park,Minchao Huang,Leqi Liu,Elias Stengel-Eskin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 为解决大语言模型在语用推理（pragmatic reasoning）上的不足，提出PragReST——一种自监督框架，通过构建语用QA数据、生成反事实推理轨迹，并借助监督微调与强化学习促使模型内化语用推理能力，无需人工标注或教师蒸馏。在四个语用基准上显著提升准确率，并保持通用领域性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在数学和逻辑推理上表现强劲，但在需要从隐含意义中做出语用推理时仍倾向于选择字面解释。现有方法依赖人工标注数据或更强教师模型的蒸馏，成本高昂且不易扩展。因此需要一种无需外部监督即可提升LLM语用推理能力的自监督方法。
