Method: 构建LOCUS原始语料库，收录几乎所有公开可用的市县法令；使用OCR处理多种文档格式；创建覆盖2,309个县（共3,144个县）的县协调访问层；训练基于ModernBERT的分类器和评分器，从多个维度（如不透明性、家长主义等）分析地方法律。

Result: 原始语料库包含9,239个市县的法规文本；协调层覆盖美国大多数人口的县；配套发布覆盖元数据和下游分析模型，支持可复现研究和法律AI应用。

Conclusion: LOCUS首次将大规模地方法令转化为机器可读格式，填补了法律AI领域的关键数据空白，并提供了多维分析工具，未来可支撑更深入的地方性法律计算研究。

Abstract: Progress in legal AI increasingly depends on access to authoritative legal text at scale. Yet one of the most consequential layers of American law remains largely absent from existing machine-readable corpora: local ordinances. Local codes govern zoning, housing, business licensing, public health, noise, animal control, and many other domains of everyday regulation, but they are fragmented across vendor platforms designed for human browsing rather than bulk research access. We introduce LOCUS - the Local Ordinance Corpus for the United States - a comprehensive corpus and county-harmonized access layer for U.S. municipal and county ordinance codes. The raw corpus, available for release to researchers, represents nearly all publicly available municipal and county ordinance codes. The resulting raw corpus contains codes from 9,239 cities and counties. A smaller county-harmonized LOCUS access layer provides coverage for the largest 2,309 of 3,144 U.S. counties, accounting for a majority of the population. We use OCR to handle the myriad of document formats that have kept the law from being a public resource. We release the corpus with coverage metadata to support reproducibility, downstream legal AI research, and the incremental expansion of machine-readable access to local law. We train a collection of ModernBERT-based classifiers and scorers to facilitate analyzing U.S. local law among several dimensions, such as opacity and paternalism, that have not previously been studied at this scale. LOCUS-v1 and its derivative models are available at: https://huggingface.co/datasets/LocalLaws/LOCUS-v1

</details>


### [141] [Learning User Simulators with Turing Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19336)
*Yingshan Susan Wang,Cedegao E. Zhang,Linlu Qiu,Zexue He,Pengyuan Li,Alex Pentland,Roger P. Levy,Yoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Turing-RL，一种基于图灵测试的强化学习方法，用于训练用户模拟器。该方法利用LLM判别器评估生成回复与真实用户回复的不可区分性，并以此作为奖励信号优化策略。在对话聊天和Reddit论坛两个领域，Turing-RL在LLM评估和人工评估上均一致优于基于响应匹配的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用户模拟器训练方法主要通过最大化对数概率或相似度奖励来匹配单一真实回复，这种"响应匹配"范式忽略了用户行为固有的多样性和不可区分性目标。作者认为，用户模拟器的核心目标应是生成与真实用户无法区分的回复，而非精确复现某一条特定回复。

Method: 提出Turing-RL框架：(1) 使用判别式图灵奖励(Turing reward)，由LLM裁判根据用户历史上下文，对生成回复与真实用户回复的不可区分性进行评分；(2) 将该评分作为强化学习的奖励信号，训练用户模拟器LLM策略，使其学会生成与真实用户行为不可区分的回复。该方法本质上优化的是不可区分性而非响应匹配。

Result: 在对话聊天和Reddit论坛讨论两个不同领域进行了实验，Turing-RL在LLM自动评估指标和人工评估指标上均一致优于所有基线方法（包括最大化对数概率、相似度奖励等方法）。

Conclusion: 优化"不可区分性"而非"响应匹配"是训练用户模拟器的有效策略。Turing-RL为交互式环境中的人类用户模拟提供了一种新的范式，可广泛应用于智能助手训练、个性化系统评估和社会科学研究等领域。

Abstract: Learning to simulate human users in interactive settings could advance the training of agent assistants, evaluation of personalization systems, research in the social sciences, and more. Existing approaches generally do so by training a large language model (LLM) to match a single ground truth response, either by maximizing the log probability or by using a similarity reward. We instead propose {Turing-RL}: a Turing-Test-based reinforcement learning approach for training user simulator models. {Turing-RL} uses a discriminative Turing reward with an LLM judge to score how indistinguishable a generated response is from the real user's given the user's history, and the user simulator LLM learns to produce responses indistinguishable from what the user could have said with such rewards. Across two different domains--conversational chat and Reddit forum discussion--we find that {Turing-RL} consistently outperforms baseline methods on both LLM and human evaluation metrics. Our study suggests that optimizing for indistinguishability, rather than response matching, is effective for learning user simulators.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Gaussian Mixture Attention: Linear-Time Sequence Mixing via Probabilistic Latent Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18283)
*Yongchao Huang,Hassan Raza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GMA 将查询和键映射到共享高斯混合分量上的后验责任向量，通过 K 槽潜记忆实现隐式亲和度计算，将内存复杂度从 O(N²) 降至 O(NK)，并提供双向和因果变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准点积注意力中稠密的 token 间交互是 Transformer 扩展至长上下文的核心瓶颈，需要一种避免显式 N×N 亲和矩阵的线性复杂度替代方案。

Method: 提出高斯混合注意力（GMA）：(1) 学习 K 个高斯混合分量作为共享路由空间；(2) 查询和键映射为后验责任向量，它们的内积构成隐式亲和度；(3) 值写入并从 K 槽潜记忆中读取；(4) 利用矩阵乘法结合律避免实例化 N×N 亲和矩阵；(5) 提供双向和因果变体，以及端到端可微的高斯混合参数化。

Result: GMA 实现了固定 K 的线性内存扩展；在长上下文分类上与注意力基线具有竞争力；因果 GMA 在 WikiText-103 上优于线性/随机特征注意力变体，但落后于优化的因果 SDPA 和 Mamba；学习的责任矩阵显示出广泛的组件使用和与表层 token 类别的适度对齐。

Conclusion: GMA 是一种概率化、可解释的固定 K 线性时间注意力替代方案，而非对优化 softmax 注意力或状态空间模型的全面替代。

Abstract: The dense token-to-token interaction pattern of standard dot-product attention remains a central bottleneck in scaling Transformer architectures to long contexts. We introduce \textbf{Gaussian Mixture Attention (GMA)}, a probabilistic attention-style sequence mixer that replaces explicit pairwise query--key comparison with routing through $K$ learned Gaussian mixture components. Queries and keys are mapped to posterior \textit{responsibility} vectors over a shared latent routing space; their overlap defines an implicit responsibility-space affinity, while values are written into and read from a $K$-slot latent memory. By exploiting the associativity of matrix multiplication, GMA avoids materializing the induced $N\times N$ affinity matrix and instead uses two responsibility matrices whose dominant activation storage scales as $\mathcal{O}(NK)$ rather than $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ for fixed $K$. We formulate bidirectional and causal variants of GMA, provide an end-to-end differentiable parameterization of the Gaussian mixture components, and analyze its responsibility-modulated gradient structure, constrained non-negative low-rank affinity interpretation, and local routing stability. Empirically, GMA exhibits the intended fixed-$K$ linear memory scaling and is competitive with attention-style baselines on long-context classification, while causal GMA improves over tested linear/random-feature attention variants on WikiText-103 but remains behind optimized causal SDPA and Mamba in the current implementation. Analysis of learned responsibilities further shows broad component usage and moderate alignment with surface-form token categories, supporting GMA as a probabilistic, interpretable, fixed-$K$ linear-time attention-style alternative rather than a universal replacement for optimized softmax attention or state-space models.

</details>


### [143] [Breaking the Solver Bottleneck: Training Task Generators at the Learnable Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18284)
*Lorenz Wolf,Connor Watts,Roger Creus Castanyer,Geoffrey Bradway,Maxwill Lin,Augustine N. Mavor-Parker,Matthew Daborn-Sargent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PROPEL 提出一种求解器摊销框架，通过训练轻量级激活探针预测目标求解器的通过率，替代求解器在环训练中的昂贵 rollout，从而在数学、代码和软件工程任务中高效地将任务生成引导至目标求解率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL 训练智能体的瓶颈正从计算资源转向前沿任务供给：需要足够难但又可解的任务。固定任务分布会饱和，而朴素合成生成常产生平凡、不可能或不适定的任务。直接用 RL 训练任务生成器需要反复进行求解器 rollout，对软件工程任务而言单次 rollout 耗时数十分钟，求解器在环训练不可行。

Method: PROPEL 框架：1) 首先生成一批任务并用目标求解器获得结果，形成一次性标注语料；2) 在冻结的生成器参考模型上训练一个轻量级激活探针，预测目标求解器的通过率；3) 将探针作为求解率的代理，在生成器优化时替代昂贵 rollout，使生成器评估仅需一次前向传播。由此实现求解器摊销的生成器训练。

Result: 在编码任务上，Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct 求解器的可学习前沿任务比例从 10.1% 提升至 20.0%，Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 从 5.3% 提升至 12.6%。在 SWE 任务上，Qwen3.5-27B 在未见过的仓库上，目标求解率生成比例从 9.8% 提升至 19.6%。

Conclusion: PROPEL 通过探针摊销求解器评估成本，在不牺牲生成质量的前提下，使任务生成器能以实用计算代价定向优化，有效提升前沿可学习任务的产出比例，为 RL 训练持续供给高质量任务。

Abstract: The limiting resource for training agents via reinforcement learning (RL) is increasingly frontier task supply: valid, solvable tasks just difficult enough to train the current model. As reasoning and agentic models improve, fixed task distributions saturate, while naive synthetic generation yields tasks that are trivial, impossible, or ill-posed. Training a task generator with RL to optimize validity and learnability can address this bottleneck, but direct optimization requires repeated solver rollouts per candidate. For software-engineering (SWE) tasks, a single rollout can take tens of minutes; solver-in-the-loop generator training is intractable. We introduce PROPEL, a solver-amortized framework for training task generators at the targeted solve rate. PROPEL trains a lightweight activation probe on a one-time labeled corpus of generated tasks and solver outcomes. The probe predicts target-solver pass rate from a frozen generator reference model and serves as a proxy for solve rate during generator optimization, reducing generator evaluation to a single forward pass. Across math, code, and software-engineering at multiple model scales, PROPEL shifts generation toward the targeted solve rate: for coding, tasks generated at the learnable frontier increase from $10.1\% \rightarrow 20.0\%$ for a Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct solver and from $5.3\% \rightarrow 12.6\%$ for a Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct solver. For SWE, PROPEL increases the share of generations at the targeted solve rate from $9.8\% \rightarrow 19.6\%$ for Qwen3.5-27B on repositories not seen during training of probe and generator.

</details>


### [144] [CODEBLOCK: Learning to Supervise Code at the Right Granularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18286)
*Zhijie Deng,Ling Li,Jinlong Pang,Kaiqin Hu,Qi Xuan,Zhaowei Zhu,Jiaheng Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CodeBlock proposes a structure-aware sparse supervision framework for code LLM fine-tuning. Instead of applying uniform loss to all tokens or selecting isolated high-value tokens (which breaks code structure), it selects complete code blocks (coding items), estimates their utility via generalized cross-entropy on core logic tokens, and reranks them using data-flow reach/bridge signals. Training with only 1.9% tokens achieves better pass@1 than full-token SFT.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码LLM监督微调对所有token施加均匀交叉熵损失，假设每个token提供同等有用的学习信号。虽然NLP领域已有token级选择方法，但直接将token级掩码迁移到代码会破坏语法和语义完整的程序单元，因为代码高度依赖结构完整性和定义-使用关系。因此需要一种尊重代码结构完整性的稀疏监督方法。

Method: CodeBlock框架分三步：(1) 先筛选高质量指令-响应对；(2) 将代码响应划分为语法连贯的"coding items"（如函数体、语句块等），通过聚合核心逻辑token上的广义交叉熵来估计每个coding item的效用；(3) 利用数据流可达性和桥接信号对coding items进行重排序，优先选择传播或连接重要程序依赖关系的代码块。训练时完整响应仍作为上下文可用，但损失仅施加于被选中的代码块和信息量大的自然语言token。

Result: 在六个代码生成基准上的实验表明，CodeBlock仅使用1.9%的监督响应token，其平均pass@1即超越了全token SFT以及同类token选择基线方法。

Conclusion: 结构感知的稀疏监督对于代码LLM微调非常有效。通过以完整代码结构单元（而非孤立token）为最小监督粒度，并结合数据流信号进行重要性排序，可以在大幅减少监督token的同时提升模型性能，证明了代码结构完整性对学习的重要性。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning of code LLMs typically applies uniform cross-entropy loss to all response tokens, implicitly assuming that every token provides equally useful learning signal. Recent token-level selection methods challenge this assumption in natural-language SFT by supervising only high-value tokens. However, directly transferring token-level masking to code can break syntactically and semantically coherent program units, because code depends on structural completeness and definition-use relations. We therefore propose CodeBlock, a structure-aware sparse supervision framework that selects structure-complete code evidence rather than isolated tokens. CodeBlock first selects high-quality instruction-response pairs, then partitions code responses into syntactically coherent coding items, estimates their utility by aggregating generalized cross-entropy over core logic tokens, and reranks them with data-flow reach and bridge signals to prioritize blocks that propagate or connect important program dependencies. During training, the full response remains available as context, while loss is applied only to selected code items and informative natural-language tokens. Experiments on six code-generation benchmarks show that CodeBlock achieves stronger average pass@1 than full-token SFT and competitive selection baselines, while using only 1.9% of supervised response tokens.

</details>


### [145] [Artemis: Anatomy-Resolved inTervention for Eliminating Multimodal NeuroImage confounderS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18287)
*Siyuan Dai,Yang Du,Kun Zhao,Zhusuyi Chen,Heng Huang,Paul Thompson,Chao Shi,Haoteng Tang,Liang Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Artemis 是一个面向多模态脑网络分析的区域级因果干预框架，通过学习每个脑区特定的混杂因子表征，消除年龄、性别等人口统计学因素带来的虚假关联，可作为即插即用模块与任意GNN骨干网络兼容，在疾病诊断、痴呆分期和性别分类三个基准上均取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果GNN方法虽在图建模层面引入了因果机制，但忽略了临床神经影像数据中真实存在的混杂因子（如年龄、性别），且未考虑不同脑区对人口统计学因素的敏感性差异，导致模型学到虚假捷径而非因果不变的表示。

Method: 提出 Artemis 框架，在区域级别对每个脑区独立进行因果干预：利用轻量级参数学习区域特定的混杂因子表示，综合运用多模态功能连接（fMRI）和结构连接（DTI）特征进行图推理，设计为即插即用模块，可兼容任意GNN骨干网络。

Result: 在 ADNI（疾病诊断）、OASIS（痴呆分期）和 HCP（性别分类）三个基准数据集上，相对于代表性GNN基线方法均取得一致性性能提升，并通过多项辅助实验验证了统计显著性和神经科学可解释性。

Conclusion: Artemis 通过区域级因果干预有效消除了人口统计学混杂因子的影响，在多模态脑网络分析的多项任务中展现出优越性能，并具备神经科学层面的可解释性。

Abstract: Multimodal neuroimaging, integrating functional connectivity from fMRI and structural connectivity from DTI, enables non-invasive analysis of brain networks using graph neural networks. However, demographic factors such as age and sex systematically confound the relationship between brain connectivity and clinical outcomes, causing GNNs to exploit spurious shortcuts rather than learning causally invariant representations. While recent causal GNN methods introduce causality at the graph-modeling level, their causal mechanisms remain domain-agnostic without accounting for the real-world confounders inherent in clinical neuroimaging data. Moreover, brain networks are constructed from atlas-based parcellations where each region exhibits distinct sensitivity to demographic factors, necessitating region-aware adjustment. We propose Artemis, a region-level causal framework that bridges this gap with causal intervention at each brain region independently by learning region-specific confounder representations with lightweight parameters. Our adjustment comprehensively utilized the multimodal functional and structural features for graph reasoning as a plug-in module compatible with arbitrary GNN backbones. Experiments on three benchmarks, ADNI for disease diagnosis, OASIS for dementia staging, and HCP for sex classification, demonstrate consistent improvements over representative GNN-based baselines. Multiple supporting experiments further demonstrate statistical significance and neuroscientific interpretability.

</details>


### [146] [A Link between Shock-wave Theory and Symmetry-reduced Stochastic Gradient Descent for Artificial Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18303)
*Taiki Miyagawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在微分几何、李群理论和流体力学的交叉框架下，建立了冲击波理论与对称性商化后的SGD学习动力学之间的显式数学联系。通过对参数对称性进行商化并施加局部熵粗粒化，有效动力学满足商流形上的粘性Hamilton-Jacobi方程；进一步，在商化空间梯度场假设下，粗粒化损失函数的梯度服从Burgers型方程，并可严格证明冲击波的形成。该理论被应用于MLP、CNN、Transformer和平均场网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中，原始参数空间存在大量对称性冗余（如重缩放对称性、排列对称性），导致参数范数等观测指标失真，无法准确反映训练动力学中的真实相变。作者旨在利用几何和流体力学工具，为对称性商化后的学习动力学提供严格的数学描述，从而建立冲击波形成与训练相变之间的对应关系，并以此为基础开发实用的训练诊断方法。

Method: （1）利用李群理论对参数空间中的连续对称性进行商化（quotient），得到商流形；（2）通过局部熵粗粒化（local-entropy coarse-graining）消除微观涨落，推导出有效动力学满足粘性Hamilton-Jacobi方程；（3）在商化空间上假设原始参数动力学可归结为梯度场，进而证明粗粒化损失函数的梯度满足Burgers型方程；（4）利用流体力学中Burgers方程的冲击波形成理论，严格证明训练过程中存在冲击波（即动力学相变）；（5）将理论框架具体应用到MLP、CNN、Transformer和平均场网络四类架构。

Result: （1）商化并粗粒化后的有效动力学严格满足商流形上的粘性Hamilton-Jacobi方程；（2）在梯度场假设下，粗粒化损失的梯度服从Burgers方程，且可严格证明冲击波的形成；（3）该理论框架对MLP、CNN、Transformer和平均场网络均适用，验证了其普适性；（4）揭示了Transformer等架构中原始参数范数因对称冗余而具有误导性，而对称性修正的商化观测量可为训练相变提供原则性监测、预测和控制基础。

Conclusion: 作者建立了冲击波理论与深度学习SGD动力学之间的严格数学桥梁。对称性商化和局部熵粗粒化是揭示有效动力学的关键步骤，所得Hamilton-Jacobi和Burgers方程结构具有跨架构的普适性。商化观测量有望成为训练相变的实用诊断工具，优于原始参数范数等受对称性污染的指标。

Abstract: We develop a mathematically explicit link between shock-wave theory and the symmetry-quotiented learning dynamics of stochastic gradient descent, drawing on differential geometry, Lie group theory, and fluid mechanics. Specifically, after quotienting parameter symmetries and applying local-entropy coarse-graining, the effective dynamics satisfy a viscous Hamilton--Jacobi equation on the quotient manifold. Moreover, under the assumption that the raw parameter dynamics can be summarized by a gradient field on the quotiented space, the gradient of the coarse-grained loss function obeys a Burgers-type equation, and shock formation can be established rigorously. We apply our theory to multilayer perceptrons, convolutional neural networks, Transformers, and mean-field networks, and show that they obey the Hamilton--Jacobi or Burgers-type equations. We conjecture that this framework also yields practical diagnostics for deep learning. In architectures such as Transformers, raw parameter norms are often distorted by symmetry redundancy and may therefore be misleading, whereas symmetry-corrected quotient observables provide a principled basis for monitoring, forecasting, and controlling training-phase transitions.

</details>


### [147] [Attribution-Guided and Coverage-Maximized Pruning for Structural MoE Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18304)
*Yifu Ding,Jiacheng Wang,Ge Yang,Yongcheng Jing,Jinyang Guo,Xianglong Liu,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对MoE模型的结构化剪枝框架，将剪枝率分配建模为通道得分覆盖最大化问题，通过归因近似高效求解，实现了在保留精度的前提下大幅压缩模型（配合4-bit量化，内存减少5.27倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE压缩方法主要在专家层面操作（移除整个专家或按粗粒度重要性排序），无法捕捉细粒度的通道级冗余。作者观察到MoE专家内部信息高度集中在少数通道上，即使被判定为"重要"的专家也存在大量冗余通道，因此需要更细粒度的结构化剪枝来充分利用压缩预算。

Method: 提出将剪枝率分配重新形式化为"通道得分覆盖最大化"问题（channel-score coverage maximization），即在给定总剪枝预算下，选择保留哪些通道以最大化累积重要性得分。采用基于归因（attribution-based）的近似方法高效求解该优化问题，实现通道级结构化剪枝。实验在DeepSeek和Qwen系列MoE模型上进行验证。

Result: 在DeepSeek和Qwen MoE模型上，结合4-bit量化，该方法在50%或25%结构化剪枝率下仍能保持模型精度。在Qwen3-30B-A3B上，内存占用减少5.27倍，且在多个基准测试上一致优于现有最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 细粒度的通道级结构化剪枝能有效挖掘MoE专家内部的冗余，相比粗粒度的专家级剪枝，可以更高效地分配剪枝预算，在显著压缩模型的同时保持精度，为MoE模型的高效部署提供了新路径。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models scale compute efficiently, yet remain expensive to deploy due to their substantial memory footprint and inference overhead. Prior compression methods mainly operate at the expert level, either removing entire experts or ranking experts by coarse-grained importance scores. However, such expert-wise decisions are often too coarse to capture fine-grained redundancy, leading to misallocated pruning budgets and limited compression. To address this problem, we observe that information within MoE experts is highly concentrated in a small subset of channels, leaving substantial redundancy even in experts deemed important. Based on this observation, we propose a structural pruning framework tailored for MoE models. Our method reformulates prune-ratio allocation as a channel-score coverage maximization problem and solves it efficiently using an attribution-based approximation. Experiments on DeepSeek and Qwen MoE models show that our method preserves model accuracy under 50% or 25% structured pruning when combined with 4-bit quantization. On Qwen3-30B-A3B, our approach reduces memory footprint by 5.27$\times$ and consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across diverse benchmarks.

</details>


### [148] [Fisher Width: A Geometric Measure of Complexity on Statistical Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18306)
*Vu Khac Ky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Fisher Width——高斯宽度在统计流形上的Fisher几何推广。通过用局部Fisher度量张量替换欧氏单位矩阵，Fisher宽度对统计曲率敏感且在光滑重参数化下不变。作者建立了其基本理论（集中性、度量扰动稳定性、谱比较界），并证明了Fisher-Lipschitz假设类的泛化界，在MNIST上进行了实证评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯宽度是高维概率、压缩感知和凸优化的核心复杂度度量，但其本质是欧氏的。统计模型天然带有Fisher信息度量诱导的黎曼几何，其中方向按统计可区分性而非欧氏长度缩放。因此需要一个能捕捉统计流形内在几何结构的类似度量。

Method: 在参数点θ处，用局部度量张量G(θ)^{1/2}替换欧氏单位矩阵，定义Fisher-rescaled集合的高斯宽度。发展Fisher宽度的基本理论，包括集中不等式、度量扰动稳定性分析、与欧氏基线的谱比较界。在应用中证明Fisher-Lipschitz假设类的泛化界，并构建可计算的估计器，在MNIST上跨三个模型类进行实证验证。

Result: Fisher宽度保留了高斯宽度的关键结构特征（集中性、度量扰动稳定性、谱比较界），同时能捕捉欧氏度量不可见的各向异性几何效应。实证结果表明所提估计器在MNIST数据集上有效。

Conclusion: Fisher宽度之于统计流形，正如高斯宽度之于欧氏凸体。该工作为弯曲统计流形上的复杂度和学习理论研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Gaussian width is a central geometric complexity measure in high-dimensional probability, compressed sensing, convex optimization, and learning theory. It quantifies the average extent of a set along random directions, thereby capturing the effective dimension of constraint sets, hypothesis classes, and descent cones. However, this notion is intrinsically Euclidean. Statistical models instead carry a natural Riemannian geometry induced by the Fisher information metric, where directions are scaled according to statistical distinguishability rather than ambient Euclidean length.
  We introduce Fisher width, a Fisher-geometric analogue of Gaussian width for statistical manifolds. At a parameter point $θ$, Fisher width replaces the Euclidean identity by the local metric tensor $G(θ)^{1/2}$, measuring the Gaussian width of the Fisher-rescaled set. This makes the resulting quantity sensitive to local statistical curvature and invariant under smooth reparameterizations.
  We develop the basic theory of Fisher width, showing that it retains key structural features of Gaussian width, including concentration, metric perturbation stability, and spectral comparison bounds with the Euclidean baseline, while also capturing anisotropic geometric effects invisible to Euclidean measures. As an application, we prove a generalization bound for Fisher-Lipschitz hypothesis classes and propose computable estimators, which we evaluate empirically on MNIST across three model classes.
  Fisher width is to statistical manifolds what Gaussian width is to Euclidean convex bodies. This work lays the foundation for studying complexity and learning on curved statistical manifolds.

</details>


### [149] [DRIFT: Refining Instruction Data via On-Policy Data Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18307)
*Zefan Wang,Lincheng Li,Tianyu Yu,Yuan Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DRIFT 提出一种基于在线策略影响函数的数据精炼方法，通过模型自身 rollout 作为验证目标、轨迹正确性加权和去偏影响分数，解决了标准 IF 在 SFT 数据筛选中的邻近性差距和梯度范数偏差问题，在 7B 模型上持续提升性能上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督微调数据筛选方法侧重于在受限预算下加速训练，但难以提升模型的性能上限。挑战在于需要精炼数据分布以找出最能提升最终模型的样本，而非仅仅找到保持性能的更小子集。标准影响函数在该场景中存在邻近性差距（off-policy 验证目标导致）和梯度范数偏差两个结构性问题。

Method: 提出 DRIFT 方法：(1) 以模型自身的 on-policy rollouts 替代外部参考数据作为验证目标，缩小参数邻近性差距并更好满足 IF 的局部邻域假设；(2) 基于轨迹正确性进行符号加权；(3) 针对梯度劫持（gradient hacking）问题对影响分数进行去偏处理，使少量验证查询即可作为全数据集归因的可靠锚点。

Result: 在 7B 参数的指令遵循和推理模型上，DRIFT 持续提升了模型性能上限，并优于现有数据筛选基线方法。

Conclusion: DRIFT 通过 on-policy IF 解决了标准影响函数在 SFT 数据归因中的两个结构性问题，为大规模语言模型的监督微调提供了一种能够真正提升性能上限的数据精炼范式。

Abstract: Optimizing the training data distribution for Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) dictates the capability of Large Language Models (LLMs). While existing data curation methods excel at accelerating training under constrained budgets, they are less suited to elevating the capability upper bound. The challenge here is no longer to identify a smaller subset that preserves performance, but to refine the data distribution toward instances most capable of improving the final model. To address this problem, we explore instance-level data attribution using Influence Functions (IF). We identify that standard IF formulations struggle in this setting due to two structural limitations: a proximity gap caused by off-policy validation targets, and a severe bias towards gradient norm. We propose DRIFT (Data Refinement via On-Policy Influence Functions for Supervised Fine-Tuning). Instead of relying on external reference data, DRIFT utilizes the model's on-policy rollouts as validation targets, which empirically minimizes the parameter proximity gap and better aligns with the local neighborhood assumption of IF. It further applies signed weighting based on trajectory correctness and debiases influence scores against the gradient hacking issue, allowing a small set of validation queries to act as reliable anchors for attributing the full dataset. Experiments on 7B-parameter instruction and reasoning models show that DRIFT consistently raises the performance ceiling on both, outperforming existing data curation baselines.

</details>


### [150] [TRIDENT: Breaking the Hybrid-Safety-Physics Coupling for Provably Safe Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18308)
*Zijie Meng,Ziwei Li,Yufei Liu,Zhiyu Li,Jiyuan Liu,Wenhua Nie,Bingcai Wei,Miao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRIDENT框架，针对网络化信息物理系统中多智能体强化学习面临的三重耦合偏差（混合离散-连续动作、训练时安全硬约束、物理驱动动力学），通过Richardson-Romberg梯度校正、Lyapunov约束序贯信任域更新和物理信息残差Critic三项协同设计，将违反率降低95.5%，奖励提升13.5%，并证明收敛到约束纳什均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络化信息物理系统（如多无人机边缘计算、自主交叉口管理）中的安全协调问题迫使学习算法需同时处理混合离散-连续动作、训练时硬安全约束和物理控制动力学。作者发现这三者形成有向偏差循环，任何简单拼接现成模块都会因偏差相互放大而失败，因此需要协同设计来消除耦合偏差。

Method: 提出TRIDENT框架，包含三个协同设计的组件：(1) Richardson-Romberg梯度校正，将Gumbel-Softmax的偏差从O(τ)降至O(τ²)；(2) Lyapunov约束序贯信任域更新，强制每次迭代满足可行性；(3) 物理信息残差Critic，对价值函数而非奖励函数进行分解。理论上证明收敛到约束纳什均衡的速率为Õ(1/√K)，累积违反上界为O(√K)。

Result: 在多无人机移动边缘计算、自主交叉口管理和混合SMAC变体三个场景中，TRIDENT的训练时约束违反率相比MADDPG降低95.5%，相比MACPO降低76.3%，同时奖励比最强无约束基线提升13.5%。

Conclusion: 混合离散-连续动作、安全约束和物理动力学之间的三向耦合偏差是网络化信息物理系统多智能体安全协调的根本挑战，只有通过梯度校正、信任域约束和物理信息Critic的协同设计才能有效消除，TRIDENT在该问题上取得显著突破。

Abstract: Safe coordination in networked cyber-physical systems forces learning algorithms to simultaneously handle hybrid discrete-continuous actions, hard training-time safety constraints, and physics-governed dynamics. We show that these three features form a directed cycle of biases that defeats any naive composition of off-the-shelf modules, and formalize this as a three-way coupling lemma. We then introduce TRIDENT, the first MARL framework whose three components are co-designed to cancel each leak: a Richardson-Romberg gradient correction reducing Gumbel-Softmax bias from O(tau) to O(tau^2), a Lyapunov-constrained sequential trust-region update enforcing per-iterate feasibility, and a physics-informed residual critic that decomposes value rather than reward. We prove an O~(1/sqrt(K)) convergence rate to a constrained Nash equilibrium and an O(sqrt(K)) cumulative-violation bound. On multi-UAV mobile-edge computing, autonomous intersection management, and a hybrid SMAC variant, TRIDENT cuts training-time violations by 95.5% over MADDPG and 76.3% over MACPO, while improving reward by 13.5% over the strongest unconstrained baseline.

</details>


### [151] [SAGE: Retain-Aware Post-Hoc Sanitization of Final Unlearning Vector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18309)
*Jingyuan Zhang,Yucheng Bai,Peixi Wen,Zhehao Huang,Zhengbao He,Hanling Tian,Xinwen Cheng,Haiyin Ran,Xiaolin Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAGE通过在LLM遗忘后对最终更新向量进行谱激活-几何校正，以闭式优化抑制与保留数据高能量方向对齐的更新分量，在多种遗忘方法和规模下持续缓解保留-遗忘权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM遗忘方法均面临遗忘与保留之间的权衡困境。作者发现保留激活偏差可用于量化遗忘方法对保留能力的损害，且这种损害可通过事后方式修复，无需重新运行原始遗忘流程。

Method: 提出SAGE（Spectral Activation-GEometry Sanitization），一种源无关的事后校正方法。从一个小型保留代理数据集收集真实模块输入，提取其主导激活几何结构，构建并求解一个源锚定的闭式优化目标，抑制与高能量保留方向对齐的更新分量，同时保留源方法的遗忘载体。

Result: 在多种遗忘方法、模型规模和基准测试上，SAGE一致地缓解了保留-遗忘权衡，验证了事后向量校正作为机器遗忘中一个实用且被低估的研究维度的有效性。

Conclusion: 对最终更新向量进行事后校正是一种实用且被低估的机器遗忘策略，SAGE为LLM遗忘提供了一个与具体遗忘方法解耦、无需重新训练的通用保留恢复方案。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) unlearning aims to remove undesirable knowledge or behaviors while preserving retained capabilities. Current unlearning methods all involve a trade-off between unlearning and retention. We have found that the retention activation bias can also be used to quantify the damage an unlearning method inflicts on retention, without considering the specific implementation of the unlearning process. This allows us to restore retention performance for any unlearning method using a post-hoc approach. Therefore, we propose a complementary post-hoc setting to sanitize the final update vector without rerunning the original unlearning pipeline. In this setting, we design SAGE, Spectral Activation-GEometry Sanitization, a source-agnostic correction for final unlearning updates. SAGE collects real module inputs from a small retain proxy, extracts their dominant activation geometry, and solves a source-anchored optimization objective in closed form, which suppresses update components aligned with high-energy retained directions while preserving the source method's forgetting carrier. Across multiple unlearning methods, model scales, and benchmarks, SAGE consistently relieves the retain-forget trade-off, identifying post-hoc sanitization of final vectors as a practical and underexplored axis for machine unlearning.

</details>


### [152] [Ghost Attractor Networks: Basin-Structured Dynamical Decoders for Closed-Loop Sequential Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18315)
*Tianyu Wang,Ying Wang,Zhihao Liu,Xi Vincent Wang,Lihui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Ghost Attractor Networks，一种理论驱动的动力学解码器，其潜变量在带漂移的学习势能下演化，天然形成盆地-吸引子结构。该解码器以恒定内存和单次前向传播实现多模态、闭环相条件动作生成。仅2.3M参数的Ghost在离线精度上匹敌1.07B参数的扩散Transformer，参数量减少462倍，延迟降低32倍；在LIBERO-10闭环基准上，相条件控制带来13.5个百分点的成功率提升，最终成功率达95.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流序列解码器（大规模Transformer和扩散模型）内存随序列长度线性增长且需迭代计算，效率低下；而小型前馈解码器虽高效，却产生无结构的潜表示，无法支持闭环控制所需的相条件动作生成和跨步潜变量传递。这驱动作者设计一种兼具效率与结构化潜空间的新型解码器。

Method: 作者从三个设计目标（多模态性、解码器级单次切换、恒定内存）出发，推导出"势能-漂移"形式的动力学系统。Ghost Attractor Networks的潜变量在学习的势能函数和漂移项驱动下演化，模态间转换表现为鞍结分岔中的ghost-attractor逃逸。采用层级相空间分解，将一阶盆地收敛与二阶本体感知精化分离。训练采用行为克隆与对比学习的端到端联合目标。

Result: 实验验证了预测的梯度流收缩特性：在1430个留出样本上，五步积分后梯度范数衰减67%。2.3M参数的Ghost在离线精度上匹配1.07B参数的扩散Transformer（参数少462倍，延迟低32倍），并在MSE上比五种同等参数量的替代解码器（MLP、Neural ODE、CVAE、Transformer、单步扩散）低5.9%-29%。LIBERO-10闭环基准上，相条件控制使成功率比MLP基线提高13.5个百分点，持久潜集成最终成功率达95.7%。

Conclusion: Ghost Attractor Networks通过在潜空间中显式构建盆地-吸引子几何结构，成功弥合了前馈解码器的效率优势与闭环控制对结构化潜空间的需求之间的鸿沟，为机器人动作生成提供了一种参数高效、推理快速且支持闭环控制的解码器方案。

Abstract: Sequential output generation with large-scale Transformer and diffusion decoders pays a memory cost that grows with sequence length, plus iterative per-step computation. Replacing them with small feed-forward decoders restores efficiency but produces unstructured latent representations that limit closed-loop control: phase-conditioned action generation and cross-step latent carry-over both require a latent geometry with stable basins. This article proposes Ghost Attractor Networks, a theoretically derived dynamical decoder whose latent evolves under a learned potential with drift and produces a basin-attractor structure by construction. Three desiderata (multi-modality, decoder-level single-pass switching, and constant memory) motivate the potential-drift form, and mode transitions arise as saddle-node bifurcations with ghost-attractor escape. A hierarchical phase-space decomposition separates first-order basin convergence from second-order proprioceptive refinement. Empirically, a Ghost trained end-to-end with a behavioral-cloning and contrastive objective exhibits the predicted gradient-flow contraction in its potential, with the gradient norm decaying by 67 percent across five integration steps on 1430 held-out samples. Ghost is evaluated as a robotic action decoder. A 2.3-million-parameter Ghost matches the offline accuracy of a 1.07-billion-parameter Diffusion Transformer at 462 times fewer parameters and 32 times lower latency, and beats five alternative 2M-parameter decoders (MLP, Neural ODE, CVAE, Transformer, 1-step Diffusion) on offline mean squared error by 5.9 to 29 percent. On the LIBERO-10 closed-loop benchmark, phase conditioning on Ghost's basin-structured latent yields a 13.5 percentage-point success-rate gain over a feed-forward MLP baseline, and persistent-latent ensembling reaches a 95.7 percent final success rate.

</details>


### [153] [A Survey on Data-Driven Models for Soil Moisture Regression and Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18316)
*Ilektra Tsimpidi,George Georgoulas,Vidya Sumathy,George Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了基于人工智能的土壤湿度估计与分类模型，将现有方法归纳为统计时间序列、地统计、经典机器学习、深度学习和概率/贝叶斯五大类，并分析了其利用多源异构数据进行回归与分类任务的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 土壤湿度建模是一个复杂的时空学习问题，面临非线性环境交互、异构数据源和地面观测稀缺等挑战。物理模型计算成本高且可扩展性差，而数据驱动的AI方法能以更少的建模假设从环境变量中提取经验关系，因此需要对现有AI土壤湿度建模方法进行系统梳理。

Method: 本文采用结构化综述方法，将现有的AI驱动土壤湿度建模文献按照方法论划分为五大类：(a) 统计时间序列模型、(b) 地统计方法、(c) 经典机器学习模型、(d) 深度学习模型、(e) 概率/贝叶斯方法。各类方法利用历史土壤湿度记录、气象变量、植被指数、地形、土壤特性及地理位置数据进行回归或分类。

Result: 综述揭示了从传统统计方法向深度学习与概率方法演进的趋势，各类模型在不同时空尺度上各有优劣。深度学习模型在捕捉非线性时空依赖方面表现突出，而概率/贝叶斯方法在不确定性量化方面具有独特优势。多源数据融合已成为提升精度的重要手段。

Conclusion: AI方法为大规模土壤湿度建模提供了灵活高效的替代方案。未来研究应关注物理知识与数据驱动模型的混合方法、不确定性量化标准化，以及有限标注数据条件下的鲁棒学习策略。

Abstract: Soil Moisture (SM) modelling constitutes a complex spatiotemporal learning problem characterised by nonlinear environmental interactions, heterogeneous data sources, and limited ground observations. Physics-based approaches, such as water balance models, rely on explicit hydrological equations and high-quality inputs, but their computational cost and scalability limitations restrict large-scale deployment. Data-driven artificial intelligence (AI) methods have emerged as flexible alternatives, enabling the extraction of empirical relationships between soil moisture and environmental variables with reduced modelling assumptions. This work presents a structured survey of AI-based models for soil moisture estimation and classification. Existing approaches are organized into five categories: (a) statistical time-series models, (b) geostatistical methods (c) classical machine learning (ML) models, (d) Deep Learning (DL) models and (e) Probabilistic/Bayesian methods. These models leverage historical soil moisture records, meteorological variables, vegetation indices, topography, soil characteristics, and geolocation data to perform regression or classification tasks.

</details>


### [154] [Enhanced Graph Neural Networks using K-Hop Gaussian Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18317)
*Xuling Zhang,Peng Wang,Daiyan Li,Aoran Huang,Zeiwei Chen,Yongkui Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出K-Hop高斯扩散核，作为GNN预处理模块，通过多跳扩散与高斯加权平衡局部与全局信息传播，在噪声和复杂图结构上显著优于传统消息传递及PPR、热核扩散方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN依赖单跳邻居的消息传递，在边存在噪声或定义不佳时信息传播受限；PPR和热核等全局扩散核虽有所改善，但仍难以处理复杂局部结构和远距离节点噪声。

Method: 提出K-Hop高斯（KHG）扩散核，作为图数据预处理模块。核心是对远程节点引入多跳扩散与高斯加权机制，在应用标准GNN之前平衡局部与全局信息传播。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，KHG显著优于传统消息传递GNN以及PPR、热核扩散方法，尤其在含噪声或结构复杂的图上优势明显。

Conclusion: KHG扩散核通过高斯加权的多跳扩散有效解决了噪声边和复杂结构下的信息传播问题，是一种通用且高效的GNN预处理方案。

Abstract: Most graph neural network (GNN) cores rely on graph convolutions, typically implemented as message passing between direct (single-hop) neighbors. In many real-world graphs, edges can be noisy or poorly defined, limiting information propagation to local neighborhoods. Existing diffusion kernels, such as Personalized PageRank (PPR) and Heat Kernel, alleviate this issue through global propagation, but still struggle with complex local structures and distant node noise. To address these limitations, we propose a K-Hop Gaussian (KHG) diffusion kernel as a preprocessing module for graph data. KHG introduces multi-hop diffusion with Gaussian weighting for remote nodes, balancing local and global information propagation before applying standard GNNs. Experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that KHG significantly outperforms traditional message-passing GNNs, as well as PPR and Heat Kernel diffusion, particularly in noisy or structurally complex graphs.

</details>


### [155] [The Implicit Bias of Steepest Descent with Mini-batch Stochastic Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11557)
*Jichu Li,Xuan Tang,Difan Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究小批量随机最速下降法在多类分类中的隐式偏置，刻画了批量大小、动量和方差缩减如何影响极限最大间隔行为与收敛速率。核心发现：无动量时仅全批量能保证收敛与正确分类；动量通过批量-动量权衡实现小批量近似最大间隔解；方差缩减可恢复精确的全批量隐式偏置；批量大小为1的无动量算法会收敛到截然不同的偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SignSGD、Muon等广泛使用的优化方法可统一视为不同范数几何下的最速下降。然而，随机梯度最速下降在小批量、动量和方差缩减条件下的隐式偏置行为尚不清晰，亟需统一的理论框架来阐明何种条件下随机优化能够复现全批量行为。

Method: 在多类分类设定下，作者对一般逐元素范数和Schatten-p范数下的随机最速下降法进行了统一分析。通过研究批量大小、动量（Polyak/重球法）和方差缩减技术之间的相互作用，推导了显式的、维度无关的收敛速率和极限最大间隔（max-margin）行为的理论刻画，并通过具体数据实例验证纯随机更新的局限性。

Result: （1）无动量时，仅全批量梯度能保证最坏情况收敛与成功分类；（2）动量机制实现了批量-动量权衡，使小批量也能收敛到近似最大间隔解，但会减慢收敛速度，且给出了维度无关的显式速率，改进了已有结果；（3）方差缩减可在任意批量大小下恢复精确的全批量隐式偏置，代价是收敛更慢；（4）批量大小为1的无动量最速下降会收敛到一种本质不同的偏置，揭示了纯随机更新的关键局限。

Conclusion: 该统一分析澄清了随机优化何时与全批量行为对齐：动量带来近似对齐，方差缩减实现精确对齐但速度更慢，纯随机更新则可能完全偏离。这为深入探索随机梯度最速下降算法的训练行为铺平了道路。

Abstract: A variety of widely used optimization methods like SignSGD and Muon can be interpreted as instances of steepest descent under different norm-induced geometries. In this work, we study the implicit bias of mini-batch stochastic steepest descent in multi-class classification, characterizing how batch size, momentum, and variance reduction shape the limiting max-margin behavior and convergence rates under general entry-wise and Schatten-$p$ norms. We show that, without momentum, worst-case convergence and successful classification can only be guaranteed with full-batch gradient. In contrast, momentum enables small-batch convergence to an approximate max-margin solution through a batch-momentum trade-off, though it slows convergence. This approach provides fully explicit, dimension-free rates that improve upon prior results. Moreover, we prove that variance reduction can recover the exact full-batch implicit bias for any batch size, albeit at a slower convergence rate. Finally, we further investigate the batch-size-one steepest descent without momentum, and reveal its convergence to a fundamentally different bias via a concrete data example, which reveals a key limitation of purely stochastic updates. Overall, our unified analysis clarifies when stochastic optimization aligns with full-batch behavior, and paves the way for perform deeper explorations of the training behavior of stochastic gradient steepest descent algorithms.

</details>


### [156] [ASTRA: A Scalable Next-Generation ATCO Training Simulator with Autonomous Simpilots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18319)
*Ethan Chew,Enjia Wu,Iruss Eng Wei Yeow,Ian Weiqin Lim,Ranen Sim,Brandon Koh Ziheng,Kaleb Nim,Caden Toh Jun Yi,Wei Dong Soin,Darius Kai Keat Koh,Galen King Yu Tay,Prannaya Gupta,Jonathan Ee Fang Koong,Yong Zhi Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ASTRA 是一个端到端的空管训练模拟器，通过微调的本地语音模型自动扮演"假想飞行员"角色，将新加坡口音航空语音的识别词错误率从 107.80% 降至 23.45%，并引入 AI 辅助评估框架，在准确性、简洁性和完整性上分别达到 91.7%、88.2% 和 86.9% 的评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空管培训严重依赖人类专家"假想飞行员"（simpilots）进行角色扮演，训练容量受限。现有自动化方案基于西方语音模型，在新加坡英语口音的航空通信场景中表现极差（WER 高达 107.80%），亟需针对本地语音环境定制的高效训练方案。

Method: 提出 ASTRA 端到端训练模拟器流水线：（1）微调自动语音识别（ASR）模型以适配新加坡口音航空语音；（2）指令解释模块理解管制员意图；（3）使用本地适配的语音生成模型合成飞行员与管制员回复；（4）基于 DSPy 和 Unsloth 开源框架构建 AI 辅助评估框架，从准确性、简洁性、完整性三个维度评估学员无线电通话表现。

Result: 微调后的 ASR 管道将 WER 从 107.80% 降至 23.45%；AI 评估框架在准确性（91.7%）、简洁性（88.2%）和完整性（86.9%）三个指标上均取得较高评分。

Conclusion: ASTRA 通过本地化语音模型和 AI 评估框架，实现了可扩展、标准化的空管培训与评估，有效降低了教员工作负担，验证了在非西方口音航空环境下定制化语音管道的必要性与可行性。

Abstract: Air Traffic Control Operators (ATCOs) are vital in ensuring the safe, orderly, and efficient flow of air traffic, yet training capacity is constrained by reliance on specialized human trainers known as simpilots, who must role-play both pilots and ATCOs in a simulated airspace. Existing automated solutions rely on Western-centric speech models that perform poorly in Singaporean operational contexts, with off-the-shelf systems exhibiting Word Error Rates (WER) of up to 107.80% on Singaporean-accented aviation speech. We introduce ASTRA, an end-to-end training simulator that automates these simpilot roles through a pipeline that transcribes ATCO speech, interprets instructions, and generates appropriate pilot and ATCO responses using locally adapted voice models. Our fine-tuned Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) pipeline reduces WER to 23.45%, substantially outperforming existing approaches in this domain. Beyond traffic simulation, ASTRA incorporates an AI-assisted performance evaluation framework that assesses trainee radiotelephony communications across accuracy, brevity, and completeness, achieving post-optimization scores of 91.7%, 88.2%, and 86.9%, respectively. Built on open-source foundations such as DSPy and Unsloth, this approach enables scalable, standardized ATCO assessment while reducing instructor workload.

</details>


### [157] [SAE Interventions are Unreliable: Post-Intervention Recovery of Suppressed Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18322)
*Mingyue Cui,Linghui Shen,Xingyi Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征的安全干预存在可恢复性漏洞：即使钳制"不安全"特征，攻击者仍可通过残差空间优化恢复有害行为，恢复率高达95.8%，且恢复路径定位于SAE重建残差——暴露出特征级控制与行为完整性之间的根本性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于SAE的潜在空间防御方法假设，识别并钳制"不安全"SAE特征即可有效阻止模型不当行为。但本文质疑这一假设的可靠性，指出可能存在一种隐藏的失效模式：干预措施仅阻断了行为的某条可见路径，而非行为本身，攻击者仍可能绕过防御。

Method: 将漏洞形式化为"干预后恢复"问题——一个受约束的残差空间优化问题。从干预后的残差状态出发，在保持目标SAE特征的后干预值不变的约束下，优化残差扰动以恢复干预前的行为。为确保恢复并非简单撤销干预，采用编码器正交更新（单层干预）和特征映射雅可比矩阵（跨层设置）。在TPP、遗忘学习、IOI及拒答转向等实验中进行压力测试，并通过恢复路径归因分析定位恢复来源。

Result: 在拒答转向安全测试中，有效样本恢复率达95.8%，被防御特征的相对漂移仅0.131，显著优于基于后缀的基线方法。恢复路径归因分析将恢复定位于SAE重建残差——即SAE未能解释的组件，表明恢复利用了SAE表征的不完备性。

Conclusion: SAE特征虽支持因果干预，但控制这些特征并不能保证控制底层行为。特征级控制与行为完整性之间存在显著差距，这对依赖SAE进行安全监控和干预的防御范式构成根本性挑战。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) decompose residual-stream activations into interpretable features. Recent latent-space defenses increasingly rely on these decompositions, assuming that identified "unsafe" SAE features serve as actionable handles for monitoring and intervention. In this paradigm, clamping a specific harmful feature is expected to reliably prevent model misbehavior. However, we show that this success may hide a recoverable failure mode: the clamp may block one visible route to a behavior without eliminating the behavior itself. We formulate this vulnerability as post-intervention recovery, a constrained residual-space optimization problem. Starting from the post-intervention residual state, we optimize residual perturbations to recover the pre-intervention behavior while preserving the post-intervention values of the targeted SAE features. Even under a strong threat model where the intervention remains active throughout optimization and generation, recovery remains possible. To rule out that recovery simply undoes the intervention, we use encoder-orthogonal updates for single-layer interventions and the corresponding feature-map Jacobian in the cross-layer setting. Across TPP, unlearning, IOI, and refusal steering experiments, this stress test reveals recoverable behavior despite successful feature-level intervention. Especially in the safety-critical refusal-steering setting, we achieve a 95.8% recovery rate on valid samples while keeping defended-feature relative drift to 0.131, substantially below suffix-based baselines. A recovery-path attribution analysis further localizes this recovery to the SAE reconstruction residual, the component left unexplained by the SAE. These results expose a gap between feature-level control and behavioral completeness: SAE features can support causal intervention, but controlling them does not guarantee control over the underlying behavior.

</details>


### [158] [Why SWAVE May Not Be All You Need:A Concept-Evolution Retrospective on Complex-Valued Recurrent Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18324)
*Ramprasath Ganesaraja,Swathika N,Sahil Dilip Panse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SWave 是一个复数循环语言模型（169.26M 参数），基于三个核心假设：复数波表示、Cayley 参数化酉变换保证状态稳定性、旋转而非收缩的隐藏状态保持长程信号完整性。经过三个开发阶段，发现了"cos-domination collapse"失效模式，最终通过独立实虚嵌入表等架构改进解决了该问题，实现了 200,000 步稳定训练（最佳 PPL 22.0）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索将语言表示为复数波而非实数向量的潜力，利用酉变换的数学性质解决长序列建模中的状态衰减/爆炸问题，并以旋转机制替代传统遗忘门来保持信号完整性。

Method: 核心组件包括：(1) 复数波语言表示；(2) Cayley 参数化酉变换确保状态范数守恒；(3) 旋转隐藏状态机制；(4) Phase-Associative Memory (PAM) 架构中的独立实虚嵌入表；(5) ComplexNorm 与 Wave Propagation Scan 作为关键组件；(6) 实值 squared-ReLU 通道混合器替代 ComplexGatedUnit；(7) 对数空间后向传播的并行扫描算法以保证数值稳定性。

Result: (1) 发现并形式化表征了"cos-domination collapse"——虚通道坍塌为全局损失极小值的失效模式；(2) 通过解耦实虚嵌入表解决了该问题，在 FineWeb-Edu 上实现 200,000 步稳定训练，最佳 PPL 22.0（步数 89,861）；(3) ComplexNorm 和 Wave Propagation Scan 在三阶段中均不可或缺；(4) 四种多尺度保留机制无显著增益；(5) 辅助训练目标在结构约束解决后无额外收益；(6) 提炼出六条可迁移的复数循环训练工程原则及 plan-to-code 可追溯性方法论。

Conclusion: SWave 验证了复数波表示与酉约束结合在语言建模中的可行性，但成功高度依赖于对复数空间中退化失效模式（如 cos-domination collapse）的识别与结构性解决。独立实虚嵌入表、ComplexNorm 和数值稳定的并行扫描是使复数 RNN 可训练的关键工程要素。该研究贡献了一套可迁移的复数模型训练原则和架构追溯方法论。

Abstract: SWave is a complex-valued recurrent language model (169.26M parameters, D=384, L=16, T=2048) trained on FineWeb-Edu using 2xH100 NVL. It was designed around three founding premises: that representing language as complex waves rather than real-valued numbers enables richer information encoding; that a Cayley-parameterised unitary transition provides a mathematical guarantee against state decay or explosion; and that a hidden state which rotates rather than shrinks preserves signal integrity over arbitrarily long contexts. The core of SWave evolved substantially across three development phases. The Resonance Head was found to structurally admit imaginary-channel collapse as a global loss minimum (a failure mode we term cos-domination collapse) and was superseded by an untied head with independent real and imaginary embedding tables from the Phase-Associative Memory (PAM) architecture. This resolved the degenerate minimum and enabled stable 200,000-step training (best-step PPL 22.0 at step 89,861). ComplexNorm and the Wave Propagation Scan proved load-bearing throughout all three phases and were retained to the final architecture. ProtectGatedScan was reframed as a structural prior rather than a learned behaviour. The four multi-scale retention concepts showed no measurable improvement under controlled evaluation and were found non-load-bearing. The ComplexGatedUnit was superseded by a real-valued squared-ReLU channel mixer with fewer parameters. The auxiliary training objectives showed no benefit once structural constraints were resolved. The investigation yields a formal characterisation of cos-domination collapse, a parallel scan with a log-space backward pass for numerical stability, six transferable engineering principles for complex-valued recurrent training, and a plan-to-code traceability methodology for catching structural divergences that conventional test suites miss.

</details>


### [159] [Zero-Shot Active Feature Acquisition via LLM-Elicitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18933)
*Binyamin Perets,Natalie Mendelson,Shiran Vainberg,Yehuda Chowers,Shai Shen-Orr,Shie Mannor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种零样本主动特征获取（AFA）框架，通过向大语言模型（LLM）仅索取其可靠的可判别统计量（一元偏差与成对协变），结合最大熵闭包解决规范歧义，构建马尔可夫随机场（MRF）来指导特征选择。在炎症性肠病（IBD）患者队列上，该方法在分类和top-k识别任务中均优于LLM直接决策及现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AFA依赖大量标注数据拟合概率模型，而LLM虽能提供无监督领域知识，却不擅长序列规划。核心动机是将"知道"与"决定"解耦：让LLM仅提供其擅长的统计量，而非端到端决策，从而在零样本条件下实现有效的主动特征获取。

Method: 提出" disciplined elicitation"框架：1）向LLM索取MRF的充分统计量——一元偏差（unary deviations）和成对协变（pairwise co-variations），即类别之间的判别性统计而非孤立类别描述；2）应用最大熵闭包（maximum-entropy closure）解决规范歧义（gauge ambiguity），使这些统计量可适配经典AFA；3）基于所得MRF构建主动特征获取策略，应用于二分类和top-k识别两种场景。

Result: 在IBD患者队列上，该框架在真实标签和LLM自身提取的信念上均优于LLM直接决策。尤其在最具挑战性的困难患者上，所提出的top-k获取策略显著超越所有现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过严格的任务解耦——LLM负责提供判别统计量、MRF负责结构化建模、最大熵闭包解决歧义——可以在零样本条件下实现优于LLM端到端决策的主动特征获取，为高不确定性临床场景（如IBD诊疗）提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Active feature acquisition (AFA) sequentially selects which features to observe to reach a classification or ranking decision. Its central limitation is reliance on large amount of labeled data to fit probabilistic models guiding acquisition. Large language models (LLMs) supply unsupervised domain knowledge, but are poor sequential planners. Asking one to both know and decide conflates capabilities best kept separate. Here, we develop a framework for zero-shot AFA through disciplined elicitation: asking the LLM only for what it can be trusted to return, the unary deviations and pairwise co-variations that are the sufficient statistics of a Markov random field (MRF). We apply our framework to two settings: binary classification and top-$k$ identification. In practice, the LLM reliably returns only discriminative statistics, what distinguishes the classes rather than each class in isolation, which precludes classical AFA. We apply a maximum-entropy closure that resolves this gauge ambiguity. We evaluate on a cohort of Inflammatory Bowel Disease (IBD) patients, an active clinical setting where diagnostic ambiguity and patient heterogeneity obstruct stable treatment strategies. Our framework outperforms the LLM both on real labels and on its own extracted beliefs. Where it matters most, on the hardest patients, our top-$k$ acquisition policy markedly outperforms all existing methods.

</details>


### [160] [Measurement noise limits the advantage of nonlinear models over linear models in biomedical prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18420)
*Marc-Andre Schulz,Kerstin Ritter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 生物医学表格数据中，非线性模型常被线性回归匹敌甚至超越，根本原因不在于模型不足，而在于测量噪声。噪声对非线性结构的衰减呈指数级（k阶交互项被衰减k次），而对线性结构仅衰减一次，导致在典型生物医学测量信度下非线性优势消失。解决方案是改进测量质量，而非扩大样本或更换模型架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学表格数据领域长期存在一个困惑：深度学习、梯度提升树、核方法等灵活模型在给定相同特征时，常被简单的线性/逻辑回归匹配或击败。已有研究惯性地将此归咎于模型侧不足。本文旨在论证真正瓶颈在于测量端——加性噪声对非线性结构的衰减远快于线性结构，使得非线性优势在典型生物医学信度水平下被抹除，且这一问题无法通过增大样本量或提升模型灵活度来解决。

Method: 本文将来自测量误差统计、心理测量学和高斯分析的经典理论拼接为一个精确的过量风险恒等式。核心推导：k阶交互项被特征信度（reliability）的k次方衰减，而线性部分仅衰减一次。通过该恒等式，可量化噪声对线性和非线性结构的差异化衰减效应。在UK Biobank的140项任务上进行了实证验证，分析灵活模型与线性模型差距中的噪声特征，并通过干预实验分离样本量、特征表示和测量信度三个条件。

Result: 推导出精确的过量风险恒等式，显示非线性结构被噪声指数级衰减。在UK Biobank 140项任务中，灵活模型与线性模型之间的差距（当存在时）携带了预测的噪声信号特征。三个条件（样本量、特征表示、测量信度）可通过干预实验分离，但无法仅通过基准测试来区分。

Conclusion: 测量信度是决定灵活模型能否超越线性模型的关键约束条件之一，与样本量和特征表示并列。三者的交集仅留下一个狭窄窗口，而大多数生物医学任务落在此窗口之外。线性与灵活模型打成平手本身并不构成对底层生物学的裁决——非线性结构是被隐藏而非缺失。唯一有效的改进路径是提升测量质量，而非增大数据规模或换用更复杂模型。

Abstract: On biomedical tabular data, flexible models such as deep networks, gradient-boosted trees, and kernel methods are repeatedly matched or beaten by linear and logistic regression given the same features. The usual reaction is to treat this as a model-side shortfall, to be fixed with more data, a better architecture, or tuning, on the assumption that the nonlinear structure is there and the model has failed to capture it. We argue that these fixes cannot help when the binding limit is the measurement rather than the model, as it frequently is in biomedicine. Additive noise blurs the population-optimal predictor, and because blurring removes a function's fine, rapidly varying detail before its broad shape, it erases nonlinear structure faster than linear structure. A degree-$k$ interaction is attenuated by the $k$-th power of feature reliability, while the linear part is attenuated only once. At the reliabilities typical of biomedical measurement, the nonlinear advantage can vanish even when the underlying biology is strongly nonlinear, and what the noise removes cannot be recovered by a larger cohort or a more flexible model, only by better measurement. The nonlinearity is hidden, not absent, and a tie between linear and flexible models is not by itself a verdict on the biology. These pieces are classical, drawn from measurement-error statistics, psychometrics, and Gaussian analysis, and we assemble them into an exact excess-risk identity. Measurement reliability is one of three conditions, alongside sample size and feature representation, that must align for a flexible model to help, and together they leave only a narrow window that most biomedical tasks fall outside. Across 140 UK Biobank tasks, the gap between flexible and linear models, where it exists, carries the predicted noise signature, and the three conditions can be separated by intervention but not by a benchmark alone.

</details>


### [161] [Neural Network Implementation of the Renormalization Group for Fault Diagnosis with Class Imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18326)
*Evgeny Nikulchev,Dmitry Ilin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RGNet，一种基于重整化群概念的神经网络架构，通过层次化粗粒化特征空间来应对类别不平衡和多维噪声挑战，在AI4I数据集上展示了其通用性、可解释性和竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在实际应用中面临类别不平衡和多维噪声两大挑战，现有方法难以同时有效处理这两个问题，需要一种能够在保留关键判别信息的同时对特征空间进行层次化压缩的新架构。

Method: 提出RGNet，借鉴重整化群（RG）理论，通过层级式逐步压缩输入维度，并将所有尺度（从细粒度到粗粒度）的特征拼接后送入分类器，以同时捕获局部细节和全局模式。引入RG-flows概念，即具有可解释性的低维表征，并通过t-SNE可视化揭示其离散曲线结构以验证粗粒化的有效性。

Result: 在不平衡的AI4I数据集上进行了实验，RG-flows的t-SNE可视化展现出清晰的离散曲线结构，证实了粗粒化策略的有效性。RGNet在故障预测任务中表现出通用、可解释且具竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: RGNet作为一种基于重整化群层次粗粒化的通用架构，为类别不平衡场景下的故障预测提供了一种可解释且有效的解决方案，其RG-flows可视化进一步增强了模型的可解释性。

Abstract: The application of machine learning models in practical tasks faces challenges such as class imbalance and multidimensional noise. This paper proposes RGNet, a neural network architecture based on the concept of the renormalization group (RG), for hierarchical coarse-graining of the feature space. The model sequentially compresses the input dimensionality and concatenates all scales before classification, allowing it to capture both local details and global patterns. The notion of RG-flows is introduced - interpretable low-dimensional representations whose visualization via t-SNE reveals a discrete curvilinear structure confirming the effectiveness of coarse-graining. Experimental results are presented on the imbalanced AI4I dataset. The obtained results demonstrate that RGNet is a universal, interpretable, and competitive solution for fault prediction in applications with imbalanced classes.

</details>


### [162] [Quantum Annealing Enhanced Reinforcement Learning for Accurate Remaining Useful Lifetime Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18503)
*Manoranjan Gandhudi,Arunkumar V.,G. R. Anil,Gangadharan G. R*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出量子退火增强Q学习(QAQL)框架，将量子退火的采样行为与Q学习的序贯决策耦合，通过D-Wave量子退火器求解QUBO来提供探索能力，避免非线性退化轨迹上的过早收敛，并在两个工业预测性维护基准数据集上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计退化模型无法捕捉真实系统的强非线性，而数据驱动模型在高维非凸搜索空间中常收敛到次优解。现有方法缺乏有效的探索机制来应对复杂非线性退化轨迹，量子退火作为可用优化器的潜力尚未在强化学习回路中得到实际验证。

Method: 提出QAQL框架：(1)将每次Q值更新编码为小型QUBO问题，其基态对应贪心动作；(2)利用D-Wave Advantage量子退火器通过minor embedding求解QUBO，不取确定性最优解，而是通过多次读取返回近最优动作的分布；(3)该随机动作选择提供探索能力，抑制过早收敛；(4)退火器嵌入强化学习循环内部而非训练后外挂；(5)在NASA C-MAPSS涡扇发动机数据集和设备群预测性维护数据集上验证。

Result: 在大量独立运行和六种误差指标上平均后，QAQL在统计上显著优于所考虑的所有经典和量子基线方法。

Conclusion: 量子退火可作为强化学习回路中实际可用的优化器，而不仅仅是理论工具，适用于工业预测性维护应用。

Abstract: Remaining useful life (RUL) estimation is central to predictive maintenance, where an unplanned failure can cost far more than the asset itself. Statistical degradation models miss the strong nonlinearity of real systems, and data-driven models often converge to suboptimal solutions in high-dimensional, non-convex search spaces. We propose a Quantum Annealing enhanced Q-Learning (QAQL) framework that couples the sampling behaviour of quantum annealing with the sequential decision making of Q-learning. Each Q-value update is encoded as a small quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) whose ground state is the greedy action; rather than acting as a deterministic optimizer, the annealer returns a distribution over near-optimal actions across many reads, and this stochastic action selection supplies the exploration that curbs premature convergence on nonlinear degradation trajectories. The QUBO is solved on the D-Wave Advantage system using minor embedding, with the annealer woven into the reinforcement-learning loop rather than bolted on after training. We validate QAQL on two public benchmarks: the NASA C-MAPSS turbofan engine datasets and a device-fleet predictive maintenance dataset. Averaged over many independent runs and across six error metrics, QAQL outperforms the classical and quantum baselines considered in this study, with statistically significant improvements. The results indicate that quantum annealing is a usable, not merely theoretical, optimizer inside a reinforcement-learning loop for industrial predictive-maintenance applications.

</details>


### [163] [Self-CTRL: Self-Consistency Training with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18327)
*Itamar Pres,Laura Ruis,Melat Ghebreselassie,Belinda Z. Li,Jacob Andreas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Self-CTRL方法，通过强化学习优化语言模型自我解释与行为之间的一致性，在概率推理和宪法AI两个领域显著提升了模型的可审计性、透明度和对齐性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型缺乏对其自身行为的忠实描述能力，导致用户难以审计、理解和信任模型。若模型能够准确解释自身决策逻辑，将极大提升AI系统的安全性与可控性，但现有方法在自我解释与实际行动的一致性方面表现不佳。

Method: 提出Self-Consistency Training with Reinforcement Learning (Self-CTRL)，包含两个优化方向：(1) 更新解释以更好地预测行为；(2) 更新行为以更好地匹配解释。在概率推理任务中，训练LM模仿有偏采样器并报告偏差；在宪法AI任务中，训练LM描述其拒绝或遵从用户请求的规则。

Result: 在概率推理任务中，自我报告的潜在偏差与行为测量偏差之间的相关性从R²=0.24提升至R²=0.64，达到直接真值监督的泛化水平；在宪法AI任务中，第三方审计模型对拒绝行为的预测准确率从36%提升至92%，同时行为更新将HarmBench失败率从15.0%降至0.5%，且无害提示的拒绝率未显著增加。

Conclusion: Self-CTRL通过将解释与行为对齐，为训练更安全、更透明、更可控的AI模型提供了通用方法框架，在多个领域验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) that faithfully describe their own behavior can more easily be audited, understood, and trusted by users. This paper describes Self-Consistency Training with Reinforcement Learning (Self-CTRL), a method that optimizes for consistency between a LM's self-explanations and behavior on related inputs by updating explanations to better predict behavior or updating behavior to better match explanations. We apply our method in two domains. First, we study a formal probabilistic reasoning task in which LMs must learn to imitate a family of biased samplers and evaluated on their ability to report the associated biases. We find that consistency training improves the correlation between self-reported and behaviorally-measured latent biases from $R^2=0.24$ to $R^2=0.64$ on a set of held-out distributions, matching the generalization of direct ground-truth supervision. Second, we study a constitutional AI domain in which LMs must describe when they will refuse or comply with user requests. Here, Self-CTRL produces rules that faithfully describe the model's behavior on held-out requests, improving the refusal predictions of a third-party auditor model from $36\%$ to $92\%$. In the other direction, behavior updates improve alignment, reducing HarmBench failure rate from $15.0\%$ to $0.5\%$ without substantially increasing refusal on harmless prompts. By aligning explanations and behavior, our work provides a general recipe for training AI models to be safer, more transparent, and more controllable.

</details>


### [164] [Concept Modulation Models: A Unified Framework for Identifiability and Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18509)
*Soheun Yi,Yizhou Lu,Chandler Squires,Pradeep Ravikumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出概念调制模型(CMMs)，一种属性索引的条件生成模型类。通过特征一致性诱导潜在概念转移，用属性势函数统一分离"提升步骤"与模型特定刚性论证，从而同时处理条件潜变量模型中的可识别性与外推问题，并统一了多个现有结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件潜变量模型（如非线性ICA、因果表示学习、扰动建模）的可识别性与外推保证高度依赖具体模型，缺乏统一框架。需要一种通用方法，将"观测到的属性间变异如何决定潜在结构"与"潜在结构如何决定未见属性上的分布"这两个问题统一起来。

Method: 作者提出概念调制模型(CMMs)，结构为A→Λ→C→X：属性A选择调制器Λ，调制器诱导潜在概念律C，概念生成观测特征X。核心贡献包括：(1)将基于转移的可识别性提升到条件设置——观测属性上的特征一致性诱导受CMM类约束的潜在概念转移；(2)引入属性势函数（属性条件概念律之间的对数密度比），将通用"提升步骤"与模型特定的刚性论证分离；(3)用同一势函数刻画外推——当迁移的属性-势恒等式延伸至未见属性时，在该属性上的一致性恰好成立，从而给出代数外推判据。

Result: 该框架识别出多个现有可识别性和外推结果背后共通的基于势函数的证明对象；与这些工作中的模型特定刚性论证结合后，能够恢复其原有结论，验证了框架的统一性和一般性。

Conclusion: CMMs框架成功地将条件潜变量模型中的可识别性与外推分析统一起来，通过属性势函数将通用提升步骤与模型特定论证解耦，为理解和推导该类模型的理论保证提供了系统化的工具。

Abstract: Reliable generalization in conditional latent variable models requires understanding both identifiability and extrapolation: how observed variation across attributes determines latent structure, and how that structure determines distributions at unseen attributes. However, existing identifiability and extrapolation guarantees are largely model-specific, with separate analyses in nonlinear ICA, causal representation learning, perturbation modeling, and related conditional latent variable models. We introduce concept modulation models (CMMs), an attribute-indexed class of conditional generative models with structure $A\to Λ\to C\to X$, where attributes select modulators, modulators induce latent concept laws, and concepts generate observed features. CMMs lift transition-based identifiability to conditional settings by showing that feature agreement on observed attributes induces a latent concept transition constrained by the CMM class. We express these constraints through attribute potentials, log-density ratios between attribute-conditioned concept laws, separating the generic lifting step from model-specific rigidity arguments. The same potentials control extrapolation: agreement at unseen attributes holds exactly when the transported attribute-potential identities extend to those attributes. This yields algebraic extrapolation criteria, identifies the common potential-based proof objects behind several existing identifiability and extrapolation results, and, when combined with the model-specific rigidity arguments in those works, recovers their stated conclusions.

</details>


### [165] [ThousandWorlds: A benchmark for climate emulation of potentially habitable exoplanets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18338)
*Edward T. Stevenson,Mei Ting Mak,Eric Wolf,Denis E. Sergeev,Tobi Hammond,N. J. Mayne,Miles Cranmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ThousandWorlds 是一个面向系外行星气候模拟的基准数据集，包含约1800个来自5个不同GCM的3D大气场模拟，旨在推动低数据量、多模拟器、参数到场回归的机器学习研究，填补了系外气候模拟领域缺乏标准化ML基准的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系外行星生命探测依赖气候模型来解释大气信号，但GCM单次运行耗时巨大（百万核时级），机器学习模拟器是破局关键。然而该领域长期缺乏统一、多模型的气候数据集来训练和评估ML方法，制约了进展。

Method: 构建ThousandWorlds数据集：整合5个GCM约1800次模拟，以8个行星参数映射到温度、湿度、风、云、辐射等3D大气场。设计三个递进难度子集（单模拟器回归、全观测多模拟器回归、结构化缺失多模拟器回归），提出两套评估协议（排序协议和相对于GCM间分歧的性能度量），并评估了简单方法、深度学习和高斯过程共7种基线。

Result: 高斯过程方法在所有基线中表现最优，表明ThousandWorlds揭示了一个现有深度学习尚不能胜任的数据场景。数据集和代码已开源。

Conclusion: ThousandWorlds填补了系外气候模拟领域ML基准的空白，暴露了深度学习在低数据、多模拟器、参数到场回归任务中的不足，为未来方法开发提供了标准化测试平台。

Abstract: The search for life beyond Earth will depend on detecting faint signatures in the atmospheres of potentially habitable exoplanets. Interpreting those signatures requires understanding the host planet's climate: the same molecule may signal life on one planet and abiotic chemistry on another. Global climate models (GCMs) provide this understanding, but individual runs can require up to millions of core-hours and substantial domain expert time. Machine-learning emulators could remove this bottleneck, but progress has been limited by the absence of a curated, multi-model exoclimate dataset. We introduce ThousandWorlds, an ML-ready benchmark for exoclimate emulation and for the broader regime of low-data, multi-simulator, parameter-to-field regression. The dataset contains approximately 1800 simulations from five GCMs, mapping eight planet parameters to 3D atmospheric fields including temperature, humidity, winds, clouds, and radiation. Three nested subsets define progressively harder challenges: single-simulator regression, multi-simulator regression with complete observations, and multi-simulator regression with structured missingness. We propose two evaluation protocols: one for ranking methods, and one that measures performance relative to the disagreement between GCMs themselves. We evaluate seven baselines spanning simple methods, deep learning, and Gaussian processes. GP-based methods perform best, suggesting that ThousandWorlds exposes a regime where off-the-shelf deep learning does not yet succeed. Data: https://doi.org/10.57967/hf/8695. Code: https://github.com/edstevenson/ThousandWorlds.

</details>


### [166] [Effects of sparsity and superposition on loss in simple autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18538)
*Mriganka Basu Roy Chowdhury,Eric McLaughlin Weiner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对Elhage等人(2022)提出的神经网络中叠加(superposition)现象进行了严格的数学分析，为幂律激活函数下的L2重建损失提供了在极稀疏区域紧的上界和下界，从理论上验证了叠加作为压缩策略的最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络机制可解释性面临的核心挑战是多义性(polysemanticity)——单个神经元承担多种功能，阻碍了对网络的清晰理解。Elhage等人(2022)将多义性归因于叠加现象，但缺乏严格的数学基础。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，为叠加的发生机制和最优性提供数学论证。

Method: 以Elhage等人(2022)的稀疏输入自编码器为分析框架，针对幂律激活函数，通过数学推导建立L2重建损失的上界和下界。分析聚焦于极稀疏区域，在该区域中上界与下界趋于紧合，从而严格验证叠加表示的最优性。

Result: 成功推导出幂律激活函数下L2重建损失的上界和下界，并证明在极稀疏条件下两个界是紧的。该结果从数学上严格支撑了Elhage等人(2022)关于叠加现象的实证发现。文末还列出了若干开放问题。

Conclusion: 叠加是神经网络在高维稀疏输入下实现高效压缩的数学上合理的策略，本文通过严格的界分析为其提供了理论基石。该工作有助于深化对神经网络表征学习的理解，并为机制可解释性研究提供了新的数学工具和视角。

Abstract: One of the major difficulties in the mechanistic interpretability of neural networks is the occurrence of polysemanticity, which suggests that each neuron is typically responsible for multiple different tasks, impeding a clean interpretation of their function. The seminal paper of Elhage et al. (2022) argues that this occurs due to superposition, a phenomenon where the neural network represents distinct features as non-orthogonal directions in a lower-dimensional space, a strategy that allows much greater compression of the data without sacrificing fidelity due to the feature sparsity of input vectors. Elhage et al. (2022) empirically validates these hypotheses in a rather natural and simple autoencoder with sparse inputs. The contribution of the present work is to analyze the mathematical basis for the occurrence and optimality of superposition, while rigorously corroborating some of their findings. In particular, we provide upper and lower bounds for the L2 reconstruction loss, tight in the very sparse regime, for power activation functions. A short list of interesting open problems are also included at the end.

</details>


### [167] [Do Time Series Foundation Model Benchmarks Hide Regime-Dependent Failures? Evidence from Traffic Speed Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18367)
*Yingshuo Wang,Xian Sun,Lingdong Kong,Wei Gao,Yanhang Li,Zhichao Fan,Zexin Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 标准基准测试通过聚合指标评估时序基础模型，但这会掩盖关键运行状态下的严重失效。本文引入按状态分层评估，发现模型在交通状态切换期间精度和预测区间覆盖率急剧下降，并提出双峰混合增强（BMA）后处理方法，在保持整体精度的同时提升过渡状态的区间覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前时序基础模型（TSFM）的评估依赖聚合指标（如整体MAE），但这些指标可能掩盖模型在关键运行状态（regime）下的严重失效，尤其是在交通流中自由流与拥堵状态之间的过渡期。需要一种能够揭示这些隐藏失效的评估方法。

Method: 提出**状态分层评估（regime-stratified evaluation）**，将交通速度数据按自由流、拥堵及过渡状态进行划分，分别评估三种TSFM的预测精度（MAE）和预测区间经验覆盖率。进一步提出**双峰混合增强（BMA）**后处理方法：将TSFM的点预测与历史条件分布（各传感器训练数据的条件采样）相结合，在保留TSFM精度的同时改善过渡状态的区间覆盖率。

Result: 过渡状态下模型性能严重退化：整体MAE为3 mph，而过渡状态MAE高达11 mph；90%预测区间的名义覆盖率在过渡状态最低降至55%（远低于90%目标）。简单的历史条件基线在过渡状态覆盖率上优于所有TSFM，但整体精度差。BMA方法成功使过渡状态覆盖率接近历史基线水平，同时保持TSFM的整体精度。

Conclusion: TSFM基准测试应纳入状态感知评估（regime-aware evaluation），以揭示聚合指标所掩盖的失效模式。BMA作为一种轻量后处理策略，可有效结合数据驱动预测与历史分布知识，弥补模型在关键状态下的不确定性量化缺陷。

Abstract: Standard benchmarks evaluate time series foundation models (TSFMs) using aggregate metrics, but these can mask severe failures in critical operating regimes. We introduce regime-stratified evaluation and apply it to three TSFMs on two standard traffic speed benchmarks. Traffic exhibits abrupt regime switching between free-flow and congested states, producing bimodal speed distributions during transitions. When we stratify by traffic regime, both accuracy and prediction-interval coverage degrade sharply during transitions: transition-regime MAE reaches 11 mph (versus 3 mph overall), and empirical coverage of 90% prediction intervals drops as low as 55%. These failures are invisible in aggregate metrics because free-flow observations dominate the sample. A simple historical conditional baseline (sampling from per-sensor training distributions) achieves better transition coverage than any TSFM, but has far worse overall accuracy. We propose bimodal mixture augmentation (BMA), a post-hoc method that combines TSFM forecasts with historical distributional knowledge, approaching the historical baseline's transition coverage while preserving the TSFM's accuracy. Our results suggest that TSFM benchmarks should incorporate regime-aware evaluation to surface failures that aggregate metrics hide.

</details>


### [168] [TS-Fault: Benchmarking Time Series Forecasters Against Structural Faults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18539)
*Yuyang Zhao,Lian Xu,Hao Miao,Chenxi Liu,Hao Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TS-Fault是一个时间序列预测鲁棒性评测基准，通过注入四种结构化故障（观测级/机制级×单变量/多变量）并配合五级语义难度，揭示了清洁数据准确率与鲁棒性反相关、机制级故障会重塑模型排名、基础模型最脆弱三大反直觉发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前TSF模型几乎仅凭清洁数据上的单一误差指标排名，隐含假设这能预测实际部署中的可靠性。但真实故障并非i.i.d.噪声，而是具有时间形态、跨变量依赖断裂、状态变化伴随缺失、因果传播等结构化特征。作者指出需要一种将鲁棒性作为数据质量问题来系统评估的方法。

Method: 提出TS-Fault基准，沿两个正交轴（观测级vs机制级、单变量vs多变量）将故障组织为四种模式，通过统一的重要性分数将故障注入预测最关键的时间窗口，并设置五个可控语义难度级别。采用配对清洁/污染协议评估模型。

Result: 在6个数据集、21个模型上实验得出三个反直觉发现：(1)清洁数据准确率与鲁棒性呈反相关；(2)清洁排名在观测级故障下保持但在机制级故障下被重新洗牌；(3)所有灾难性失败均发生在机制级故障下，基础模型清洁准确率最高却最脆弱。

Conclusion: 仅凭清洁数据准确率排名不足以反映TSF模型的部署可靠性，TS-Fault为评估结构化故障下的鲁棒性提供了系统化工具，并呼吁社区关注模型在机制级故障下的脆弱性问题。

Abstract: Time series forecasting (TSF) underpins consequential decisions in energy, transportation, finance, and healthcare, yet TSF models are almost universally ranked by a single number (e.g., average error) on clean held-out data, under the implicit assumption that it predicts deployed reliability. However, real faults are not i.i.d noise but structured events with temporal shape, broken cross-variable dependencies, regime change coupled with missingness, and causal propagation across a sensing pipeline. Treating TSF robustness as a data-quality problem, we present TS-Fault, a benchmark that evaluates forecasting models under explicit, parameterized fault scenarios with controllable semantic difficulty. TS-Fault organizes recurring failures into four modes along two orthogonal axes (observation- vs mechanism-level; univariate vs multivariate) and injects each fault into the most prediction-critical window via a unified importance score. This design enables robustness to be tested against the structures models actually rely on, rather than reduced to generic noise sensitivity. We evaluate 21 models across 6 datasets, 4 modes, and 5 difficulty levels under a paired clean/corrupt protocol. The results reveal three findings that contradict common leaderboard intuition: (i) clean-data accuracy anti-correlates with robustness; (ii) clean rankings are preserved under observation-level faults but reshuffled under mechanism-level faults; and (iii) all catastrophic failures occur under mechanism-level faults, with foundation models achieving the highest clean-data accuracy yet exhibiting the greatest fragility. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/Ray-zyy/TS-Fault.

</details>


### [169] [From Sparse Features to Trustworthy Proxies: Certifying SAE-Based Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18383)
*Dibyanayan Bandyopadhyay,Asif Ekbal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种事后泛化框架，通过稀疏代理模型对语言模型的SAE解释进行忠实性认证。该框架利用代理风险、SAE重构间隙、概念池不匹配和稀疏复杂度四个可测量量，推导出基础模型期望风险的上界，并将非空上界作为解释忠实性的可操作判据。在GPT-2 Small、Gemma-2B和Llama-3-8B上验证了该上界的非空性，发现后期层更易认证，且特征混洗消融实验能区分真正的语义对齐与纯粹的统计稀疏性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决一个核心问题：基于稀疏自编码器（SAE）的解释何时可以被视为底层冻结语言模型的忠实视图。目前缺乏一个严格的操作性标准来判定SAE提取的可解释特征是否忠实地反映了原始模型的行为。

Method: 提出事后泛化框架：用预训练SAE的重构结果替换原生隐藏激活，得到一个稀疏代理模型。在此基础上，使用四个可测量量（代理风险、SAE重构间隙、概念池不匹配、稀疏复杂度）推导出基础模型期望风险的PAC风格上界。该上界非空则表明稀疏特征保留了有意义的预测信息，重构和失配误差小则表明代理模型与原始模型行为接近。

Result: 在GPT-2 Small、Gemma-2B和Llama-3-8B上，该上界在实用样本量下达到非空。Llama-3-8B的逐层分析显示，后期层更易认证，这与更强的局部保真度和更弱的下游误差放大相关。特征混洗消融实验表明，该框架能区分真正的语义对齐与纯粹的统计稀疏性。

Conclusion: 该框架为SAE解释的忠实性提供了首个严格的可操作认证标准，能够有效诊断SAE解释何时变得不可靠，并揭示了模型深度与可解释性忠实度之间的强关联。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are increasingly used to extract interpretable features from language models (LMs), yet a central question remains: when can an SAE-based explanation be treated as a faithful view of an underlying frozen LM We study this through a post-hoc generalization framework that certifies the LM via a sparse proxy, obtained by replacing a native hidden activation with its pretrained SAE reconstruction. Our framework derives an upper bound on the base model's expected risk using four measurable quantities: proxy risk, SAE reconstruction gap, concept-pool mismatch, and sparse complexity. We interpret this certificate as an operational criterion for explanatory faithfulness. In particular, a non-vacuous bound indicates that the extracted sparse features retain meaningful predictive information, while small reconstruction and mismatch errors indicate that the proxy remains behaviorally close to the original model. Empirically, we show that the bound becomes non-vacuous on GPT-2 Small, Gemma-2B, and Llama-3-8B at practical sample sizes. A detailed layerwise analysis of Llama-3-8B reveals a strong depth dependence, with later layers becoming much easier to certify, associated with both stronger local fidelity and weaker downstream error amplification. Finally, through feature-shuffling ablations, we show that the decomposition distinguishes genuine semantic alignment from mere statistical sparsity, providing a useful diagnostic for when SAE-based explanations become less reliable.

</details>


### [170] [Online Distributional Prediction via Latent Cluster Geometry Under Drift and Corruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18778)
*Navyansh Mahla,Prateek Chanda,Ganesh Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于潜在聚类几何和可逆跳MCMC的在线分布预测方法，能在漂移和对抗性破坏下实现亚线性Wasserstein遗憾，无需对数据流、漂移或破坏机制做参数假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多实际应用需要预测完整的数据生成分布而非点估计，但在非平稳数据流中，漂移和对抗性破坏使得在线分布预测极具挑战。现有方法通常依赖参数化假设，限制了适用性。

Method: 将每个候选分布表示为可变大小的潜在聚类中心配置（latent cluster geometry），通过Gibbs拟后验（quasi-posterior）对这些配置加权，并用可逆跳MCMC（reversible-jump MCMC）采样变维后验。引入重启变体（restarted variant）对拟贝叶斯更新进行时间局部化，以应对长期记忆陈旧问题。

Result: 高概率遗憾界可分解为PAC-Bayesian复杂度项、破坏敏感的扰动项和动态最优传输项（由累积W₂²驱动）。在有限支撑、稳定潜在几何、预测映射正则、Oracle可实现性、局部重启窗口、亚线性传输动作和亚线性破坏预算条件下，重启预测器实现了亚线性累积Wasserstein遗憾。

Conclusion: 该方法无需对流、漂移或破坏过程建模，即可在非平稳对抗环境下实现可证明的在线分布预测。

Abstract: Online learning in non-stationary streams is often formulated as tracking a point estimate, but many applications require predicting the full data-generating distribution. We study online distributional prediction under drift and adversarial corruption. Our approach represents each candidate law through a latent cluster geometry: a variable-size configuration of centers that organizes probability mass and induces a predictive distribution. A Gibbs quasi-posterior over these configurations yields an online predictor by posterior averaging, and the resulting variable-dimensional posterior can be sampled with reversible-jump MCMC. The method therefore avoids specifying a parametric streaming law while retaining a structured latent space for uncertainty, regularization, and comparison.
  We evaluate performance by cumulative Wasserstein-1 regret against the time-varying true law. The analysis separates two effects: corruption perturbs the loss-based posterior update, whereas drift makes long-horizon posterior memory stale. We address the latter with a restarted variant that temporally localizes the same quasi-Bayesian update. The resulting high-probability bounds decompose into a PAC-Bayesian complexity term, a corruption-sensitive posterior perturbation term, and a dynamic optimal-transport term driven by \(A_T^{\mathrm{OT}}=\sum_{t=2}^T W_2^2(p_{t-1}^*,p_t^*)\). Under bounded support, stable latent geometry, predictive-map regularity, oracle realizability, localized restart windows, sublinear transport action, and sublinear corruption budget, the restarted predictor achieves sublinear cumulative Wasserstein regret. These guarantees require no parametric model for the stream, drift mechanism, or corruption process.

</details>


### [171] [SCOPE-FL: A Strategy-proof Chain-based Optimal pareto efficient Federated Learning System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18384)
*Seyed Salar Ghazi,Kaiwen Zhang,Mehdi feizi,Hans-Arno Jacobsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCOPE-FL 通过将 HFL 客户端选择建模为双边学校选择问题，并利用 TTC 算法同时实现帕累托效率和策略防伪性，辅以基于单轮重构的 Shapley 值近似进行公平奖励分配，并借助区块链智能合约确保防篡改环境，在准确性、收敛速度和奖励效率上全面超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 HFL 客户端选择机制存在两个根本缺陷：一是优先考虑稳定性而牺牲帕累托效率（PE），导致资源分配次优；二是缺乏策略防伪性（SP），激励参与者虚报真实偏好，二者在实践中共同损害系统整体福利。

Method: SCOPE-FL 是一种同步 HFL 框架，将客户端选择建模为双边学校选择问题，采用 Top Trading Cycle (TTC) 算法同时保证 PE 和 SP；奖励分配采用基于单轮重构（OR）的可扩展 Shapley 值近似；整体机制通过区块链智能合约执行，提供防篡改环境以保障 SP 的实际有效性。

Result: 在 MNIST、Fashion-MNIST 和 CIFAR-10 上的综合评估表明，SCOPE-FL 在模型准确率、收敛速度和奖励效率方面均优于 DA、IAS 等 SOTA 方法；通信延迟与 DA 相当，区块链开销在大规模场景下显著低于 DA。

Conclusion: SCOPE-FL 通过 TTC 算法与 Shapley 值奖励机制的结合，在 HFL 中首次同时实现了帕累托效率和策略防伪性，实验验证了其性能优势与实用性。

Abstract: Hierarchical Federated Learning (HFL) enables scalable collaborative model training across distributed devices while preserving data privacy. However, existing HFL client selection mechanisms suffer from a fundamental strategic inefficiency. By prioritizing stability over Pareto efficiency (PE), they produce suboptimal resource allocations, and without strategy proofness (SP), participants are incentivized to misrepresent their true preferences, both failures degrading system overall welfare in the Pareto sense in practice. To address it, we propose SCOPE-FL (Strategy-proof Chain-based Optimal pareto efficient Federated Learning), a synchronous HFL framework that formulates client selection as a two-sided school choice problem solved through the Top Trading Cycle (TTC) algorithm that simultaneously guarantees PE and SP. For reward distribution, SCOPE-FL employs a scalable Shapley value approximation based on One-Round Reconstruction (OR), ensuring compensation proportional to each client's contribution. The entire mechanism executes via blockchain smart contracts, providing the tamper-proof environment required for the SP guarantees to hold in practice. A comprehensive evaluation on MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and CIFAR-10 demonstrates that SCOPE-FL outperforms state-of-the-art approaches, including DA, IAS, and other methods across model accuracy, convergence rate, and reward efficiency, while achieving communication latency comparable to DA and blockchain overhead significantly lower than DA at scale.

</details>


### [172] [Strategic Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18867)
*Jivat Neet Kaur,Pratik Patil,Divya Shanmugam,Emma Pierson,Michael I. Jordan,Nika Haghtalab,Meena Jagadeesan,Ahmed Alaa,Serena Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化研究了在策略性分类中，通过特征选择与岭正则化的协同设计来应对输入特征被人为操纵的问题。核心发现是仅基于特征的可操纵性排除特征通常是次优的；文章给出了最优正则化下特征子集性能的精细刻画，并提出了联合选择特征集与岭正则化水平的实用算法，最后通过医疗支付基准案例进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险领域（如医疗），基于算法预测进行资源分配时，决策者常面临输入特征被策略性操纵的问题。实践中，决策者往往受限于在现有预测流程中调整粗粒度杠杆（如基于可操纵性排除特征、使用标准正则化收缩系数），而非从头重新设计预测器。然而，这种启发式做法缺乏理论指导，需要系统研究特征选择与正则化在策略性分类中的交互关系。

Method: 作者形式化建模了策略性分类中的特征选择问题，分析了不同特征子集在最优岭正则化下的均衡表现。通过推导特征子集性能的精细理论刻画，揭示了仅按可操纵性排除特征的局限性。基于这一理论刻画，提出了联合优化特征集合与岭正则化系数的实用算法，并在医疗支付真实基准数据集上进行案例研究。

Result: （1）理论上证明了仅依据特征可操纵性进行特征排除通常是次优的；（2）给出了最优正则化下任意特征子集性能的精细刻画；（3）提出了一种联合选择特征与正则化水平的实用算法；（4）在医疗支付基准案例中验证了算法的有效性，展示了其指导粗粒度策略杠杆设计的能力。

Conclusion: 本文为缓解算法决策系统中策略性行为的影响提供了一个有原则且实用的框架。结果表明，特征选择与正则化应联合设计而非分离决策，为实践中受限于粗粒度调整的决策者提供了理论依据和可操作的工具。

Abstract: When algorithmic predictors inform resource allocation in high-stakes domains such as healthcare, these predictors must account for strategic manipulation of input features. The typical solution is to redesign the predictor itself to explicitly account for strategic interactions. In practice, however, decision makers are often constrained to adjusting coarser levers within existing prediction pipelines. For example, healthcare organizations often select which features to exclude based on perceived manipulability, while using standard regularization procedures to shrink the coefficients of retained features. In this work, we initiate a formal study of strategic classification through feature selection and its interaction with ridge regularization. Our main finding is that excluding individual features based on their manipulability alone is generally suboptimal. We provide a fine-grained characterization of the performance of a feature subset under optimal regularization, yielding new insights for policy design. Motivated by this characterization, we develop a practical algorithm for jointly choosing the feature set and the level of ridge regularization. Through a real-world case study on a healthcare payments benchmark, we illustrate how our algorithm can guide the design of coarse policy levers in practice. Our results provide a principled, practical framework for mitigating the effects of strategic behavior in algorithmic decision-making systems.

</details>
