A critical challenge, however, is that the neural component may relearn mechanistic parts, yielding redundant and uninterpretable models, especially when the symbolic structure itself is discovered from data. Existing methods based on standard $L^2$ regularization rely on a projection argument that breaks when the symbolic component is learned through sparse discovery, allowing the neural augmentation to overlap with symbolic structure. We introduce \textbf{OrthoReg} (Orthogonal Regularization), which directly penalizes overlap between the symbolic and neural components, preventing symbolic structure from being absorbed by the neural residual. This yields a complementary decomposition: the symbolic part captures what the library can express, and the neural part captures what remains. On benchmark dynamical systems with partial library mismatch, OrthoReg improves symbolic recovery and out-of-distribution behavior.

</details>


### [242] [Seeing Before Reasoning: Decoupling Perception and Reasoning for Shortcut-Resilient Multimodal On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19120)
*Sihan Wang,Xiyao Liu,Lianqing Liu,Zhi Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ViGOS 提出一种视觉 grounded 的在线策略自蒸馏框架，通过分离视觉描述监督与推理监督，并仅在无效轨迹上引入参考教师，解决了多模态大模型后训练中 OPSD 的文本捷径问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接将在纯文本 LLM 上有效的 OPSD 方法迁移到 MLLM 会导致模型走捷径：特权教师的目标主要依赖文本参考答案而非图像信息，削弱了模型的视觉 grounding 能力。

Method: ViGOS 框架将学生模型的前向过程分为两步：先生成视觉描述，再进行推理回答。对于有效 rollout，仅用图像感知教师监督视觉描述部分，用特权推理教师在相同前缀上监督推理与最终答案；对于无效 rollout，引入参考教师以恢复输出格式。

Result: 在通用视觉语言、专家推理、视觉数学、空间 grounding 和视觉-语言先验等基准上，ViGOS 保持了 OPSD 的主要优势，并在易产生捷径的场景中显著提升了图像 grounded 行为。

Conclusion: 通过解耦视觉感知与推理的监督信号并控制参考教师的介入条件，ViGOS 有效缓解了 MLLM 后训练中的文本捷径问题，为多模态自蒸馏提供了更合理的训练范式。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a model on its own rollouts and uses a frozen copy to provide dense token-level targets conditioned on a reference target. This works well for LLM reasoning, but a direct extension to multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can create a shortcut: the privileged target may guide tokens mainly based on the text reference target rather than the image. We propose ViGOS, a visually grounded OPSD framework for MLLM post-training. The student first writes a visual description and then reasons toward the final answer. For valid rollouts, an image-only perception teacher supervises the description, while a privileged reasoning teacher supervises the reasoning and final answer on the same student prefix. A reference teacher is used only for invalid rollouts to recover the output format. Across general vision-language, expert reasoning, visual math, spatial grounding, and visual-language-prior benchmarks, ViGOS keeps the main benefits of OPSD and improves image-grounded behavior in shortcut-prone settings.

</details>


### [243] [DIPHINE: Diffusion-based $Φ$-ID Neural Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18997)
*Simon Pedro Galeano Munoz,Mustapha Bounoua,Giulio Franzese,Pietro Michiardi,Maurizio Filippone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了DIPHINE，首个基于扩散模型的神经网络估计器，用于计算集成信息分解(ΦID)的16个原子，突破了现有方法仅适用于高斯或离散系统的限制，可处理连续非高斯动态系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ΦID计算方法局限于高斯系统或离散系统，无法应用于现实世界中大量存在的连续非高斯动态系统，亟需一种无分布假设的通用估计方法。

Method: 提出DIPHINE——基于得分匹配扩散模型的神经网络估计器。核心思路：(1)利用单一摊销网络联合估计ΦID所需的所有互信息项；(2)通过Möbius反演从互信息中恢复16个信息原子；(3)对误差传播进行理论分析，证明互信息到原子的映射雅可比矩阵为整数值，且synergy-to-synergy原子理论上最难估计。

Result: 在合成基准测试上准确恢复了真实原子值；互信息估计性能优于现有方法；在真实数据应用中成功提取了具有生理可解释性的信息动态结构，且无需任何分布假设。

Conclusion: DIPHINE首次实现了对任意连续动态系统的ΦID计算，通过扩散模型和摊销推理克服了传统方法的分布限制，理论分析揭示了各原子估计难度的差异，为复杂系统的信息动态分析开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Uncovering the true informational architecture of real-world complex systems requires disentangling how their components uniquely store, redundantly share, and synergistically integrate information over time. Integrated Information Decomposition ($Φ$ID) is a framework for decomposing the information dynamics of multivariate systems into sixteen non-overlapping atoms that characterize redundant, unique, and synergistic modes of information storage, transfer, and integration. Existing methods to compute $Φ$ID are restricted to Gaussian or discrete systems, preventing its application to continuous non-Gaussian dynamical systems. We address this limitation by proposing DIPHINE (Diffusion-based $Φ$-ID Neural Estimator), the first neural estimator that leverages score-based diffusion models to jointly estimate all the mutual information terms required by $Φ$ID from a single amortized network, recovering the sixteen atoms through Möbius inversion. We provide a theoretical analysis of error propagation through the inversion, showing that the Jacobian of the mapping from mutual informations to atoms is integer-valued and that the synergy-to-synergy atom is provably the hardest to estimate. We demonstrate accurate recovery of ground-truth atoms on synthetic benchmarks, superior performance compared to established mutual information estimators, and the ability to extract physiologically interpretable information-dynamic structure on an application involving real data without any distributional assumptions.

</details>


### [244] [Essential Subspace Merging for Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19164)
*Longhua Li,Lei Qi,Xin Geng,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出必要子空间分解（ESD），将任务更新的能量集中于少数主方向构成的"必要子空间"，其余方向累积导致合并干扰。基于此提出ESM（静态合并）和ESM++（动态合并），前者正交化融合必要成分，后者将残差分解为低秩专家并通过原型路由选择。实验表明两种方法有效保留任务知识并减少干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并的核心挑战是任务特定参数更新之间的相互干扰。作者观察到任务更新引起的输出偏移能量高度集中于少量主方向（即"必要子空间"），而大多数剩余方向虽携带很少任务相关能量，但在多任务更新中累积后会造成严重合并干扰。

Method: 提出必要子空间分解（ESD），根据激活偏移的主成分分解每个任务更新。基于ESD，设计两种合并方法：(1) ESM——训练无关的静态合并，将必要分量正交化并融合为紧凑多任务模型；(2) ESM++——训练无关的动态合并，将任务特定残差分解为低秩专家，推理时通过基于原型的路由选择最相关专家。

Result: 在多个任务集和模型规模上的广泛实验表明，ESM和ESM++在保留任务知识的同时有效减少了任务间干扰。

Conclusion: 通过必要子空间分解，可以有效隔离任务更新的核心信息与干扰成分，ESM和ESM++为模型合并提供了训练无关的高效解决方案，在保持任务性能的同时显著降低干扰。

Abstract: Model merging aims to enable multi-task learning by integrating the capabilities of multiple models fine-tuned from the same pre-trained checkpoint into a single model. Its core challenge is inter-task interference among task-specific parameter updates. In this paper, we analyze the output shifts induced by task updates and observe that their energy is concentrated in a small number of principal directions. We call the subspace spanned by these directions the essential subspace. In contrast, most remaining directions carry little task-relevant energy, but their accumulation across multiple task updates can cause severe interference during merging. Motivated by this observation, we propose Essential Subspace Decomposition (ESD), which decomposes each task update according to the principal components of its activation shift. Based on ESD, we introduce Essential Subspace Merging (ESM), a training-free static merging method that orthogonalizes and fuses essential components into one compact multi-task model. We further extend ESM to ESM++, a training-free dynamic merging method that decomposes task-specific residuals into low-rank experts and selects the most relevant expert through prototype-based routing during forward inference. Extensive experiments across multiple task sets and model scales demonstrate that ESM and ESM++ effectively preserves task knowledge while reducing inter-task interference.

</details>


### [245] [The Reward Was in Your Data All Along: Correcting Flow Matching with Discriminator-Guided RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19162)
*Nicolas Beltran-Velez,Felix Friedrich,Zhang Xiaofeng,Reyhane Askari-Hemmat,Xiaochuang Han,Adriana Romero-Soriano,Michal Drozdzal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出匹配类生成模型的训练损失与样本质量的感知/语义属性存在结构性不匹配，提出判别器引导强化学习（DRL），用预训练表示空间中的判别器logit作为奖励进行KL正则化RL，在多个骨干网络上显著降低FID和语义FD，且无需人类偏好数据即可提升偏好奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前score/flow匹配模型常需借助基于偏好的RL来恢复视觉真实感和物体结构——这本应是匹配训练从数据中习得的。作者认为这暴露了匹配损失（训练时边际分布下的ℓ₂回归）与推理时决定样本质量的视觉语义属性之间的结构性错位。RL通过直接沿奖励景观优化规避了这一问题，但获取奖励通常依赖昂贵且混入标注者倾向的人类偏好。

Method: 提出DRL（Discriminator-Guided RL）：(1) 在预训练表示空间（如DINOv3）中训练判别器区分真实数据与基模型生成样本；(2) 将该判别器的logit作为KL正则化强化学习的奖励信号。预训练空间将判别器约束在感知有意义的方向上，logit天然估计数据与模型的对数似然比，是瞄准数据分布的最优奖励。

Result: 在SiT、JiT、REPA、RAE四个骨干网络上，DRL一致地大幅降低guidance-free FID（SiT上从9.38降至2.62）和语义空间FD（SiT上DINOv3 FD从88.2降至19.3）。在不训练人类偏好奖励的情况下提升人类偏好奖励，且在后续偏好后训练中实现了偏好奖励与图像保真度之间更优的帕累托前沿，增加对齐同时减少过饱和、过亮等低级伪影。

Conclusion: 匹配损失与感知质量之间的结构不匹配是score/flow模型需借助RL的根本原因；DRL通过在预训练语义空间中训练判别器作为奖励，无需人类偏好即可有效弥合这一裂痕，既能独立提升样本质量，又能与后续偏好RL形成互补，实现更优的质量-对齐权衡。

Abstract: Score- and flow-matching models often rely on preference-based reinforcement learning for two purposes: aligning with subjective preferences and, surprisingly, recovering properties such as visual realism and coherent object structure that matching-based training is intended to learn from the data itself. We argue that this reflects a structural mismatch. Matching losses measure $\ell_2$ regression error on the velocity or score field under training-time marginals, a proxy poorly aligned with the visual and semantic properties that determine sample quality at inference. Given a reward aligned with these properties, RL sidesteps the mismatch by evaluating the model on its own samples and following the reward landscape directly. The challenge is to obtain such a reward without relying on human preferences, which are expensive and conflate data realism with annotator inclinations.
  We propose Discriminator-Guided RL (DRL). DRL trains a discriminator to separate data from base-model samples in a pretrained representation space and uses its logit as the reward in KL-regularized RL. The pretrained space restricts the discriminator to perceptually meaningful directions, and the logit estimates the log-likelihood ratio between data and model, which is the optimal reward for targeting the data distribution. Across SiT, JiT, REPA, and RAE, DRL reduces guidance-free FID (e.g., $9.38 \to 2.62$ on SiT) and semantic-space FD (e.g., $88.2 \to 19.3$ on DINOv3 for SiT), with consistent gains across all backbones, and improves human-preference rewards without training on them. It also yields a better Pareto frontier between preference reward and image fidelity under subsequent preference-based post-training, increasing alignment while reducing low-level artifacts such as oversaturation and excessive brightness.

</details>


### [246] [Geometric and Stochastic Analysis of Discontinuities in Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19036)
*Tho Tran Huu,Huu-Tuan Nguyen,Thien-Hai Nguyen,Nhat-Tri Ho,Viet-Hoang Tran,Tho Quan,Tan Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对稀疏混合专家（SMoE）架构中由Top-k路由引入的不连续性进行严格的几何与随机分析，证明低阶不连续占主导地位，并提出一种局部平滑机制以增强连续性并提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SMoE中的Top-k专家选择机制使模型映射本质上不连续，在间断曲面附近，任意接近的输入可能激活完全不同的专家集合，导致输出显著变化。理解这些不连续的几何结构与随机行为，对于提升模型鲁棒性至关重要。

Method: 首先按切换事件中并列专家数对不连续进行分类（阶数）；利用测度论切片论证建立增厚不连续曲面的渐近体积估计；通过扩散过程建模输入空间的随机扰动，证明路径首次命中不连续的概率界及停留时间界；基于理论洞察提出一种简单平滑机制，在间断附近软性融合邻近专家。

Result: 低阶不连续集在体积上占主导，高阶不连续仅占极小相对体积；随机路径几乎必然首次命中1阶不连续，并有显式有限时间概率界；提出的平滑机制计算开销小，在语言和视觉任务上不仅增强了SMoE映射的连续性，还提升了经验性能。

Conclusion: 理论表明输入更可能位于低阶不连续附近，据此设计的局部平滑策略能以较小的计算代价有效改善SMoE的连续性与实际表现。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (SMoE) architectures are now widely deployed in state-of-the-art language and vision models, where conditional routing allows scaling to very large networks. However, this very Top-$k$ expert selection that enables conditional routing also renders the SMoE map inherently discontinuous. In the vicinity of these discontinuity surfaces, even inputs that are arbitrarily close may activate substantially different sets of experts resulting in significantly different outputs. In this work we give a rigorous geometric and stochastic analysis of these discontinuities. We first classify them by order, determined by the number of tied experts at a switching event. Using measure-theoretic slicing arguments, we establish asymptotic volume estimates for the thickened discontinuity surfaces, showing that lower-order discontinuity sets dominate, whereas higher-order ones occupy a vanishingly small relative volume. Next, modeling random perturbations in the input space via a diffusion process, we prove that the path eventually encounter a discontinuity, and moreover that the first hit almost surely occurs on an order-1 discontinuity with explicit finite-time probability bounds. We further derive occupation-time bounds that quantify the duration the random path spend in the neighborhoods of each discontinuity order. These theoretical results imply that inputs are more likely to lie near lower order discontinuities. Motivated by this insight, we propose a simple smoothing mechanism that can be directly applied to existing SMoEs, softly incorporating experts near discontinuities; our analysis guarantees that the added computational overhead remains small while providing localized smoothing near discontinuities, and experiments across language and vision tasks show that smoothing not only enforces continuity of the SMoE map but also enhances empirical performance.

</details>


### [247] [Forecasting what Matters: Decision-Focused RL for Controlled EV Charging with Unknown Departure Times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19199)
*Giuseppe Gabriele,Fabio Pavirani,Seyed Soroush Karimi Madahi,Chris Develder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种决策聚焦的强化学习框架（DF-RL），通过端到端联合训练预测器和充电控制器，解决电动汽车智能充电中因缺失关键特征（如离开时间）导致的决策质量下降问题，相比基线方法，总奖励提升14%，未供电能量减少55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动汽车普及给电力系统带来峰值负荷和电网稳定性挑战，基于强化学习的智能充电可缓解此问题，但现实中关键特征（如离开时间）常不可用。现有预测模型以精度为目标训练，其误差会传播并损害下游控制器的决策质量，而非以最终决策效果为导向。

Method: 提出决策聚焦的强化学习框架（DF-RL），将预测器与RL充电策略代理进行端到端联合训练。预测器不仅追求预测精度，更接收来自RL代理充电动作的反馈信号，使预测器训练直接服务于下游决策质量，从而避免预测误差的负面传播。

Result: DF-RL方法在充电决策上显著优于其他基线：总奖励最高提升14%，未供电能量（即因EV已离开而未能完成的充电量）减少55%（相对于无离开时间预测的RL方法）。

Conclusion: 决策聚焦的端到端联合训练范式能有效缓解预测误差对下游决策的负面影响，使RL充电策略在不完整信息条件下仍能做出高质量决策，证明了以决策质量为导向训练预测模块的优越性。

Abstract: The recent growth of EV adoption poses challenges for power systems, including increased peak demand and potential grid instability. Smart control of EV charging -- e.g., based on reinforcement learning (RL) -- can alleviate these issues by learning temporal and contextual patterns from historical data. Yet, in real-world scenarios, key features, such as departure time, often are unavailable. This, in turn, makes it harder for an RL agent to learn and execute an effective charging policy. To mitigate this uncertainty, a trained forecaster can approximate the unknown features from available data. However, since these forecasting models are typically trained for accuracy (rather than their impact on a downstream agent's decision quality), their errors may propagate and hinder the overall performance of a controller that is using the forecasts. To avoid this, we propose a decision-focused RL (DF-RL) framework in which the forecaster is trained end-to-end, i.e., with feedback from the charging policy actions taken by the RL agent. Such joint training of both the forecaster and controller ultimately results in higher-quality actions: our proposed DF-RL method yields superior charging decisions compared to other baselines, achieving up to a 14% improvement in total reward and a 55% reduction of unsupplied energy (i.e., charging that failed to happen because the EV already left), relative to the RL method without departure time forecasting.

</details>


### [248] [Machine Unlearning for the XGBoost Model with Network Intrusion Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19220)
*Diana Magalhães,Eva Maia,João Vitorino,Isabel Praça*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 XGBoost-Forget，一种针对 XGBoost 模型的机器遗忘方法，用于表格型网络入侵检测数据。在两个数据集上验证表明，该方法能在保持预测性能的同时实现高效的遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘研究主要聚焦于深度学习和图像数据，忽略了依赖表格数据的网络入侵检测领域。该领域亟需针对 XGBoost 等表格数据主流模型的遗忘方案，以在不重新训练的情况下移除特定数据点。

Method: 提出 XGBoost-Forget 方法，专门为 XGBoost 模型设计的机器遗忘策略，并在 IoT-23 和 GeNIS 两个表格型网络入侵数据集上进行多维度评估。

Result: XGBoost-Forget 的预测性能接近原始模型（未遗忘前），同时遗忘速度显著快于完全重训练，验证了其在表格型网络入侵检测场景中的遗忘质量与效率。

Conclusion: XGBoost-Forget 填补了表格数据领域机器遗忘的空白，在网络入侵检测中展现出实用潜力，为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Machine Unlearning (MU) has emerged as an important technique for removing specific data points from trained models without requiring full retraining. However, most existing MU research focuses on deep learning and image data, leaving a gap in the domain of network intrusion detection, which relies heavily on tabular data. This work introduces XGBoost-Forget, an unlearning approach for the XGBoost model, to address this gap. The approach is evaluated on two tabular Network Intrusion (NI) datasets, IoT-23 and GeNIS, using multiple metrics to assess model performance, unlearning efficiency, and forgetting quality. The results show that XGBoost-Forget maintains predictive performance close to the original model while providing significantly faster unlearning, demonstrating its potential for MU in tabular NI settings.

</details>


### [249] [JourneyFormer: Encoding Airbnb Guest Journey with Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19108)
*Daochen Zha,Chun How Tan,Xin Liu,Bin Xu,Han Zhao,Xiaowei Liu,Tracy Yu,Hui Gao,Huiji Gao,Liwei He,Stephanie Moyerman,Sanjeev Katariya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了JourneyFormer——Airbnb搜索排序中部署的序列建模方案，详细阐述了在真实生产环境中应对长序列、稀疏预订标签等挑战的设计决策，包括事件选择、ID嵌入、模型架构和标签归因，并提出了加速训练与推理的工程策略。该方案在离线指标和在线A/B测试中均取得了显著的业务收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列建模在推荐和排序中具有建模用户历史行为、推断用户意图的能力，但在实际生产部署中面临两大核心挑战：(1) 用户行为序列长且复杂、具有探索性；(2) 预订标签极为稀疏。因此需要在数据与建模层面做出权衡，兼顾效果与可扩展性。

Method: 提出JourneyFormer序列建模方案，关键设计包括：(1) 客事件选择策略以筛选有效行为；(2) ID嵌入表示设计；(3) 模型架构设计以捕捉序列依赖；(4) 标签归因策略应对稀疏标签问题；(5) 多项针对模型训练和推理的加速优化策略。

Result: JourneyFormer已在Airbnb的生产环境中成功部署，在离线排序指标上表现提升，并在2个产品场景的在线A/B测试中取得了关键业务指标的显著增长。

Conclusion: 序列建模在真实工业场景中的落地需要综合考虑数据选择、模型设计与工程优化，JourneyFormer验证了经过精心设计的序列建模方案能够在稀疏标签、长序列的复杂场景下取得显著的业务效果。

Abstract: Sequence modeling has become increasingly popular in recommendation and ranking algorithms, owing to its capacity to model users' historical behaviors and infer user intentions. Despite its theoretical simplicity, the practical deployment of a sequence model in production is non-trivial due to complexity of the sequence and sparse labels. For example, in Airbnb, guest sequences are often long, exploratory and complex, and we focus on booking labels, which are sparse. As such, we are often required to make various design decisions regarding data and modeling to strike a balance between effectiveness and scalability. This work delved into these production challenges and deployed JourneyFormer, a sequence modeling solution for search ranking at Airbnb. We detail crucial design considerations, covering aspects such as guest event selection, ID embeddings, model architecture, and label attribution. Additionally, we describe several tailored strategies to accelerate model training and inference. JourneyFormer has been successfully deployed within Airbnb's production, where its effectiveness and impact have been evidenced not only by improved offline ranking metrics but also by significant gains in key business metrics through online A/B testing across 2 production surfaces.

</details>


### [250] [Mechanism-Guided Selective Unlearning for RLVR-Induced Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19222)
*Chenyu Zhou,Qiliang Jiang,Shuning Wu,Xu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MAST是一种机制对齐的选择性遗忘方法，通过仅更新注意力投影张量中排名靠前的子集，实现RLVR诱导推理的定向遗忘，同时在GSM8K和MATH保留集上几乎不造成附带损伤，显著优于全参数梯度上升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR（强化学习验证奖励）训练虽能提升数学推理能力，但可能引入不良推理模式。全参数梯度上升进行遗忘会导致严重的附带损伤——破坏保留的MATH和GSM8K性能。因此需要一种低附带损伤的机制引导选择性遗忘方法。

Method: MAST方法：首先分析SFT到RLVR的token级delta-log-probability增量，发现其与SFT更新模式截然不同；然后按三个指标对注意力投影张量进行排序——(1)离主成分能量（off-principal energy）、(2)更新幅度、(3)遗忘梯度耦合幅度；最后仅更新排名靠前的子集参数。在Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B和Qwen3-1.7B-Base的匹配SFT/RLVR检查点上进行实验，对比NPO和SimNPO两种遗忘目标。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B上，MAST实现了统计显著的定向遗忘：目标MATH遗忘量从45/150降至37/150（McNemar检验p=0.0078），同时GSM8K性能提升0.8个百分点，MATH保留集仅下降0.5个百分点。该优势在不同随机种子、NPO/SimNPO目标函数以及Qwen3-1.7B-Base上均得到复现，其中Qwen3上全参数遗忘导致GSM8K崩溃而MAST成功保留。

Conclusion: MAST证明，通过对模型更新进行机制层面的约束——即选择性更新注意力投影张量中基于结构能量、更新幅度和梯度耦合排名靠前的子集——可以在实现有效遗忘的同时将附带损伤降至最低，为RLVR后安全对齐提供了一种实用方案。

Abstract: We propose MAST (Mechanism-Aligned Selective Targeting), a mechanism-guided method for unlearning RLVR-induced reasoning with substantially lower collateral damage than standard full-parameter updates. In matched SFT/RLVR checkpoints on Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B and Qwen3-1.7B-Base, the SFT-to-RLVR increment differs sharply from the SFT update in token-level delta-log-probability, and full-parameter gradient ascent forgets only by damaging retain MATH and GSM8K. MAST ranks attention-projection tensors by off-principal energy, update magnitude, and forget-gradient coupling magnitude, then updates only the top-ranked subset. On the primary model, MAST induces statistically significant target forgetting (MATH forget 45/150 to 37/150; McNemar p=0.0078) while preserving GSM8K (+0.8 pp) and MATH retain (-0.5 pp). The advantage reproduces across seeds, NPO/SimNPO objectives, and Qwen3, where MAST preserves GSM8K while full-parameter unlearning collapses it.

</details>


### [251] [ChronoSurv: A Clinical Pathway-Guided Graph Framework for Multimodal Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19140)
*Hugo Miccinilli,Theo Di Piazza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ChronoSurv 提出了一种基于异构层次有向图的多模态生存分析框架，将患者诊疗过程建模为有向临床轨迹，通过层次化拓扑结构和异构消息传递捕获模态间的复杂关系，在两个公开数据集上取得最优判别性能且校准可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的头颈癌生存预测对个性化治疗至关重要，但多模态临床数据的高维异构特性使该任务极具挑战。现有深度生存模型多采用静态融合策略或时间无关建模，未能捕捉结构化临床工作流中的时序依赖关系。

Method: 提出 ChronoSurv 框架：(1) 将患者诊疗过程建模为与关键诊断步骤对齐的有向图，体现临床进展感知的轨迹；(2) 构建层次化拓扑结构，包含细粒度、粗粒度和全局三种表示尺度，并支持缺失模态的灵活适配；(3) 采用异构消息传递机制，建模不同模态和临床步骤之间的复杂非对称关系。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上，ChronoSurv 在判别性能上达到 state-of-the-art 水平，同时保持统计上可靠的校准性能。消融实验验证了各架构组件的独立贡献。

Conclusion: 轨迹感知的图建模方法在多模态生存预测中具有显著潜力，层次化有向图结构和异构消息传递能有效捕捉临床工作流中的时序与模态间复杂关系。

Abstract: Accurate survival prediction is essential for personalized treatment planning in head and neck cancer, yet remains challenging due to the heterogeneous and high-dimensional nature of multimodal clinical data. While deep survival models have improved predictive performance over classical statistical approaches, existing methods typically rely on static fusion strategies or temporally agnostic modeling, limiting their ability to capture structured clinical workflows. In this work, we propose ChronoSurv, a heterogeneous hierarchical directed graph framework for multimodal survival analysis. ChronoSurv represents patient care as a progression-aware clinical trajectory using directed graphs aligned with key diagnostic steps. A hierarchical topology incorporates fine-grained, coarse, and global representations, further supporting flexible adaptation to missing modalities, while heterogeneous message passing models complex and asymmetric relationships across modalities and clinical steps. Experimental results on two public datasets demonstrate that ChronoSurv achieves state-of-the-art discriminative performance while maintaining statistically reliable calibration. Comprehensive ablation studies further confirm the contribution of each architectural component, highlighting the potential of trajectory-aware graph modeling for multimodal survival prediction.

</details>


### [252] [Explaining Attention with Program Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19317)
*Amiri Hayes,Belinda Li,Jacob Andreas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用可执行的Python程序来近似Transformer模型中注意力头的行为，通过在随机样本上提取注意力矩阵、用预训练语言模型生成候选程序并重排序，实现了用不到1000个程序以超过75%的IoU相似度复现GPT-2、TinyLlama-1.1B和Llama-3B的注意力模式，且替换25%的注意力头仅带来16%的平均困惑度上升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释深度学习的一个核心愿景是用人类可理解的符号化描述取代不透明的神经计算，但现有方法难以将模型组件的行为精确转化为可读、可执行的程序。本文旨在弥合这一鸿沟，为Transformer注意力头提供可扩展的逆向工程管道。

Method: 1) 在随机采样的训练样本上计算目标注意力头的注意力矩阵；2) 将注意力矩阵的摘要信息作为提示，引导预训练语言模型生成一组能复现该注意力模式的Python程序；3) 在留出输入上评估程序预测注意力行为的准确度，据此重排序筛选最优程序；4) 用筛选出的程序代理替换原始注意力头，评估对模型整体性能的影响。

Result: 在TinyStories数据集上，不到1000个生成程序即可复现三个模型（GPT-2、TinyLlama-1.1B、Llama-3B）的注意力头行为，平均IoU相似度超过75%；替换25%的注意力头仅导致平均困惑度上升16%，且在下游问答基准上保持性能。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一条可扩展的、生成人类可读可执行代码的注意力头逆向工程路径，向神经模型的符号透明性迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: A longstanding goal of research on interpretable deep learning is to replace opaque neural computations with human-meaningful symbolic descriptions. In this paper, we propose an approach for approximating the behavior of components of deep networks with executable programs. We focus on attention heads in transformer language models. For a given head, we first compute its associated attention matrices on a collection of randomly selected training examples. Next, we prompt a pre-trained language model with a summary of these matrices, and instruct it to generate a set of Python programs that can reproduce the associated attention patterns given only text from the input sentence. Finally, we re-rank programs according to how well our final set of programs predict behavior on held-out inputs. We demonstrate that a set of fewer than 1,000 such generated programs can reproduce the attention patterns of heads in GPT-2, TinyLlama-1.1B, and Llama-3B, achieving an average Intersection-over-Union similarity above 75% on TinyStories. Moreover, the best-fit programs can replace neural attention heads without substantially affecting model behavior: replacing 25% of attention heads with programmatic surrogates across the three models incurs only a 16% average perplexity increase, while maintaining performance on a variety of downstream question answering benchmarks. This work contributes a scalable pipeline for reverse-engineering attention heads in transformer models using human-readable, executable code, advancing a path toward symbolic transparency in neural models.

</details>


### [253] [Complementary Attention Head Pruning for Efficient Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19150)
*Yaniv Livertovsky,Shahar Somin,Gonen Singer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAHP 是一种无需预定义稀疏率的事后剪枝框架，将注意力头选择重新定义为全局图论问题，通过图聚类与信息论距离度量识别并保留拓扑上互补的注意力头子集，在高压缩场景下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 模型因参数规模庞大难以部署于资源受限环境。现有结构化剪枝方法依赖梯度重要性排序或随机门控，存在不稳定性、结构退化及需要大量手工超参数调优等问题。此外，现有方法往往孤立评估每个注意力头，忽视了注意力头之间的互补性与拓扑多样性。

Method: CAHP 将注意力头选择建模为全局图论问题：(1) 将各注意力头作为图中节点，采用信息论距离度量（如 JS 散度）构建头间相似度图；(2) 通过图聚类识别功能相似的注意力头群组；(3) 从每个聚类中选取代表头，保留拓扑多样的互补子集；(4) 通过检测边际性能递减曲线（以多项式次数刻画）自动确定各层保留头数，无需预设稀疏率。

Result: 在 SST-5 和 MNLI 基准上的实验表明，CAHP 在不同规模的 Transformer 模型上均一致优于竞争基线方法，尤其在高压缩率下优势显著。结构性分析还表明，CAHP 避免了梯度剪枝方法的"邻近偏差"（即倾向保留靠近输出层的头），而是保留了位于模型中间层的功能关键注意力头。

Conclusion: CAHP 通过图论与信息论结合的方式，提供了一种稳定、自动化且结构感知的注意力头剪枝方案，为 Transformer 模型在资源受限场景下的高效部署提供了新路径。

Abstract: The remarkable success of Transformer-based models in natural language processing stems from architectural scaling, which leads to a large number of parameters and hinders deployment in resource-constrained environments. While structured pruning offers a pathway to compression, existing state-of-the-art methods often rely on gradient-based importance ranking or stochastic gating, which suffer from instability, structural degeneration, and the need for extensive manual hyperparameter tuning. In this paper, we introduce CAHP (Complementary Attention Head Pruning), a novel post-hoc framework that redefines head selection as a global graph-theoretical problem. Rather than evaluating heads in isolation, CAHP utilizes graph-based clustering combined with information-theoretic distance measures to identify and preserve a topologically diverse subset of complementary attention heads. Without requiring a predefined sparsity level or pruning ratio, the framework automatically determines the number of selected attention heads across layers by identifying a diminishing marginal performance curve, where pruning additional heads leads to a sharp degradation in performance, as determined by the chosen polynomial degree. Extensive evaluations on the SST-5 and MNLI benchmarks, across different Transformer model scales, demonstrate that CAHP consistently outperforms competitive baselines, particularly in high-compression regimes. Furthermore, our structural analysis shows that CAHP avoids the "proximity bias" of gradient-based pruning methods, which tend to preserve heads mainly in layers close to the output, and instead retains a functionally critical set of attention heads in the model's intermediate layers.

</details>


### [254] [UBP2: Uncertainty-Balanced Preference Planning for Efficient Preference-based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19328)
*Mohamed Nabail,Leo Cheng,Jingmin Wang,Nicholas Rhinehart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于模型的偏好强化学习方法UBP2，通过联合考虑奖励、动力学和价值函数的不确定性来主动引导探索，在元世界基准上展现出显著优于现有方法的样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于偏好的RL方法依赖被动数据收集，尤其在早期学习阶段样本效率低下。需要一种能够主动探索、平衡利用与信息获取的方法来解决这一问题。

Method: UBP2使用奖励、动力学和价值函数的集成模型，通过一个统一的评分（融合期望奖励、终值价值和认知不确定性）来评估候选轨迹。在此目标下进行规划，无需临时探索启发式即可实现利用与信息获取的显式权衡。同时建立了有限时域和无限时域下的次线性遗憾保证。

Result: 在Meta-World基准上，UBP2的样本效率显著优于无模型偏好方法和非乐观的基于模型的基线方法。

Conclusion: UBP2通过基于模型的不确定性平衡规划，有效解决了偏好RL中早期探索效率低下的问题，兼具理论遗憾保证和优异的实证表现。

Abstract: Preference-based RL provides an approach to learning reward models from pairwise comparisons of behaviors, bypassing the need for explicit reward design. However, existing methods typically rely on passive data collection and suffer from poor sample efficiency, especially during the early stages of learning. We introduce a model-based approach that actively directs exploration by jointly reasoning over uncertainties in the reward, dynamics, and value functions. Our method, Uncertainty-Balanced Preference Planning (UBP2), uses ensembles of reward, dynamics, and value function models to evaluate candidate trajectories according to a unified score that combines expected reward, terminal value, and epistemic uncertainty. Planning under this objective yields an explicit tradeoff between exploitation and information acquisition without requiring ad hoc exploration heuristics. Under standard regularity assumptions, we establish sublinear regret guarantees for both finite-horizon and infinite-horizon settings. Empirically, experiments on the Meta-World benchmark show UBP2 achieves substantially higher sample efficiency than model-free preference-based methods and non-optimistic model-based baselines.

</details>


### [255] [AGDN: Learning to Solve Traveling Salesman Problem with Anisotropic Graph Diffusion Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19185)
*Bolin Shen,Ziwei Huang,Zhiguang Cao,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出各向异性图扩散网络（AGDN），通过MixScore转移矩阵融合节点相似性与成对距离，配合各向异性图扩散策略，解决TSP中全连接图缺乏拓扑先验及图稀疏化丢失最优解节点的问题，在多种实例规模和节点分布上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图学习方法求解TSP面临两大核心困难：(1) TSP全连接图缺乏信息性拓扑先验，(2) 常用图稀疏化技术会丢失最优解中的连通节点。如何更有效地利用图结构来解决TSP仍是一个开放问题。

Method: 提出AGDN框架：(1) 构建MixScore转移矩阵，将节点相似性与成对距离信息融合；(2) 开发各向异性图扩散策略，支持跨多跳的高效信息交换。两者协同克服全连接图拓扑先验缺失和稀疏化导致的关键连接丢失问题。

Result: 在多样化实例规模和节点分布的全面实验中，AGDN一致优于现有方法，且计算时间保持竞争力；同时展现出对训练分布以外的泛化能力。

Conclusion: AGDN通过MixScore和各向异性扩散策略有效解决了TSP中图结构利用的两大难题，表现出优越的性能和泛化性，为图神经网络求解组合优化问题提供了新思路。代码已开源。

Abstract: The Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) is a cornerstone of combinatorial optimization and arises in many practical scenarios. Although graph-based learning approaches have been explored for TSP, the question of how to exploit graph structure more effectively remains open. We present the Anisotropic Graph Diffusion Network (AGDN), a new Graph Neural Network framework designed to solve TSP. Our method tackles two central difficulties: (1) the lack of informative topological prior in fully connected TSP graphs, and (2) losing connected nodes in the optimal solution after the commonly used graph sparsification techniques. To overcome these issues, we construct a MixScore transition matrix that merges node similarity with pairwise distance, and we develop an anisotropic graph diffusion strategy that supports efficient information exchange across multiple hops. Comprehensive experiments spanning diverse instance sizes and node distributions show that AGDN consistently outperforms existing methods while keeping computation time competitive. Furthermore, AGDN generalizes well to problem sizes and distributions beyond those seen during training. The implementation is publicly available at: https://github.com/LabRAI/AGDN.

</details>


### [256] [SCAN: Enhance Time Series Anomaly Detection via Multi-Scale Neighborhood-Centered Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19255)
*Xingze Zheng,Hanyin Cheng,Siyuan Wang,Yiting Hao,Peng Chen,Yuan Jun,Yang Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SCAN方法，通过引入多尺度聚类增强重建式异常检测：在表示层面用聚类中心约束模型聚焦正常模式重建，在异常判据层面结合聚类隶属度与重建误差形成双重检测标准，并利用邻域中心表示提升多视图聚类性能，在多个真实数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重建式时间序列异常检测方法存在过度泛化（over-generalization）和泛化不足（under-generalization）两大问题，且二者难以平衡。过度泛化会导致异常也被准确重建而漏检，泛化不足则会将正常波动误判为异常。

Method: SCAN方法包含三个核心设计：(1) 在表示层面，将正常模式的聚类中心表示集成到模型中，约束其聚焦于代表性正常模式进行重建，防止模型强大容量带来的过度泛化；(2) 在异常判据层面，基于聚类隶属度概率推导异常置信度分数，与重建误差结合形成双重检测标准；(3) 提取邻域中心表示（neighborhood-centered representations）进行多视图聚类，以提升聚类性能，从而保障聚类中心表示和异常置信度分数的有效性。

Result: 在来自不同应用领域的多个真实世界数据集上进行广泛实验，结果表明SCAN达到了最先进（state-of-the-art）的性能。

Conclusion: 通过多尺度聚类增强重建式异常检测方法，SCAN有效缓解了过度泛化与泛化不足的平衡难题，其双重判据机制和邻域中心多视图聚类设计显著提升了时间序列异常检测的准确性。

Abstract: Time series anomaly detection plays a crucial role in a wide range of real-world applications. Reconstruction-based methods have become the mainstream paradigm, but they suffer from over-generalization and under-generalization problems, which are challenging to balance. To address this, we introduce multi-scale clustering to enhance reconstruction-based methods. At the representation level, we integrate the cluster center representations of normal patterns to constrain the model to target representative normal patterns for reconstruction, preventing dominance of powerful capacity and representation capability. At the anomaly criterion level, we derive anomaly confidence score based on cluster membership probability and combine it with reconstruction error, providing dual criteria for detection. Furthermore, the effectiveness of the cluster center representations and anomaly confidence score depends on the clustering performance. Accordingly, we extract neighborhood-centered representations for multi-view clustering to improve clustering performance. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets from diverse application domains demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance of SCAN.

</details>


### [257] [Detecting Hidden ML Training With Zero-Overhead Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19262)
*Robi Rahman,Sabiha Tajdari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了基于NVML遥测数据的GPU工作负载分类在对抗攻击下的鲁棒性，在5轮攻防迭代中测试了20种规避策略，分类器对训练工作负载的识别准确率达98.2%，对对抗性伪装的挑战性工作负载仍保持43-87%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI算力治理（compute governance）的许多提案依赖于对GPU工作负载的硬件级监控。然而，若开发者能够规避这些监控机制，治理方案将形同虚设。因此，需要评估仅使用零开销、隐私保护的NVML遥测信号（不访问模型权重、训练数据或超参数）进行GPU工作负载分类的对抗鲁棒性，以验证其在现实治理场景中的可行性。

Method: 采用攻防迭代范式：在5轮"监控者-规避者"对抗迭代中，针对9款GPU（覆盖4代架构），系统评估了20种规避策略族。监控方仅使用NVML遥测信号——即内容无关的物理效应信号（如功耗、温度、利用率等），不触及隐私数据。通过构建分类器，在对抗伪装条件下识别训练工作负载。

Result: 在全量语料库上，分类器对训练工作负载的二元识别准确率达到98.2%；在面对最具挑战性的、经过对抗性伪装的非预期工作负载时，准确率仍维持在43-87%之间。

Conclusion: 仅依赖零开销NVML遥测的GPU工作负载分类器具备较强的对抗鲁棒性，即使在多轮攻防迭代后仍能有效识别经过伪装的工作负载，为AI算力治理中的硬件级监控方案提供了可行性支撑。但面对最强规避策略时准确率下降显著，表明该方向仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Hardware-enabled monitoring of GPU workloads underpins many proposals for AI compute governance, but if developers can defeat monitoring mechanisms, such schemes are unworkable. We evaluate the adversarial robustness of GPU workload classification using only zero-overhead, privacy-preserving NVML telemetry: content-agnostic signals that observe physical effects of computation without accessing model weights, training data, or hyperparameters. Across 5 rounds of monitor-evader iteration, we evaluate 20 evasion strategy families on 9 GPU models spanning 4 architecture generations. We develop a classifier that achieves 98.2% binary accuracy at identifying training workloads across the whole corpus, and 43-87% accuracy against the most challenging unexpected workloads even when they are adversarially disguised.

</details>


### [258] [Risk Stratification for ICU Delirium using Pervasive Ambient Sensing Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19292)
*Jiaqing Zhang,Sabyasachi Bandyopadhyay,Miguel Contreras,Jessica Sena,Yuanfang Ren,Andrea Davidson,Ziyuan Guan,Tezcan Ozrazgat-Baslanti,Subhash Nerella,Azra Bihorac,Parisa Rashidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了ICU环境中的声音和光照能否独立预测谵妄。通过9个ICU、309名患者的数据，使用四种序列神经网络模型评估了10个预测窗口。卷积模型在声音和联合数据上AUC达0.80，声音是主要预测因子，整合光照可改善短期(<1周)预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谵妄是ICU常见且严重的并发症，但早期预测仍然困难。环境因素（如声音和光照）常被忽视，其作为独立预测因子的潜力尚未被充分验证。

Method: 收集9个ICU共309名患者的环境传感数据（光照强度、声压级），使用四种高效序列神经网络模型（含卷积模型），在10个不同预测窗口下预测谵妄发生；采用SHAP分析评估特征重要性及影响方向。

Result: 卷积模型在声音数据和联合数据上均取得AUC=0.80的最佳区分度；声音特征是主导预测因子；声音与光照联合可改善短期（<1周）预测，联合模型在传感期结束后即刻给出最高风险评分。

Conclusion: 被动环境传感（尤其是声音）可为谵妄风险评估提供具有临床意义且可解释的信号，为丰富ICU多模态预测和预防策略提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Delirium is a common and serious complication in the Intensive Care Unit (ICU), associated with increased morbidity, prolonged hospital stays, and higher healthcare costs. Despite its prevalence, early prediction and prevention remain challenging. Environmental factors such as ambient sound and light may influence the onset of delirium, yet they are often overlooked in risk assessments. In this study, we examined whether light intensity and sound pressure levels can independently predict delirium across multiple prediction horizons. We evaluated four efficient sequential neural network models on data collected from 9 ICUs across 309 patients to predict delirium for 10 prediction-window sizes. We reported feature importance and direction of influence using Shapley Additive Explanations analysis. The convolutional model achieved the strongest discrimination, with AUC = 0.80 on sound data and on combined data. Sound features were the dominant predictors overall. Integrating sound with light improved short-term ($<1$ week) prediction, with the combined model assigning the highest risk immediately after the sensing period. These findings suggest that passive ambient sensing, especially sound, can add a clinically meaningful, interpretable signal for delirium risk estimation and offer a practical pathway to enrich multimodal ICU prediction and prevention strategies.

</details>


### [259] [Does VLA Even Know the Basics? Measuring Commonsense and World Knowledge Retention in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19297)
*Nikita Kachaev,Andrey Moskalenko,Matvey Skripkin,Nikita Kurlaev,Daria Pugacheva,Albina Burlova,Mikhail Kolosov,Denis Shepelev,Andrey Kuznetsov,Elena Tutubalina,Aleksandr I. Panov,Alexey K. Kovalev,Vlad Shakhuro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Act2Answer 是一个轻量级评估协议，将 VLM 知识基准转化为 VLA 评估，要求智能体通过物体放置动作来回答知识问题。研究发现 VLA 模型在简单概念上表现尚可，但在丰富语义类别上与源 VLM 存在较大差距，且答案相关信息在 VLA 中间层达到峰值后在上层衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前具身 VLA 模型通过在机器人数据上微调预训练 VLM 获得，但微调后模型保留了多少常识和事实知识尚不明确。在知识敏感任务上的失败存在歧义——可能源于知识缺失，也可能源于底层控制的泛化能力不足。为此需要一种能解耦知识与控制能力的评估方法。

Method: 提出 Act2Answer 协议：(1)将 VLM 知识基准问题转化为桌面操作场景，每个问题对应一个简短的桌面任务，智能体通过单次物体放置动作从候选答案中选择，产生以动作落地为基础的成功率；(2)构建覆盖多种常识和世界知识的测试套件；(3)引入逐层意图探测（layerwise intent probing），在 VLM 骨干网络和动作头中定位与答案相关的信息。

Result: 对 7 个 VLA 模型和 9 个 VLM 基线的大规模研究表明：(1)VLA 在简单概念上表现稳健，但在更丰富的语义类别上与源 VLM 差距更大；(2)VQA 联合训练与更好的知识保留相关联；(3)答案相关信号在 VLA 中间层达到峰值，但在上层衰减。

Conclusion: Act2Answer 成功将知识评估与具身动作桥接，揭示了 VLA 微调过程中的知识保留模式：简单知识保留较好，复杂语义知识丢失较多；中间层是答案信息编码的关键位置，上层信号衰减可能解释了知识-动作对齐的不足。VQA 联合训练可作为一种缓解策略。

Abstract: Embodied Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are typically obtained by fine-tuning powerful pretrained VLMs on robotics data, yet it is unclear how much commonsense and factual knowledge they retain after adaptation. Failures on knowledge-sensitive tasks are ambiguous, conflating missing knowledge with poor generalization of low-level control. We introduce Act2Answer, a lightweight protocol that adapts VLM knowledge benchmarks to VLA evaluation by requiring agents to answer through action. Each question becomes a short tabletop episode where the agent performs a single object-placement action to select among candidate answers, yielding an action-grounded success rate with reduced control confounds. We curate a test suite of such environments across diverse commonsense and world-knowledge categories and introduce layerwise intent probing to localize answer-relevant information across the VLM backbone and action head. In a large-scale study of 7 VLA models and 9 VLM baselines, we systematically rank models across categories, finding that VLAs show solid performance on simple concepts while exhibiting larger gaps on richer semantic categories relative to their source VLMs, that VQA co-training is associated with better knowledge retention, and that answer-relevant signals peak in middle VLA layers but attenuate in upper layers. Act2Answer is available at https://tttonyalpha.github.io/act2answer/.

</details>


### [260] [P-K-GCN: Physics-augmented Koopman-enhanced Graph Convolutional Network for Deep Spatiotemporal Super-resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19303)
*Xizhuo,Zhang,Zekai Wang,Fei Liu,Bing Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理增强的Koopman图卷积网络（P-K-GCN），用于不规则几何体上的时空超分辨率重建。该方法结合了样条GCN的空间特征提取、Koopman算子的非线性动力学线性化，以及物理损失函数的约束，并通过理论分析证明其能降低Rademacher复杂度、收紧泛化界。在3D心脏电生理重建任务上验证了优越精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真时空动力学模拟计算成本过高，传统数据驱动的超分辨率方法缺乏物理约束，而简单的物理信息学习难以处理不规则空间几何和复杂演变的时间动力学。因此需要一种能同时处理不规则几何、融入物理先验、并有效建模非线性时间演化的方法。

Method: （1）设计基于连续样条的GCN，直接从粗粒度图提取空间依赖关系；（2）引入Koopman算子理论，将非线性动力学投影到紧致潜在空间中进行时间线性化演化；（3）在优化目标中增广物理损失项，约束重建结果符合物理定律；（4）提供严格的Rademacher复杂度与泛化界理论分析。

Result: 在3D心脏几何体上从稀疏低分辨率测量重建高分辨率心脏电动力学数据的数值实验中，P-K-GCN相比基线模型取得了更高的重建精度。

Conclusion: P-K-GCN通过物理增强和Koopman正则化有效降低了超分辨率误差，理论上保证泛化能力的提升，并在不规则几何体的时空超分辨率任务上验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: High-fidelity simulation of spatiotemporal dynamics is computationally prohibitive, necessitating efficient super-resolution techniques to reconstruct high-resolution data from coarse-grained inputs. Traditional data-driven methods often lack physical constraints, and simple physics-informed learning struggles with irregular spatial geometries and intricately evolving temporal dynamics. To tackle these challenges, we propose a Physics-augmented Koopman-enhanced Graph Convolutional Network (P-K-GCN) for spatiotemporal super-resolution on irregular geometries. Specifically, a continuous spline-based GCN is first designed to extract spatial dependencies directly from coarse graph, and Koopman operator theory is incorporated to project the nonlinear dynamics into a compact latent space where temporal progression is linearized. Second, we augment the optimization objective with a physics-based loss to force the data-driven reconstructions to adhere to physical laws for improving predictive fidelity and robustness. Finally, we provide a rigorous theoretical analysis, establishing that the physics augmentation and Koopman regularization mathematically guarantees a reduction in super-resolution error by diminishing Rademacher complexity and tightening generalization bounds. We evaluate our framework on reconstructing spatially high-resolution cardiac electrodynamics across a 3D heart geometry from sparse low-resolution measurements. Numerical experiments demonstrate that our method achieves superior accuracy compared to baseline models.

</details>


### [261] [Diffusion-Proof: Recipe for Formal Theorem Proving Beyond Auto-Regressive Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19315)
*Ruida Wang,Rui Pan,Pengcheng Wang,Shizhe Diao,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Diffusion-Proof，首个将扩散大语言模型（dLLM）应用于形式化定理证明的框架,包含 dLLM-Prover-7B（全证明生成）和 dLLM-Corrector-7B（基于大块扩散的局部证明修正）两个模型，在 ProofNet-Test 和 MiniF2F-Test 上分别相对基线提升 1.61% 和 6.14%，并成功解决了一道 DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B 无法求解的 IMO 问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归 LLM 在形式化定理证明中存在长程连贯性不足和长序列错误累积的问题，而扩散 LLM 通过多 token 块迭代去噪生成文本，有望缓解这些缺陷，但其在形式数学领域的应用尚未被探索。

Method: 提出 Diffusion-Proof 框架：(1) dLLM-Prover-7B，使用扩散生成方式进行全证明写作，保持长程连贯的策略使用；(2) dLLM-Corrector-7B，利用 dLLM 的填空能力，基于双向信息进行局部证明修正。两者均在相同数据集下训练。

Result: 在 ProofNet-Test 上绝对提升 1.61%，在 MiniF2F-Test 上绝对提升 6.14%，均显著优于同数据集训练的 AR LLM 基线。此外，成功解决了一道 DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B 无法求解的 IMO 问题。

Conclusion: Diffusion-Proof 验证了扩散大语言模型在形式化定理证明中的独特优势，尤其在需要长程连贯推理和局部修正的场景下显著优于自回归模型，为形式数学推理开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Enhancing the formal math reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) has become a key focus in both mathematical and computer science communities in recent years. While significant progress has been made in using state-of-the-art Auto-Regressive (AR) LLMs for formal theorem proving, these models suffer from inherent limitations. Their next-token prediction generation methods may yield suboptimal performance due to the challenges of long-range coherence and the compounding of errors over long sequences. Recent advancements in diffusion LLMs (dLLMs), which generate text through iterative denoising of a multi-token block, offer a promising alternative. However, the application of dLLMs to formal mathematics, where maintaining long-range coherence is critical, remains largely understudied. To address the challenges above, we propose **Diffusion-Proof**, to the best of our knowledge, the first framework to train and apply dLLMs for formal theorem proving. Our frameworks contain training and inference methods for two models. The first one is *dLLM-Prover-7B*, which performs whole-proof writing with long-range coherent tactic usage. The second one is *dLLM-Corrector-7B*, which is a novel large block diffusion-based correction model. It leverages the in-filling capabilities of dLLMs to perform local proof correction using bi-directional information. Extensive experiments demonstrate that **Diffusion-Proof** relatively significantly outperforms the AR LLM baseline trained under the same dataset. **Diffusion-Proof** achieves an absolute improvement of **1.61%** on ProofNet-Test and **6.14%** on MiniF2F-Test benchmarks compare to the baseline. Notably, **Diffusion-Proof** successfully resolves one IMO problem that more advanced thinking model DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B could not solve, showcasing the unique advantage of dLLMs in formal theorem proving.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [262] [NAVI-Orbital: First In-Orbit Demonstration of a Zero-Shot Vision-Language Model for Autonomous Earth Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18271)
*Juan Manuel Delfa Victoria,Taran Cyriac John,Andrew W. Herson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍NAVI-Orbital系统——首个在轨部署视觉语言模型（Gemma 3）完成自主多模态推理的软件系统。该系统通过自然语言交互实现星上语义压缩，将传统"采集后全部下传"模式逆转为仅下传语义化结果，地面基准测试达88.16%分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测数据生成速度远超下行链路带宽和人工闭环处理能力，星上采集与地面可行情报之间差距日益扩大。亟需将智能处理前移至卫星端，实现星上语义压缩以突破带宽瓶颈。

Method: 部署视觉语言模型Gemma 3于LEO航天器边缘计算机，结合LangGraph图状态机编排检测与对话专用智能体；支持自然语言提示重任务，替代传统指令序列；利用GPU硬件加速推理，无需针对飞行载荷微调，对YAM-9原始未校正图像进行星上处理。

Result: 在7,960张AID基准数据集上达88.16%分类准确率；通过Flatsat验证及在轨实测（含首次获取的未见地球影像），完成星上场景分类、文本描述生成及自然语言对话交互。

Conclusion: 基础模型可在卫星级边缘计算机上运行，通过星上地球观测语义压缩，逆转传统"采集-全量下传"的带宽模式，验证了在轨自主多模态推理的工程可行性。

Abstract: As Earth Observation data generation outpaces downlink bandwidth and human-in-the-loop processing, a widening gap has emerged between onboard collection and actionable ground intelligence. This paper presents NAVI-Orbital, a software system deployed on a Low Earth Orbit (LEO) spacecraft. On April 16, 2026, NAVI-Orbital achieved what is, to the authors' knowledge, the first in-orbit demonstration of a vision-language model performing autonomous multi-modal inference entirely onboard. NAVI-Orbital uses a local vision-language model (Gemma 3) to classify each captured scene, produce a text description of its content and the relationships between its features, and respond to operator follow-up via natural-language dialogue. The system is re-tasked through plain-English prompts in place of conventional command sequences, and is orchestrated by a graph-based state machine (LangGraph) coordinating dedicated agents for detection and dialogue. Results across ground benchmarking (88.16% accuracy on the 7,960-image curated AID benchmark), Flatsat validation, and live in-orbit captures of newly acquired, previously unseen Earth imagery (including uncorrected YAM-9 imagery, processed onboard with hardware-accelerated GPU inference and no fine-tuning for the flight instrument) demonstrate the feasibility of running foundation models on satellite-class edge computers to invert the conventional acquire-then-downlink-everything bandwidth profile through semantic compression of Earth observations in-orbit.

</details>


### [263] [CaVe-VLM-CoT: An Interpretable Vision-Language Model Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18385)
*Sneha Rao,Shaina Raza,Dhanesh Ramachandram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CaVe-VLM-CoT，一个基于反思的模块化agentic-RAG框架，通过五阶段闭环流水线强制视觉语言模型进行证据锚定的推理，在检测到无根据声明时触发定向重检索，并引入包含23个组件指标的评估套件（以CaVeScore为核心）来联合衡量检索质量、逐步引用忠实度和跨模态锚定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM的思维链和检索增强方法仅部分解决幻觉问题，因为它们既不强制步骤级引用锚定，也不将验证失败路由回检索进行纠正，缺乏一个联合评估检索质量、引用忠实度和跨模态锚定的框架。

Method: 提出CaVe-VLM-CoT框架，包含五个模块化阶段：Extractor（提取查询）、Retriever（检索证据）、Solver（生成答案）、Citation Injector（注入引用）、Verifier（验证），形成闭环，无根据声明触发结构化反馈至Extractor进行定向重检索；同时提出23个组件级评估指标和复合指标CaVeScore（加权准确率、引用精度/召回率、归因和证据锚定）。

Result: 在ScienceQA上达到87.1%准确率和56.6% CaVeScore；在MMMU（30个学科）上达到55.2%准确率和35.7% CaVeScore，且无需任何架构或提示修改。

Conclusion: 模块化反思型agentic-RAG框架通过闭环验证与重检索机制有效减轻VLM幻觉，增强步骤级证据锚定，CaVeScore评估套件为多模态推理的可信度提供了更全面的衡量标准。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) remain prone to hallucinations, producing fluent but visually unfaithful outputs. Existing chain-of-thought and retrieval-augmented methods only partially address this, as they neither enforce step-level citation grounding nor route verification failures back to retrieval for correction. We present CaVe-VLM-CoT, a modular reflection-based agentic-RAG framework that enforces evidence-grounded reasoning through a five-stage closed-loop pipeline: Extractor, Retriever, Solver, Citation Injector, and Verifier, in which detected ungrounded claims trigger structured feedback to the Extractor for targeted re-retrieval. Since no existing framework jointly measures retrieval quality, step-wise citation faithfulness, and cross-modal grounding, we propose a suite of 23 component-wise metrics across all stages, anchored by CaVeScore, a composite metric weighting accuracy, citation precision and recall, attribution, and evidence grounding. Without any architectural or prompt modifications, CaVe-VLM-CoT achieves 87.1\% accuracy and 56.6\% CaVeScore on ScienceQA , and 55.2\% accuracy and 35.7\% CaVeScore on MMMU (30 subjects).

</details>


### [264] [Searching for Synergy in Shared Workspace Human-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18413)
*Nachiket Kotalwar,Rohini Das,Carolyn Rose*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究共享工作空间中人-AI团队的协调问题，发现单纯增加协作者可能导致过程损失（协调开销），而引入共享群体记忆与模拟人机回路审批门的支架机制能提升团队绩效，尤其对三人团队效果显著，表明协调与整合专业能力的方式与可用能力本身同等重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动AI代理能力日益增强，但许多科学和专业任务仍需人类判断与情境专业知识。现有研究多关注单个AI或人机交互，而对共享工作空间中多个人与AI代理如何有效协调责任、避免过程损失缺乏深入理解。

Method: 使用Collaborative Gym环境配合DiscoveryBench任务，在1,482次会话中考察：1）添加模拟人类协作者对团队绩效的影响；2）引入两种支架机制——共享群体记忆（shared group memory）和模拟人机回路审批门（simulated HITL gates），即特定行动需经指定参与者批准。通过对比不同团队规模和有无支架条件下的绩效表现进行分析。

Result: 1）缺乏协调结构时，添加相关协作者反而降低绩效，产生过程损失；2）共享群体记忆与HITL审批门的支架机制带来更高平均绩效，尤其在三人团队中效果最明显；3）支架机制使责任信号更清晰，专业能力更有效地路由到相应团队行动中。

Conclusion: 人-AI团队中，如何协调与整合专业能力与团队可用的能力本身同等重要。仅增加协作者而不提供协调结构可能导致反效果，而设计良好的支架机制能显著提升团队协作效能。

Abstract: Automated AI agents are increasingly capable, yet many scientific and professional tasks require human judgment and contextual expertise. We study shared-workspace human-AI teams, where AI agents and human collaborators must coordinate responsibilities before submitting a final answer. Using the Collaborative Gym environment with DiscoveryBench tasks, we examine when adding simulated human collaborators improves performance and when process loss turns additional collaborators into coordination overhead. Across 1,482 sessions, adding relevant collaborators can lower performance when teams lack structure to coordinate their contributions. We then evaluate scaffolding that combines shared group memory with simulated human-in-the-loop (HITL) gates, where selected actions require approval from a designated simulated participant. This scaffolding yields higher mean performance, most clearly in three-person teams, with clearer responsibility signals and stronger routing of expertise to team actions. Overall, how human-AI teams coordinate and integrate expertise matters as much as the capability available to them.

</details>


### [265] [CEO-Bench: Can Agents Play the Long Game?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18543)
*Haozhe Chen,Karthik Narasimhan,Zhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CEO-Bench 是一个评估语言模型智能体长期经营管理能力的基准测试，模拟运营初创公司500天。智能体需通过Python接口处理定价、营销、预算等多方面业务决策，应对噪声环境、不确定性以及多目标协调等挑战。即便是最强模型（Claude Opus 4.8 和 GPT-5.5）也仅能勉强保本，难以持续盈利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型智能体在孤立的短周期任务上表现日益成熟，但现实世界中的复杂挑战要求智能体同时具备长周期规划、噪声环境下的信息获取、动态世界的适应能力以及多模块协调等高级技能。这些能力在现有基准测试中未被充分检验，因此需要一个新的综合评估框架。

Method: 提出 CEO-Bench 基准，模拟一个代表性现实任务——运营一家初创公司500天。智能体通过可编程 Python 接口管理公司的定价、营销、预算等多个业务维度，与人类CEO面临相同的模拟环境和挑战。评估涵盖分析含噪声的互联业务数据库、将信号转化为战略决策、以及通过编程协调大量决策的能力。

Result: 最强智能体能够编写复杂代码来模拟客户群体以预测未来现金流，并挖掘谈判历史以发现隐藏的客户偏好。然而，大多数最先进模型在此环境中仍表现挣扎：仅 Claude Opus 4.8 和 GPT-5.5 的最终资产高于100万美元的初始余额，且两者均未能持续盈利。

Conclusion: CEO-Bench 是衡量智能体驱动持续、自适应长期进展所需智能的初步尝试。结果表明，即便当前最强的大模型在需要综合多种复杂技能的长期经营任务中仍显著不足，揭示了通往通用人工智能道路上的关键能力缺口。

Abstract: Language model agents are becoming proficient executors at isolated, short-horizon tasks such as software engineering and customer service. Yet real-world challenges require a combination of sophisticated skills that remain largely untested in agents: (1) navigating long horizons amid uncertainty; (2) acquiring information in noisy environments; (3) adapting to a changing world; (4) orchestrating multiple moving parts toward a coherent goal. We introduce CEO-Bench, which evaluates these capabilities together by simulating a representative real-world task: operating a startup for 500 days. An agent manages pricing, marketing, budgeting, and many other aspects of a fictional company through a programmable Python interface, operating in the same environment and facing the same challenges as a human CEO. Success demands analyzing noisy, interconnected business databases, translating signals into sound strategy, and coordinating many decisions with programming. The strongest agents write sophisticated code that simulates customer cohorts to forecast future cash and mines negotiation history to uncover hidden customer preferences. Even so, most state-of-the-art models struggle in this environment. Only Claude Opus 4.8 and GPT-5.5 finish above the $1M starting balance, and neither consistently turns a profit. CEO-Bench takes a first step toward measuring the intelligence required to drive sustained, adaptive progress over time.

</details>


### [266] [DeFAb: A Verifiable Benchmark for Defeasible Abduction in Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18557)
*Patrick Cooper,Alvaro Velasquez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeFAb是一个基于规则逻辑的可废止溯因推理基准，包含超37万实例，源自18个知识库的数十年积累。符号求解器在50微秒内以100%准确率解决所有实例，而前沿语言模型最多仅达65%，在渲染鲁棒评估中骤降至23.5%。论文还发布了DeFAb-Hard困难变体和CONJURE创造性变体，揭示当前模型缺乏真正的可废止推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在流畅文本生成中可能破坏逻辑一致性（"theory-destroying prose"），缺乏严谨的理论修正能力。作者旨在以逻辑严格性为工具，精确测量模型的创造力和理论推理能力，尤其是可废止溯因（defeasible abduction）——即通过覆盖默认规则来解释异常、同时保持无关预期不变的能力。

Method: 构建DeFAb数据集与生成流水线：将四十年公开资助的知识库（OpenCyc、YAGO、Wikidata的分类层次，以及ConceptNet、UMLS的行为属性图）转换为形式化可废止溯因实例。每个假设必须通过多项式时间可验证的三重检查：有效推导（valid derivation）、保守性（conservativity）和最小性（minimality）。共生成372,648+实例，涵盖33.75M具体化规则、18个来源、三个难度层级。同时发布DeFAb-Hard（235实例的难度变体）和CONJURE（560个Lean 4/Mathlib实例，答案为目标定义中原本不包含的新概念，由内核验证）。

Result: 基于规则的符号求解器在50微秒内100%准确解决所有基准实例。四个前沿模型未能可靠内化可废止推理：渲染鲁棒的Level 2准确率仅为7.8-23.5%；思维链方差（~36个百分点）超过任何模型间差距；匹配污染控制实验隔离出Level 3上+19.4个百分点的差距。DeFAb-Hard上最佳模型达53.3% vs 符号方法的100%。CONJURE初步实验发现零个新概念。验证器可直接用作DPO和RLVR/GRPO偏好优化的精确奖励信号。

Conclusion: 当前前沿语言模型缺乏真正的可废止推理能力，无法可靠地进行有纪律的理论修正。DeFAb通过逻辑严格性为衡量创造力和理论推理提供了可扩展的基准，其验证器还可作为偏好优化的精确奖励。数据与代码以MIT协议开源。

Abstract: A rule-based logic solver resolves every instance in our benchmark in under 50 microseconds with 100% accuracy; the best frontier language model reaches 65% at best and drops to 23.5% under rendering-robust evaluation (worst case over four surface renderings). We introduce DeFAb (Defeasible Abduction Benchmark), a dataset and generation pipeline that converts four decades of publicly funded knowledge bases into formally grounded instances for defeasible abduction: constructing hypotheses that explain anomalies by overriding defaults while preserving unrelated expectations. Because every hypothesis must pass polynomial-time checks for valid derivation, conservativity, and minimality, DeFAb makes logical rigor the instrument for measuring creativity and theoretical reasoning, scoring the disciplined construction of theory revisions rather than fluent but theory-destroying prose. The pipeline pairs taxonomic hierarchies (OpenCyc, YAGO, Wikidata) with behavioral property graphs (ConceptNet, UMLS) to produce 372,648+ instances across 33.75M materialized rules from 18 sources, in three levels with polynomial-time verifiable gold standards. Four frontier models do not reliably internalize defeasible reasoning: rendering-robust Level 2 accuracy is 7.8-23.5%; chain-of-thought variance (~36 pp) exceeds any inter-model gap; and a matched contamination control isolates a +19.4 pp Level 3 gap. We further release DeFAb-Hard (a 235-instance Level 3 difficulty variant; best model 53.3% vs 100% symbolic) and CONJURE (a kernel-verified transformative-creativity variant of 560 Lean 4/Mathlib instances whose gold answers are definitions the proof kernel did not previously contain, judge-free verifier; a pilot finds zero novel concepts). The same verifier doubles as an exact reward for preference optimization (DPO, RLVR/GRPO). Released under MIT at https://huggingface.co/datasets/PatrickAllenCooper/DeFAb.

</details>


### [267] [Optimizing Lithium Production Decisions under Geological, Demand, and Pricing Uncertainties: A POMDP Framework for Multi-Objective Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18598)
*Anna C. Edmonds,Mansur M. Arief,Robert J. Moss,Mykel J. Kochenderfer,Jef Caers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）框架，用于在价格、需求和开采技术三重不确定性下优化锂矿开采决策，结果表明POMDP求解器在多种价格情景下均优于人类启发的启发式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有锂矿开采决策模型未同时考虑价格不确定性、需求不确定性以及不同开采技术（如直接锂提取、硬岩开采）的选择问题，导致决策策略的鲁棒性不足。

Method: 将锂矿开采决策建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），采用信念状态规划方法求解，整合了多种定价模型（静态、线性、指数、随机）和不同开采技术，实现勘探、生产和技术选择的最优序贯决策。

Result: POMDP求解器在所有定价情景（静态、线性、指数、随机）和矿床情景下，均通过信念状态规划和显式不确定性管理，动态适应锂价格机制的变化，在需求满足度和经济-环境平衡方面均优于人类启发的启发式方法。

Conclusion: 基于POMDP的信念状态规划方法能够为锂矿开采提供更鲁棒的最优决策策略，有效应对价格、需求和技术多重不确定性，实现更高需求满足度和更均衡的经济环境效益。

Abstract: Decision making in lithium production is challenging, whether from an investor's perspective or a strategic production standpoint. Determining which mines to open and when to open them involves not only geological and price uncertainties, but also complexities around the choice of extraction method, from direct lithium extraction to hard rock mining. Prior work explored models of this problem and different methods to optimize mining decisions; these models did not account for uncertainty in pricing, uncertainty in demand, or different mining technologies to extract lithium. Incorporating different pricing models and extraction technology into these models enables more robust strategies for determining not only when and where to open a mine, but also which method of production to pursue. We frame the problem as a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP) and solve using belief state planning methods to get optimal decision making. In our study, we show that POMDP solvers outperform human inspired heuristics by dynamically adapting to shifting lithium price regimes (static, linear, exponential, and stochastic) through belief state planning and explicit uncertainty management. By optimally sequencing exploration, production, and technology choice, the framework achieves higher demand fulfillment and more balanced economic environmental outcomes over the projects lifetime in all different pricing and deposit scenarios.

</details>


### [268] [ForecastBench-Sim: A Simulated-World Forecasting Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18686)
*Jaeho Lee,Nick Merrill,Ezra Karger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ForecastBench-Sim 是一个基于 Freeciv 游戏模拟的预测基准，通过可控的模拟世界生成可立即解决的预测问题，包括连续/二元问题、干预世界中的因果问题以及稀有事件，旨在补充真实世界预测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的预测基准存在结果解析缓慢、尾部事件稀有、反事实问题难以评分等固有约束。需要一个可控、可立即解析的模拟环境来研究动态世界状态下的概率推理。

Method: 基于回合制策略游戏 Freeciv 构建模拟世界管道：从游戏 rollout 中生成固定的世界报告（结构化游戏状态快照），预测者据此回答关于隐藏未来状态的问题；随后继续模拟并评分。支持连续/二元问题、任意时间范围、干预世界配对用于条件或因果问题，以及稀有或破坏性结果的已解析示例。

Result: 报告了基准管道、问题族、评分协议和发布工件，并提供了模型评估的验证切片和匿名人类试点的结果。

Conclusion: ForecastBench-Sim 作为真实世界预测基准的补充，为研究动态世界状态下的概率推理提供了可控、可立即解析的任务环境。

Abstract: Forecasting benchmarks for general-purpose AI systems usually inherit the constraints of the real world: outcomes resolve slowly, tail events are rare, and counterfactual questions are difficult to score. We introduce ForecastBench-Sim, a simulated-world forecasting benchmark built on game rollouts from Freeciv, a turn-based strategy game modelled on the Civilization series. Forecasters receive a fixed world report (a structured snapshot of the current game state) and answer questions about hidden future states; the benchmark then continues the simulation and scores forecasts. Because the world is simulated, the same setup can generate continuous or binary forecasting questions at arbitrary time horizons, paired intervention worlds for conditional or causal questions, and resolved examples of rare or disruptive outcomes. We describe the benchmark pipeline, question families, scoring protocol, and release artifacts, and report validation slices from model evaluations and an anonymized human pilot. ForecastBench-Sim is intended to complement real-world forecasting benchmarks by providing controlled, immediately resolvable tasks for studying probabilistic reasoning under dynamic world states.

</details>


### [269] [What Must Generalist Agents Remember?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18746)
*Khurram Yamin,Namrata Deka,Maitreyi Swaroop,Albert Ting,Jeff Schneider,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从形式化角度证明，通用智能体要在多环境多目标下接近最优地行动，必须在记忆中存储领域相关信息。当两个领域共享观测瓶颈但要求不相容的最优动作时，任何一致近优策略必然在该瓶颈处产生可区分的记忆分布。进一步，若记忆包含足够信息来估计相关目标的价值，则该记忆可近似重建智能体的局部转移动态。由此，记忆被刻画为支持领域消歧、转移模型重建和规划的基底层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用智能体需要在多个不同环境和目标下表现良好，但不同领域可能共享相同的即时观测却要求不同的最优动作（观测瓶颈）。研究者试图从理论层面回答：智能体的记忆必须存储什么信息，才能实现跨领域近优行为？

Method: 采用形式化分析和信息论方法。首先定义通用智能体在多环境多目标下的近优策略框架；然后证明分离定理：当两个领域存在观测瓶颈且最优动作不相容时，任何一致近优策略必然在瓶颈处诱导出可区分的记忆分布；进一步建立记忆信息量与转移模型重建之间的等价关系。

Result: （1）分离定理：足够成功的智能体不能仅依赖当前状态观测，必须在记忆中保留领域相关信息；（2）记忆-模型重建定理：若记忆包含足够信息来估计相关目标的价值，则该记忆可用于近似重建局部转移动态；（3）综合刻画记忆作为领域消歧、转移模型重建和规划的基础支撑。

Conclusion: 记忆是通用智能体的核心基础设施，其功能远超简单的历史记录——它为领域消歧提供信号、为转移模型重建提供信息源、为规划提供支撑。该理论为设计和理解通用智能体的记忆机制提供了形式化基础。

Abstract: This paper develops a formal account of what generalist agents must store in memory in order to act near-optimally across multiple environments and goals. It shows that when two domains share an observational bottleneck but require incompatible optimal actions, any uniformly near-optimal policy must induce distinct memory distributions at that bottleneck. The result yields a separation theorem: sufficiently successful agents cannot rely only on current state observations, but must preserve domain-relevant information in memory. The paper further shows that if an agent's memory contains enough information to estimate values for related goals, then that memory can be used to approximately reconstruct the agent's local transition dynamics. Together, these results characterize memory as the substrate that supports domain disambiguation, transition-model reconstruction, and planning for generalist agents.

</details>


### [270] [R2D-RL: A RoboCup 2D Soccer Environment for Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18786)
*Haobin Qin,Baofeng Zhang,Hidehisa Akiyama,Keisuke Fujii*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: R2D-RL是一个将RoboCup 2D足球仿真平台与Python多智能体强化学习工作流相连接的强化学习环境，通过共享内存通信和周期级同步实现，支持多种动作空间、奖励塑形和并行执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RCSS2D是成熟的机器人足球平台，但其面向竞赛的服务器-客户端架构难以直接与现代Python MARL工作流集成，存在接口不兼容、使用门槛高等问题。

Method: 通过共享内存通信和周期级同步机制，将RCSS2D服务器与基于HELIOS的球员客户端桥接到Python MARL接口；支持全场和场景化训练、可配置对手、Base离散和Hybrid参数化动作空间、动作掩码、基于预期控球价值(EPV)的奖励塑形以及并行执行。

Result: 提供了前场进球场景和11对11全场基准测试，并给出了基线实验结果。

Conclusion: R2D-RL成功弥合了RCSS2D与传统Python MARL框架之间的鸿沟，为机器人足球领域的多智能体强化学习研究提供了标准化、易用的环境。

Abstract: Robot soccer is a challenging testbed for multi-agent reinforcement learning because it combines partial observability, cooperative and adversarial interaction, sparse rewards, and long-horizon tactical behavior. RoboCup 2D Soccer Simulation (RCSS2D) provides a mature robot-soccer platform, but its competition-oriented server-client architecture is difficult to use directly with modern Python-based MARL workflows. We introduce R2D-RL, a reinforcement learning environment that connects RCSS2D and HELIOS-based player clients to a Python MARL interface through shared-memory communication and cycle-level synchronization. R2D-RL supports full-field and scenario-based training with configurable opponents, Base discrete and Hybrid parameterized action spaces, action masks, expected possession value (EPV)-based reward shaping, and parallel execution. We provide front-goal scenarios and an 11-vs-11 full-field benchmark, together with baseline results.

</details>


### [271] [ProfiLLM: Utility-Aligned Agentic User Profiling for Industrial Ride-Hailing Dispatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18803)
*Tengfei Lyu,Zirui Yuan,Xu Liu,Kai Wan,Zihao Lu,Li Ma,Hao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProfiLLM是一个面向网约车派单场景的智能LLM数据管道，通过工具增强的全局知识挖掘和效用对齐的用户画像探索两模块，将LLM生成的用户画像特征引入生产级匹配系统，在滴滴平台实现GMV、完单率等核心指标的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级网约车派单系统长期依赖结构化数值特征，但关键行为信号（如司机对特定区域的习惯性厌恶）本质上是语境化的，适合以LLM生成用户画像表达。现有挑战在于：(1)平台日订单量远超LLM上下文窗口；(2)长尾用户交互稀疏，无法逐用户画像；(3)表面流畅的画像未必提升下游预测效用。这三重约束在此前未被同时解决。

Method: 提出ProfiLLM，包含两个核心模块：(1)工具增强的全局知识挖掘——为LLM Agent配备27种分析工具，挖掘平台级数据，产出可复用的全局知识、自适应用户聚类规则及区域级供需先验；(2)效用对齐的画像探索——为每个聚类生成多个候选画像，通过轻量下游效用代理评估，迭代优化最优候选，并构建偏好对进行DPO微调。

Result: 在滴滴生产派单系统中，ProfiLLM在结果预测任务上取得最高+6.14%相对AUC提升；在派单模拟中实现最高+4.35% GMV增益；在14天在线A/B测试中，GMV提升+0.47%，完单率提升+0.33%，接单前取消率下降-0.82%。

Conclusion: ProfiLLM证明将LLM作为语义特征提取器嵌入工业级派单系统是可行且有效的，通过全局知识挖掘解决规模问题、聚类解决长尾问题、效用对齐解决画像质量与下游任务脱节问题，为LLM在数据系统中的落地提供了新范式。

Abstract: Bringing Large Language Models (LLMs) into industrial ride-hailing dispatch as semantic feature extractors over platform-scale behavioral logs is a compelling but under-explored data systems problem. Production matching pipelines remain dominated by structured numerical features, yet decisive behavioral signals (e.g., a driver's habitual aversion to certain regions) are inherently contextual and naturally expressible as LLM-generated user profiles. However, scaling such profiling to a live, millisecond-latency dispatcher faces three intertwined constraints rarely addressed together: on a platform with millions of daily orders, logs exceed any LLM's context window by orders of magnitude; most users are long-tail, with too few interactions for per-user profiling; and surface-fluent profiles do not necessarily improve downstream prediction utility. We present ProfiLLM, an agentic LLM data pipeline that operationalizes utility-aligned user profiling for production matching systems through two modules. (1) Tool-Augmented Global Knowledge Mining equips an LLM agent with 27 analytical tools to mine platform-scale data, producing reusable global knowledge, adaptive user clustering rules, and region-level supply-demand priors. (2) Utility-Aligned Profile Exploration generates multiple candidate profiles per cluster, evaluates them via a lightweight downstream utility proxy, iteratively refines the best candidates and constructs preference pairs for DPO fine-tuning. Deployed on DiDi's production dispatcher, ProfiLLM achieves up to +6.14% relative AUC improvement in outcome prediction, up to +4.35% GMV gain in dispatching simulation, and consistent improvements in a 14-day online A/B test including +0.47% GMV, +0.33% Completion Rate, and -0.82% Cancel-Before-Accept rate.

</details>


### [272] [WorldLines: Benchmarking and Modeling Long-Horizon Stateful Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18847)
*Yehang Zhang,Jianchong Su,Haojian Huang,Yifan Chang,Tianhao Zhou,Xinli Xu,Yingjie Xu,Yinchuan Li,Zexi Li,Ying-Cong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 WorldLines 基准和 ObsMem 框架，评估具身代理在长期家庭辅助中的记忆能力，揭示部分可观测性和状态覆盖等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆基准侧重语言检索，具身基准则聚焦短期任务执行，缺乏在动态环境中测试长期记忆使用的评测。需要填补这一空白，评估具身代理在真实家庭场景中跨时间尺度的记忆与规划能力。

Method: 构建 WorldLines 基准，包含带时间戳的家庭轨迹（对话、动作、反馈、设备和物体状态变化），转化为 Memory QA 和 Embodied Task Planning 两类证据链接样本；提出 ObsMem 框架，维护可见性感知记忆和原生动作状态轨迹，支持状态感知决策。

Result: 实验表明，在部分可观测性、覆盖式世界状态更新、以及将长期记忆转化为具身计划等方面存在持续挑战；ObsMem 在该场景下提供了更强的参考架构。

Conclusion: WorldLines 首次将长期记忆评估与具身任务规划系统结合，揭示了当前方法的关键瓶颈，ObsMem 为未来研究提供了更优的架构参考。

Abstract: To assist humans over extended periods in real homes, embodied agents must remember user routines, world states, and past interactions. Existing long-term memory benchmarks mainly evaluate language-centric retrieval and question answering, while embodied benchmarks often focus on short-horizon task execution without testing long-term memory use in dynamic environments. We introduce WorldLines, a project-driven benchmark for long-horizon embodied household assistance. It constructs temporally extended household traces with dialogues, actions, execution feedback, object and device state changes, and converts them into evidence-linked samples for Memory QA and Embodied Task Planning. We further propose ObsMem, an observer-grounded memory framework that maintains visibility-aware memories and action-native state trails for state-aware decisions. Experiments reveal persistent challenges in partial observability, overwritten world states, and translating long-term memory into embodied plans, while ObsMem offers a stronger reference architecture for this setting.

</details>


### [273] [Externalizing Research Synthesis and Validation in AI Scientists through a Research Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18874)
*Zijian Wang,Hanqi Li,Ziyue Yang,Zijian Hu,Shenghan Zuo,Yunzhe Zhang,Da Ma,Danyu Luo,Chenrun Wang,Jing Peng,Tiancheng Huang,Sijia Guo,Huayang Wang,Zichen Zhu,Senyu Han,Yilu Cao,Kai Yu,Lu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Xcientist 是一个将科研合成与实验验证外化为可审查、受合约约束的流程框架，通过持久化研究产物来防止"声明漂移"，确保 AI 科研过程可追溯、可归因和科学上可问责。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 AI 系统虽能自动化科研工作流，但连接先验证据、生成想法、实验和最终结论的推理链往往隐式存在于模型推理中，缺乏透明性。此外，自动化研究中存在"声明漂移"（claim drift）的失败模式，即最终可运行产物不再支撑最初声称的机制。因此需要一种将推理过程外化并持久化的框架。

Method: 提出 Xcientist 框架，将文献证据、想法状态、实现计划、消融记录和修复轨迹组织为持久化的研究产物。整个过程受"合约"（contract）约束，实现生成的机制能被 grounded、执行、测试和修订，同时不丢失其证据基础。在三个领域验证：无训练记忆系统、图结构交通预测、多尺度物理信息神经网络。

Result: 在三个不同领域中，Xcientist 成功保持了从问题形成到机制设计、验证和有界修订的可追溯轨迹，有效防止了声明漂移。

Conclusion: AI 科学家的评估不应仅看最终产物，还应考察其合成与验证过程是否保持可归因、可审查和科学上可问责。Xcientist 为实现这一目标提供了可行的工程方案。

Abstract: AI systems can increasingly automate scientific workflows, but the reasoning that links prior evidence, generated ideas, experiments and final claims often remains implicit inside model inference. Here we introduce Xcientist, a research harness that externalizes research synthesis and experimental validation into inspectable, contract-governed processes. Xcientist organizes literature evidence, idea states, implementation plans, ablation records and repair traces as persistent research artifacts, so that generated mechanisms can be grounded, executed, tested and revised without losing their evidential basis. We identify claim drift as a failure mode of automated research, where runnable artifacts no longer support the mechanism originally claimed. Across training-free memory systems, graph-structured traffic forecasting and multi-scale physics-informed neural networks, Xcientist preserves traceable trajectories from problem formulation to mechanism design, validation and bounded revision. These results suggest that AI scientists should be evaluated not only by their final artifacts, but by whether their synthesis and validation processes remain attributable, inspectable and scientifically accountable.

</details>


### [274] [Generative-Model Predictive Planning for Navigation in Partially Observable Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18888)
*Thomas Quilter,Yifan Zhu,Guorui Quan,Mingfei Sun,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BeliefDiffusion 是一种结合扩散模型与模型预测控制（MPC）的框架，通过扩散模型显式建模多模态信念分布，并利用 MPC 在聚合的环境配置上进行前瞻规划，从而在部分可观测导航任务中显著优于无模型强化学习和现有生成式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观测环境中的导航面临感知混叠（perceptual aliasing）问题，导致信念空间呈现多模态特性。现有基于神经网络近似信念的方法难以捕获这种多模态性；而生成式方法虽能表征多模态分布，却缺乏显式的长期规划机制，且往往依赖大量数据或专家示范。本文旨在填补这一空白，将生成建模与规划能力有机融合。

Method: 提出 BeliefDiffusion 框架，分两步：（1）基于历史观测，利用扩散模型生成多个可信的环境配置（即想象可能的世界状态），显式表征多模态信念分布；（2）在聚合后的环境配置上，采用模型预测控制（MPC）进行前瞻规划，生成高效的导航策略。扩散模型负责信念空间的生成式建模，MPC 负责在信念空间上进行滚动时域优化。

Result: 在合成地图环境中进行了大量实验。BeliefDiffusion 在导航成功率和路径效率两项指标上，均显著优于无模型强化学习基线及现有其他生成式导航方法，验证了将多模态信念表征显式融入规划流程的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式建模多模态信念分布并将其纳入规划框架，能够显著提升部分可观测环境下的导航鲁棒性。BeliefDiffusion 为信念空间的生成式建模与基于模型的规划提供了统一范式，在部分可观测导航领域展现出优越的性能。

Abstract: Navigation in partially observable environments presents a significant challenge for autonomous agents, requiring effective decision-making with limited sensory information in unknown environments. Belief-based methods, particularly those using neural networks to approximate the belief space, often fail to capture the inherent multimodality of belief spaces, especially in high-dimensional cases with perceptual aliasing. While generative models present a compelling alternative, they typically require substantial data or expert demonstrations and lack explicit mechanisms for long-term planning. In this paper, we introduce BeliefDiffusion, a novel framework that combines the benefits of both generation and planning. BeliefDiffusion leverages diffusion models to explicitly characterize multimodal belief distributions and utilizes Model Predictive Control (MPC) to simultaneously plan ahead. It consists of two steps: (1) Imagining plausible environment configurations based on observation history and (2) Planning efficient navigation strategies across an aggregated configurations. Through extensive experiments in synthetic map environments, we demonstrate that BeliefDiffusion significantly outperforms both model-free reinforcement learning baselines and other generative approaches in navigation success rate and path efficiency. Our results validate that explicitly incorporating multimodal belief representations into planning enables more robust navigation in partially observable settings.

</details>


### [275] [Skill-Guided Continuation Distillation for GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18890)
*Zhimin Fan,Hongwei Yu,Yeqing Shen,Haolong Yan,Guozhen Peng,Tianhao Peng,Yudong Zhang,Xiaowen Zhang,Kaijun Tan,Zheng Ge,Xiangyu Zhang,Daxin Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对GUI智能体在闭环执行中遇到专家轨迹之外的状态而缺乏监督的问题，提出技能引导的延续蒸馏框架(SGCD)。该框架通过技能引导策略从偏离轨迹的状态中生成成功延续，与专家轨迹混合以填补监督空白，将三种基座模型在OSWorld-Verified上的成功率从30%左右提升至50%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前GUI智能体训练主要依赖行为克隆专家轨迹，但闭环执行时策略偏离会导致进入专家轨迹之外的状态（策略诱导的离轨状态）。这些状态在专家演示中无对应示范，因而缺乏有效监督，导致策略无法选择正确动作，形成监督缺口。

Method: 提出SGCD迭代自改进框架：(1)先用无技能引导的普通策略运行若干步，达到真实的离轨状态；(2)从这些状态出发，由技能引导策略完成任务并生成成功延续轨迹；(3)将这些延续与专家轨迹混合，为离轨状态提供监督。技能从成功和失败回滚中提取，包括延续计划、关键目标、失败陷阱和成功标准四类。

Result: 在OSWorld-Verified基准上，SGCD将三种基座模型的成功率从低30%区间提升至超过50%，验证了方法的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: SGCD通过技能引导的迭代自改进机制，有效弥合了GUI智能体在闭环执行中的监督缺口，显著提升了任务成功率，且对多种基座模型均有效，展现了良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Improving GUI agents typically relies on behavior cloning on expert trajectories. However, as the current policy deviates from the expert policy, it inevitably encounters policy-induced off-trajectory states during closed-loop execution, i.e., states that fall outside the expert trajectories. Since expert trajectories provide no demonstrations for these unseen states, such states receive no effective supervision, leaving the policy unable to select the correct action. To close this supervision gap, we propose Skill-Guided Continuation Distillation (SGCD), an iterative self-improvement framework. SGCD first runs the plain policy without skill guidance for a few steps to reach realistic off-trajectory states. From these states, a skill-guided policy then completes the task and produces successful continuations, which are mixed with expert trajectories to supply supervision over policy-induced off-trajectory states. The skills are extracted from both successful and failed rollouts, consisting of Continuation Plans, Critical Targets, Failure Traps, and Success Criteria. On OSWorld-Verified, SGCD improves the success rate of three base models from the low-30\% range to over 50\%, demonstrating its effectiveness and generality.

</details>


### [276] [SciRisk-Bench: A Risk-Dimension-Aware Benchmark for AI4Science Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18936)
*Linghao Feng,Yinqian Sun,Dongqi Liang,Sicheng Shen,Chenfei Yan,Yuxuan Peng,Yilin Zhao,Haibo Tong,Kai Li,FeiFei Zhao,Yi Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SciRisk-Bench 是一个双维度 AI4Science 安全基准，覆盖 7 个学科、31 个子学科和 10 个风险维度，用于细粒度评估 LLM 在科学场景下的安全风险识别与规避能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI4Science 安全数据集仅覆盖若干学科和任务格式，未明确界定底层风险维度，缺乏系统性的安全评估框架，无法精细诊断科学模型在何处仍不安全。

Method: 提出 SciRisk-Bench 基准，从显式风险维度与科学学科两个互补视角构建评估体系，覆盖 7 个学科、31 个子学科和 10 个风险维度，并对主流 LLM 和科学导向 LLM 进行跨维度、跨学科、跨子学科的细粒度评估。

Result: 实验部分展示了主流 LLM 和科学导向 LLM 在不同风险维度、学科和子学科上的安全表现差异，实现了对科学模型安全性薄弱环节的精细诊断。

Conclusion: SciRisk-Bench 提供了一个多维度的 AI4Science 安全评估框架，能够揭示 LLM 在高风险科学场景下的安全缺陷分布，为后续安全对齐研究提供诊断工具。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly embedded in AI for Science (AI4Science) workflows, from scientific question answering and literature analysis to laboratory planning and autonomous discovery. This progress creates an urgent need for safety benchmarks that evaluate not only scientific competence, but also whether models recognize and avoid risks in high-stakes scientific contexts. Existing AI4Science safety datasets cover several disciplines and task formats, leaving the underlying risk dimensions underspecified. We introduce \textbf{SciRisk-Bench}, a benchmark designed to evaluate AI4Science safety from two complementary perspectives: explicit risk dimensions and scientific disciplines. SciRisk-Bench covers 7 disciplines, 31 subdisciplines and 10 risk dimensions. In the experimental section, we evaluate both mainstream LLMs and science-oriented LLMs across risk dimensions, disciplines, and sub-disciplines, enabling fine-grained diagnosis of where scientific models remain unsafe.

</details>


### [277] [Decoupling Search from Reasoning: A Vendor-Agnostic Grounding Architecture for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18947)
*Emmanuel Aboah Boateng,Kyle MacDonald,Amardeep Kumar,Siddharth Kodwani,Sudeep Das*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出解耦搜索接地（DSG），将搜索接地从推理模型中剥离为一个与模型无关的MCP兼容网关，暴露路由、缓存、回退等可调控制点。实验表明DSG在接近原生搜索准确率的同时，大幅降低搜索成本（91%-98%）和延迟，并避免了搜索引发的冗余生成问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
