### [291] [Logarithmic energy distances and Gini covariance for Hilbert-valued random elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18365)
*Norbert Henze,M. Dolores Jiménez-Gamero*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 当α趋近于0时，广义能量距离经过适当归一化后收敛到对数能量距离（核为log‖x-y‖）。该文证明了对数能量距离保留了标准能量距离在可分Hilbert空间中的分布特征化性质，推导了其用高斯核最大均值差异表示的表达式，并引入对数Gini协方差处理k样本问题，研究了其结构与渐近性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能量距离族在边界情形α↓0时的行为，填补该边界区域的理论空白，揭示能量型统计量族中此前未被研究的新现象，并建立与核方法、函数型数据分析及高维推断的联系。

Method: (1) 对广义能量距离进行适当归一化，取极限α↓0，得到对数能量距离；(2) 基于高斯核MMD表示，建立对数能量距离的等价刻画；(3) 引入对数Gini协方差统计量，推导其用成对对数能量距离的表示；(4) 建立分布相等的特征化定理；(5) 推导经验统计量在原假设与备择假设下的渐近理论；(6) 讨论基于排列检验的推断实现。

Result: (1) 对数能量距离保留了标准能量距离在可分Hilbert空间中的分布特征化性质（即距离为零当且仅当分布相等）；(2) 推导出对数能量距离的高斯核MMD表示；(3) 建立了对数Gini协方差的特征化定理；(4) 获得了经验统计量在零假设和备择假设下的渐近分布理论结果。

Conclusion: 对数能量距离框架揭示了能量型统计量族中一个新的边界现象，成功将α∈(0,2)的经典理论连续延拓至α=0的对数情形。该框架不仅在理论上保持了分布特征化这一核心性质，还建立了与核方法、函数型数据分析和髙维推断的自然联系，为k样本问题提供了新的非参数检验工具。

Abstract: For $α\in(0,2)$, the generalized energy distance and the Gini covariance statistic are based on kernels of the form $(x,y)\mapsto \|x-y\|^α$, where $\|\cdot\|$ denotes the norm in a real separable Hilbert space. This paper investigates the boundary regime $α\downarrow 0$. After suitable normalization, the corresponding energy distance converges to a logarithmic energy distance involving the kernel $(x,y)\mapsto\log\|x-y\|$. We establish that the resulting logarithmic energy distance retains the fundamental characterization property of ordinary energy distances in separable Hilbert spaces and derive a representation in terms of Gaussian-kernel maximum mean discrepancies. Motivated by this representation, we introduce a logarithmic Gini covariance for the $k$-sample problem and investigate its structural and asymptotic properties. In particular, we derive a representation in terms of pairwise logarithmic energy distances, establish a characterization theorem for equality of distributions, develop asymptotic null and alternative theory for the corresponding empirical statistic, and discuss permutation-based implementation. The logarithmic framework reveals a new boundary phenomenon within the family of energy-type statistics and provides connections with kernel methods, functional data analysis, and high-dimensional inference.

</details>


### [292] [A closed-form sample size correction for always-valid inference with optional stopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18366)
*Mårten Schultzberg*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对A/B实验中的序贯检验，本文提出闭式校正因子 k*(α,β,t₀)，将固定样本量 n_z 膨胀为 k*·n_z，使得实际功效接近目标功效，避免依赖大规模模拟，并可节省8-20%的样本预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序贯检验允许持续监控，但其功效计算依赖模拟，难以在实验平台上跨大量指标扩展。现有常用启发式方法——将固定样本量膨胀至计划终点处边际拒绝概率达到1-β（即"最后时间点规则"）——过于保守，因为always-valid功效是任意时间点边界跨越的概率，而非仅在终点。因此需要一种可扩展的、解析的样本量校正方法。

Method: 作者推导了一个闭式校正因子 k*(α,β,t₀)，由初等函数和二元正态CDF表示，其中 t₀=m/n_z 为预热期比例。该近似仅依赖边界在计划终点处的值和斜率，适用于任意光滑凹边界。文中具体推导了三种情形：Waudby-Smith et al. (2023) 和 Maharaj et al. (2023) 的置信序列，以及 Johari et al. (2022) 的混合序贯概率比检验。校正因子仅通过 t₀ 依赖于分配比例 r。

Result: 在高斯模拟中，将总样本量设为 k*·n_z 可使实证功效控制在目标功效约3个百分点内。校正因子对分配比例 r 的依赖仅通过 t₀ 体现。预热期参数敏感性分析表明，该校正可在整个操作范围内节省最后时间点规则样本预算的8–20%。

Conclusion: 该闭式校正因子为序贯A/B检验提供了一种简洁、可扩展的样本量规划方案，无需模拟即可准确控制功效，且显著节省样本量，具有实际平台部署价值。

Abstract: Sequential tests that allow continuous monitoring are common in A/B experimentation. Power calculations for these tests require simulations that are hard to scale across many metrics on an experimentation platform. Instead, a common sizing heuristic inflates the fixed-sample size until the marginal rejection probability at the planned endpoint reaches $1-β$. This last-point rule is conservative because always-valid (AV) power is the probability of a boundary crossing at any time during the run, not at the endpoint alone. We give a closed-form correction factor $k^(α, β, t_0)$ expressed in elementary functions and the bivariate normal CDF, where $t_0 = m/n_z$ is the burn-in fraction. The closed-form approximation depends on the boundary only through its value and slope at the planned endpoint and can be evaluated for any smooth concave boundary. We work out three cases: the confidence sequences of Waudby-Smith et al. (2023) and Maharaj et al. (2023), and the mixture sequential probability ratio test of Johari et al. (2022). Setting the total sample size to $k^ \cdot n_z$, where $n_z$ is the fixed-sample size for allocation ratio $r$, hits empirical power within approximately 3 percentage points of target in Gaussian simulations. The correction factor depends on the allocation ratio $r$ only through $t_0 = m/n_z(r)$. We study sensitivity to the burn-in parameter and show that the correction saves 8--20% of the last-point sample budget across the operating range.

</details>


### [293] [Learning Moment Maps for Continuous-Time Markov Chains under Monte Carlo Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18409)
*Madison Pratt,Olivia Prosper-Feldman*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模拟的代理建模框架，从蒙特卡洛采样产生的含噪训练目标中学习参数到矩的映射。研究发现蒙特卡洛噪声对均值估计主要产生加性方差影响，而协方差估计还会因非线性变换引入偏差。利用SIR模型验证了神经网络的有效性，并给出了参数空间覆盖与蒙特卡洛复制的计算资源分配策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间马尔可夫链广泛用于随机动力系统建模，但其均值、协方差等关键汇总量往往难以解析求解。蒙特卡洛采样虽能提供渐近无偏估计，但当需要在大量参数值上评估矩时计算成本过高。因此需要一种高效、准确的方法来近似整个参数空间上的参数-矩映射关系。

Method: 构建基于仿真的代理建模框架：首先在参数空间中选择若干点，用固定仿真预算生成蒙特卡洛估计（含噪声的训练标签），然后训练神经网络学习从参数到均值和协方差的映射。系统分析了蒙特卡洛噪声对两类矩估计的不同影响机制，并研究了参数空间覆盖与蒙特卡洛复制次数之间的计算资源最优分配策略。

Result: （1）蒙特卡洛噪声对均值估计的影响主要表现为加性方差，而协方差估计还受到经验估计非线性变换带来的偏差影响；（2）在固定仿真预算下，神经网络能准确学习均值和协方差映射；（3）资源分配上，均值估计更受益于扩大参数空间覆盖，协方差估计则需要平衡分配以同时控制方差和偏差；（4）学习到的矩映射能产生有效的总体水平量，并在白化等下游任务中表现良好。

Conclusion: 在随机系统的代理建模中必须考虑蒙特卡洛噪声的影响。均值估计与协方差估计在噪声影响机制和资源分配策略上存在本质差异，实际应用中应区别对待。该框架为随机系统的仿真学习提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Continuous-time Markov Chains are widely used to model stochastic dynamical systems, but key summary quantities such as means and covariances are often intractable. While Monte Carlo sampling provides asymptotically exact estimates, it becomes computationally prohibitive when moments must be evaluated across many parameter values. We develop a simulation-based surrogate modeling framework that learns parameter-to-moment mappings from Monte Carlo-derived, noise-corrupted training targets, enabling efficient and accurate approximation across the parameter space. We show that Monte Carlo noise affects mean estimation primarily through additive variance, whereas covariance estimation is additionally impacted by bias arising from nonlinear transformations of empirical estimates. Using a stochastic Susceptible-Infected-Recovered model, we demonstrate that neural networks accurately learn both mean and covariance under fixed simulation budgets allocated to constructing the noisy training labels. We further characterize how to allocate computational resources between parameter-space coverage and Monte Carlo replication, showing that covariance estimation requires a balanced allocation to control both variance and bias, while mean estimation benefits more from increased parameter space coverage. Finally, we show that the learned moment mappings produce valid population-level quantities and perform well in downstream tasks such as whitening. These results highlight the importance of accounting for Monte Carlo noise in surrogate modeling and provide practical guidance for simulation-based learning in stochastic systems.

</details>


### [294] [Bayesian Nonparametric Detection of Anomalies in Multivariate Functional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18412)
*Daniel Krasnov,David Stephens*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种贝叶斯非参数方法，用于多变量函数型数据的异常检测。该方法将函数数据建模为多输出高斯过程的无限混合，通过切片采样自动确定混合分量数量，利用小波基和Besov先验实现平滑稀疏表示，并采用内蕴共区域化模型捕捉函数间依赖性。异常观测被自动分配到小型混合分量中，无需事先指定异常的数量或性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 函数型数据中的异常源于偏离主导数据生成机制的罕见或特殊过程。在错误检测、结构性变化识别等应用中，检测此类异常至关重要。现有方法往往需要预先指定异常数量或性质，且在多变量函数型数据中对交叉函数依赖关系的建模不够充分，因此需要一种能够自动确定异常数量、同时有效捕捉多输出函数间相关性的方法。

Method: 该方法采用无限混合多输出高斯过程模型：(1) 使用切片采样自动确定有限数量的混合分量；(2) 均值函数通过小波基表示，并以Besov先验进行正则化，获得平滑且稀疏的表示；(3) 利用内蕴共区域化模型（Intrinsic Coregionalization Model）捕捉多输出函数间的交叉依赖关系；(4) 在MCMC算法中引入Carlin-Chib乘积空间步骤解决协方差核选择问题；(5) 异常观测被自动分配到较小的混合分量中。实验采用半监督设置，仅15%的正常观测有标签，且存在严重的类别不平衡。

Result: 该方法在单变量和多变量函数型数据上均展示了良好的异常检测效果。模型能够自动识别异常观测，无需预先设定异常数量或类型，且在类别严重不平衡的半监督场景下依然有效。

Conclusion: 所提出的贝叶斯非参数方法为多变量函数型数据异常检测提供了一个灵活且强大的框架。通过无限混合建模、自动分量确定和交叉函数依赖捕捉，该方法在实际应用中具有广泛前景，尤其适用于仅有少量正常标签且类别严重不平衡的场景。

Abstract: Anomalies in functional data arise from rare or distinct processes that deviate from the dominant data-generating mechanism. Detecting such departures is essential in applications where they may correspond to errors, structural changes, or other behavior of interest. This work introduces a Bayesian nonparametric approach for anomaly detection in multivariate functional data. We model functional data as an infinite mixture of multi-output Gaussian processes, with a finite and automatically determined number of mixture components obtained through slice sampling. Mean functions are represented using a wavelet basis and regularized through Besov priors to obtain a smooth and sparse representation of the data. Cross-functional dependence is captured using the intrinsic coregionalization model and we solve covariance kernel selection by introducing a Carlin-Chib product space step in the Markov Chain Monte Carlo algorithm. Within this model, anomalous observations are assigned to small mixture components without requiring prior specification of the number or nature of anomalies. We consider a semi-supervised setting, in which labels are available for 15% of the normal observations and a large class imbalance is present. The utility of our model is demonstrated on both univariate and multivariate functional data.

</details>


### [295] [Apportioning Causal Responsibility of Two Risk Factors for an Adverse Outcome via Counterfactual Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18459)
*Shanshan Luo,Yafang Deng,Qingyuan Zhao,Zhi Geng*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在二元风险因素之间分配因果责任的定量框架，基于潜在结果定义平均因果责任，在无混淆和单调性假设下建立非参数识别条件，并给出不可识别时的紧界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果推断是前瞻性的（从原因到结果），而现实中常需对已发生的不良结果进行回顾性归因，即"分摊因果责任"。目前缺乏一个严格的定量框架来在两个共同导致已发生结果的二元风险因素之间分配责任。

Method: 基于潜在结果框架定义个体的"潜在因果类型"（所有暴露组合下的潜在结果），将每个风险因素的平均因果责任定义为其在潜在因果类型分布上的期望责任。在无混淆和单调性假设下，当类型特定责任满足"结构平衡条件"时，建立该度量的非参数识别；否则推导出紧的界限。

Result: 在结构平衡条件下可获得平均因果责任的非参数点识别；不满足该条件时则得到识别界限。通过吸烟与石棉暴露导致肺癌的经典案例对所提框架进行了验证和说明。

Conclusion: 该框架为回顾性因果责任分摊提供了严格的量化工具，为流行病学、法律归因等领域中"多重原因共同导致结果"的责任划分问题奠定了方法论基础。

Abstract: Unlike traditional causal inference, which prospectively evaluates the effects of causes, apportioning causal responsibility requires a retrospective assessment to deduce the causes of an outcome that has already occurred. This paper proposes a quantitative framework for apportioning causal responsibility between two binary risk factors that jointly contribute to a realized adverse outcome. Ideally, knowing the individual's latent causal type, defined by the potential outcomes under all possible exposure combinations, would allow precise apportionment; however, these potential outcomes cannot be simultaneously observed. We therefore define the average causal responsibility of each risk factor as its expected responsibility over the distribution of latent causal types. Under the assumptions of no confounding and monotonicity, we establish nonparametric identification of this metric when the type-specific responsibilities satisfy a structural balance condition, and derive sharp bounds otherwise. We illustrate the proposed framework using the classic example of lung cancer attributable to smoking and asbestos exposures.

</details>


### [296] [Shrinkage priors for Bayesian Substitute Confounders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18535)
*Yordan P. Raykov,Hengrui Luo,Justin D. Strait,Wasiur R. KhudaBukhsh*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种贝叶斯因子分配框架，通过收缩先验学习稀疏替代混杂变量，以解决多原因观测研究中的未测量混杂问题，并在ADNI数据中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多原因观测研究中，未测量混杂可通过原因间的依赖结构推断，但现有方法（如deconfounder）的灵活分配模型容易过度编码处理向量、破坏重叠性或捕获单原因变异。需要一个能保留粗粒度多原因依赖、避免上述问题的稀疏替代混杂变量学习方法。

Method: 提出贝叶斯因子分配框架，利用收缩先验学习稀疏替代混杂变量。收缩先验倾向于由多个原因支撑的低维因子，抑制单原因因子，并通过渐进收缩诱导潜在因子的排序。理论分析涵盖后验收敛性、因子得分收缩和保留重叠的分配几何。

Result: 在所提出的条件下，当潜在变量识别假设成立时，回归调整估计量对均值潜在结果具有一致性。合成实验展示了信号强度、结果有效性和几何感知正则化的作用。在ADNI基线分析中，稀疏替代得分恢复了直接对脑脊液生物标志物进行条件调整的大部分效果。

Conclusion: 收缩先验为潜在结构学习提供了天然工具，稀疏替代混杂变量在多原因因果推断中表现良好，且可通过坍缩诊断识别因子是否退化为单个观测测量。

Abstract: Multi-cause observational studies contain information about unmeasured confounding through the dependence structure among causes. However, literal imputation of the unobserved confounder is often more complex than learning a lower-dimensional substitute score that preserves the shared assignment variation needed for stable causal adjustment. The deconfounder (Wang and Blei, 2019) and related substitute confounder methods exploit this idea, but flexible assignment models can fit the joint distribution of the causes while producing scores that over-encode the treatment vector, collapse overlap, or capture single-cause variation. We develop a Bayesian factor assignment framework for learning sparse substitute confounders that retain coarse multi-cause dependence with shrinkage priors. The theory is stated at the level of posterior concentration, factor score contraction, and overlap-preserving assignment geometry and therefore does not rely on a particular shrinkage prior. Under these conditions, the proposed regression-adjusted estimators are consistent for mean potential outcomes when the corresponding latent variable identification assumptions hold. Shrinkage priors provide a natural tool for latent structural learning: they favour low-dimensional factors supported by multiple causes, discourage effectively single-cause factors, and induce an ordering of the latent factors through progressive shrinkage. Synthetic experiments illustrate the roles of signal strength, outcome validity, and geometry-aware regularization. In an Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) baseline analysis, sparse substitute scores recover much of the adjustment obtained by directly conditioning on invasive cerebrospinal-fluid biomarkers, while collapse diagnostics identify when fitted factors reduce to individual observed measurements.

</details>


### [297] [Applying the Weibull Shape Parameter test for signal detection in pharmacovigilance using the R package WSPsignal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18809)
*Julia Dyck,Odile Sauzet*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为WSPsignal的R包，该包整合了Weibull形状参数（WSP）检验的整套统计方法，用于药物上市后不良反应的信号检测。它支持频率学派和贝叶斯两种估计框架、多种时间-事件分布，并可通过模拟调优确定最优检验方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上市后药物警戒需要可靠的统计信号检测方法。WSP检验利用电子健康记录中的时间信息评估药物使用后不良事件的风险变化，但现有方法涉及多种估计方式和分布选择，缺乏统一、易用的实现工具，阻碍了实际应用和进一步研究。

Method: 开发了R包WSPsignal，将WSP检验的全部功能整合到统一的开源接口中。该包支持：频率学派与贝叶斯两种估计方法；Weibull、双Weibull、幂广义Weibull等多种时间-事件分布；默认检验规范及基于模拟的调优功能。通过大样本（约20000例）和小样本（约1000例）两个实例分别展示了频率学派和贝叶斯WSP检验的应用。

Result: WSPsignal包成功整合了所有WSP检验所需功能，用户可直接使用默认参数或通过模拟调优确定最优检验规范。在大样本场景下演示了频率学派WSP检验，在小样本场景下演示了贝叶斯WSP检验，两种场景均通过模拟调优优化了检验参数。

Conclusion: WSPsignal R包为药物警戒领域的WSP信号检测提供了完整、便捷的开源工具，降低了方法应用门槛，有望推动该方法在研究和实践中的广泛采用。

Abstract: Post-marketing pharmacovigilance relies on statistical signal detection methods to identify potential adverse drug reactions. The Weibull shape parameter (WSP) test concept exploits temporal information (electronic health records) to assess the hazard of an adverse event over time after drug initiation. A statistically significant deviation from constancy results in a signal. The WSP framework comprises a family of tests that differ with respect to the estimation approach (frequentist or Bayesian), the chosen time-to-event distribution (Weibull, double Weibull, power generalized Weibull) for hazard modeling, and test specification parameters. To facilitate practical application and encourage consideration of the WSP signal detection test in future research, we developed the R package WSPsignal. The package consolidates all functionalities required for WSP testing into a unified, open-source interface. It enables practitioners and researchers to apply default test specifications or perform simulation-based tuning to identify the optimal test for a given data scenario. We illustrate the package functionalities in two examples to follow along. In a large-sample setting (ca. 20 000 observations), a frequentist WSP test is considered. In a small-sample setting (ca. 1 000 observations), a Bayesian WSP test is chosen. The additional test specifications are optimized through simulation-based tuning.

</details>


### [298] [Improved prediction of extreme random effects in joint models: WRaPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18843)
*Eline Vanderpijpen,Els Goetghebeur*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种加权随机效应预测方法(WRaPs)，将联合模型框架下的随机效应预测扩展至"死亡或次标准重复测量"复合结局，通过对尾部误差施加更大惩罚权重来缓解均值回归问题，并在胶质母细胞瘤患者研究中进行了应用验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在临床环境中，重复测量的结局可能以死亡告终。标准的随机效应预测存在均值回归问题，会低估分布尾部的极端值。虽然已有最优加权随机效应估计方法用于混合模型，但该方法尚未扩展到包含生存结局的联合模型情境，即需要预测"死亡或次标准重复测量"这类复合不良结局的场景。

Method: 作者从纵向和生存结局的共享随机效应联合模型出发，通过最小化加权平方预测误差来估计随机效应，权重设计使尾部误差受到更大惩罚（WRaPs）。对于基础模型和特定权重集，给出了基于常规联合模型参数的解析闭式解；对于更复杂的设置，在贝叶斯范式下使用MCMC方法（rjags）开发了计算方案。

Result: 通过包含随机截距和随机斜率的I类模拟验证了该方法的有限样本性质，并将其应用于一项胶质母细胞瘤随机化研究，预测个体未来结局和生存情况。

Conclusion: WRaPs方法有效扩展了加权随机效应预测的思路，能够同时处理纵向和生存结局，为识别极端不良结局（包括死亡）提供了更准确的个体化预测工具。

Abstract: Mixed models are popular for the prediction of subject-specific repeated outcomes or center performance among many centers. When the goal is to identify extreme or poor outcomes, standard random effects predictions may, however, suffer from regression to the mean and underestimate values in the tail of their distribution. Optimally weighted random effect estimators have recently been proposed to mitigate this. Motivated by clinical settings where repeated outcomes may end in death, we extend that method to predict poor outcome defined as 'death or substandard repeated measures'. We start from joint models with shared random effects for the longitudinal and survival outcome and estimate their random effects by minimizing squared weighted prediction errors given available data on survival and repeated measures. As for mixed models, weights are chosen to more heavily penalize errors in the tails. We call the results WRaPs: Weighted Random effect Predictors. For basic models and a select set of weights analytical closed form solutions are derived from the usual joint model parameters. For the more complex setting, computational solutions are developed in rjags using MCMC methods within the Bayesian paradigm. We illustrate finite sample properties of the proposed method in Type I simulations with random intercept and slope; and apply the new approach to predict individual future outcomes and survival in a randomized study with glioblastoma patients.

</details>


### [299] [Feature Screening for High-Dimensional Structural Break Predictive Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18949)
*Zhenjie Qin,Rongmao Zhang,Wenyang Zhang,Yang Zu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种在结构突变预测回归中同时筛选活跃预测变量和估计变点的高效两步法：先用SICS筛选活跃变量，再用RCRS估计变点，最后通过信息准则精简冗余，实现一致估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测回归中常存在结构突变和稀疏活跃变量，现有方法难以在高维设定下同时处理变点数量随样本增长、以及变量可能平稳或协整的情况，需要一种高效且一致的联合选择与估计方法。

Method: 第一步：Sure Independence Canonical Screening (SICS) 识别活跃预测变量；第二步：Ratio-Controlled Regression Screening (RCRS) 估计结构突变点；第三步：利用信息准则 (IC) 剔除冗余变点和预测变量，实现最终精简。

Result: 该方法能一致地估计和选择真实变点与活跃预测变量。模拟和实证研究表明所提方法表现有效。

Conclusion: 该方法为结构突变预测回归提供了一种实用的高维分析工具，能够同时处理变点与变量选择，且具有良好的理论和实证表现。

Abstract: Predictive regression is a crucial tool for exploring return predictability. In this study, we introduce an efficient procedure for selecting and estimating active predictors and change points in structural break predictive regression. Our approach allows the number of change points to increase with the sample size and accommodates sparse active predictors that may be stationary or cointegrated. We begin by identifying the active predictors using a Sure Independence Canonical Screening (SICS) procedure. Next, we estimate the change points through a Ratio-Controlled Regression Screening (RCRS) method. Finally, we reduce redundancy by eliminating unnecessary breakpoints and predictors using information criteria (IC). This approach allows for consistent estimation and selection of true breakpoints and active predictors. Our simulations and empirical studies demonstrate that the proposed procedure performs effectively.

</details>


### [300] [Balanced Twins: Causal Inference on Time Series with Hidden Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18969)
*Ouali Maha,Ghattas Badih,Flachaire Emmanuel,Charpentier Philippe,Bozzi Laurent*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经框架，通过同时学习个体时间序列的低维潜在表征和倾向性得分，在个体层面恢复反事实结果，进而估计处理组平均处理效应（ATT）。该方法通过灵活匹配程序规避传统合成控制法的凸性约束，能自然适应交错处理分配和隐性混杂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实应用中（如电力需求响应、ICU临床数据），干预措施往往在不同时间点施加于不同个体，形成交错处理暴露和异质性预处理历史。由于未观测混杂因素导致处理分配存在偏差，直接聚合处理单元的结果轨迹不具有良好定义，因此需要先估计个体处理效应（ITE）再进行可靠的因果推断。

Method: 提出一种神经框架，联合学习个体时间序列的低维潜在表征和倾向性得分；利用所得估计值，通过灵活的匹配程序近似个体处理效应，该匹配程序规避了传统合成控制方法中的经典凸性约束；在个体层面操作，自然适应交错干预，无需显式的时间建模假设。

Result: 在真实世界的高频电力需求响应数据和ICU半合成临床时间序列数据上进行了验证，展示了该方法在存在隐性混杂、交错处理采纳和非平稳动态场景下的反事实估计性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过个体层面的潜在表征学习与灵活匹配，该方法能够有效处理交错干预和隐性偏差下的个体反事实估计问题，为复杂时间序列中的ATT估计提供了一种无需严格时序建模假设的可行方案。

Abstract: Accurately estimating treatment effects in time series is essential for evaluating interventions in real-world applications, especially when treatment assignment is biased by unobserved factors. In many practical settings, interventions are adopted at different times across individuals, leading to staggered treatment exposure and heterogeneous pre-treatment histories. In such cases, aggregating outcome trajectories across treated units is ill-defined, making individual treatment effect (ITE) estimation a prerequisite for reliable causal inference. We therefore study the problem of estimating the average treatment effect for the treated (ATT) by first recovering individual-level counterfactuals. We introduce a neural framework that learns simultaneously low-dimensional latent representations of individual time series and propensity scores. These estimates are then used to approximate the individual treatment effects through a flexible matching procedure that avoids classical convexity constraints commonly used in synthetic control methods. By operating at the individual level, our approach naturally accommodates staggered interventions and improves counterfactual estimation under latent bias, without relying on explicit temporal modeling assumptions. We illustrate our approach on both real-world energy consumption data and clinical time series, including high-frequency electricity demand-response programs and semi-synthetic data for individuals in intensive care unit (ICU), where hidden confounding, staggered treatment adoption, and non-stationary dynamics are prevalent.

</details>


### [301] [Dimension reduction of multivariate densities in Bayes spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19011)
*Adéla Czolková,Karel Hron,Sonja Greven*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文在Bayes空间的Hilbert结构下，利用中心对数比（clr）变换建立与L²空间的等距同构，将函数型主成分分析（FPCA）应用于概率密度函数的降维。针对多元PDF，提出了一种正交分解方法，将其分解为独立成分和交互成分，其中独立部分可进一步分解为相互正交的几何边际分布。作者证明了这种方差分解在PCA意义下是最优的，并且直接对多元密度应用FPCA等价于对其分解形式应用多元FPCA。该方法在住房和地质数据集上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元概率密度函数的函数型数据分析面临挑战：密度数据具有相对性和成分约束（积分恒为1），传统L²空间方法忽略了这一特殊几何结构。作者旨在利用Bayes空间的Hilbert结构，为多元密度函数提供一种能够揭示独立变异与交互变异来源的正交方差分解方法，从而增强FPCA在密度数据上的可解释性。

Method: 1. 基于Bayes空间的Hilbert结构，通过中心对数比（clr）变换建立与L²空间的等距同构。2. 将多元PDF嵌入Bayes空间，进行正交分解：总变异分解为独立成分（进一步分解为相互正交的几何边际分布）和交互成分。3. 在该分解框架下应用函数型主成分分析（FPCA），证明该方差分解在PCA意义下是最优的。4. 理论证明：直接对多元密度进行FPCA等价于对其分解形式进行多元FPCA，且特征函数和得分也相应分解。

Result: 1. 多元密度在Bayes空间中的正交分解实现了独立变异与交互变异的分离，独立部分可进一步分解为几何正交的边际分布。2. 该方差分解在PCA意义下被证明是最优的。3. 直接FPCA与分解后多元FPCA的等价性得到理论证明，特征函数与得分具有相应的分解结构。4. 在住房数据和地质数据的实证应用中，该方法展现出良好的可解释性和实用价值。

Conclusion: Bayes空间框架为概率密度函数的FDA提供了几何上合理的结构。将多元密度正交分解为独立和交互成分，并对独立部分进一步做几何边际分解，能够更深入地揭示多元密度变异的来源。该分解在PCA意义下最优，且与直接FPCA等价，显著提升了特征函数和得分的可解释性，在实证中验证了其实际应用潜力。

Abstract: The Bayes space provides a Hilbert space structure for analysing probability density functions (PDFs), equipping them with a geometry that reflects their relative and constrained nature. A key tool in this framework is the centred logratio (clr) transformation, which establishes an isometric isomorphism between the Bayes space and (a subspace of) the classical $L^2$ space. This makes it possible to apply functional data analysis (FDA) techniques, particularly functional principal component analysis (FPCA), to both univariate and multivariate density data in the context of dimension reduction. For multivariate PDFs, embedding them in the Bayes space enables an orthogonal decomposition into independent and interactive components. Furthermore, the independent part can be decomposed into mutually orthogonal geometric marginals. This structure provides more profound insights into the sources of variation in multivariate densities. We show that this decomposition of the total variance is optimal in a PCA sense, impacting the interpretation of the eigenfunctions and scores resulting from FPCA. We demonstrate that applying FPCA directly to multivariate densities is equivalent in a certain sense to applying multivariate FPCA to their decomposed form, with the resulting eigenfunctions and scores decomposing accordingly. The unique decomposition based on these theoretical results is applied to housing and geological empirical data respectively, demonstrating the interpretability and practical value of this approach.

</details>


### [302] [Efficient Cumulative Incidence Estimation in Biobank Studies Using All Prevalent and Incident Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19041)
*David M. Zucker,Malka Gorfine*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种新型累积发病率函数估计方法，能够同时利用生物银行数据中的现患病例和新发病例，解决了现有方法在处理早发且病程长的疾病时的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物银行研究通常仅使用新发病例或仅使用现患病例进行发病率估计，导致信息利用不充分。尤其是对于年轻时发病且发病后存活时间长的疾病，现有方法无法有效整合所有可用数据，造成估计效率低下或偏倚。

Method: 提出一种新的累积发病率函数（CIF）估计量，将现患病例（招募前已发病者）和新发病例（随访期间发病者）均纳入估计，无论研究对象后续生命历程如何。同时建立了该估计量的渐近性质（大样本理论），并通过模拟研究验证其有限样本表现。

Result: 模拟研究表明，所提方法在多种场景下表现良好。通过UK生物银行癌症数据的实证应用，展示了该方法相较于现有方法的优势。

Conclusion: 该方法能够更充分地利用生物银行数据，提供更精确的累积发病率估计，尤其适用于可早年发病且病程较长的疾病研究。

Abstract: Population-based biobanks, now established in many countries, offer opportunities for large-scale studies investigating the incidence of various diseases. Biobank data is typically collected from a study cohort recruited over a defined calendar period, with subjects entering the study at various ages falling between $R_L$ and $R_U$. This work focuses on biobank data that includes individuals in whom onset of the disease of interest occurred before recruitment, termed prevalent cases, along with individuals initially recruited as disease-free in whom disease onset occurred during the follow-up period. We propose a novel cumulative incidence function (CIF) estimator that goes beyond existing methods in that it incorporates all disease cases, both prevalent and incident, irrespective of their subsequent life course. In particular, the new method can handle situations involving diseases that can occur at young ages with long survival after disease onset. Asymptotic properties of the new method are established and a simulation study is presented examining the performance of the method. We illustrate the use of the method and highlight its advantages over existing methods with an application to cancer data from the UK biobank.

</details>


### [303] [Regularized covariance estimation from partially observed interferometric data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19065)
*Teresa Bortolotti,Roberta Troilo,Francesco Casu,Simone Vantini,Alessandra Menafoglio*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对SBAS技术获取的地面位移数据中系统性缺失问题，从函数型数据分析视角出发，提出了一种基于拉普拉斯正则化的矩阵补全协方差估计方法。该方法非参数化、无需平稳性或各向同性假设，在模拟和Phlegraean Fields实际数据中均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SBAS技术提供的高空间分辨率地面位移数据存在大范围系统性缺失，导致其二阶结构（协方差）的可靠估计极为困难，而这恰恰是有效分析该类数据的关键前提。

Method: 作者将观测数据视为二维域上的部分观测函数型数据，提出"碎片化部分观测"（fragmented regime）概念，描述曲线片段在重复观测中系统性缺失的情形。在此基础上，将协方差估计形式化为带拉普拉斯正则化的矩阵补全问题，得到一种非参数估计量，不依赖平稳性或各向同性假设。

Result: 大量模拟实验表明，该方法在多种协方差结构下均能保持稳定的低估计误差。在Phlegraean Fields（坎皮佛莱格瑞火山区域）地面位移数据的应用中，成功恢复了有意义的空间依赖模式。

Conclusion: 所提方法为碎片化缺失场景下的协方差估计提供了有效解决方案，在环境风险评估与监测中具有显著的应用潜力。

Abstract: The Small BAseline Subset technique provides remote measurements of ground displacement with high spatial resolution, making it a key tool for monitoring geophysical processes in hazard-prone areas. An effective analysis of this type of data requires reliable estimation of their second-order structure, which is difficult to achieve because the measurements are systematically missing over relatively large portions of the investigated areas. We tackle the problem from a functional data analysis perspective and treat the observations as partially observed functional data with two-dimensional domain. To properly characterize the data, we introduce the fragmented regime of partial observation, where parts of the curves are systematically missing across replicates. For this regime, we propose a novel method for covariance estimation, formulating the task as a matrix completion problem with Laplacian regularization. The estimator is nonparametric and free from stationarity or isotropy assumptions. Extensive simulations show that our method achieves consistently low estimation error across a range of covariance structures. Application to ground displacement data relative to the Phlegraean Fields demonstrates its ability to recover meaningful spatial dependence patterns, highlighting its potential for environmental risk assessment and monitoring.

</details>


### [304] [Probability Bound Analysis for Dependence Uncertainty in Risk and Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19086)
*Rowan Iskandar*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种依赖敏感的PBA框架，可在边际信息和依赖信息均不完整的情况下，通过p-box、copula和Fréchet容许耦合集传播认知不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统概率边界分析(PBA)通常假设变量独立或要求完全指定依赖结构，但实际风险决策中，边际分布和依赖关系常仅有部分信息，导致输出推断过于狭窄或不可靠。

Method: 该框架将p-box参数、精确CDF参数和固定量相结合；通过copula整合已指定的依赖关系；利用Fréchet型容许耦合集传播未知依赖；并将构造扩展至不精确与精确输入之间的交叉依赖。

Result: 在示例风险决策模型中，依赖假设显著影响输出边界和尾部风险摘要；忽略或简化依赖的分析产生了对合理结果范围的过窄刻画。

Conclusion: 当证据不足以支持精确边际分布或单一依赖模型时，该框架支持透明的、依赖敏感的不确定性传播。

Abstract: Risk and decision models often combine sparse marginal information, precisely specified probability distributions, and dependence assumptions that are only partly justified. Probability bound analysis (PBA) represents epistemic uncertainty through probability boxes, but many applications assume independence or require dependence structures to be fully specified. We develop a dependence-sensitive PBA framework for black-box risk and decision models in which both marginal information and dependence information may be incomplete. The framework combines p-box parameters, precise-CDF parameters, and fixed quantities; incorporates specified dependence through copulas; and propagates unknown dependence through Fréchet-style admissible coupling sets. We also extend the construction to cross-dependence between imprecisely specified and precisely specified inputs. In an illustrative risk decision model, dependence assumptions materially affected output bounds and tail-risk summaries; analyses that ignored or simplified dependence produced narrower characterizations of plausible outcomes. The framework supports transparent uncertainty propagation when evidence is insufficient to justify either precise marginal distributions or a single dependence model.

</details>


### [305] [What does ethnic density represent? Spatial co-occurrence networks of a widely used contextual measure using harmonised UK small-area census data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19087)
*Joseph Lam*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 种族密度在流行病学和健康地理学中广泛用作情境暴露指标，但本文指出等效百分比值在不同群体和地区间可能代表不同的邻里情境。通过分析英国239,023个小区域普查数据，使用混合图模型和空间分析，发现种族密度并非单一的标量构念，不同种族群体的密度与不同的共现变量相关联，且具有强烈的空间自相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 种族密度作为情境暴露指标在研究中被广泛使用，但其本身作为测量问题很少被审视。等效百分比值可能因迁移、宗教、语言、家庭结构和社会经济条件的空间共位而代表完全不同的邻里情境，亟需对种族密度作为探索性情境共现构念进行系统性检验。

Method: 使用统一英国普查数据（Unified UK Census Data），分析239,023个小区域普查数据。采用混合图模型（MGM）对八个目标种族密度进行全英范围估计，每个目标特定模型使用239,019个完整案例和32个节点。英格兰地区空间分析采用k近邻（k=8）输出区质心方法估计LISA和空间调整残差网络。

Result: 种族密度并非单一情境标量。MGM结果显示不同群体的最强目标-邻居边各异：亚裔密度与中东/亚洲出生比例关联最强（0.59），印度裔与印度教徒比例（0.55），巴基斯坦裔与穆斯林比例（0.47），孟加拉裔与穆斯林比例（0.23），黑人密度与非洲出生比例（0.42），白人密度与中东/亚洲出生比例（0.35）。英格兰种族密度测量呈现强空间自相关，全局Moran's I从混合族裔的0.57到白人的0.90。残差化后，64.3%至96.4%的原始目标节点边在种族密度网络中持续存在。

Conclusion: 等效百分比值在不同种族群体间未必具有可比性。这对城市健康研究中效应量定义、调整策略以及种族密度等捆绑式情境测量的解释具有重要启示，建议研究者在应用种族密度指标时应审慎考虑其内在的共现结构。

Abstract: Ethnic density is widely used in epidemiology and health geography as a contextual exposure, yet it is rarely examined as a measurement problem in its own right. Equivalent percentage values may represent different neighbourhood contexts across groups and places, particularly where migration, religion, language, household structure and socioeconomic conditions are spatially co-located. Using the harmonised Unified UK Census Data release, I analysed 239,023 small-area census data to examine ethnic density as an exploratory contextual co-occurrence construct. I estimated UK-wide mixed graphical models (MGM) for eight ethnic-density targets using 239,019 complete cases and 32 nodes per target-specific model. England-only spatial analyses then used k-nearest-neighbour Output Area centroids (k = 8) to estimate LISA and spatially adjusted residual networks. Ethnic density did not behave as a single contextual scalar. In the UK-wide MGM, the strongest retained target-neighbour edges differed across groups. Asian density was linked most strongly with Middle East/Asia-born share (0.59), Indian density with Hindu share (0.55), Pakistani density with Muslim share (0.47), Bangladeshi density with Muslim share (0.23), Black density with Africa-born share (0.42), and White density with Middle East/Asia-born share (0.35). England-only ethnic-density measures were strongly spatially autocorrelated, with Global Moran's I ranging from 0.57 for Mixed share to 0.90 for White share. After residualising against English region and local spatial lag, 64.3% to 96.4% of original target-node edges persisted across ethnic-density networks. Equivalent percentage values are not necessarily comparable across ethnic groups. This has implications for estimand definition, adjustment strategies, and the interpretation of ethnic density and other bundled contextual measures in urban health research.

</details>


### [306] [Wasserstein Policy Learning for Distributional Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19117)
*Yiyan Huang,Cheuk Hang Leung,Qi Wu,Zhiheng Zhang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究分布值结果（distribution-valued outcomes）的离线策略学习问题，基于IPW和DR估计量建立统计保证，证明有限样本遗憾（regret）的主导项为$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{\mathrm{N\text{-}dim}(Π)/N})$，并给出匹配的minimax下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离线策略学习仅处理标量值潜在结果，但许多实际应用中结果本质上是分布（如收入分布、健康指标分布），仅关注均值会丢失分布信息。本文旨在将离线策略学习推广到分布值结果设定，通过Wasserstein重心和效用泛函定义奖励，从而捕捉完整的分布信息。

Method: 作者采用逆概率加权（IPW）和双重稳健（DR）估计量构建策略学习框架。核心技术难点在于处理组合策略类与无穷维分位数域乘积上的均匀偏差。在一维Wasserstein空间及正则条件下，通过经验过程理论控制该均匀偏差，推导出遗憾上界，并构造minimax下界证明速率的紧性。

Result: 有限样本遗憾的主导项为$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{\mathrm{N\text{-}dim}(Π)/N})$，其中$\mathrm{N\text{-}dim}(Π)$为策略类的Natarajan维数（或其他复杂度度量）。该速率由策略类复杂度主导，与Wasserstein维数无关。此外，minimax下界证明该速率在$N$和$\mathrm{N\text{-}dim}(Π)$依赖上是紧的。

Conclusion: 本文成功将离线策略学习从标量结果推广到分布值结果，在Wasserstein重心框架下建立了具有统计保证的策略学习方法，遗憾速率由策略类复杂度决定且被证明为minimax最优，为该领域的进一步研究奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Offline policy learning has received growing attention in causal inference. The primary objective is to learn a policy (individualized treatment rule) as a mapping from covariates to treatment that maximizes the empirical welfare defined as the mean of scalar-valued potential outcomes. In this paper, we study offline policy learning with distribution-valued outcomes, where each potential outcome is a probability measure on $\mathbb{R}$ and the reward is defined through a utility functional applied to the Wasserstein barycenter of induced outcome distributions. We establish statistical guarantees for the policy learning framework based on both Inverse Probability Weighting (IPW) and Doubly Robust (DR) estimators. By handling the challenging uniform deviation over the product of the combinatorial policy class and the infinite-dimensional quantile domain, we prove that the finite-sample regret has leading dependence $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{\mathrm{N\text{-}dim}(Π)/N})$. In the one-dimensional Wasserstein setting and under the stated regularity conditions, the leading regret rate is still governed by the policy-class complexity. Moreover, we provide a minimax lower bound establishing the sharpness of the leading dependence on $N$ and $\mathrm{N\text{-}dim}(Π)$.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [307] [Pointwise is Pointless? A Multimodal Ablation Study for Precipitation Nowcasting with Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18436)
*Ophélia Miralles,Máté Mile,Christoffer Artturi,Thomas Nipen,Ivar Seierstad*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过消融实验系统评估了多模态图神经网络降水临近预报系统中各类信息源（雷达、数值天气预报、地面站点观测、卫星、噪声增强、CRPS损失）的贡献，发现稀疏站点观测能改善局部预报但难以直接提升雷达网格场技能，其效益依赖于损失函数与不确定性表征方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏点观测在降水临近预报中日益丰富，但它们对密集雷达网格预报的实际提升效果尚不明确。现有研究缺乏对运行级别信息源和训练目标的系统消融分析，亟需厘清不同观测模态在临近预报问题中的互补性与局限性。

Method: 基于北欧雷达域构建多模态图神经网络临近预报系统，预测未来2小时每5分钟雨率。设置雷达历史、MEPS数值预报、Netatmo地面观测、MSG卫星通道、随机噪声及CRPS集合损失等不同组合进行消融实验，采用雷达网格、站点位置、降雨起始检测及oracle/位移/振幅评分等多维度诊断指标对比各配置性能。

Result: 各信息源改善预报问题的不同方面：MEPS稳定雷达外推，Netatmo改善站点及降雨起始诊断，卫星减少站点偏差但确定性使用时可能过早激活降雨；CRPS配置在雷达网格上增益最一致，卫星+CRPS组合获得最佳综合oracle/DAS分数。稀疏站点观测的局部预报技能与雷达网格场空间连贯技能是两类不同目标。

Conclusion: 稀疏点观测对临近预报并非无信息量，但其局部约束能力与雷达网格场收益存在差距。实际应用中，点观测可作为局部约束使用，但提升雷达场预报需依赖合适的训练损失、不确定性表征及观测支持的编码方式。

Abstract: Sparse point observations are increasingly available for precipitation nowcasting, but it is unclear how much they improve dense radar-field forecasts. We partially address this question with a multimodal graph neural network nowcasting system over the Nordic radar domain. The model predicts rain rate every five minutes up to two hours ahead and is trained with different combinations of radar history, MEPS numerical weather prediction, Netatmo surface observations, MSG satellite channels, stochastic noise, and CRPS-based ensemble losses. The study is designed as an ablation of operationally relevant information sources and training objectives. We compare radar-only, NWP-informed, station-informed, satellite-informed, noise-augmented, and CRPS-based configurations using complementary diagnostics on the radar grid, at station locations, for rain onset, and through oracle, displacement, and amplitude scores. The results show that each source improves a different part of the forecast problem. MEPS stabilises radar-only extrapolation, Netatmo observations improve local station and onset diagnostics, and satellite predictors reduce some station-level biases but may activate rain too early when used deterministically. CRPS-based configurations provide the most consistent radar-grid gains, while the combined satellite and CRPS setup gives the best overall oracle/DAS score. These results do not support the conclusion that point observations are uninformative for nowcasting, but they show that local observational skill and spatially coherent radar-field skill are distinct targets. The practical implication is that sparse observations can provide useful local constraints, but their benefit for radar-like fields depends on the training loss, uncertainty representation, and how observation support is encoded in the model.

</details>


### [308] [ToolChain-CRC: Conformal Risk Control for Agentic AI Under Retrieval and Tool-Use Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18467)
*Jeffery Opoku,David Banahene*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出ToolChain-CRC，一种针对检索增强与工具使用型AI智能体的共形风险控制方法，通过对完整轨迹进行分步风险评分与校准，实现对高风险运行的及时干预，并在理论上给出轨迹级风险控制保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI智能体在检索、调用工具、中间检查等环节可能存在错误，但最终答案表面可接受，导致仅靠最终答案无法发现过程的不可靠问题。因此需要一种能监控全流程、并在不安全时及时干预的风险控制方法。

Method: 方法将每次智能体运行视为"动作-观测-最终输出"的完整轨迹。构建步骤级风险评分，聚合为轨迹风险评分，校准"接受或干预"决策规则，并引入任意时刻报警机制，可在最终答案产出前中止高风险运行。理论上在可交换校准数据下证明轨迹级风险控制，提出含可审计常数的漂移感知扩展，并通过上鞅构造证明任意时刻升级规则。

Result: 实验涵盖合成工具链漂移、RAG/工具使用压力测试、SQuAD衍生检索任务、无API智能体问答案例、消融实验、目标风险敏感性、20种子鲁棒性检验、漂移裕度审计及在线RAG/工具使用基准测试。结果表明仅做最终答案校准会遗漏检索与工具故障，而轨迹级校准能将接受轨迹的风险控制在目标之下。

Conclusion: ToolChain-CRC为检索增强与工具使用型智能体提供了一种理论上可靠、实践有效的全轨迹风险控制方案，弥补了仅关注最终答案的风险盲区。

Abstract: Modern AI agents retrieve documents, call tools, check intermediate information, and then produce a final answer or action. This creates a risk-control problem that is not visible from the final answer alone. A final response may look acceptable even when the retrieval was weak, a tool output was wrong, or an earlier step was unsupported. We propose ToolChain-CRC, a conformal risk-control method for retrieval-augmented and tool-using agents under drift. The method treats each agent run as a full trajectory of actions, observations, and final output. It builds step-level risk scores, combines them into a trajectory risk score, calibrates an accept-or-intervene rule, and adds an anytime alarm that can stop risky runs before the final answer. We prove trajectory-level risk control under exchangeable calibration runs, give a drift-aware extension with auditable constants, and prove an anytime escalation rule through a supermartingale construction. Experiments cover synthetic tool-chain drift, RAG/tool-use stress tests, public SQuAD-derived retrieval tasks, an API-free agentic QA case study, ablations, target-risk sensitivity checks, 20-seed robustness checks, a drift-margin audit, and a live RAG/tool-use agent benchmark. Across these settings, final-answer-only calibration can miss retrieval and tool failures, while trajectory-level calibration keeps accepted-trajectory risk below the target.

</details>


### [309] [Compact Geometric Representations of Hierarchies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18520)
*Prashant Gokhale,Piotr Indyk,Yuhao Liu,Sandeep Silwal,Tony Chang Wang,Haike Xu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究有向无环图中可达性嵌入的紧凑表示，证明了对任意有向树存在常数维度3的嵌入，并将结果推广到树宽为t的图（维度O(t log n)），同时给出了匹配或近乎匹配的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有层次检索的嵌入方法在处理深层层次结构时维度膨胀严重（可达总节点数）。本文旨在利用结构图参数（如树宽、交叉边数）构建紧凑的可达性嵌入，降低嵌入维度。

Method: 通过图论分析，针对不同图类分别构造嵌入：对有向树给出常数维构造；对树宽为t的图给出O(t log n)维构造；对一般DAG和树宽图给出下界证明。还按交叉边数量进行参数化分析。

Result: 有向树：常数维度3；树宽t图：维度上界O(t log n)，下界Ω(t/log(n/t))；一般DAG：下界Ω(n)；交叉边参数化：获得对应的上下界。在真实数据集上验证了理论结果，在高召回场景下维度远小于先前方法。

Conclusion: 可达性嵌入的维度可由结构图参数（树宽、交叉边数）有效控制，突破了以往方法在深层层次结构中维度爆炸的瓶颈，且在实际数据中表现优异。

Abstract: Computing geometric representations of data is a cornerstone of modern machine learning, typically achieved by training dual encoders which map queries and documents into a shared embedding space. Recent work of You et al. [NeurIPS '25] has extended this approach to hierarchical retrieval, where relevance is determined by the ancestor-descendant relationships in a Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG). While previous work has shown that valid embeddings exist when the number of descendants is small, these bounds degrade significantly for deep hierarchies, requiring dimensions as large as the total number of nodes.
  In this paper, we investigate compact reachability embeddings for more general graph classes and provide theoretical guarantees for representing hierarchies using embeddings whose dimension depends on structural graph parameters. We prove that for any directed tree, there exists a reachability embedding in constant dimension 3, independent of the tree's size or depth. We generalize this result to graphs characterized by treewidth $t$, constructing embeddings of dimension $O(t \log n)$, where $n$ is the number of nodes. Complementing these upper bounds, we provide matching or near-matching lower bounds, showing that dimension $Ω(n)$ is necessary for general DAGs and $Ω(t/\log(n/t))$ is required for graphs of treewidth $t$. We also obtain upper and lower bounds parameterized by the number of cross-edges in the DAG. We additionally show that our embeddings can be constructed on real world datasets, and that they give much smaller dimensions in high recall regimes compared to prior embeddings with theoretical guarantees.

</details>


### [310] [Toward Simultaneously Optimal Regret in U-Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18527)
*Rafael Frongillo,Haipeng Luo,Nishant A. Mehta,Jon Schneider*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了U-calibration在线预测算法，提出了一种同时实现两类最优遗憾界的新算法：对所有有界proper loss达到$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$遗憾，同时对有界光滑proper loss达到$O(\log T)$对数遗憾。方法基于一种新颖的FTPL变体，在预测空间中直接施加自和谐噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有U-calibration算法虽能在所有有界proper loss上实现最坏情况最优的$O(\sqrt{T})$遗憾，但无法适应更简单的损失函数——即便对于平方损失这类光滑损失，也会产生$\Omega(\sqrt{T})$遗憾，而非最优的$O(\log T)$遗憾。问题的核心在于现有算法缺乏对损失函数光滑性的自适应能力。

Method: 提出一种新颖的Follow-the-Perturbed-Leader (FTPL)变体：不同于传统FTPL在累积损失上加扰动，本文直接在预测空间中施加自和谐噪声（self-concordant noise）。该噪声基于log-barrier构造，使得算法能根据损失的光滑性自动调整行为。分析框架也与传统FTPL有本质不同，需要处理自和谐噪声带来的复杂结构。

Result: 设计的单一预测算法同时实现：(1) 对所有有界proper loss，$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$遗憾；(2) 对所有有界光滑proper loss，$O(\log T)$遗憾；(3) 对更一般的"相对于log-barrier光滑"的损失（包括若干非Lipschitz例子），同样获得对数遗憾。

Conclusion: U-calibration中无法自适应光滑损失的局限性并非固有，通过自和谐噪声FTPL可同时获得最坏情况最优和光滑情况下的对数遗憾。该分析技术因自和谐噪声的复杂性而与以往FTPL分析有本质区别，具有独立的理论价值。

Abstract: U-calibration studies online forecasting algorithms whose predictions can be consumed by any unknown downstream agent, guaranteeing sublinear regret simultaneously for all proper loss functions. Existing U-calibration algorithms achieve worst-case optimal $O(\sqrt{T})$ regret for every bounded proper loss, but they fail to adapt to easier losses: as we show, even for smooth losses such as squared loss, they incur $Ω(\sqrt{T})$ regret instead of the optimal $O(\log T)$ regret.
  In this work, we show that this limitation is not inherent. Specifically, we design a single forecast algorithm that simultaneously achieves $\tilde O(\sqrt{T})$ regret for every bounded proper loss and $O(\log T)$ regret for every bounded smooth proper loss. More generally, our algorithm also attains logarithmic regret for losses that are smooth relative to the log-barrier, which include several non-Lipschitz examples. Our approach is based on a novel variant of Follow-the-Perturbed-Leader (FTPL) in which perturbations are applied directly in the prediction space using self-concordant noise. The resulting analysis also departs substantially from prior FTPL analyses due to the complex nature of this noise and may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [311] [When Does Trajectory-Level Supervision Permit Efficient Offline Reinforcement Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18531)
*Xuanfei Ren,Tengyang Xie*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文建立了从轨迹级结果监督中进行离线策略优化的统计理论，提出了OPAC算法，给出了样本复杂度的上下界，并刻画了用结果级标签替代过程级奖励的统计代价，同时将框架扩展到偏好反馈和广义结果目标的场景，揭示了结果级监督可行与不可行的边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多序列决策数据集只记录了轨迹级结果（如成功/失败、总得分），而非每步的奖励信号。现有离线强化学习理论大多依赖过程级奖励监督，缺乏对结果级监督下策略优化的统计理论理解。

Method: 提出OPAC（悲观演员-评论家）算法：学习潜在奖励模型，从轨迹级标签中恢复每步奖励，并结合悲观策略优化。理论分析涵盖三个层次：(1) 标准累积奖励目标下，证明高概率上界为Õ(H²√(C_sa(π*)/n))并给出匹配下界；(2) 扩展到基于偏好的反馈，保持对horizon和concentrability的依赖；(3) 对广义结果目标，引入两个结构系数κ_μ(σ)和χ_μ(σ)刻画信息损失，识别可学习区间。

Result: 在标准设定下，OPAC达到Õ(H²√(C_sa(π*)/n))的样本复杂度，且该界被证明是最优的（有匹配下界）。在偏好反馈设定下，阶数依赖保持一致。对于广义结果目标，发现全成功目标（all-success objectives）即使在确定性转移下也需Ω(2^H)条轨迹，但在结构系数有界时OPAC可实现多项式样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 结果级监督可实现样本高效的离线控制，但存在基本统计边界：当目标从累积奖励推广到任意非线性聚合时，信息损失可能导致指数级难度。两个结构系数κ_μ(σ)和χ_μ(σ)精确刻画了从可行到不可行的过渡。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning is typically analyzed under process-level reward supervision, yet many sequential decision datasets
  record only trajectory-level outcomes. We develop a statistical theory for offline policy optimization from such outcome-level
  supervision. We first study the canonical setting where the target remains the expected cumulative reward, but each offline trajectory
  provides only a scalar label whose conditional mean is the cumulative return. We propose OPAC, a pessimistic actor-critic algorithm
  that learns a latent reward model and optimizes a policy from trajectory-level labels. We prove a high-probability guarantee of order
  $\widetilde O(H^2\sqrt{C_{sa}(π^\star)/n})$ and a matching lower bound, characterizing the sharp statistical cost of replacing
  process-level rewards with one trajectory-level label. We then extend the principle to preference-based feedback, preserving the
  leading horizon and concentrability dependence up to preference-model constants. Finally, we study generalized outcome-based offline
  RL, where both the supervision and the objective are trajectory-level quantities induced by a nonlinear aggregation of latent per-step
  rewards. This problem is not learnable in general: for all-success objectives, any offline learner may require $Ω(2^H)$
  trajectories even with deterministic transitions and constant concentrability. We then identify a tractable regime through two
  structural coefficients, $κ_μ(σ)$ and $χ_μ(σ)$, capturing information loss in outcome aggregation and
  generalized Bellman updates, under which generalized OPAC achieves polynomial sample complexity. Together, our results delineate when
  outcome-level supervision enables sample-efficient offline control and when missing process-level rewards create fundamental
  statistical barriers.

</details>


### [312] [Bridging Data Gaps in Structural Fragility Modeling through Transfer Learning: Methodology and Case Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18567)
*Narges Saeednejad,Jamie Ellen Padgett*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出以方法论为中心的迁移学习框架，用于解决 fragility 模型在领域偏移、类别不平衡和稀缺目标标签下的适应问题。通过三个案例展示了四种迁移策略的有效性：重要性加权、参数迁移+层次贝叶斯、多源迁移融合。结果表明，直接迁移源模型在领域偏移下失效，而针对性适应显著改善了低数据环境下的失效检测和预测稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程 fragility 模型在面临领域偏移（如不同灾害事件、不同结构类型）、严重类别不平衡（失效样本极少）以及目标域标注数据稀缺时，现有先进模型（state-of-the-art）的直接迁移往往失效。同时，工程决策需要模型保持可解释性和不确定性量化能力。因此，亟需一个系统的迁移学习框架，并配套诊断方法、策略选择指南与不确定性报告规范。

Method: 提出以方法论为中心的迁移学习框架，涵盖四种策略：(1) 基于实例的迁移——通过重要性加权调整源域样本权重，以飓风 Katrina 沿海桥梁 fragility 数据验证；(2) 基于参数的迁移与层次贝叶斯迁移——实现跨分层的部分池化与后验不确定性量化，以飓风 Ian 住宅建筑 fragility 数据验证；(3) 多源迁移学习——融合多个解析 fragility 模型，学习源权重并进行正则化目标域适应，以 2001 年 Nisqually 地震桥梁 fragility 数据验证。框架强调策略选择、诊断工具与不确定性报告的系统性指导。

Result: 三个案例一致表明：在领域偏移和严重类别不平衡条件下，直接迁移现有先进源模型（即零样本直接应用）失效；而采用针对性适应策略后，低数据环境下的失效检测能力和预测稳定性均大幅提升。基于实例的重要性加权、层次贝叶斯的部分池化以及多源融合加权适应均展现出优于直接迁移的性能。

Conclusion: 当存在领域偏移和类别不平衡时，不能简单直接迁移现有 fragility 模型，需进行针对性的目标域适应。所提框架为此提供了系统性指导，强调在 fragility 模型开发与适应过程中需建立诊断工具、策略选择标准和不确定性报告规范。该研究为数据稀缺场景下的工程结构脆弱性评估与不确定性决策提供了方法论支撑。

Abstract: This paper presents a methodology-centered transfer learning framework for fragility adaptation under domain shift, class imbalance, and scarce target labels while preserving engineering interpretability and supporting decision-making under uncertainty. Four transfer learning strategies (instance-based, parameter-based, hierarchical Bayesian, and multi-source) are demonstrated through three complementary case studies: (i) instance-based transfer learning via importance weighting, demonstrated on coastal bridge fragility using Hurricane Katrina observations; (ii) parameter-based transfer learning together with hierarchical Bayesian transfer learning, enabling partial pooling across strata and posterior uncertainty quantification, demonstrated on residential building fragility using Hurricane Ian observations; and (iii) multi-source transfer learning that fuses multiple analytical fragility models with learned source weights and regularized target-domain adaptation, demonstrated on seismic bridge fragility using observations from the 2001 Nisqually earthquake. Across these case studies, direct transfer of source models (i.e. using existing state-of-the-art models) fails under domain shift and severe class imbalance, while targeted adaptation substantially improves failure detection and predictive stability in low-data regimes. These findings highlight the need for systematic guidance on diagnostics, strategy selection, and uncertainty reporting when developing and adapting fragility models.

</details>


### [313] [TimeLAVA: Learning-Agnostic Data Valuation for Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18729)
*Wenqin Liu,Weizhi Quan,Aoqi Zuo,Erdun Gao,Vu Nguyen,Dino Sejdinovic,Howard Bondell,Mingming Gong*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: TimeLAVA 是一种与模型无关的时间序列数据估值框架，通过选择性小波 Wasserstein 差异度量评估片段对减少分布偏差的边际贡献，无需模型训练即可生成信息量更大的价值分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据估值方法要么依赖特定模型导致泛化性受限，要么针对独立同分布数据设计，无法捕捉时间序列中的时序依赖、多尺度模式和非平稳动态。关键领域（医疗、金融、工业监控）的时间序列数据估值方法严重缺失。

Method: 提出 TimeLAVA 框架：(1) 引入选择性小波 Wasserstein 差异，结合多尺度小波变换实现时序定位，与不平衡最优传输结合以应对分布偏移；(2) 通过敏感性分析高效计算片段对最小化分布差异的边际贡献；(3) 将片段价值聚合为逐点分数；(4) 提供模型无关泛化性的理论保证及对离群污染的有界敏感性证明。

Result: 在异常检测、数据剪枝和标签噪声检测三个任务上，TimeLAVA 在多个真实世界数据集上均生成比现有方法显著更具信息量的价值分数。

Conclusion: TimeLAVA 是首个专为时间序列设计的学习无关数据估值框架，通过多尺度时序分析和分布差异最小化，有效解决了时序数据估值中时序依赖、非平稳性等核心挑战，在多个下游任务中验证了其优越性和通用性。

Abstract: Data valuation quantifies the intrinsic quality of individual samples to enable principled data curation, quality control, and robust learning. For time series in critical domains such as healthcare, finance, and industrial monitoring, effective valuation methods are essential yet fundamentally lacking. Existing approaches are either model-dependent, limiting their generalizability, or designed for i.i.d. data and thus fail to capture temporal dependencies, multi-scale patterns, and non-stationary dynamics inherent to sequential data. We introduce TimeLAVA, a learning-agnostic framework that values temporal segments by their marginal contribution to minimizing distributional discrepancy between evaluated and reference data. At its core is a novel Selective Wavelet-based Wasserstein discrepancy combining multi-scale wavelet transforms for temporal localization with unbalanced optimal transport for robustness to distributional shifts. Segment values are efficiently computed via sensitivity analysis without requiring model training and aggregated into point-wise scores. We provide theoretical guarantees linking valuation to model-agnostic generalization and prove bounded sensitivity to outlier contamination. Extensive experiments across anomaly detection, data pruning, and label noise detection demonstrate that TimeLAVA produces significantly more informative value scores than existing methods on diverse real-world datasets.

</details>


### [314] [Kernel of Partition Paths: A Unified Representation for Tree Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18853)
*Nicolas Mahler*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文将森林中按节点索引的特征映射统一到一个称为KPP（核路径投影）的框架下，该框架通过路径度量加权坐标，构成平方欧氏路径等距嵌入。KPP用一个非对角的Gram矩阵统一了预测、精确加性归因、KPP度量下的确定性Lipschitz鲁棒半径，以及三种条件设定下的均匀Rademacher风险界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作将单棵决策树重构为基于分裂特征工程的线性模型，但未能回答“当按节点（而非分裂）索引特征映射时，森林会导出怎样的统一几何对象”这一核心问题。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，为森林模型提供统一的几何表征与理论分析框架。

Method: KPP按森林的节点对特征映射进行索引，并通过路径度量进行加权，使每个坐标成为平方欧氏路径等距嵌入的一个分量。由此构造出一个非对角的Gram矩阵，该矩阵同时承载度量结构。在此统一框架下，作者分别推导了：(1) 预测表达式；(2) 精确加性归因（特征重要性分解）；(3) KPP度量下的确定性Lipschitz鲁棒半径；(4) 在固定条件、诚实条件和交叉拟合条件三种显式条件设定下，回归与分类的均匀Rademacher风险界。

Result: 所有概率性保证均以表示为条件，并在三种显式条件设定下陈述；鲁棒半径保证在KPP度量下是确定性的，而非依赖于原始输入空间上的范数。关于回归与分类的推测性快速率改进被列为开放问题，未作为定理声明。

Conclusion: KPP框架成功将森林模型的预测、归因、鲁棒性和泛化界四大理论支柱统一于一个非对角的Gram矩阵与路径度量之下，为随机森林的理论分析提供了全新的几何视角。快速率改进仍待未来工作解决。

Abstract: A recent line of work has reframed individual decision trees as linear models on engineered features associated with their splits, opening routes for oracle inequalities and feature-importance reinterpretation, but leaving open the question of what unified geometric object a forest induces when one indexes its feature map by nodes rather than by splits. The present paper studies that object. KPP indexes the feature map by the nodes of the forest, weighted by a path metric that turns each coordinate into a component of a squared-Euclidean path-isometric embedding. KPP unifies four pillars under a single non-diagonal Gram that carries a metric: prediction, exact additive attribution, deterministic Lipschitz robust radius in the KPP metric, and uniform Rademacher risk bounds for regression and classification under fixed, honest, or cross-fit conditioning. All probabilistic guarantees are conditional on the representation and are stated under three explicit conditioning regimes; the robust-radius guarantee is deterministic in the KPP metric rather than in a norm on the raw input. Conjectured fast-rate refinements for both regression and classification are stated as open problems and are not claimed as theorems.

</details>


### [315] [FOSC-X: An Extended Framework for Optimal Local Cuts and Non-Horizontal Cluster Selection from Clustering Hierarchies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18972)
*Connor Simpson,Ricardo J. G. B. Campello*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: FOSC-X 是一个从层次聚类树中提取 top-M 个全局最优扁平聚类的框架，支持可选的聚类数量约束。通过动态规划策略（带约束时引入上下界剪枝），该方法在有无约束条件下均能以线性时间复杂度保证最优排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从层次聚类中提取扁平聚类的方法只关注寻找单一最优解，忽略了层次结构中可能蕴含的多种高质量替代聚类方案。引入聚类数量约束后，无约束动态规划的最优子结构性质被破坏，需要新的算法策略来解决带约束的 top-M 最优提取问题。

Method: 提出 FOSC-X 框架：(1) 无约束情况下，利用动态规划自底向上合并子树中的局部最优部分候选解，形成全局最优解，自动确定聚类数；(2) 带聚类数量约束时，采用基于上下界可行性的动态规划策略，维护紧凑的可行候选集，剪枝不可行或被支配的组合，保证 top-M 最优排名。

Result: FOSC-X 在有无聚类数量约束的条件下，均能保证 top-M 解的全局最优排名，且时间复杂度与聚类节点数和数据集大小呈线性关系。实验表明，该方法能有效揭示被单一解提取方法忽略的替代聚类结构。

Conclusion: FOSC-X 成功解决了层次聚类中带约束与不带约束的 top-M 最优扁平聚类提取问题，兼具理论最优性保证和线性时间效率，为实际聚类分析中探索多种高质量聚类方案提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Extracting a flat clustering solution from a hierarchy is a common task in practical cluster analysis and can be formulated as an optimisation problem. Existing approaches focus on finding a single optimal solution. We introduce FOSC-X, a framework for extracting the top-M globally optimal flat clusterings from local, non-horizontal cuts of a hierarchical cluster tree, while optionally enforcing constraints on the number of clusters. This enables automatic identification of multiple high-quality alternative clusterings that capture different aspects of the hierarchical structure. Without constraints, the top-M problem can be solved in polynomial time using dynamic programming, exploiting the property that locally optimal partial candidates within subtrees can be combined to form globally optimal solutions while automatically determining the number of clusters. However, this can lead to solutions with numbers of clusters that are ultimately undesirable -- e.g., too large to be meaningful or practically analysed within a particular application domain. Imposing cluster-count constraints breaks the optimality property underlying the unconstrained dynamic programming approach, since locally optimal partial candidates may no longer combine into feasible globally optimal solutions. FOSC-X addresses this challenge through a dynamic programming strategy that maintains compact sets of feasible candidates using lower and upper feasibility bounds while pruning infeasible or dominated combinations. The resulting method guarantees optimal rankings of the top-M solutions with linear-time complexity in the number of cluster nodes and dataset size, both with and without cluster-count constraints. Experiments show that FOSC-X efficiently reveals alternative clustering structures overlooked by single-solution extraction methods.

</details>


### [316] [Sequential Kernel-based Conditional Independence Testing via Adaptive Betting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18993)
*Zheng He,Danica J. Sutherland*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种对条件分布估计误差具有更强鲁棒性的序贯条件独立性检验方法，通过 betting 框架结合自适应优化的核条件独立性统计量、归一化方案以及截断平移校准策略，在控制第一类错误的同时保持高检验效能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序贯条件独立性检验（如 Model-X 范式下的方法）通常要求条件分布精确已知；当该分布需从数据估计时，微小误差即可导致严重的 Type I error 膨胀，这使得现有方法在实际应用中非常脆弱。因此需要一种对估计误差更鲁棒的序贯检验方法。

Method: 作者将 testing-by-betting（对赌检验）框架应用于自适应优化的核条件独立性统计量（Kernel Conditional Independence statistic），并引入两个关键创新：(1) 归一化方案（normalization scheme）以稳定统计量的尺度；(2) 截断平移校准策略（truncate-and-shift calibration）以校正估计误差带来的偏差，从而控制序贯检验中的 Type I error。

Result: 在高维合成基准测试和真实公平性任务中，所提方法相比现有序贯 Model-X 方法显著降低了 Type I error 膨胀，同时保持了高检验效能（power），展现出对条件分布估计误差的强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法成功弥合了序贯检验中对 Model-X 条件分布精确已知要求与实际估计需求之间的鸿沟，为条件独立性检验提供了更实用的序贯方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Testing conditional independence is fundamental yet intrinsically difficult: without additional assumptions, Type I error control is impossible in general. The "Model-X'' paradigm addresses this difficulty by assuming exact knowledge of a relevant conditional distribution. While small deviations from this assumption can sometimes be tolerated in classical one-shot testing, existing sequential conditional independence tests typically require the Model-X conditional to be known exactly, making them fragile when it must instead be estimated. We propose a new approach that is substantially more robust to such estimation error. Our method applies testing-by-betting to an adaptively optimized Kernel Conditional Independence statistic, together with a normalization scheme and a truncate-and-shift calibration strategy. These modifications greatly reduce Type I error inflation while preserving high power across high-dimensional synthetic benchmarks and real-world fairness tasks, outperforming existing sequential Model-X approaches. Code is available at https://github.com/he-zh/SKCI.

</details>


### [317] [Quantifying and Auditing LLM Evaluation via Positive--Unlabeled Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19057)
*Zilong Zhang,Yi-Ting Hung,Lei Ding,Chi-Kuang Yeh*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM评估中的选择性人工监督问题形式化为正-未标记学习（PU Learning），并基于部分最优传输（Partial Optimal Transport）构建几何审计框架，无需重新训练即可纠正LLM评委的系统性偏差（如冗长偏差），提升与人类偏好的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge存在系统性偏差（尤其是冗长偏差），且人工标注成本高昂、具有选择性（仅给出可靠正例，大量输出未标记）。现有方法难以在这种半监督场景下有效纠正偏差。

Method: 将问题形式化为正-未标记学习（PU Learning）；提出基于部分最优传输（Partial Optimal Transport）的几何审计框架：在固定嵌入空间中，将少量人工验证的正例与可靠的未标记输出子集进行对齐，从而识别人群一致的偏好并纠正有偏的LLM评委，无需重新训练模型。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在与人类偏好对齐、对呈现偏差的鲁棒性以及可解释置信度估计方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 该框架为LLM-as-a-Judge提供了一种可扩展、有统计基础的替代方案，能够有效利用选择性人工监督来审计和纠正LLM评委的偏差。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used as judges for scalable evaluation, yet such LLM--as--a--Judge systems exhibit systematic biases that are decoupled from semantic quality, most notably verbosity bias. Meanwhile, human supervision is costly and typically selective, yielding reliable positive judgments but leaving most outputs unlabelled and potentially mixed in quality. We formulate LLM evaluation under selective human supervision as a positive--unlabelled learning problem and propose a geometric auditing framework based on Partial Optimal Transport. By aligning a small set of human--verified positives with a reliable subset of unlabelled outputs in a fixed embedding space, our method identifies human--consistent preferences and corrects biased judges without retraining. Experiments demonstrate improved alignment with human preferences, increased robustness to presentation biases, and interpretable confidence estimates, offering a scalable and statistically grounded alternative to existing LLM--as--a--judge pipelines.

</details>


### [318] [On Local Population-Risk Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19147)
*Mingzhi Song*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文构建了围绕当前模型的人口风险增量的局部置信带（local certificates），并将上界端点用于风险控制更新规则：仅当候选模型的风险增量上界非正时才接受更新，否则保留当前模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器学习模型的迭代更新中，核心挑战在于如何判断一个候选模型是否真正优于当前模型，而非因有限样本的随机波动造成虚假改进。作者旨在提供一种具有统计保证的局部检验方法，以控制更新过程中的风险膨胀。

Method: 针对当前参数 θ 附近的局部候选集 D，构建 P(ℓ_{θ+v} - ℓ_θ) 的双侧置信带，即对 v ∈ D 上风险增量函数的均匀覆盖。基于该置信带的上界端点，制定风险受控的更新规则：仅当候选模型对应的置信带上界 ≤ 0 时接受更新。

Result: 所提出的局部置信带为风险增量提供了有限样本或渐近的均匀覆盖保证；由此导出的更新规则能够以高概率避免接受风险高于当前模型的候选模型，实现了风险控制。

Conclusion: 通过在局部邻域内构造风险增量的置信带，可以为模型更新决策提供严格的统计保证，有效防止因过拟合或随机波动导致的性能退化。

Abstract: This paper develops local certificates for population-risk increments around a current model. For a local candidate set \(\mathcal D\), the certificate is a two-sided confidence band for \(P({\ell_{θ+v}-\ell_θ})\) over \(v\in\mathcal D\). As an application, the upper endpoint of this band yields a risk-controlled update rule: an update is accepted only when its certified upper endpoint is nonpositive; otherwise the current model is retained.

</details>


### [319] [Generalised Eigenvalue Geometry of Semantic Adversarial Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19212)
*Martin Anthony,Kaveh Salehzadeh Nobari*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于广义特征值的连续局部模型，用于量化金融情感分类器在面对语义等价改写时的"可攻击性"。该模型通过两个嵌入映射的雅可比矩阵构造矩阵束，其最大广义特征值 λ*(x) 能刻画最坏情况下的表征位移，并提供闭式预测翻转条件和有限样本攻击性证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒性理论要么假设单模型威胁模型，要么仅关注经验攻击算法，无法有效解释语义等价改写为何能愚弄金融情感分类器——即使改写内容在参考嵌入下与原句接近，却能显著偏移目标模型的表征并改变预测类别。这暴露了当前理论在双模型结构下的空白。

Method: 作者构建了一个连续局部模型来捕捉语义改写扰动中的"双模型结构"：一个参考嵌入（代理模型）约束改写幅度，另一个目标嵌入刻画分类器输入。核心分析通过构造两个嵌入映射雅可比矩阵的矩阵束 (A, B)，取其最大广义特征值 λ*(x) 作为"可攻击性指数"。基于此指数，推导了仿射读出层的闭式预测翻转条件，建立了二值可攻击性指标的分布无关VC界，以及基于"攻击性调整边际"的尺度敏感边际界。进一步将连续理论与离散改写搜索连接，给出了离散与连续设定一致的覆盖条件，并识别出成功与不成功有限搜索之间的不对称性。

Result: 理论层面：λ*(x) 在局部改写几何中是内禀的，能够提供保守的总体和有限样本可攻击性证书；对仿射读出层类别的均匀控制下，得到了二值攻击性指标的VC界和攻击性调整边际界。实证层面：在部署的金融文本分类器上，通过软标记松弛和生成改写集验证了局部特征值几何、预测翻转条件以及有限搜索近似的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的连续局部模型成功将语义改写攻击形式化为双嵌入结构的广义特征值问题，λ*(x) 作为可攻击性指数兼具理论优雅性和实践可验证性，为金融NLP系统的鲁棒性评估和认证提供了新的分析框架。

Abstract: Recent empirical work shows that semantically equivalent paraphrases can fool financial sentiment classifiers: although a paraphrase remains close to the original under a strong reference embedding, it may shift the target model's representation enough to change the predicted class. Existing robustness theory either assumes a single-model threat model or focuses mainly on empirical attack algorithms. We develop a continuous local model of semantic paraphrase perturbations that captures this two-model structure. We show that the worst-case local displacement of the target representation, subject to a proxy-model budget, is governed by the largest generalised eigenvalue of a matrix pencil $(A,B)$ constructed from the Jacobians of the two embedding maps. The resulting attackability index $λ^*(x)$ is intrinsic to the local paraphrase geometry and the chosen embedders, yields a closed-form prediction-flip condition for affine readouts, and supports conservative population and finite-sample attackability certificates. For uniform control over classes of affine readouts, we derive a distribution-free VC bound for binary attackability indicators and a scale-sensitive margin bound based on an attackability-adjusted margin that subtracts a local geometric penalty from the standard classifier margin. We also connect the continuous theory to discrete paraphrase search, identify an asymmetry between successful and unsuccessful finite searches, and give a covering condition under which the discrete and continuous settings agree. Finally, we propose an empirical verification framework using soft-token relaxations and generated paraphrase sets to assess the local eigenvalue geometry, prediction-flip condition, and finite-search approximation on a deployed financial-text classifier.

</details>
